000000r 2               SYSTEM_TASK_NUM     = 0
000000r 2               SHELL_TASK_NUM      = $01       ; Forth / WOZMON shell (the default serial-capture task)
000000r 2               PIPE_TASK_NUM       = $0D       ; Pipe server (resident)
000000r 2               STORAGE_TASK_NUM    = $0C       ; Storage: the SD card (and later FAT32) server (resident)
000000r 2               SOUND_TASK_NUM      = $0E       ; Sound driver (resident)
000000r 2               SERIAL_TASK_NUM     = $0F       ; Serial driver (resident)
000000r 2               SERIAL_OWNER_TASK   = SERIAL_TASK_NUM   ; Task whose ZP holds the serial driver state
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ERROR VALUES
000000r 2               ERR_SUCCESS         = $00
000000r 2               ERR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK = $01
000000r 2               ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY   = $02
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ERR_MATH_INVD       = $31       ; invalid digit
000000r 2               ERR_MATH_INVB       = $32       ; invalid base
000000r 2               ERR_MATH_OVF        = $33       ; overflow
000000r 2               ERR_MATH_UNF        = $34       ; underflow
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_NOT_ALLOC   = $40
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID   = $41
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_NOT_SUPPORTED = $42
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG     = $43
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES  = $44
000000r 2               ERR_MEM_LOCKED      = $45
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL  = $50
000000r 2               ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND   = $51
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND    = $70       ; no such file / device
000000r 2               ERR_IO_BAD_FD       = $71       ; fd not open (or out of range)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_MODE         = $72       ; not opened for that (e.g. write on a read-only fd)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK  = $73       ; no data yet (non-blocking fd; servers return it to make the IO layer wait)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_EOF          = $74       ; end of file (IO_GETC)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NO_FDS       = $75       ; all of the task's fds are open
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NO_DEVS      = $76       ; the device table is full
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NAME         = $77       ; bad or duplicate device name
000000r 2               ERR_IO_BAD_REQ      = $78       ; the server doesn't support that request
000000r 2               ERR_IO_DEVICE       = $79       ; the device didn't respond
000000r 2               ERR_IO_BROKEN       = $7A       ; write to a pipe nobody reads
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NO_PIPES     = $7B       ; all pipes are in use
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NS_FULL      = $7C       ; the task's namespace is full
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NS_LOOP      = $7D       ; too many binds in a row (a loop?)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_NOT_READY    = $7E       ; the device isn't started (e.g. the SD card: open /dev/sd first)
000000r 2               ERR_IO_MEDIA        = $7F       ; the medium refused the command or the data (e.g. the SD card)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  IO (see IO_PLAN.md)  ***
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; File descriptors: per task, in the task system page after the IRQ tables (IRQ_SPURIOUS ends at $7D9F)
000000r 2               IO_MAX_FDS          = 12
000000r 2               IO_FD_SIZE          = 8
000000r 2               IO_FD_TABLE         = $7DA0     ; IO_MAX_FDS x IO_FD_SIZE
000000r 2               IO_FD_SERVER        = IO_FD_TABLE + 0   ; device table index ($FF = fd not open)
000000r 2               IO_FD_FID           = IO_FD_TABLE + 1   ; the server's handle for the open file
000000r 2               IO_FD_MODE          = IO_FD_TABLE + 2   ; IO_MODE_* bits
000000r 2               IO_FD_OFS           = IO_FD_TABLE + 4   ; 32-bit offset
000000r 2               IO_FD_CLOSED        = $FF
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IO_MODE_READ        = $01
000000r 2               IO_MODE_WRITE       = $02
000000r 2               IO_MODE_RDWR        = IO_MODE_READ | IO_MODE_WRITE
000000r 2               IO_MODE_NONBLOCK    = $80       ; reads return ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK instead of waiting
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; The system's data in shared RAM: shared bank ID $00 (U = 0, bank $F0): the shared memory tables
000000r 2               ; (shared.s) and the device table (below)
000000r 2               SYS_SHARED_U        = 0
000000r 2               SYS_BANK            = $F0
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Save RAM_BANK_REG and U on the stack and select shared bank ID $00.  Uses .Y
000000r 2               .macro _M_SYS_ENTER
000000r 2                           ldy         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         #SYS_SHARED_U
000000r 2                           sty         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ldy         #SYS_BANK
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Restore U and RAM_BANK_REG.  Uses .Y; preserves .A and C
000000r 2               .macro _M_SYS_LEAVE
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Device table: in shared bank ID $00 (see shared.s)
000000r 2               IO_MAX_DEVS         = 16
000000r 2               IO_DEV_SIZE         = 16
000000r 2               IO_DEV_TABLE        = $8800     ; IO_MAX_DEVS x IO_DEV_SIZE
000000r 2               IO_DEV_NAME         = 0         ; 8 bytes, zero-padded (free entry: first byte 0)
000000r 2               IO_DEV_NAME_LEN     = 8
000000r 2               IO_DEV_TASK         = 8         ; task the serve routine runs in ($FF = the calling task)
000000r 2               IO_DEV_SERVE        = 9         ; serve routine (ROM page 0 address)
000000r 2               IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK  = $FF
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; IO transfer areas: shared bank ID $09 (U = 0, bank $F9), 512 bytes per task at $8000 + task * $200:
000000r 2               ; the request block, then (at + $100) up to IO_UNIT bytes of data
000000r 2               IO_XFER_BANK_ID     = $09
000000r 2               IO_XFER_BANK        = $F0 | IO_XFER_BANK_ID
000000r 2               IO_UNIT             = 256
000000r 2               IO_BLK_TYPE         = 0         ; H9_* request
000000r 2               IO_BLK_FID          = 1
000000r 2               IO_BLK_MODE         = 2         ; open mode
000000r 2               IO_BLK_CLIENT       = 3         ; the task making the request
000000r 2               IO_BLK_OFS          = 4         ; 32-bit offset (read / write)
000000r 2               IO_BLK_COUNT        = 8         ; 16-bit byte count: requested (in), done (out)
000000r 2               IO_BLK_CTL_CODE     = 11        ; H9_CTL
000000r 2               IO_BLK_CTL_ARG      = 12
000000r 2               IO_BLK_NS           = $20       ; The task's namespace (below)
000000r 2               IO_BLK_DATA         = $100
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Per-task namespace (IO_MOUNT, IO_BIND): in the task's IO transfer area, after the request block.
000000r 2               ; IO_OPEN applies the entry with the longest matching path prefix: a mount sends the rest of the name to
000000r 2               ; a device's server, a bind rewrites the name (prefix -> target) and looks again.  No match: the global
000000r 2               ; /dev.  New tasks get a copy of their parent's namespace.
000000r 2               NS_ENTRIES          = 7         ; ($20-$FF of the area)
000000r 2               NS_ENTRY_SIZE       = 32
000000r 2               NS_TYPE             = 0         ; NS_FREE, NS_MOUNT or NS_BIND
000000r 2               NS_DEV              = 1         ; Mount: the device (device table index)
000000r 2               NS_PREFIX           = 2         ; The path prefix, zero-terminated (13 characters at most)
000000r 2               NS_PREFIX_MAX       = 13
000000r 2               NS_TARGET           = 16        ; Bind: what the prefix stands for, zero-terminated (15 at most)
000000r 2               NS_TARGET_MAX       = 15
000000r 2               NS_FREE             = 0
000000r 2               NS_MOUNT            = 1
000000r 2               NS_BIND             = 2
000000r 2               NS_MAX_REWRITES     = 4         ; Binds applied to one name, at most
000000r 2               .assert     IO_BLK_NS + NS_ENTRIES * NS_ENTRY_SIZE <= IO_BLK_DATA, error, "The namespace must fit below the data area"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; H9P requests (serve routine: .A = request, .X = client task, .Y = fid; the request block is in the
000000r 2               ; client's transfer area, see IO_SRV_MAP.  OUT: C = 0 (.A = fid for H9_OPEN), or C = 1 and .A = error)
000000r 2               H9_OPEN             = 1         ; name remainder (after the device name) in the data area -> fid
000000r 2               H9_READ             = 2         ; offset, count -> data, count done
000000r 2               H9_WRITE            = 3         ; offset, count, data -> count done
000000r 2               H9_CLUNK            = 4         ; close fid
000000r 2               H9_STAT             = 5         ; -> 16-byte stat block in the data area
000000r 2               H9_CTL              = 6         ; device-specific control: code, arg
000000r 2               H9_DUP              = 7         ; another fd refers to the fid now (IO_DUP2, or inherited by a new task)
000000r 2               ; Every request also carries the fd's mode (IO_BLK_MODE), so a server can tell the ends of a pipe apart.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Sound driver: /dev/snd (sound.s, snd_srv.s).  Writes are YM2151 register/value byte pairs.
000000r 2               SND_CTL_INIT        = 1         ; IO_CTL code: clear the YM2151
000000r 2               SND_CTL_TEST        = 2         ; IO_CTL code: play the test sound
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Storage: /dev/sd (storage.s, spi.s, sd.s, sd_srv.s; ROM page 3)
000000r 2               SD_SPI_DEVICE       = 0         ; The SD card's SPI device (header J18)
000000r 2               SD_STATE_SDSC       = 1         ; SD_STATE: 0 = not started; byte addresses
000000r 2               SD_STATE_SDHC       = 2         ;   block addresses (SDHC, SDXC)
000000r 2               SD_CTL_INIT         = 1         ; IO_CTL code: start the card again (e.g. after changing it)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Pipe server: /dev/pipe, in its own Resident task (io_p0.s, pipe_srv.s).  Each pipe has a 256-byte ring
000000r 2               ; (255 bytes of data) and counts the fds on each end; the fid is the pipe's index.
000000r 2               PIPE_MAX            = 8
000000r 2               PIPE_TABLE          = $0200     ; In the pipe task's RAM: PIPE_MAX x PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000000r 2               PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE     = 8
000000r 2               PIPE_READERS        = PIPE_TABLE + 0    ; fds on the read end
000000r 2               PIPE_WRITERS        = PIPE_TABLE + 1    ; fds on the write end (none left: readers get end of file)
000000r 2               PIPE_HEAD           = PIPE_TABLE + 2    ; next byte in
000000r 2               PIPE_TAIL           = PIPE_TABLE + 3    ; next byte out (empty: head = tail)
000000r 2               PIPE_RD_WAIT        = 4         ; (Entry offsets, 2 bytes each) tasks waiting to read (bit = task)
000000r 2               PIPE_WR_WAIT        = 6         ;   and to write
000000r 2               PIPE_BUFS           = $0300     ; In the pipe task's RAM: the rings, one page each
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Serial driver: the /dev/cons and /dev/ser server (bios.s, ser_srv.s)
000000r 2               SER_RX_BUF          = $0200     ; 256-byte RX ring, in the serial task's RAM
000000r 2               SER_TX_BUF          = $0300     ; 256-byte TX ring, in the serial task's RAM
000000r 2               SER_FID_CONS        = 0         ; /dev/cons: reads only for the foreground task
000000r 2               SER_FID_SER         = 1         ; /dev/ser: the raw port, for any task
000000r 2               SER_CTL_FOREGROUND  = 1         ; IO_CTL code: .Y = the new foreground task (it gets the console input)
000000r 2               ; Console control keys (the serial IRQ handler acts on the first two; /dev/cons on the EOF keys)
000000r 2               SER_KEY_BREAK       = $03       ; Ctrl-C: break the foreground task (back to its prompt: TASK_SET_BREAK)
000000r 2               SER_KEY_KILL        = $1C       ; Ctrl-\: kill the foreground task (the shell starts again)
000000r 2               SER_KEY_EOF         = $04       ; Ctrl-D: end of input (a /dev/cons read returns end of file)
000000r 2               SER_KEY_EOF2        = $1A       ; Ctrl-Z: the same
000000r 2               
000000r 2               RESET_ENTRY     = $E000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IO_PORT_BASE    = $FF00
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; TIMING
000000r 2               ; CPU clock: a build-time setting for now (board V2 will have a clock jumper register to read).  Timing
000000r 2               ; constants are derived from it.  Above 3.58 MHz the YM2151 (on its own 3.58 MHz clock) needs bus wait
000000r 2               ; states, which need the V2 RDY hold hardware (see IO_PLAN.md), so don't use the sound chip until then.
000000r 2               CPU_CLOCK_MULT  = 1         ; 1 = 3.58 MHz, 2 = 7.16 MHz
000000r 2               .assert     CPU_CLOCK_MULT = 1 .or CPU_CLOCK_MULT = 2, error, "CPU_CLOCK_MULT must be 1 (3.58 MHz) or 2 (7.16 MHz)"
000000r 2               CLK_BASE_CPS    = 3579545   ; ~3.58 MHz (the 14.318 MHz crystal / 4)
000000r 2               CLK_CPS         = CLK_BASE_CPS * CPU_CLOCK_MULT
000000r 2               CLK_CPMS        = (CLK_CPS / 1000) + 1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER, 65C51  ***
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_2400         = $0A
000000r 2               SR_4800         = $0C
000000r 2               SR_9600         = $0E
000000r 2               SR_19200        = $0F
000000r 2               SR_115200       = $00
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_SELECT       = SR_19200
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if SR_SELECT = SR_2400
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 2400
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_4800
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 4800
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_9600
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 9600
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_19200
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 19200
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 115200
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; The ACIA chip (a build option).  The Rockwell R65C51 sends from its transmitter-empty (TDRE) interrupt.
000000r 2               ; The WDC W65C51N's TDRE status bit and interrupt don't work (a known chip bug), so its sending is paced
000000r 2               ; by the VIA's timer 2 instead: a one-shot of one character's time (10 bits, and a margin) per byte, whose
000000r 2               ; interrupt sends the next.  Receiving is the same on both.  (VIA T2 is the serial driver's then.)
000000r 2               SER_ACIA_ROCKWELL = 0
000000r 2               SER_ACIA_WDC    = 1
000000r 2               SER_ACIA        = SER_ACIA_ROCKWELL
000000r 2               SER_CHAR_CYCLES = CLK_CPS * 11 / SERIAL_RATE                ; (WDC: the timer 2 count per character)
000000r 2               .assert     SER_CHAR_CYCLES < $10000, error, "SER_CHAR_CYCLES must fit VIA timer 2: a faster serial rate"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  END OF ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER  ***

000000r 2               
000000r 2               ACIA_R_DATA         = IO_PORT_BYTE ACIA, 0
000000r 2               ACIA_R_STATUS       = IO_PORT_BYTE ACIA, 1
000000r 2               ACIA_R_CMD          = IO_PORT_BYTE ACIA, 2
000000r 2               ACIA_R_CTRL         = IO_PORT_BYTE ACIA, 3
000000r 2               
000000r 2               VIA_T1_INT_BIT      = $40       ; VIA_R_INT_FLAGS / VIA_R_INT_ENABLE: timer 1 (the scheduler's tick)
000000r 2               VIA_T2_INT_BIT      = $20       ;   timer 2 (the WDC ACIA's TX pacing)
000000r 2               VIA_INT_ENABLE      = $80       ; VIA_R_INT_ENABLE: set (1) or clear (0) the bits given
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Send .A to the ACIA (the serial task, IRQs off, the transmitter idle or its last byte just sent), and
000000r 2               ; for the WDC 65C51 start timer 2, which stands in for its missing TDRE interrupt (SER_ACIA).  Uses .Y
000000r 2               .macro _M_SER_TX_BYTE
000000r 2                           sta         ACIA_R_DATA
000000r 2               .if ::SER_ACIA = ::SER_ACIA_WDC                             ; (:: so it's constant in the page scopes too)
000000r 2                           ldy         #<SER_CHAR_CYCLES
000000r 2                           sty         VIA_R_T2C_L
000000r 2                           ldy         #>SER_CHAR_CYCLES
000000r 2                           sty         VIA_R_T2C_H                 ; (Starts the one-shot, and clears its IRQ)
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .define  IRQ_NUMBER(num)    (num ^ 7)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; IRQs, from highest priority (0) to lowest (15)

000000r 2               IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL = IRQ_NUMBER(1)      ; On-board serial

000000r 2               ; SPI DATA BITS
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CLK     = 1     ; bit 0, so INC/DEC cycle the clock
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CSB     = 2     ; bit 1
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MOSI    = 4     ; bit 2
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_1    = 8     ; bit 3
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_2    = $10   ; bit 4
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_4    = $20   ; bit 5
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_8    = $40   ; bit 6
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MISO    = $80   ; bit 7, so BIT opcode stores MISO in N
000000r 2

000000r 2               SPI_DEV_1       = SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_2       = SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_3       = SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_4       = SPI_BIT_CS_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_5       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_6       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_7       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_8       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_0
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_9       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_A       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_B       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_3
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_C       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_D       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_5
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_E       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_6
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_F       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_7
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DDR_BITS    = SPI_BIT_CLK | SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_DEV_F
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_INIT_DELAY_CYCLES = 80
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; max task idle
000000r 2               MAX_TASK_NUMBER = $0F           ; 16 tasks, numbered 0-F
000000r 2               NUM_RAM_MODULES = 15            ;
000000r 2               NUM_BANKS_PER_MODULE = 16
000000r 2               NUM_RAM_BANKS   = NUM_RAM_MODULES * NUM_BANKS_PER_MODULE
000000r 2               
000000r 2               T_REGISTER = $FFF0 ; IO_PORT_BYTE IO_PORT_F, 0
000000r 2               U_REGISTER = $FFF1 ; IO_PORT_BYTE IO_PORT_F, 1
000000r 2               V_REGISTER = $FFF2 ; IO_PORT_BYTE IO_PORT_F, 2
000000r 2               W_REGISTER = $FFF3 ; IO_PORT_BYTE IO_PORT_F, 3
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ERROR CODES
000000r 2               ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE = $F1
000000r 2               ERR_TASK_BUSY       = $F2
000000r 2               ERR_BAD_TASK        = $F3
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  TASK ZERO-PAGE ALLOCATION  ***
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; OS/BIOS ZP grows up from $02 (the ZEROPAGE segment, zero.s) and has the same layout in every task.
000000r 2               ; Task ZP grows down from $FF.  Each task image (shell, sound driver, ...) brackets its ZP with:
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;       TASK_ZP_BEGIN
000000r 2               ;       TASK_ZP     NAME, size      ; NAME = next free address below the previous one
000000r 2               ;       TASK_ZP_END                 ; link-time check that task ZP doesn't collide with OS ZP
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; Task images run in different tasks, so their task ZP may overlap each other.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .import     __ZEROPAGE_RUN__, __ZEROPAGE_SIZE__
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro TASK_ZP_BEGIN
000000r 2               task_zp_top .set $100
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro TASK_ZP  name, size
000000r 2               task_zp_top .set task_zp_top - (size)
000000r 2               name = task_zp_top
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro TASK_ZP_END
000000r 2               .assert     task_zp_top >= __ZEROPAGE_RUN__ + __ZEROPAGE_SIZE__, lderror, "Task ZP overlaps OS ZP"
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Task switcher interrupt timer (one interrupt per 5ms or so, with 64 cycles for INT Handler overhead)
000000r 2               SCHED_TICK_HZ   = 200
000000r 2               TIMER_TASK_INT  = (CLK_CPS / SCHED_TICK_HZ) - 64           ; 17833 at 3.58 MHz
000000r 2               .assert     TIMER_TASK_INT < $10000, error, "Scheduler tick doesn't fit VIA T1: raise SCHED_TICK_HZ"
000000r 2               TIMER_TASK_INT_H = >TIMER_TASK_INT
000000r 2               TIMER_TASK_INT_L = <TIMER_TASK_INT
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ASCII CODES
000000r 2               ASCII_BACKSPACE = $08
000000r 2               ASCII_DEL       = $7F       ; (What many terminals send for the Backspace key: /dev/cons makes it a BS)
000000r 2               ASCII_TAB       = $09
000000r 2               ASCII_LF        = $0A
000000r 2               ASCII_CR        = $0D
000000r 2               ASCII_ESC       = $1B
000000r 2               ASCII_SPACE     = ' '
000000r 2               ASCII_BANG      = '!'
000000r 2               ASCII_DQUOTE    = '"'
000000r 2               ASCII_SQUOTE    = '''
000000r 2               ASCII_HASH      = '#'
000000r 2               ASCII_DOLLAR    = '$'
000000r 2               ASCII_PERCENT   = '%'
000000r 2               ASCII_CARET     = '^'
000000r 2               ASCII_AMP       = '&'
000000r 2               ASCII_SQOUTE    = '''
000000r 2               ASCII_LPAREN    = '('
000000r 2               ASCII_RPAREN    = ')'
000000r 2               ASCII_STAR      = '*'
000000r 2               ASCII_PLUS      = '+'
000000r 2               ASCII_COMMA     = ','
000000r 2               ASCII_MINUS     = '-'
000000r 2               ASCII_DASH      = ASCII_MINUS
000000r 2               ASCII_HYPHEN    = ASCII_MINUS
000000r 2               ASCII_PERIOD    = '.'
000000r 2               ASCII_DOT       = ASCII_PERIOD
000000r 2               ASCII_SLASH     = '/'
000000r 2               ASCII_0         = '0'
000000r 2               ASCII_1         = '1'
000000r 2               ASCII_2         = '2'
000000r 2               ASCII_3         = '3'
000000r 2               ASCII_4         = '4'
000000r 2               ASCII_5         = '5'
000000r 2               ASCII_6         = '6'
000000r 2               ASCII_7         = '7'
000000r 2               ASCII_8         = '8'
000000r 2               ASCII_9         = '9'
000000r 2               ASCII_COLON     = ':'
000000r 2               ASCII_SEMI      = ':'
000000r 2               ASCII_LT        = '<'
000000r 2               ASCII_EQ        = '='
000000r 2               ASCII_GT        = '>'
000000r 2               ASCII_QUESTION  = '?'
000000r 2               ASCII_A         = 'A'
000000r 2               ASCII_B         = 'B'
000000r 2               ASCII_C         = 'C'
000000r 2               ASCII_D         = 'D'
000000r 2               ASCII_E         = 'E'
000000r 2               ASCII_F         = 'F'
000000r 2               ASCII_G         = 'G'
000000r 2               ASCII_H         = 'H'
000000r 2               ASCII_I         = 'I'

000000r 2               ASCII_K         = 'K'
000000r 2               ASCII_L         = 'L'
000000r 2               ASCII_M         = 'M'

000000r 2               ASCII_f         = 'f'
000000r 2               ASCII_m         = 'm'
000000r 2               ASCII_q         = 'q'
000000r 2               ASCII_s         = 's'

000000r 2               ASCII_PIPE      = '|'

000000r 2               ASCII_BACKSLASH = '\'
000000r 2               ASCII_RBRACKET  = ']'
000000r 2               ASCII_LBRACE    = '{'

000000r 2               
000000r 2               ASCII_LETTER_OFFSET = ASCII_A-ASCII_0-10

000000r 2               
000000r 2               F_CELL          := 2

000000r 2               ; write a byte in A to the IO PORT
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_WRITE    port, byte, imm
000000r 2               .ifnblank       imm
000000r 2                               lda     #imm
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               sta     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               sta     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; read a byte into A from the IO PORT/Byte (PORT_N | BYTE_M)
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_READ     port, byte
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               lda     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2                               pha
000000r 2                               phx

000000r 2                               pla
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ; convenience macros
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; MOV
000000r 2               ; Modifies: A
000000r 2               .macro MOV              addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               lda     addr1
000000r 2                               sta     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVA             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVX             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               ldx     addr1
000000r 2                               stx     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVY             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               ldy     addr1
000000r 2                               sty     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOV16            addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2                               MOV     {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOV16_HL         addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2                               MOV     {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVA16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV16   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVA16_HL        addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV16_HL   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVX16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVX    {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2                               MOVX    {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVX16_HL        addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVX    {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2                               MOVX    {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVY16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVY    {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2                               MOVY    {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVY16_HL        addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVY    {addr1 + 1}, {addr2 + 1}
000000r 2                               MOVY    {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAX            addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               lda     addr1, X
000000r 2                               sta     addr2, X
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAY            addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               lda     addr1, Y
000000r 2                               sta     addr2, Y
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAX16          addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVAX   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2                               inx
000000r 2                               MOVAX   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAY16          addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVAY   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2                               iny
000000r 2                               MOVAY   {addr1}, {addr2}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; X: # of bytes to move
000000r 2               ; Clobbers A
000000r 2               .macro BLKMOVX          addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               phy
000000r 2                               txa
000000r 2                               tay
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dey
000000r 2                               lda     addr1, Y
000000r 2                               sta     addr2, Y
000000r 2                               bne     :-
000000r 2                               ply
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Y: # of bytes to move
000000r 2               ; Clobbers A, Y
000000r 2               .macro BLKMOVY          addr1, addr2
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dey
000000r 2                               lda     addr1, Y
000000r 2                               sta     addr2, Y
000000r 2                               bne     :-
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_ADDTO        addr
000000r 2                               adc     {addr}
000000r 2                               sta     {addr}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; _M_INCC: inc and set C/V if rollover.  Clobbers .A, C
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC    addr
000000r 2                               sec
000000r 2                               lda     #0
000000r 2                               _M_ADDTO    {addr}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC16       addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               _M_INCC addr + 1
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr + 1
000000r 2                               ora     #1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC32       addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               _M_INCC16   addr+2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  INC16_BARE      addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr + 1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC16_BARE      addr
000000r 2                               lda     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               dec     addr+1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  INC16           addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr + 1
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr + 1
000000r 2                               ora     #1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  INC32           addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               INC16   addr+2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC16           addr
000000r 2                               lda     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               dec     addr+1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               lda     addr+1      ; LOB is zero, so use Z and N from HOB
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr+1
000000r 2                               ora     #1          ; reset Z, if set, without affecting N
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC32           addr
000000r 2                               lda     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+++
000000r 2                               cmp     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :++
000000r 2                               cmp     addr+2
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               dec     addr+3
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr+2
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr+1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro BNE16    addr1, addr2, dest
000000r 2                               lda     addr1
000000r 2                               cmp     addr2
000000r 2                               bne     dest
000000r 2                               lda     addr1 + 1
000000r 2                               cmp     addr2 + 1
000000r 2                               bne     dest
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; No-clobber (NC) macros to wrap another macro that overwrites one or more registers
000000r 2               .macro  NC_A            MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               pha
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               pla
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_X            MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               phx
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               plx
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_Y            MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               phy
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               ply
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_AX           MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               PUSH_AX
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               PULL_XA
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_AY           MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               PUSH_AY
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               PULL_YA
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_XY           MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               PUSH_XY
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               PULL_YX
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  NC_AXY          MAC, p1, p2
000000r 2                               PUSH_AXY
000000r 2                               MAC     p1, p2
000000r 2                               PULL_YXA
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC16_NC_A      addr
000000r 2                               NC_A    DEC16, addr
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .define LOADA(arg)      lda arg

000000r 2               .macro  LDA_CORA    CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .ifnblank   CharOrAddr
000000r 2                               LOADA           CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_CALL    call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_CALL       {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_JMP     call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jmp             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_JMP        {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000085r 2               ; Serial driver task ZP (valid in the serial task; see bios.s).  Here, so page 2 (ser_srv.s) sees them
000085r 2               ; as zero page addresses.  The rings (SER_RX_BUF, SER_TX_BUF) are empty when head = tail.
000085r 2               TASK_ZP_BEGIN
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_RX_HEAD, 1          ; RX ring: next byte in (the IRQ handler)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_RX_TAIL, 1          ;   next byte out (reads)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_TX_HEAD, 1          ; TX ring: next byte in (writes)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_TX_TAIL, 1          ;   next byte out (the IRQ handler)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_RD_WAIT, 2          ; Tasks waiting to read (bit = task), woken when a byte arrives
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SER_WR_WAIT, 2          ; Tasks waiting to write, woken when the TX ring has room
000085r 2               TASK_ZP_END
000085r 2               
000085r 2               ; Storage task ZP (valid in the storage task: SPI, the SD card and its server; spi.s, sd.s, sd_srv.s)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP_BEGIN
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SPI_PORT, 1             ; Port B for the selected device (/CS enable, device, MOSI high)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SPI_IN, 1               ; The byte coming in
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SPI_OUT, 1              ; The byte going out
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_STATE, 1             ; 0 = not started, SD_STATE_SDSC, SD_STATE_SDHC
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_R1, 1                ; The card's last answer (for diagnosis)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_TMP, 1
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_COUNT, 2             ; Tries left
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_ARG, 4               ; A command's argument, MSB first
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_LBA, 4               ; Block number (for SD_READ_BLOCK / SD_WRITE_BLOCK)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_BUF, 2               ;   and its 512 bytes
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_CACHE, 2             ; The block cache (512 bytes)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_CBLOCK, 4            ;   the block in it
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_CVALID, 1            ;   <> 0: it's there
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_CLIENT, 1            ; The server: the request's task
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_OP, 1                ;   H9_READ or H9_WRITE
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_POS, 4               ;   the offset
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_LEFT, 2              ;   bytes left
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_DONE, 1              ;   bytes done (0-255; 256 when finished: then 0)
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_N, 2                 ;   bytes in this block
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_SRC, 2               ;   the cache, and ...
000085r 2               TASK_ZP     SD_DST, 2               ;   the data area, where this block's bytes go
000085r 2               TASK_ZP_END
000085r 2

000085r 2               .feature org_per_seg
000085r 2               .segment "STACK"
000000r 2               
000000r 1               .include "common.s"     ; COMMON block (every ROM page) and gate macros
000000r 2               .debuginfo
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; Code common to every BIOS ROM page (W = $0-$F)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   The COMMON block is emitted into every ROM page at the same address ($FD00), so code in it keeps
000000r 2               ;   running correctly across a write to W: the next instruction is fetched from the new page, at the
000000r 2               ;   same address, and is the same instruction.  It holds:
000000r 2               ;       * the 16 IRQ entry stubs (the IRQ vector RAM always points here) and the IRQ exit
000000r 2               ;       * the NMI entry
000000r 2               ;       * the far-call / far-jump trampolines used to call between ROM pages
000000r 2               ;   Labels are defined by the page 0 copy; the other copies assert that they line up with it.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               common_define .set 1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Define a label in the page 0 copy of the common block; check the address in the other copies
000000r 2               .macro CLABEL   name
000000r 2               .if common_define
000000r 2               name:
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2               .assert     * = name, lderror, "COMMON block copies are misaligned"
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro COMMON_BLOCK
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; IRQ entry stubs, one per logical IRQ# (the vector RAM is indexed by IRQ_NUMBER(n))
000000r 2               .repeat 16, I
000000r 2                           CLABEL      .ident(.sprintf("IRQ_STUB_%X", I))
000000r 2                           pha
000000r 2                           lda         #I
000000r 2                           jmp         IRQ_ENTRY
000000r 2               .endrepeat
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; .A = logical IRQ#.  Save the caller's ROM page and switch to page 0 for the dispatcher.
000000r 2                           CLABEL      IRQ_ENTRY
000000r 2                           phx
000000r 2                           ldx         W_REGISTER
000000r 2                           stz         W_REGISTER                          ; Now on page 0 (this same code)
000000r 2                           jmp         IRQ_DISPATCH
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; .A = ROM page to return to.  Stack: .X, .A, then the interrupt frame.
000000r 2                           CLABEL      IRQ_EXIT
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Back on the interrupted page (this same code)
000000r 2                           plx
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           rti
000000r 2               
000000r 2                           CLABEL      NMI_ENTRY
000000r 2                           pha
000000r 2                           lda         W_REGISTER
000000r 2                           pha
000000r 2                           stz         W_REGISTER                          ; Now on page 0 (this same code)
000000r 2                           jsr         NMI_HANDLER
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Back on the interrupted page (this same code)
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           rti
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Far call: ZP_FAR_A = .A, ZP_FAR_VEC = routine, ZP_FAR_PAGE = its ROM page.  Use FAR_GATE.
000000r 2               ; .A, .X, .Y, C and V pass through in both directions; N/Z on return reflect .A.
000000r 2               ; Not for use from IRQ handlers.
000000r 2                           CLABEL      FAR_CALL_A
000000r 2                           lda         W_REGISTER
000000r 2                           pha                                             ; Caller's page
000000r 2                           lda         ZP_FAR_PAGE
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Now on the far page (this same code)
000000r 2                           lda         ZP_FAR_A
000000r 2                           jsr         FAR_JMP_VEC
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_A
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Back on the caller's page (this same code)
000000r 2                           lda         ZP_FAR_A
000000r 2                           rts
000000r 2               
000000r 2                           CLABEL      FAR_JMP_VEC
000000r 2                           jmp         (ZP_FAR_VEC)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Compact far call: `jsr FAR_INLINE` followed by `.word routine` and `.byte page` (FAR_GATE_INLINE).
000000r 2               ; Reads the inline data (on the caller's page), then continues as FAR_CALL_A.
000000r 2                           CLABEL      FAR_INLINE
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_A
000000r 2                           pla                                             ; Address of the inline data - 1
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         #3
000000r 2                           lda         (ZP_FAR_VEC),Y                      ; Page
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_PAGE
000000r 2                           dey
000000r 2                           lda         (ZP_FAR_VEC),Y                      ; Routine, high
000000r 2                           pha
000000r 2                           dey
000000r 2                           lda         (ZP_FAR_VEC),Y                      ; Routine, low
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC
000000r 2                           pla
000000r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           jmp         FAR_CALL_A                          ; Returns to the gate's caller
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; .A = byte at (ZP_D_XAM), with ROM page ZP_D_PAGE selected (only $E000-$FDFF is paged).
000000r 2               ; For the disassembler, which runs on page 1 but usually examines the BIOS (page 0).
000000r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y, C; N/Z reflect .A
000000r 2                           CLABEL      PEEK_D_XAM
000000r 2                           phx
000000r 2                           ldx         W_REGISTER
000000r 2                           lda         ZP_D_PAGE
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Now on page ZP_D_PAGE (this same code)
000000r 2                           lda         (ZP_D_XAM)
000000r 2                           stx         W_REGISTER                          ; Back on the caller's page (this same code)
000000r 2                           plx
000000r 2                           ora         #0
000000r 2                           rts
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Far jump (no return): ZP_FAR_VEC = destination, ZP_FAR_PAGE = its ROM page.  Use FAR_JMP_GATE.
000000r 2                           CLABEL      FAR_JUMP
000000r 2                           lda         ZP_FAR_PAGE
000000r 2                           sta         W_REGISTER                          ; Now on the far page (this same code)
000000r 2                           jmp         (ZP_FAR_VEC)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .endmacro

0000D4r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0000D4r 2               ; Gates: a local label that calls (or jumps to) a routine on another ROM page.
0000D4r 2               ; .A, .X, .Y and C pass through to the routine and back (see FAR_CALL_A).
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               .macro FAR_GATE     name, target, page
0000D4r 2               name:
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_A
0000D4r 2                           lda         #<(target)
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC
0000D4r 2                           lda         #>(target)
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
0000D4r 2                           lda         #page
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_PAGE
0000D4r 2                           jmp         FAR_CALL_A
0000D4r 2               .endmacro
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               ; Compact version of FAR_GATE (6 bytes instead of 15; a little slower): .A, .X, .Y and C pass through
0000D4r 2               .macro FAR_GATE_INLINE  name, target, page
0000D4r 2               name:
0000D4r 2                           jsr         FAR_INLINE
0000D4r 2                           .word       target
0000D4r 2                           .byte       page
0000D4r 2               .endmacro
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               ; Gate into a driver task: runs target in task (via TASK_CALL, see tasks.s).
0000D4r 2               ; .A, .X, .Y and C pass through to the routine and back.
0000D4r 2               .macro TASK_GATE    name, target, task
0000D4r 2               name:
0000D4r 2                           pha
0000D4r 2                           LOAD_ADDR   target, ZP_TC_VEC
0000D4r 2                           lda         #task
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_TC_TASK
0000D4r 2                           pla
0000D4r 2                           jmp         TASK_CALL
0000D4r 2               .endmacro
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               .macro FAR_JMP_GATE name, target, page
0000D4r 2               name:
0000D4r 2                           lda         #<(target)
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC
0000D4r 2                           lda         #>(target)
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
0000D4r 2                           lda         #page
0000D4r 2                           sta         ZP_FAR_PAGE
0000D4r 2                           jmp         FAR_JUMP
0000D4r 2               .endmacro
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 1               
0000D4r 1               ; BIOS ROM page 2 (W = 2): the IO layer.  Its own scope, so page 2 code binds to the page 2 gates in
0000D4r 1               ; page2.s.  Before PAGE1, whose gates refer to PAGE2::
0000D4r 1               .scope PAGE2
0000D4r 1               .include "page2.s"      ; must be first in the scope
0000D4r 2               .debuginfo
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0000D4r 2               ; BIOS ROM page 2 (W = 2): the IO layer (io.s) and its self test (io_test.s).
0000D4r 2               ;
0000D4r 2               ;   This file is included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s), BEFORE io.s, so the gate labels below take
0000D4r 2               ;   precedence over the page 0 routines of the same name for all page 2 code.  Page 2 code runs with
0000D4r 2               ;   W = 2; it's entered through the compact gates in io_p0.s (page 0) and page1.s (page 1).

000070r 2  rr 00

00009Br 1               .include "io.s"
00009Br 2               .debuginfo
00009Br 2               
00009Br 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00009Br 2               ; The IO layer (see IO_PLAN.md): all IO goes through file descriptors.  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside
00009Br 2               ; `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s); page 0 and page 1 reach it through gates.
00009Br 2               ;
00009Br 2               ;   IO_OPEN "/dev/<name>[/<rest>]" finds <name> in the device table (DEV_REGISTER), and the device's
00009Br 2               ;   serve routine (a file server, usually a driver) opens <rest>.  Reads and writes go to the server as
00009Br 2               ;   H9P requests: the request block and up to IO_UNIT bytes of data travel through the task's IO transfer
00009Br 2               ;   area in shared RAM (bank ID $09), and the serve routine runs in the server's task (TASK_CALL).
00009Br 2               ;
00009Br 2               ;   All calls: C = 0 on success, C = 1 with the error in .A; they preserve .X and .Y (except where they
00009Br 2               ;   return something in them).  Names and buffers must be in task RAM ($0000-$7FFF); a name can also be on
00009Br 2               ;   ROM page 2.
00009Br 2               ;
00009Br 2               ;   Blocking: a server that has no data yet returns ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK, and later wakes the task
00009Br 2               ;   (IO_WAKE).  The task marks itself waiting *before* calling the server, so a wake that comes early
00009Br 2               ;   isn't lost; if the fd is IO_MODE_NONBLOCK, the error is returned instead.
00009Br 2               
00009Br 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Save RAM_BANK_REG / U on the stack and map the IO transfer bank (uses .Y)
000000r 2               .macro _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000000r 2                           ldy         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           ldy         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           phy
000000r 2                           stz         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ldy         #IO_XFER_BANK
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Restore U / RAM_BANK_REG (uses .Y; preserves .A and C)
000000r 2               .macro _M_IO_UNMAP
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         U_REGISTER
000000r 2                           ply
000000r 2                           sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2  2F 64 65 76  S_DEV_PREFIX:   .byte "/dev/"
000004r 2  2F           
000005r 2               DEV_PREFIX_LEN  = 5
000005r 2               
000005r 2               ; ---- helpers
000005r 2               
000005r 2               ; ZP_IO_XFER / ZP_IO_DATA = the current task's transfer area: $8000 + task * $200.  Modifies: .A
000005r 2               IO_XFER_SETUP:
000005r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000008r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00000Ar 2  0A                       asl
00000Br 2  09 80                    ora         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
00000Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
00000Fr 2  1A                       inc
000010r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_DATA + 1
000012r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_XFER
000014r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_DATA
000016r 2  60                       rts
000017r 2               
000017r 2               ; Check an fd is open.  IN: .A = fd.  OUT: .X = fd * IO_FD_SIZE, ZP_IO_FD = fd, C = 0; or .A = ERR_IO_BAD_FD, C = 1
000017r 2               IO_FD_CHECK:
000017r 2  C9 0C                    cmp         #IO_MAX_FDS
000019r 2  B0 0F                    bcs         @bad
00001Br 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_FD
00001Dr 2  0A                       asl
00001Er 2  0A                       asl
00001Fr 2  0A                       asl
000020r 2  AA                       tax
000021r 2  BD A0 7D                 lda         IO_FD_SERVER,X
000024r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IO_FD_CLOSED
000026r 2  F0 02                    beq         @bad
000028r 2  18                       clc
000029r 2  60                       rts
00002Ar 2               
00002Ar 2               @bad:
00002Ar 2  A9 71                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_FD
00002Cr 2  38                       sec
00002Dr 2  60                       rts
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2               ; Send the request in the transfer area to fd ZP_IO_FD's server (transfer bank mapped).
00002Er 2               ; IN: .A = H9_* request.  OUT: C = 0 and .A from the server; or .A = error, C = 1
00002Er 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00002Er 2               IO_SERVE:
00002Er 2  A0 00                    ldy         #IO_BLK_TYPE
000030r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
000032r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_FD
000034r 2  0A                       asl
000035r 2  0A                       asl
000036r 2  0A                       asl
000037r 2  AA                       tax
000038r 2  BD A1 7D                 lda         IO_FD_FID,X
00003Br 2  A0 01                    ldy         #IO_BLK_FID
00003Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
00003Fr 2  BD A2 7D                 lda         IO_FD_MODE,X                ; (A pipe's ends differ only in their modes)
000042r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #IO_BLK_MODE
000044r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
000046r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000049r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00004Br 2  A0 03                    ldy         #IO_BLK_CLIENT
00004Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
00004Fr 2  BD A0 7D                 lda         IO_FD_SERVER,X              ; The device: its task and serve routine
000052r 2  0A                       asl
000053r 2  0A                       asl
000054r 2  0A                       asl
000055r 2  0A                       asl
000056r 2  AA                       tax
000057r 2  A0 F0                    ldy         #SYS_BANK               ; Device table (shared bank ID $00)
000059r 2  84 rr                    sty         RAM_BANK_REG
00005Br 2  BD 08 88                 lda         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_TASK,X
00005Er 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK
000060r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000062r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                  ; Serve in the calling task
000065r 2               :
000065r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
000067r 2  BD 09 88                 lda         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE,X
00006Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC
00006Cr 2  BD 0A 88                 lda         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE + 1,X
00006Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
000071r 2  A0 F9                    ldy         #IO_XFER_BANK
000073r 2  84 rr                    sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000075r 2               
000075r 2               @request:
000075r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_NO_PREEMPT               ; No task switch while we're marked waiting but not yet in
000077r 2  08                       php                                     ;   the server's wait list: nobody would wake us (NO_PREEMPT)
000078r 2  78                       sei
000079r 2  A7 rr                    smb2        TASK_STATUS_REG             ; Waiting, until the server says otherwise (TASK_WAITING_FLAG)
00007Br 2  28                       plp
00007Cr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #IO_BLK_TYPE
00007Er 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
000080r 2  48                       pha                                     ; .A = request
000081r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #IO_BLK_FID
000083r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
000085r 2  A8                       tay                                     ; .Y = fid
000086r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000089r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00008Br 2  AA                       tax                                     ; .X = client
00008Cr 2  68                       pla
00008Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                   ; The serve routine, in the server's task
000090r 2  90 19                    bcc         @done
000092r 2  C9 73                    cmp         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
000094r 2  D0 15                    bne         @done
000096r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_FD                    ; No data yet: wait?
000098r 2  0A                       asl
000099r 2  0A                       asl
00009Ar 2  0A                       asl
00009Br 2  AA                       tax
00009Cr 2  BD A2 7D                 lda         IO_FD_MODE,X
00009Fr 2  30 07                    bmi         @no_wait                    ; IO_MODE_NONBLOCK
0000A1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         YIELD                       ; Sleep until the server wakes us (at once if it did already)
0000A4r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000A6r 2  80 CD                    bra         @request
0000A8r 2               
0000A8r 2               @no_wait:
0000A8r 2  A9 73                    lda         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
0000AAr 2  38                       sec
0000ABr 2               
0000ABr 2               @done:
0000ABr 2  27 rr                    rmb2        TASK_STATUS_REG             ; Not waiting (rmb doesn't change the flags)
0000ADr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PREEMPT                     ; (Preserves .A and the flags; switches if a switch came due)
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = min(ZP_IO_LEFT, IO_UNIT); also the request's count.  Modifies: .A, .Y
0000B0r 2               IO_SET_CHUNK:
0000B0r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
0000B2r 2  F0 08                    beq         :+
0000B4r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_CHUNK                 ; 256 or more left: a full IO_UNIT
0000B6r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0000B8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000BAr 2  80 06                    bra         @count
0000BCr 2               :
0000BCr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_LEFT
0000BEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0000C0r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000C2r 2               
0000C2r 2               @count:
0000C2r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
0000C4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0000C6r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
0000C8r 2  C8                       iny
0000C9r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000CBr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
0000CDr 2  60                       rts
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               ; Copy fd ZP_IO_FD's offset into the request.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0000CEr 2               IO_SET_OFS:
0000CEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_FD
0000D0r 2  0A                       asl
0000D1r 2  0A                       asl
0000D2r 2  0A                       asl
0000D3r 2  AA                       tax
0000D4r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #IO_BLK_OFS
0000D6r 2               
0000D6r 2               @copy:
0000D6r 2  BD A4 7D                 lda         IO_FD_OFS,X
0000D9r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
0000DBr 2  E8                       inx
0000DCr 2  C8                       iny
0000DDr 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_BLK_OFS + 4
0000DFr 2  D0 F5                    bne         @copy
0000E1r 2  60                       rts
0000E2r 2               
0000E2r 2               ; The request's count (bytes the server did): into ZP_IO_CHUNK.  Z = 1 if none.  Modifies: .A, .Y
0000E2r 2               IO_GET_DONE:
0000E2r 2  A0 09                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT + 1
0000E4r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
0000E6r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000E8r 2  88                       dey
0000E9r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
0000EBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0000EDr 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000EFr 2  60                       rts
0000F0r 2               
0000F0r 2               ; Advance by ZP_IO_CHUNK bytes: the caller's buffer, the fd's offset, the count done (ZP_IO_CNT) and the
0000F0r 2               ; count left.  Modifies: .A, .X
0000F0r 2               IO_ADVANCE:
0000F0r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF
0000F2r 2  18                       clc
0000F3r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0000F5r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF
0000F7r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
0000F9r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0000FBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
0000FDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0000FFr 2  18                       clc
000100r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000102r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT
000104r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
000106r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000108r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT + 1

000138r 2               
000138r 2               @done:
000138r 2  60                       rts
000139r 2               
000139r 2               ; Copy ZP_IO_CHUNK (1-256) bytes: transfer data -> caller's buffer (IO_COPY_OUT) or back (IO_COPY_IN).
000139r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
000139r 2               IO_COPY_OUT:
000139r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00013Br 2               :
00013Br 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
00013Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
00013Fr 2  C8                       iny
000140r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_CHUNK                 ; (256: ZP_IO_CHUNK = 0, so Y wraps round to it)
000142r 2  D0 F7                    bne         :-
000144r 2  60                       rts
000145r 2               
000145r 2               IO_COPY_IN:
000145r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000147r 2               :
000147r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
000149r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
00014Br 2  C8                       iny
00014Cr 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_CHUNK
00014Er 2  D0 F7                    bne         :-
000150r 2  60                       rts
000151r 2               
000151r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000151r 2               ; The calls
000151r 2               
000151r 2               ; Open a file.  The name goes through the task's namespace first (IO_MOUNT, IO_BIND); a name it doesn't
000151r 2               ; match must be "/dev/<device>" or "/dev/<device>/<rest>".  The server opens the rest of the name.
000151r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = name (zero-terminated, 255 characters at most), .X = IO_MODE_* bits
000151r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = fd, C = 0
000151r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND, ERR_IO_NO_FDS, ERR_IO_NS_LOOP, ERR_IO_NAME or the server's
000151r 2               ;                error, C = 1
000151r 2               IO_OPEN:
000151r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000153r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF
000155r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
000157r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_MODE
000159r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
00015Cr 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000160r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
000164r 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
00016Ar 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                          ; The name -> the data area, where the namespace can
00016Cr 2               
00016Cr 2               @copy:                                              ;   rewrite it, and the server finds the rest of it
00016Cr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
00016Er 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
000170r 2  F0 08                    beq         @copied
000172r 2  C8                       iny
000173r 2  D0 F7                    bne         @copy
000175r 2  88                       dey                                     ; (255 characters at most)
000176r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000178r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
00017Ar 2               
00017Ar 2               @copied:
00017Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_RESOLVE                  ; C = 0: a mount: .A = the device, the rest of the name
00017Dr 2  90 28                    bcc         @found                      ;   is in the data area
00017Fr 2  AA                       tax
000180r 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
000182r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @fail                       ; (An error)
000185r 2               :
000185r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #DEV_PREFIX_LEN - 1         ; "/dev/"?
000187r 2               
000187r 2               @prefix:
000187r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
000189r 2  D9 rr rr                 cmp         S_DEV_PREFIX,Y
00018Cr 2  D0 1D                    bne         @not_found
00018Er 2  88                       dey
00018Fr 2  10 F6                    bpl         @prefix
000191r 2  A9 05                    lda         #DEV_PREFIX_LEN             ; ZP_IO_LEFT = the device name
000193r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT

000197r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1

00019Cr 2  B0 0D                    bcs         @not_found
00019Er 2  48                       pha
00019Fr 2  98                       tya
0001A0r 2  18                       clc
0001A1r 2  69 05                    adc         #DEV_PREFIX_LEN
0001A3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_CUT                      ; The rest of the name, for the server
0001A6r 2  68                       pla
0001A7r 2               
0001A7r 2               @found:
0001A7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT                   ; ZP_IO_CNT = device index
0001A9r 2  80 04                    bra         @find_fd
0001ABr 2               
0001ABr 2               @not_found:
0001ABr 2  A9 70                    lda         #ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND
0001ADr 2  80 5F                    bra         @fail
0001AFr 2               
0001AFr 2               @find_fd:
0001AFr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0001B1r 2               
0001B1r 2               @fd:
0001B1r 2  BD A0 7D                 lda         IO_FD_SERVER,X
0001B4r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IO_FD_CLOSED
0001B6r 2  F0 0D                    beq         @got_fd
0001B8r 2  8A                       txa
0001B9r 2  18                       clc
0001BAr 2  69 08                    adc         #IO_FD_SIZE
0001BCr 2  AA                       tax
0001BDr 2  E0 60                    cpx         #IO_MAX_FDS * IO_FD_SIZE
0001BFr 2  D0 F0                    bne         @fd
0001C1r 2  A9 75                    lda         #ERR_IO_NO_FDS
0001C3r 2  80 49                    bra         @fail
0001C5r 2               
0001C5r 2               @got_fd:
0001C5r 2  8A                       txa                                     ; fd = offset / 8
0001C6r 2  4A                       lsr
0001C7r 2  4A                       lsr
0001C8r 2  4A                       lsr
0001C9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_FD
0001CBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0001CDr 2  9D A0 7D                 sta         IO_FD_SERVER,X
0001D0r 2  9E A1 7D                 stz         IO_FD_FID,X

0001DEr 2  9E A6 7D                 stz         IO_FD_OFS + 2,X

0001EFr 2  B0 10                    bcs         @open_failed

000219r 2               ; Close an fd (the server's fid too).
000219r 2               ; IN: .A = fd

00024Fr 2               
00024Fr 2               ; Read from an fd.  Returns when the count is done, at end of file, or when the server has less (e.g.
00024Fr 2               ; the keyboard: the bytes typed so far).
00024Fr 2               ; IN: .A = fd, ZP_IO_BUF = buffer, ZP_IO_CNT = bytes to read
00024Fr 2               ; OUT (success): ZP_IO_CNT = bytes read (0 = end of file), C = 0
00024Fr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = error, C = 1 (ZP_IO_CNT = bytes read before the error)
00024Fr 2               IO_READ:
00024Fr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000251r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_FD_CHECK

000256r 2  BD A2 7D                 lda         IO_FD_MODE,X

00025Br 2  D0 05                    bne         :+

000260r 2  80 5F                    bra         @done
000262r 2               :

0002C4r 2               
0002C4r 2               ; Write to an fd.  Returns when the count is done, or when the server takes nothing (a server that takes
0002C4r 2               ; less than offered, like the console when its TX ring fills up, is offered the rest again).
0002C4r 2               ; IN: .A = fd, ZP_IO_BUF = buffer, ZP_IO_CNT = bytes to write
0002C4r 2               ; OUT (success): ZP_IO_CNT = bytes written, C = 0
0002C4r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = error, C = 1 (ZP_IO_CNT = bytes written before the error)
0002C4r 2               IO_WRITE:
0002C4r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0002C6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_FD_CHECK

0002CBr 2  BD A2 7D                 lda         IO_FD_MODE,X

0002D7r 2               :

0003A7r 2               ; Device-specific control (e.g. a serial port's baud rate).
