000506r 2  60                       rts
000507r 2               
000507r 2               ; Start a copy of the current task, like fork: a new task gets a copy of this task's RAM ($0200-$7CFF, and
000507r 2               ; the MMU area $7E00-$7FFF; not the stack page or the task system page), its task ZP (everything above
000507r 2               ; the OS ZP) and its open fds.  It starts at .A.Y on ROM page .X (a routine: when it returns, the task
000507r 2               ; ends).  The pages between the MMU's page floor (MM_SET_FLOOR) and its lowest allocated page are free,
000507r 2               ; so they aren't copied: a task that keeps data in task RAM outside the MMU keeps it below its page
000507r 2               ; floor (HyForth keeps its floor just above its dictionary).  The copy goes a page at a time through the
000507r 2               ; IO transfer area (TASK_CLONE_PAGE, run in the new task), before the new task runs: about 1/400 second
000507r 2               ; per page at 3.58 MHz.
000507r 2               ; OUT: .A = the new task, C = 0; or .A = ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE, C = 1.  Preserves .X, .Y
000507r 2               TASK_CLONE:
000507r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000509r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC                 ; (TASK_BUILD_FRAME's inputs)
00050Br 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00050Dr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_TEMP
00050Fr 2  08                       php
000510r 2  78                       sei
000511r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         RESERVE_TASK                ; C = 1: .A = the task (busy, and paused for now)
000514r 2  B0 06                    bcs         :+
000516r 2  28                       plp
000517r 2  A9 F1                    lda         #ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE
000519r 2  38                       sec
00051Ar 2  80 5E                    bra         @done
00051Cr 2               :
00051Cr 2  AA                       tax
00051Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_BUILD_FRAME            ; (Its fds too)
000520r 2  28                       plp
000521r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_TC_TASK
000523r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   ::TASK_CLONE_PAGE, ZP_TC_VEC ; (Its page 0 gate)
000527r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00052Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
00052Er 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000532r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
000536r 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
00053Cr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                          ; Page
00053Er 2               
00053Er 2               @page:
00053Er 2  E0 01                    cpx         #$01                        ; Not the stack page
000540r 2  F0 28                    beq         @next
000542r 2  E0 7D                    cpx         #MMU_SYS_PAGE               ; Not the task system page (IRQ tables, fds)
000544r 2  F0 24                    beq         @next

000549r 2  90 05                    bcc         :+                          ;   its lowest allocated page
00054Br 2  EC 63 7E                 cpx         MMU_LOW_WATER

000552r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_BUF + 1

000556r 2  8A                       txa
000557r 2  D0 02                    bne         @byte
000559r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #<(__ZEROPAGE_RUN__ + __ZEROPAGE_SIZE__) ; ZP: just the task ZP
00055Br 2               
00055Br 2               @byte:
00055Br 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y               ; This task's page -> its transfer area
00055Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
00055Fr 2  C8                       iny
000560r 2  D0 F9                    bne         @byte

000567r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                   ; The new task copies it in
00056Ar 2               
00056Ar 2               @next:
00056Ar 2  E8                       inx
00056Br 2  10 D1                    bpl         @page                       ; Pages $00-$7F
00056Dr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP

000574r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_TASK

0005ADr 2               
0005ADr 2               ; Give a new task copies of the current task's namespace and open fds (TASK_BUILD_FRAME), telling each
0005ADr 2               ; fd's server (H9_DUP).
0005ADr 2               ; The fd table goes through the current task's IO transfer area (tasks can't see each other's RAM), and
0005ADr 2               ; the new task copies it in (IO_ADOPT_FDS, run in it with TASK_CALL).  IRQs must be off.

000600r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER

00063Ar 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00063Ar 2               ; The IO layer's own devices.  They run in the calling task (IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK).
00063Ar 2               
00063Ar 2               ; /dev/null: reads are empty (end of file), writes take everything
00063Ar 2               NULL_SERVE:

000646r 2

000673r 2  60                       rts
000674r 2               
000674r 2               @read:                                              ; count bytes of zeros (the count stays)
000674r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000677r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000679r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
00067Br 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP
00067Dr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1               ; The data area
00067Fr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000681r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000683r 2               :
000683r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000685r 2  C8                       iny
000686r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_TMP                   ; (256: 0, so .Y wraps round to it)
000688r 2  D0 F9                    bne         :-
00068Ar 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
00068Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
00068Fr 2  80 DF                    bra         @ok
000691r 2               
000691r 2               @stat:
000691r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
000694r 2  80 DA                    bra         @ok
000696r 2               
000696r 2               @bad:
000696r 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000698r 2  38                       sec
000699r 2  60                       rts
00069Ar 2               
00069Ar 2               ; An all-zero stat block (size 0).  IN: .X = client
00069Ar 2               STAT_ZERO:
00069Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
00069Dr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1               ; The data area
00069Fr 2  A0 0F                    ldy         #15
0006A1r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0006A3r 2               :
0006A3r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0006A5r 2  88                       dey
0006A6r 2  10 FB                    bpl         :-
0006A8r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
0006AAr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0006ADr 2               
0006ADr 1               .include "ns.s"         ; Per-task namespaces (IO_MOUNT, IO_BIND)
0006ADr 2               .debuginfo
0006ADr 2               
0006ADr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0006ADr 2               ; Per-task namespaces (see IO_PLAN.md, Phase 3; the entry layout is in defines.s).  BIOS ROM page 2,
0006ADr 2               ; included inside `.scope PAGE2` after io.s (see all.s).  Each task's namespace is in its IO transfer
0006ADr 2               ; area (IO_BLK_NS), next to the data area IO_OPEN resolves names in, so everything here works with the
0006ADr 2               ; IO transfer bank mapped (_M_IO_MAP_XFER).
0006ADr 2               ;
0006ADr 2               ;   IO_MOUNT "/sd", "sd"          names under /sd go to the device sd (its server gets the rest: /x/y)
0006ADr 2               ;   IO_BIND "/null", "/dev/null"  names under /null stand for names under /dev/null
0006ADr 2               ;   IO_UNMOUNT "/sd"              removes the entry for that path (mount or bind)
0006ADr 2               ; A prefix matches a whole path element: /sd matches /sd and /sd/x, not /sdx.  The longest match wins.
0006ADr 2               ; New tasks get a copy of their parent's namespace (IO_INHERIT); a task's is cleared when it ends.
0006ADr 2               
0006ADr 2               .segment "IO_P2"
0006ADr 2               
0006ADr 2               ; Find a device by name.  IN: ZP_IO_LEFT = the name (it ends at '/' or 0; readable with the IO
0006ADr 2               ; transfer bank mapped: the data area, task RAM or this ROM page)
0006ADr 2               ; OUT: .A = the device (device table index), .Y = the name's length, C = 0; or C = 1 (not there)
0006ADr 2               ; The IO transfer bank is mapped (again) on return.  Modifies: .X, ZP_IO_CHUNK, ZP_IO_TMP
0006ADr 2               IO_DEV_FIND:
0006ADr 2  A9 00                    lda         #<IO_DEV_TABLE
0006AFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK                 ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = the entry
0006B1r 2  A9 88                    lda         #>IO_DEV_TABLE
0006B3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0006B5r 2               
0006B5r 2               @entry:
0006B5r 2  A2 F0                    ldx         #SYS_BANK               ; The device table (shared bank ID $00; U is 0)
0006B7r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
0006B9r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)
0006BBr 2  F0 37                    beq         @next_entry                 ; Free entry
0006BDr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0006BFr 2               
0006BFr 2               @compare:
0006BFr 2  A2 F9                    ldx         #IO_XFER_BANK
0006C1r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
0006C3r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y              ; The name's character; '/' or 0 ends it
0006C5r 2  C9 2F                    cmp         #'/'
0006C7r 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
0006C9r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0006CBr 2               :
0006CBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP
0006CDr 2  A2 F0                    ldx         #SYS_BANK
0006CFr 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
0006D1r 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
0006D3r 2  F0 0C                    beq         @all_8                      ; 8 characters matched
0006D5r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
0006D7r 2  D0 1B                    bne         @next_entry
0006D9r 2  C8                       iny
0006DAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
0006DCr 2  D0 E1                    bne         @compare                    ; Both ended: a match
0006DEr 2  88                       dey                                     ; .Y = the name's length
0006DFr 2  80 04                    bra         @found
0006E1r 2               
0006E1r 2               @all_8:
0006E1r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
0006E3r 2  D0 0F                    bne         @next_entry                 ; The name is longer than 8
0006E5r 2               
0006E5r 2               @found:
0006E5r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK                 ; Device index = (entry - table) / 16
0006E7r 2  38                       sec
0006E8r 2  E9 00                    sbc         #<IO_DEV_TABLE
0006EAr 2  4A                       lsr
0006EBr 2  4A                       lsr
0006ECr 2  4A                       lsr
0006EDr 2  4A                       lsr
0006EEr 2  A2 F9                    ldx         #IO_XFER_BANK
0006F0r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
0006F2r 2  18                       clc
0006F3r 2  60                       rts
0006F4r 2               
0006F4r 2               @next_entry:
0006F4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0006F6r 2  18                       clc
0006F7r 2  69 10                    adc         #IO_DEV_SIZE
0006F9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0006FBr 2  D0 B8                    bne         @entry                      ; 16 entries: $8800-$88FF
0006FDr 2  A2 F9                    ldx         #IO_XFER_BANK
0006FFr 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
000701r 2  38                       sec
000702r 2  60                       rts
000703r 2               
000703r 2               ; Remove the first .A characters of the name in the data area.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000703r 2               NS_CUT:
000703r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
000705r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_DATA + 1
000707r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000709r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00070Br 2               
00070Br 2               @copy:
00070Br 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
00070Dr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
00070Fr 2  F0 03                    beq         @done
000711r 2  C8                       iny
000712r 2  D0 F7                    bne         @copy
000714r 2               
000714r 2               @done:
000714r 2  60                       rts
000715r 2               
000715r 2               ; Apply the namespace to the name in the data area (IO_OPEN).
000715r 2               ; OUT: C = 0: a mount: .A = its device, and the rest of the name is in the data area
000715r 2               ;      C = 1: .A = 0: no entry matches (the name, maybe rewritten by binds, is in the data area);
000715r 2               ;             or .A = ERR_IO_NS_LOOP or ERR_IO_NAME (a bind made the name too long)
000715r 2               ; Modifies: .X, .Y, ZP_IO_BUF, ZP_IO_CHUNK, ZP_IO_CNT, ZP_IO_TMP, ZP_IO_BYTE, ZP_IO_LEFT, ZP_IO_OFS
000715r 2               NS_RESOLVE:
000715r 2  A9 05                    lda         #NS_MAX_REWRITES + 1
000717r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BYTE
000719r 2               
000719r 2               @again:
000719r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_BYTE
00071Br 2  D0 04                    bne         :+
00071Dr 2  A9 7D                    lda         #ERR_IO_NS_LOOP
00071Fr 2  38                       sec
000720r 2  60                       rts
000721r 2               :
000721r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_TMP                   ; The longest prefix so far (0: none)
000723r 2  A9 FE                    lda         #<(-NS_PREFIX)              ; ZP_IO_LEFT: (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y with .Y = NS_PREFIX + n
000725r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT                  ;   = the name's character n
000727r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_DATA + 1
000729r 2  CA                       dex
00072Ar 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
00072Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIRST                    ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = the first entry, .X = count
00072Fr 2               
00072Fr 2               @entry:
00072Fr 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
000731r 2  F0 25                    beq         @next
000733r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #NS_PREFIX
000735r 2               
000735r 2               @compare:
000735r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000737r 2  F0 07                    beq         @prefix_end
000739r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
00073Br 2  D0 1B                    bne         @next
00073Dr 2  C8                       iny
00073Er 2  80 F5                    bra         @compare
000740r 2               
000740r 2               @prefix_end:                                        ; The whole prefix matched: a whole path element?
000740r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
000742r 2  F0 04                    beq         @match
000744r 2  C9 2F                    cmp         #'/'
000746r 2  D0 10                    bne         @next
000748r 2               
000748r 2               @match:
000748r 2  98                       tya
000749r 2  38                       sec
00074Ar 2  E9 02                    sbc         #NS_PREFIX                  ; Its length
00074Cr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IO_TMP
00074Er 2  90 08                    bcc         @next                       ; (Shorter than the best so far)
000750r 2  F0 06                    beq         @next
000752r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP
000754r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000756r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF                   ; ZP_IO_BUF = the best entry (its low byte: they're
000758r 2                                                                   ;   all in the same page)
000758r 2               @next:
000758r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_NEXT
00075Br 2  D0 D2                    bne         @entry
00075Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
00075Fr 2  D0 02                    bne         @apply
000761r 2  38                       sec                                     ; (.A = 0: no match)
000762r 2  60                       rts
000763r 2               
000763r 2               @apply:
000763r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF
000765r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000767r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
000769r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #NS_MOUNT
00076Br 2  D0 0B                    bne         @bind
00076Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
00076Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_CUT                      ; The rest of the name, for the server
000772r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #NS_DEV
000774r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000776r 2  18                       clc
000777r 2  60                       rts
000778r 2               
000778r 2               @bind:                                              ; The name = the target, then the rest of the name
000778r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #NS_TARGET
00077Ar 2               :
00077Ar 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
00077Cr 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
00077Er 2  C8                       iny
00077Fr 2  80 F9                    bra         :-
000781r 2               :
000781r 2  98                       tya
000782r 2  38                       sec
000783r 2  E9 10                    sbc         #NS_TARGET
000785r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CNT                   ; The target's length
000787r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_SHIFT                    ; The rest: from offset ZP_IO_TMP to ZP_IO_CNT
00078Ar 2  B0 17                    bcs         @too_long
00078Cr 2  A9 F0                    lda         #<(-NS_TARGET)              ; (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y with .Y = NS_TARGET + n = the
00078Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT                  ;   name's character n
000790r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_DATA + 1
000792r 2  CA                       dex
000793r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000795r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #NS_TARGET
000797r 2               
000797r 2               @target:
000797r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000799r 2  F0 05                    beq         @rewritten
00079Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
00079Dr 2  C8                       iny
00079Er 2  80 F7                    bra         @target
0007A0r 2               
0007A0r 2               @rewritten:
0007A0r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @again                      ; (The new name can match an entry too)
0007A3r 2               
0007A3r 2               @too_long:
0007A3r 2  A9 77                    lda         #ERR_IO_NAME
0007A5r 2  60                       rts
0007A6r 2               
0007A6r 2               ; Move the end of the name in the data area, from offset ZP_IO_TMP to offset ZP_IO_CNT (the terminating
0007A6r 2               ; 0 too).  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1 if it wouldn't fit (nothing moved).  Modifies: .A, .Y, ZP_IO_LEFT,
0007A6r 2               ; ZP_IO_OFS
0007A6r 2               NS_SHIFT:
0007A6r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_TMP                   ; .Y = the name's end (its 0)
0007A8r 2               :
0007A8r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_DATA),Y
0007AAr 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
0007ACr 2  C8                       iny
0007ADr 2  80 F9                    bra         :-
0007AFr 2               :
0007AFr 2  98                       tya
0007B0r 2  38                       sec
0007B1r 2  E5 rr                    sbc         ZP_IO_TMP                   ; .A = the bytes to move - 1
0007B3r 2  18                       clc
0007B4r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CNT
0007B6r 2  B0 30                    bcs         @done                       ; (Past the end of the data area: C = 1)
0007B8r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP                   ; ZP_IO_LEFT = from, ZP_IO_OFS = to
0007BAr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
0007BCr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0007BEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS
0007C0r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_DATA + 1
0007C2r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
0007C4r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
0007C6r 2  98                       tya
0007C7r 2  38                       sec
0007C8r 2  E5 rr                    sbc         ZP_IO_TMP
0007CAr 2  A8                       tay                                     ; .Y = the bytes to move - 1
0007CBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CNT
0007CDr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IO_TMP
0007CFr 2  F0 16                    beq         @same
0007D1r 2  B0 0B                    bcs         @up
0007D3r 2               
0007D3r 2               @down:                                              ; To a lower offset: first byte first
0007D3r 2  A0 FF                    ldy         #$FF
0007D5r 2               :
0007D5r 2  C8                       iny
0007D6r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
0007D8r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y
0007DAr 2  D0 F9                    bne         :-                          ; (Up to and including the 0)
0007DCr 2  18                       clc
0007DDr 2  60                       rts
0007DEr 2               
0007DEr 2               @up:                                                ; To a higher offset: last byte first
0007DEr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
0007E0r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y
0007E2r 2  88                       dey
0007E3r 2  C0 FF                    cpy         #$FF
0007E5r 2  D0 F7                    bne         @up
0007E7r 2               
0007E7r 2               @same:
0007E7r 2  18                       clc
0007E8r 2               
0007E8r 2               @done:
0007E8r 2  60                       rts
0007E9r 2               
0007E9r 2               ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = this task's first namespace entry, .X = NS_ENTRIES.  Modifies: .A
0007E9r 2               NS_FIRST:
0007E9r 2  A9 20                    lda         #IO_BLK_NS
0007EBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0007EDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
0007EFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0007F1r 2  A2 07                    ldx         #NS_ENTRIES
0007F3r 2  60                       rts
0007F4r 2               
0007F4r 2               ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = the next entry; Z = 1 after the last one.  Modifies: .A, .X
0007F4r 2               NS_NEXT:
0007F4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0007F6r 2  18                       clc
0007F7r 2  69 20                    adc         #NS_ENTRY_SIZE
0007F9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0007FBr 2  CA                       dex
0007FCr 2  60                       rts
0007FDr 2               
0007FDr 2               ; Clear every task's namespace (IO_INIT, at boot).  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0007FDr 2               NS_CLEAR_ALL:
0007FDr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_XFER
0007FFr 2  A9 80                    lda         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
000801r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
000803r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000807r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
00080Br 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
000811r 2               
000811r 2               @task:
000811r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_CLEAR_MAPPED
000814r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_XFER + 1              ; The next task's area: + $200
000816r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
000818r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
00081Ar 2  C9 A0                    cmp         #>(PAGED_RAM_BASE + 16 * $200)
00081Cr 2  D0 F3                    bne         @task
00081Er 2  80 14                    bra         NS_UNMAP_RTS
000820r 2               
000820r 2               ; Clear this task's namespace (IO_CLOSE_ALL, when the task ends).  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000820r 2               NS_CLEAR:
000820r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
000823r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000827r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
00082Br 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
000831r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_CLEAR_MAPPED
000834r 2               
000834r 2               NS_UNMAP_RTS:
000834r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP
000838r 2  7A 84 rr     
00083Br 2  60                       rts
00083Cr 2               
00083Cr 2               ; (The IO transfer bank mapped; ZP_IO_XFER = the task's area)
00083Cr 2               NS_CLEAR_MAPPED:
00083Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIRST
00083Fr 2               
00083Fr 2               @entry:
00083Fr 2  A9 00                    lda         #NS_FREE
000841r 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
000843r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_NEXT
000846r 2  D0 F7                    bne         @entry
000848r 2  60                       rts
000849r 2               
000849r 2               ; Give task .A a copy of this task's namespace (IO_INHERIT).  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000849r 2               NS_COPY_TO:
000849r 2  0A                       asl                                     ; Its area: $8000 + task * $200
00084Ar 2  09 80                    ora         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
00084Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
00084Er 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_LEFT
000850r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
000853r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000857r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
00085Br 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
000861r 2  A0 20                    ldy         #IO_BLK_NS
000863r 2               
000863r 2               @copy:
000863r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_XFER),Y
000865r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
000867r 2  C8                       iny
000868r 2  D0 F9                    bne         @copy                       ; (To the end of the request block page)
00086Ar 2                           .assert     IO_BLK_NS + NS_ENTRIES * NS_ENTRY_SIZE = $100, error, "NS_COPY_TO copies to $FF"
00086Ar 2  80 C8                    bra         NS_UNMAP_RTS
00086Cr 2               
00086Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00086Cr 2               ; The calls
00086Cr 2               
00086Cr 2               ; Attach a device's server at a path in this task's namespace: names under the path go to it, with the
00086Cr 2               ; rest of the name.  An entry for the same path is replaced.
00086Cr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = the path ("/...", 13 characters at most), ZP_IO_BUF = the device's name (e.g. "zero")
00086Cr 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_IO_NAME, ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND (no such device) or ERR_IO_NS_FULL, C = 1
00086Cr 2               IO_MOUNT:
00086Cr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00086Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS                   ; ZP_IO_OFS = the path
000870r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
000872r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
000875r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000879r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
00087Dr 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
000883r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF
000885r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
000887r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
000889r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
00088Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_DEV_FIND                 ; .A = the device
00088Er 2  B0 13                    bcs         @not_found
000890r 2  48                       pha
000891r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_ENTRY_FOR                ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = the path's entry
000894r 2  7A                       ply
000895r 2  B0 0E                    bcs         @done
000897r 2  98                       tya
000898r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #NS_DEV
00089Ar 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
00089Cr 2  A9 01                    lda         #NS_MOUNT
00089Er 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
0008A0r 2  18                       clc
0008A1r 2  80 02                    bra         @done
0008A3r 2               
0008A3r 2               @not_found:
0008A3r 2  A9 70                    lda         #ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND
0008A5r 2               
0008A5r 2               @done:
0008A5r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP
0008A9r 2  7A 84 rr     
0008ACr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0008AEr 2  60                       rts
0008AFr 2               
0008AFr 2               ; Make a path stand for another in this task's namespace: IO_OPEN of a name under the path opens the same
0008AFr 2               ; name under the target.  An entry for the same path is replaced.
0008AFr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = the path ("/...", 13 characters at most), ZP_IO_BUF = the target ("/...", 15 at most)
0008AFr 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_IO_NAME or ERR_IO_NS_FULL, C = 1
0008AFr 2               IO_BIND:
0008AFr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0008B1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS                   ; ZP_IO_OFS = the path
0008B3r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
0008B5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
0008B8r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
0008BCr 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
0008C0r 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
0008C6r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF)                 ; The target: "/...", short enough?
0008C8r 2  C9 2F                    cmp         #'/'
0008CAr 2  D0 33                    bne         @bad_name
0008CCr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0008CEr 2               :
0008CEr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
0008D0r 2  F0 07                    beq         :+
0008D2r 2  C8                       iny
0008D3r 2  C0 10                    cpy         #NS_TARGET_MAX + 1
0008D5r 2  D0 F7                    bne         :-
0008D7r 2  80 26                    bra         @bad_name
0008D9r 2               :
0008D9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_ENTRY_FOR                ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = the path's entry
0008DCr 2  B0 24                    bcs         @done
0008DEr 2  A9 10                    lda         #NS_TARGET                  ; ZP_IO_LEFT: (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y with .Y = NS_TARGET + n =
0008E0r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF                        ;   the target's character n
0008E2r 2  38                       sec
0008E3r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_BUF
0008E5r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
0008E7r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
0008E9r 2  E9 00                    sbc         #0
0008EBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
0008EDr 2  A0 10                    ldy         #NS_TARGET
0008EFr 2               
0008EFr 2               @copy:
0008EFr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
0008F1r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
0008F3r 2  F0 03                    beq         @copied
0008F5r 2  C8                       iny
0008F6r 2  80 F7                    bra         @copy
0008F8r 2               
0008F8r 2               @copied:
0008F8r 2  A9 02                    lda         #NS_BIND
0008FAr 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
0008FCr 2  18                       clc
0008FDr 2  80 03                    bra         @done
0008FFr 2               
0008FFr 2               @bad_name:
0008FFr 2  A9 77                    lda         #ERR_IO_NAME
000901r 2  38                       sec
000902r 2               
000902r 2               @done:
000902r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP
000906r 2  7A 84 rr     
000909r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
00090Br 2  60                       rts
00090Cr 2               
00090Cr 2               ; Remove the entry for a path (a mount or a bind) from this task's namespace.
00090Cr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = the path.  OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND, C = 1
00090Cr 2               IO_UNMOUNT:
00090Cr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00090Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS                   ; ZP_IO_OFS = the path
000910r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
000912r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
000915r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
000919r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
00091Dr 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
000923r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIND                     ; ZP_IO_CHUNK = its entry
000926r 2  B0 05                    bcs         @done
000928r 2  A9 00                    lda         #NS_FREE
00092Ar 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
00092Cr 2  18                       clc
00092Dr 2               
00092Dr 2               @done:
00092Dr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP
000931r 2  7A 84 rr     
000934r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000936r 2  60                       rts
000937r 2               
000937r 2               ; The entry for the path at ZP_IO_OFS: the existing one, or a free one with the path filled in (and
000937r 2               ; NS_TYPE = NS_FREE until the caller sets it).  OUT: ZP_IO_CHUNK = the entry, C = 0; or .A = ERR_IO_NAME
000937r 2               ; or ERR_IO_NS_FULL, C = 1.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y, ZP_IO_LEFT
000937r 2               NS_ENTRY_FOR:
000937r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_OFS)                 ; "/...", short enough?
000939r 2  C9 2F                    cmp         #'/'
00093Br 2  D0 0B                    bne         @bad_name
00093Dr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00093Fr 2               :
00093Fr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y
000941r 2  F0 09                    beq         :+
000943r 2  C8                       iny
000944r 2  C0 0E                    cpy         #NS_PREFIX_MAX + 1
000946r 2  D0 F7                    bne         :-
000948r 2               
000948r 2               @bad_name:
000948r 2  A9 77                    lda         #ERR_IO_NAME
00094Ar 2  38                       sec
00094Br 2  60                       rts
00094Cr 2               :
00094Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIND
00094Fr 2  90 1B                    bcc         @done                       ; It's there: replace it
000951r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIRST                    ; A free one
000954r 2               
000954r 2               @free:
000954r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
000956r 2  F0 09                    beq         @fill
000958r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_NEXT
00095Br 2  D0 F7                    bne         @free
00095Dr 2  A9 7C                    lda         #ERR_IO_NS_FULL
00095Fr 2  38                       sec
000960r 2  60                       rts
000961r 2               
000961r 2               @fill:
000961r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #NS_PREFIX
000963r 2               
000963r 2               @copy:
000963r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y              ; (NS_FIND left ZP_IO_LEFT set up for the path)
000965r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000967r 2  F0 03                    beq         @done
000969r 2  C8                       iny
00096Ar 2  80 F7                    bra         @copy
00096Cr 2               
00096Cr 2               @done:
00096Cr 2  18                       clc
00096Dr 2  60                       rts
00096Er 2               
00096Er 2               ; Print this task's namespace, an entry a line: "/path -> device" (a mount) or "/path = /target" (a bind).
00096Er 2               ; (Printing uses the IO transfer area too, so each byte is read on its own: NS_GETC.)
00096Er 2               IO_NS_LIST:
00096Er 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000971r 2  A2 20                    ldx         #IO_BLK_NS                  ; .X = the entry's offset
000973r 2               
000973r 2               @entry:
000973r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #NS_TYPE
000975r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_GETC
000978r 2  F0 40                    beq         @next
00097Ar 2  48                       pha
00097Br 2  A0 02                    ldy         #NS_PREFIX
00097Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_PUTS
000980r 2  68                       pla
000981r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #NS_MOUNT
000983r 2  D0 1E                    bne         @bind
000985r 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #' ', #'-', #'>', #' '
000989r 2  rr A9 2D 20  
00098Dr 2  rr rr A9 3E  
000999r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #NS_DEV
00099Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_GETC                     ; .A = the device
00099Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_PUT_DEV
0009A1r 2  80 14                    bra         @eol
0009A3r 2               
0009A3r 2               @bind:
0009A3r 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #' ', #'=', #' '
0009A7r 2  rr A9 3D 20  
0009ABr 2  rr rr A9 20  
0009B2r 2  A0 10                    ldy         #NS_TARGET
0009B4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_PUTS
0009B7r 2               
0009B7r 2               @eol:
0009B7r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0009BAr 2               
0009BAr 2               @next:
0009BAr 2  8A                       txa
0009BBr 2  18                       clc
0009BCr 2  69 20                    adc         #NS_ENTRY_SIZE
0009BEr 2  AA                       tax
0009BFr 2  D0 B2                    bne         @entry                      ; (The last entry ends at $FF)
0009C1r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0009C4r 2  18                       clc
0009C5r 2  60                       rts
0009C6r 2               
0009C6r 2               ; Print a string of the entry at offset .X, from its byte .Y.  Modifies: .A, .Y
0009C6r 2               NS_PUTS:
0009C6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_GETC
0009C9r 2  F0 06                    beq         @done
0009CBr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
0009CEr 2  C8                       iny
0009CFr 2  80 F5                    bra         NS_PUTS
0009D1r 2               
0009D1r 2               @done:
0009D1r 2  60                       rts
0009D2r 2               
0009D2r 2               ; Byte .Y of the entry at offset .X in this task's namespace.  OUT: .A (and Z).  Preserves .X, .Y
0009D2r 2               NS_GETC:
0009D2r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_TMP
0009D4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_XFER_SETUP
0009D7r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
0009D9r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_XFER + 1
0009DBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
0009DDr 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_IO_MAP_XFER
0009E1r 2  F1 FF 5A 9C  
0009E5r 2  F1 FF A0 F9  
0009EBr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_TMP
0009EDr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
0009EFr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_IO_UNMAP
0009F3r 2  7A 84 rr     
0009F6r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_TMP
0009F8r 2  09 00                    ora         #0
0009FAr 2  60                       rts
0009FBr 2               
0009FBr 2               ; Print device .A's name (from the device table, in shared bank ID $00).  Preserves .X
0009FBr 2               NS_PUT_DEV:
0009FBr 2  0A                       asl                                     ; Its entry: IO_DEV_TABLE + device * 16
0009FCr 2  0A                       asl
0009FDr 2  0A                       asl
0009FEr 2  0A                       asl
0009FFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS                   ; (Printing changes ZP_IO_CHUNK, not ZP_IO_OFS)
000A01r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000A03r 2               
000A03r 2               @char:
000A03r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_OFS
000A05r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000A07r 2  A9 88                    lda         #>IO_DEV_TABLE
000A09r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000A0Br 2  5A                       phy
000A0Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         RAM_BANK_REG                ; Map the device table (U = 0, bank $F0)
000A0Er 2  48                       pha
000A0Fr 2  AD F1 FF                 lda         U_REGISTER
000A12r 2  48                       pha
000A13r 2  9C F1 FF                 stz         U_REGISTER
000A16r 2  A9 F0                    lda         #SYS_BANK
000A18r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000A1Ar 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000A1Cr 2  7A                       ply                                     ; (Back: pull without touching .A)
000A1Dr 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER
000A20r 2  7A                       ply
000A21r 2  84 rr                    sty         RAM_BANK_REG
000A23r 2  7A                       ply
000A24r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
000A26r 2  F0 08                    beq         @done
000A28r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
000A2Br 2  C8                       iny
000A2Cr 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
000A2Er 2  D0 D3                    bne         @char
000A30r 2               
000A30r 2               @done:
000A30r 2  60                       rts
000A31r 2               
000A31r 2               ; Find the entry for the path at ZP_IO_OFS (an exact match).  OUT: ZP_IO_CHUNK = the entry, C = 0; or
000A31r 2               ; .A = ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND, C = 1.  ZP_IO_LEFT is set so that (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y with .Y = NS_PREFIX + n is the
000A31r 2               ; path's character n.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000A31r 2               NS_FIND:
000A31r 2  A9 02                    lda         #NS_PREFIX
000A33r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
000A35r 2  38                       sec
000A36r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_OFS
000A38r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
000A3Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
000A3Cr 2  E9 00                    sbc         #0
000A3Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000A40r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_FIRST
000A43r 2               
000A43r 2               @entry:
000A43r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_CHUNK)               ; (NS_TYPE)
000A45r 2  F0 11                    beq         @next
000A47r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #NS_PREFIX
000A49r 2               
000A49r 2               @compare:
000A49r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_LEFT),Y
000A4Br 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_IO_CHUNK),Y
000A4Dr 2  D0 09                    bne         @next
000A4Fr 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0                          ; Both ended: the same
000A51r 2  F0 03                    beq         @found
000A53r 2  C8                       iny
000A54r 2  80 F3                    bra         @compare
000A56r 2               
000A56r 2               @found:
000A56r 2  18                       clc
000A57r 2  60                       rts
000A58r 2               
000A58r 2               @next:
000A58r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_NEXT
000A5Br 2  D0 E6                    bne         @entry
000A5Dr 2  A9 70                    lda         #ERR_IO_NOT_FOUND
000A5Fr 2  38                       sec
000A60r 2  60                       rts
000A61r 2               
000A61r 1               .include "ser_srv.s"    ; The serial driver's file server (/dev/cons, /dev/ser)
000A61r 2               .debuginfo
000A61r 2               
000A61r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000A61r 2               ; The serial driver's file server: /dev/cons (fid SER_FID_CONS) and /dev/ser (fid SER_FID_SER).  BIOS ROM
000A61r 2               ; page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s); the driver itself (init, IRQ handler, rings) is in
000A61r 2               ; bios.s.  The serve routines run in the serial task (their page 0 gates are in bios.s), with a client's
000A61r 2               ; request (IO_SRV_MAP); the IRQ handler can come in at any time, and fills the RX ring and empties the
000A61r 2               ; TX ring.
000A61r 2               ;   Read: whatever is in the RX ring, up to the count (at least 1 byte); if it's empty, or the client
000A61r 2               ;         isn't in the foreground (/dev/cons only), the client waits (ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK) until a byte
000A61r 2               ;         arrives or the foreground changes.  /dev/cons echoes what it reads, and returns end of file
000A61r 2               ;         for an end-of-input key (SER_KEY_EOF, SER_KEY_EOF2), which isn't echoed.  It turns DEL (many
000A61r 2               ;         terminals' Backspace key) into BS, and echoes a BS as BS, space, BS (erasing the character).
000A61r 2               ;   Write: as much as fits in the TX ring; if nothing fits, the client waits until there's room.
000A61r 2               ;   Ctl: SER_CTL_FOREGROUND (.Y = task).  Stat: all zero.
000A61r 2               ; Server ZP (in the serial task): ZP_IO_TMP = count, ZP_IO_CHUNK = client.
000A61r 2               
000A61r 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000A61r 2               
000A61r 2  01 02 04 08  P2_BIT_MASKS:   .byte   $01, $02, $04, $08, $10, $20, $40, $80
000A65r 2  10 20 40 80  
000A69r 2               
000A69r 2               ; /dev/cons
000A69r 2               CONS_SERVE:
000A69r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #H9_OPEN
000A6Br 2  D0 0C                    bne         SER_REQUEST
000A6Dr 2  A9 00                    lda         #SER_FID_CONS
000A6Fr 2  18                       clc
000A70r 2  60                       rts
000A71r 2               
000A71r 2               ; /dev/ser
000A71r 2               SER_SERVE:
000A71r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #H9_OPEN
000A73r 2  D0 04                    bne         SER_REQUEST
000A75r 2  A9 01                    lda         #SER_FID_SER
000A77r 2  18                       clc
000A78r 2  60                       rts
000A79r 2               
000A79r 2               ; A request on an open fid.  IN: .A = request, .X = client, .Y = fid
000A79r 2               SER_REQUEST:
000A79r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000A7Br 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
000A7Dr 2  F0 36                    beq         SER_READ
000A7Fr 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
000A81r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000A83r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_WRITE
000A86r 2               :
000A86r 2  C9 06                    cmp         #H9_CTL
000A88r 2  F0 0B                    beq         SER_CTL
000A8Ar 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
000A8Cr 2  D0 03                    bne         SER_OK                              ; H9_CLUNK: nothing to do
000A8Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
000A91r 2               
000A91r 2               SER_OK:
000A91r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000A93r 2  18                       clc
000A94r 2  60                       rts
000A95r 2               
000A95r 2               SER_CTL:
000A95r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000A98r 2  A0 0B                    ldy         #IO_BLK_CTL_CODE
000A9Ar 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000A9Cr 2  48                       pha
000A9Dr 2  C8                       iny
000A9Er 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000AA0r 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = argument
000AA1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000AA4r 2  68                       pla
000AA5r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SER_CTL_FOREGROUND
000AA7r 2  D0 08                    bne         @bad
000AA9r 2  98                       tya
000AAAr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000AACr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SERIAL_SET_CAPTURE
000AAFr 2  80 E0                    bra         SER_OK
000AB1r 2               
000AB1r 2               @bad:
000AB1r 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000AB3r 2  38                       sec
000AB4r 2  60                       rts
000AB5r 2               
000AB5r 2               SER_READ:
000AB5r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BYTE                          ; (The fid: /dev/cons echoes what it reads)
000AB7r 2  C0 00                    cpy         #SER_FID_CONS
000AB9r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+                                  ; /dev/ser: any task
000ABBr 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_SER_CAPTURE
000ABDr 2  D0 7F                    bne         @wait                               ; /dev/cons: only the foreground task
000ABFr 2               :
000ABFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000AC2r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000AC4r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000AC6r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes wanted (1-256; 256 = 0)
000AC8r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000ACAr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Bytes done (the ring holds 255 at most)
000ACCr 2               
000ACCr 2               @next:
000ACCr 2  A4 FE                    ldy         SER_RX_TAIL
000ACEr 2  C4 FF                    cpy         SER_RX_HEAD
000AD0r 2  F0 3E                    beq         @empty
000AD2r 2  B9 00 02                 lda         SER_RX_BUF,Y
000AD5r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE                          ; /dev/cons: an end-of-input key?
000AD7r 2  D0 0E                    bne         @take
000AD9r 2  C9 04                    cmp         #SER_KEY_EOF
000ADBr 2  F0 2C                    beq         @eof
000ADDr 2  C9 1A                    cmp         #SER_KEY_EOF2
000ADFr 2  F0 28                    beq         @eof
000AE1r 2  C9 7F                    cmp         #ASCII_DEL                          ; DEL (many terminals' Backspace key) is a BS
000AE3r 2  D0 02                    bne         @take
000AE5r 2  A9 08                    lda         #ASCII_BACKSPACE
000AE7r 2               
000AE7r 2               @take:
000AE7r 2  E6 FE                    inc         SER_RX_TAIL
000AE9r 2  48                       pha
000AEAr 2  8A                       txa
000AEBr 2  A8                       tay
000AECr 2  68                       pla
000AEDr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000AEFr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE
000AF1r 2                           .assert     SER_FID_CONS = 0, error, "SER_READ: echo when the fid is 0"
000AF1r 2  D0 11                    bne         :+
000AF3r 2  C9 08                    cmp         #ASCII_BACKSPACE                    ; A backspace: BS, space, BS erases the character
000AF5r 2  D0 0A                    bne         @echo
000AF7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ECHO
000AFAr 2  A9 20                    lda         #ASCII_SPACE
000AFCr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ECHO
000AFFr 2  A9 08                    lda         #ASCII_BACKSPACE
000B01r 2               
000B01r 2               @echo:
000B01r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ECHO
000B04r 2               :
000B04r 2  E8                       inx
000B05r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_IO_TMP
000B07r 2  D0 C3                    bne         @next
000B09r 2               
000B09r 2               @eof:                                                       ; End of input: after the bytes read so far,
000B09r 2  8A                       txa                                             ;   or (none) this read is the end of file
000B0Ar 2  D0 21                    bne         @done
000B0Cr 2  E6 FE                    inc         SER_RX_TAIL                         ; (Taken: it's not seen again)
000B0Er 2  80 1D                    bra         @done                               ; (.A = 0 bytes)
000B10r 2               
000B10r 2               @empty:
000B10r 2  8A                       txa
000B11r 2  D0 1A                    bne         @done
000B13r 2  08                       php                                             ; Nothing: check again with IRQs off, so a
000B14r 2  78                       sei                                             ;   byte can't slip in before we're waiting
000B15r 2  A5 FF                    lda         SER_RX_HEAD
000B17r 2  C5 FE                    cmp         SER_RX_TAIL
000B19r 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
000B1Br 2  28                       plp                                             ; It just came in
000B1Cr 2  80 AE                    bra         @next
000B1Er 2               :
000B1Er 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000B20r 2  A0 FA                    ldy         #SER_RD_WAIT
000B22r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
000B25r 2  28                       plp
000B26r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000B28r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000B2Br 2  80 19                    bra         SER_WOULD_BLOCK
000B2Dr 2               
000B2Dr 2               @done:
000B2Dr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000B2Fr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count read
000B31r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000B33r 2  C8                       iny
000B34r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000B36r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000B38r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000B3Br 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_OK
000B3Er 2               
000B3Er 2               @wait:                                                      ; Not in the foreground: wait until it is
000B3Er 2  08                       php
000B3Fr 2  78                       sei
000B40r 2  A0 FA                    ldy         #SER_RD_WAIT
000B42r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
000B45r 2  28                       plp
000B46r 2               
000B46r 2               SER_WOULD_BLOCK:
000B46r 2  A9 73                    lda         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
000B48r 2  38                       sec
000B49r 2  60                       rts
000B4Ar 2               
000B4Ar 2               SER_WRITE:
000B4Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000B4Dr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000B4Fr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000B51r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes offered (1-256; 256 = 0)
000B53r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000B55r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Bytes taken
000B57r 2               
000B57r 2               @next:
000B57r 2  8A                       txa
000B58r 2  A8                       tay
000B59r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000B5Br 2  08                       php
000B5Cr 2  78                       sei
000B5Dr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000B5Fr 2  D0 07                    bne         @queue                              ; Busy: the TX IRQ sends it
000B61r 2  8D rr rr                 _M_SER_TX_BYTE                                  ; Idle (so the ring is empty): send it now
000B64r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS                  ; SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
000B66r 2  80 0C                    bra         @taken
000B68r 2               
000B68r 2               @queue:
000B68r 2  A4 FD                    ldy         SER_TX_HEAD
000B6Ar 2  99 00 03                 sta         SER_TX_BUF,Y
000B6Dr 2  C8                       iny
000B6Er 2  C4 FC                    cpy         SER_TX_TAIL
000B70r 2  F0 10                    beq         @full                               ; (The byte stored isn't counted: head stays)
000B72r 2  84 FD                    sty         SER_TX_HEAD
000B74r 2               
000B74r 2               @taken:
000B74r 2  28                       plp
000B75r 2  E8                       inx
000B76r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_IO_TMP
000B78r 2  D0 DD                    bne         @next
000B7Ar 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; All of it: the count stays
000B7Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000B7Fr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_OK
000B82r 2               
000B82r 2               @full:
000B82r 2  8A                       txa
000B83r 2  D0 0F                    bne         @short
000B85r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK                         ; Nothing fits: wait for room (IRQs are
000B87r 2  A0 F8                    ldy         #SER_WR_WAIT                        ;   still off, so the TX IRQ can't slip in)
000B89r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SER_ADD_WAIT
000B8Cr 2  28                       plp
000B8Dr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000B8Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000B92r 2  80 B2                    bra         SER_WOULD_BLOCK
000B94r 2               
000B94r 2               @short:
000B94r 2  28                       plp
000B95r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000B97r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count taken
000B99r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000B9Br 2  C8                       iny
000B9Cr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000B9Er 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000BA0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000BA3r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SER_OK
000BA6r 2               
000BA6r 2               ; Echo a byte read from /dev/cons (like a terminal: programs reading from a pipe or a file don't echo):
000BA6r 2               ; into the TX ring.  If it's full, wait for the TX IRQ to make room (about a character's time), or drop
000BA6r 2               ; the byte if IRQs are off.  Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000BA6r 2               SER_ECHO:
000BA6r 2  5A                       phy
000BA7r 2  08                       php
000BA8r 2               
000BA8r 2               @try:
000BA8r 2  78                       sei
000BA9r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000BABr 2  D0 07                    bne         @queue                              ; Busy: the TX IRQ sends it
000BADr 2  8D rr rr                 _M_SER_TX_BYTE                                  ; Idle (so the ring is empty): send it now
000BB0r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS                  ; SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
000BB2r 2  80 0C                    bra         @done
000BB4r 2               
000BB4r 2               @queue:
000BB4r 2  A4 FD                    ldy         SER_TX_HEAD
000BB6r 2  99 00 03                 sta         SER_TX_BUF,Y
000BB9r 2  C8                       iny
000BBAr 2  C4 FC                    cpy         SER_TX_TAIL
000BBCr 2  F0 05                    beq         @full
000BBEr 2  84 FD                    sty         SER_TX_HEAD
000BC0r 2               
000BC0r 2               @done:
000BC0r 2  28                       plp
000BC1r 2  7A                       ply
000BC2r 2  60                       rts
000BC3r 2               
000BC3r 2               @full:
000BC3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT                          ; (The byte)
000BC5r 2  68                       pla                                             ; The caller's flags: IRQs on?
000BC6r 2  48                       pha
000BC7r 2  29 04                    and         #$04                                ; (I)
000BC9r 2  F0 04                    beq         :+
000BCBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_LEFT                          ; No: drop it
000BCDr 2  80 F1                    bra         @done
000BCFr 2               :
000BCFr 2  58                       cli
000BD0r 2  CB                       wai                                             ; Until the TX IRQ (or another)
000BD1r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_LEFT
000BD3r 2  80 D3                    bra         @try
000BD5r 2               
000BD5r 2               ; Add a task to a wait mask (SER_RD_WAIT or SER_WR_WAIT).  IRQs must be off.
000BD5r 2               ; IN: .X = task, .Y = the mask's ZP address.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000BD5r 2               SER_ADD_WAIT:
000BD5r 2  DA                       phx
000BD6r 2  8A                       txa
000BD7r 2  29 07                    and         #7
000BD9r 2  AA                       tax
000BDAr 2  BD rr rr                 lda         P2_BIT_MASKS,X
000BDDr 2  FA                       plx
000BDEr 2  E0 08                    cpx         #8
000BE0r 2  90 01                    bcc         :+
000BE2r 2  C8                       iny                                             ; Tasks 8-15: the mask's high byte
000BE3r 2               :
000BE3r 2  19 00 00                 ora         a:$0000,Y
000BE6r 2  99 00 00                 sta         a:$0000,Y
000BE9r 2  60                       rts
000BEAr 2               
000BEAr 1               .include "snd_srv.s"    ; The sound driver's file server (/dev/snd)
000BEAr 2               .debuginfo
000BEAr 2               
000BEAr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000BEAr 2               ; The sound driver's file server: /dev/snd.  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see
000BEAr 2               ; all.s); the driver itself is sound.s.  The serve routine runs in the sound task (its page 0 gate is in
000BEAr 2               ; sound.s).
000BEAr 2               ;   Write: YM2151 register/value byte pairs (an odd last byte is left over); if the chip doesn't
000BEAr 2               ;          respond, the pairs written so far count, or ERR_IO_DEVICE if none.
000BEAr 2               ;   Read: end of file.  Ctl: SND_CTL_INIT, SND_CTL_TEST.  Stat: all zero.
000BEAr 2               ; Server ZP (in the sound task): ZP_IO_TMP = pair bytes offered.
000BEAr 2               
000BEAr 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000BEAr 2               
000BEAr 2               SND_SERVE:
000BEAr 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
000BECr 2  F0 44                    beq         @write
000BEEr 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
000BF0r 2  F0 0F                    beq         @read
000BF2r 2  C9 06                    cmp         #H9_CTL
000BF4r 2  F0 1C                    beq         @ctl
000BF6r 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
000BF8r 2  D0 03                    bne         @ok                                 ; H9_OPEN (fid 0), H9_CLUNK, H9_DUP
000BFAr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
000BFDr 2               
000BFDr 2               @ok:
000BFDr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000BFFr 2  18                       clc
000C00r 2  60                       rts
000C01r 2               
000C01r 2               @read:                                                      ; Nothing to read: end of file
000C01r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000C04r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000C06r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000C08r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C0Ar 2  C8                       iny
000C0Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C0Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000C10r 2  80 EB                    bra         @ok
000C12r 2               
000C12r 2               @ctl:
000C12r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000C15r 2  A0 0B                    ldy         #IO_BLK_CTL_CODE
000C17r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C19r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000C1Cr 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SND_CTL_INIT
000C1Er 2  D0 05                    bne         :+
000C20r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SOUND_INIT
000C23r 2  80 D8                    bra         @ok
000C25r 2               :
000C25r 2  C9 02                    cmp         #SND_CTL_TEST
000C27r 2  D0 05                    bne         @bad
000C29r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SOUND_TEST
000C2Cr 2  80 CF                    bra         @ok
000C2Er 2               
000C2Er 2               @bad:
000C2Er 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000C30r 2  38                       sec
000C31r 2  60                       rts
000C32r 2               
000C32r 2               @write:
000C32r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
000C35r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000C37r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ; Bytes offered (1-256: 256 = low 0, high 1)
000C39r 2  29 FE                    and         #$FE                                ; The count taken: whole pairs (an odd
000C3Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ;   last byte is left over)
000C3Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP
000C3Fr 2  C8                       iny
000C40r 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C42r 2  F0 16                    beq         @done                               ; Just 1 byte: nothing taken
000C44r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000C46r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000C48r 2               
000C48r 2               @pair:
000C48r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C4Ar 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = register
000C4Br 2  C8                       iny
000C4Cr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y                       ; .A = value
000C4Er 2  C8                       iny
000C4Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         YM_WRITE
000C52r 2  B0 0B                    bcs         @timeout
000C54r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_TMP
000C56r 2  D0 F0                    bne         @pair
000C58r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000C5Ar 2               
000C5Ar 2               @done:
000C5Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000C5Dr 2  80 9E                    bra         @ok
000C5Fr 2               
000C5Fr 2               @timeout:
000C5Fr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000C61r 2  88                       dey                                             ; This pair didn't go
000C62r 2  88                       dey
000C63r 2  D0 07                    bne         @short
000C65r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000C68r 2  A9 79                    lda         #ERR_IO_DEVICE
000C6Ar 2  38                       sec
000C6Br 2  60                       rts
000C6Cr 2               
000C6Cr 2               @short:
000C6Cr 2  98                       tya
000C6Dr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000C6Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C71r 2  C8                       iny
000C72r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000C74r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000C76r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000C79r 2  80 82                    bra         @ok
000C7Br 2

000D4Cr 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
000D4Er 2               @play_loop:
000D4Er 2                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
000D4Er 2                               ; 1. key off channel 0
000D4Er 2                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
000D4Er 2                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
000D4Er 2                               ; 4. one delay
000D4Er 2                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
000D4Er 2                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 5e.  one delay
000D4Er 2                               ; 5f.  increment counter
000D4Er 2                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
000D4Er 2                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
000D4Er 2                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
000D4Er 2                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
000D4Er 2               
000D4Er 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000D50r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
000D52r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000D55r 2
