000D7Dr 2  B1 FE                        lda         (YMN1L),y
000D7Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000D82r 2               
000D82r 2                               ; Key On
000D82r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000D84r 2  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
000D86r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000D89r 2               
000D89r 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
000D89r 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000D8Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000D8Er 2               
000D8Er 2  C8                           iny
000D8Fr 2  C0 07                        cpy         #7
000D91r 2  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
000D93r 2               
000D93r 2                               ; Next note on CH0
000D93r 2  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
000D94r 2  C8                           iny
000D95r 2  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
000D97r 2  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop
000D99r 2               
000D99r 2                               ; Stop sound
000D99r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
000D9Br 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00
000D9Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000DA0r 2               
000DA0r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
000DA2r 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01
000DA4r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         YM_WRITE
000DA7r 2               
000DA7r 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
000DA7r 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
000DA7r 2  08                           php
000DA8r 2  78                           sei
000DA9r 2               
000DA9r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
000DA9r 2  86 F8                        stx AZP0L
000DABr 2  84 F9                        sty AZP0H
000DADr 2  18                           clc
000DAEr 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
000DB0r 2  AA                           tax
000DB1r 2               
000DB1r 2  B2 F8                        lda (AZP0L)
000DB3r 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
000DB5r 2               
000DB5r 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
000DB5r 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
000DB5r 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
000DB5r 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
000DB5r 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
000DB7r 2               
000DB7r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000DBAr 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
000DBCr 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
000DBEr 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
000DBFr 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
000DBFr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
000DBFr 2  69 10                        adc #$10
000DC1r 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
000DC2r 2               @next:
000DC2r 2  8A                           txa
000DC3r 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
000DC3r 2  69 08                        adc #$08
000DC5r 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
000DC7r 2  C8                           iny
000DC8r 2  AA                           tax
000DC9r 2  B1 F8                        lda (AZP0L),y
000DCBr 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000DCCr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000DCFr 2  7A                           ply
000DD0r 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
000DD2r 2               @fail:
000DD2r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000DD3r 2  38                           sec
000DD4r 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
000DD5r 2               @success:
000DD5r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
000DD6r 2  18                           clc
000DD7r 2  60                           rts
000DD8r 2               
000DD8r 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
000DD8r 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
000DD8r 2               ; The loop below is tuned for 3.58 MHz; at a faster CPU clock it runs CPU_CLOCK_MULT times.
000DD8r 2               YM_DELAY_64:
000DD8r 2               .if ::CPU_CLOCK_MULT > 1
000DD8r 2                               pha
000DD8r 2                               jsr         YM_DELAY_64_1
000DD8r 2                               pla
000DD8r 2               .endif
000DD8r 2               
000DD8r 2               YM_DELAY_64_1:
000DD8r 2  DA 5A                        PUSH_XY
000DDAr 2  85 FA                        sta         YMTMP1
000DDCr 2               
000DDCr 2               @far_outer:
000DDCr 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
000DDEr 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
000DE0r 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
000DE2r 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
000DE4r 2               
000DE4r 2               @outer:
000DE4r 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
000DE6r 2               
000DE6r 2               @mid:
000DE6r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
000DE8r 2               
000DE8r 2               @inner:
000DE8r 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
000DE9r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000DEBr 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
000DECr 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000DEEr 2               
000DEEr 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
000DEEr 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
000DEFr 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000DF1r 2  C6 FA                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
000DF3r 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
000DF5r 2  7A FA                        PULL_YX
000DF7r 2               
000DF7r 1               .include "pipe_srv.s"   ; The pipe server (/dev/pipe)
000DF7r 2               .debuginfo
000DF7r 2               
000DF7r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000DF7r 2               ; The pipe server: /dev/pipe.  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2` (see all.s); it runs in
000DF7r 2               ; its own Resident task (PIPE_TASK_NUM; PIPE_INIT and the gate are in io_p0.s), which holds the pipe
000DF7r 2               ; table and the rings (PIPE_TABLE, PIPE_BUFS).  Requests run one at a time (TASK_CALL: no task switch
000DF7r 2               ; while the server runs), and no IRQ handler touches the pipes, so no locking is needed.
000DF7r 2               ;
000DF7r 2               ;   Opening /dev/pipe makes a new pipe; the fid is its index.  IO_PIPE opens the read end and adds a
000DF7r 2               ;   write end.  Each request carries the fd's mode (IO_BLK_MODE), so the server counts the fds on each
000DF7r 2               ;   end: H9_OPEN and H9_DUP add one, H9_CLUNK takes one away; the pipe is free when both counts are 0.
000DF7r 2               ;   Read: what's in the ring, up to the count; if it's empty, the reader waits, or gets end of file if
000DF7r 2               ;         there are no writers left.
000DF7r 2               ;   Write: as much as fits; if nothing fits, the writer waits; ERR_IO_BROKEN if there are no readers.
000DF7r 2               ; Server ZP (in the pipe task): ZP_IO_CHUNK = client, ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1 = the pipe's table offset,
000DF7r 2               ; ZP_IO_OFS = its ring, ZP_IO_TMP = bytes asked for, ZP_IO_BYTE = bytes done, ZP_IO_LEFT = a wait mask.
000DF7r 2               
000DF7r 2               .segment "IO_P2"
000DF7r 2               
000DF7r 2               ; IN: .A = request, .X = client, .Y = fid
000DF7r 2               PIPE_SERVE:
000DF7r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000DF9r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #H9_OPEN
000DFBr 2  F0 3A                    beq         PIPE_OPEN
000DFDr 2  48                       pha
000DFEr 2  98                       tya
000DFFr 2  29 07                    and         #PIPE_MAX - 1
000E01r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS + 1                       ; (Its ring: set up below)
000E03r 2  0A                       asl
000E04r 2  0A                       asl
000E05r 2  0A                       asl
000E06r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1                     ; The pipe's table offset
000E08r 2                           .assert     PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE = 8, error, "PIPE_SERVE: entry offset = fid * 8"
000E08r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
000E0Ar 2  18                       clc
000E0Br 2  69 03                    adc         #>PIPE_BUFS
000E0Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_OFS + 1
000E0Fr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_OFS
000E11r 2  68                       pla
000E12r 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
000E14r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000E16r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_READ
000E19r 2               :
000E19r 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
000E1Br 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000E1Dr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_WRITE
000E20r 2               :
000E20r 2  C9 07                    cmp         #H9_DUP
000E22r 2  F0 3F                    beq         PIPE_DUP
000E24r 2  C9 04                    cmp         #H9_CLUNK
000E26r 2  F0 40                    beq         PIPE_CLUNK
000E28r 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
000E2Ar 2  D0 07                    bne         PIPE_BAD
000E2Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STAT_ZERO
000E2Fr 2               
000E2Fr 2               PIPE_OK:
000E2Fr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000E31r 2  18                       clc
000E32r 2  60                       rts
000E33r 2               
000E33r 2               PIPE_BAD:
000E33r 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
000E35r 2  38                       sec
000E36r 2  60                       rts
000E37r 2               
000E37r 2               PIPE_OPEN:
000E37r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; A free pipe
000E39r 2               
000E39r 2               @find:
000E39r 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_READERS,X
000E3Cr 2  1D 01 02                 ora         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000E3Fr 2  F0 0D                    beq         @free
000E41r 2  8A                       txa
000E42r 2  18                       clc
000E43r 2  69 08                    adc         #PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000E45r 2  AA                       tax
000E46r 2  E0 40                    cpx         #PIPE_MAX * PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000E48r 2  D0 EF                    bne         @find
000E4Ar 2  A9 7B                    lda         #ERR_IO_NO_PIPES
000E4Cr 2  38                       sec
000E4Dr 2  60                       rts
000E4Er 2               
000E4Er 2               @free:
000E4Er 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000E50r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE
000E52r 2               
000E52r 2               @clear:
000E52r 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X                        ; Empty, nobody waiting
000E55r 2  E8                       inx
000E56r 2  88                       dey
000E57r 2  D0 F9                    bne         @clear
000E59r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_ADD_FD                         ; The opener's end(s)
000E5Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1                     ; fid = the pipe's index
000E5Er 2  4A                       lsr
000E5Fr 2  4A                       lsr
000E60r 2  4A                       lsr
000E61r 2  18                       clc
000E62r 2  60                       rts
000E63r 2               
000E63r 2               PIPE_DUP:
000E63r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_ADD_FD
000E66r 2  80 C7                    bra         PIPE_OK
000E68r 2               
000E68r 2               PIPE_CLUNK:
000E68r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_MODE
000E6Br 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = read end
000E6Cr 2  90 0C                    bcc         @write_end
000E6Er 2  48                       pha
000E6Fr 2  DE 00 02                 dec         PIPE_READERS,X
000E72r 2  D0 05                    bne         :+
000E74r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT                       ; No readers left: the writers find out
000E76r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000E79r 2               :
000E79r 2  68                       pla
000E7Ar 2               
000E7Ar 2               @write_end:
000E7Ar 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = write end
000E7Br 2  90 B2                    bcc         PIPE_OK
000E7Dr 2  DE 01 02                 dec         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000E80r 2  D0 AD                    bne         PIPE_OK
000E82r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; No writers left: the readers get end of file
000E84r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000E87r 2  80 A6                    bra         PIPE_OK
000E89r 2               
000E89r 2               PIPE_READ:
000E89r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000E8Br 2  BD 02 02                 lda         PIPE_HEAD,X
000E8Er 2  DD 03 02                 cmp         PIPE_TAIL,X
000E91r 2  D0 1B                    bne         @data
000E93r 2  BD 01 02                 lda         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000E96r 2  F0 05                    beq         @eof
000E98r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; Empty: wait for a writer
000E9Ar 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_WAIT
000E9Dr 2               
000E9Dr 2               @eof:
000E9Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000EA0r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000EA2r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000EA4r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000EA6r 2  C8                       iny
000EA7r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000EA9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000EACr 2  80 81                    bra         PIPE_OK
000EAEr 2               
000EAEr 2               @data:
000EAEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000EB1r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000EB3r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000EB5r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes wanted (1-256; 256 = 0)
000EB7r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000EB9r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_BYTE
000EBBr 2               
000EBBr 2               @next:
000EBBr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000EBDr 2  BC 03 02                 ldy         PIPE_TAIL,X
000EC0r 2  98                       tya
000EC1r 2  DD 02 02                 cmp         PIPE_HEAD,X
000EC4r 2  F0 10                    beq         @end
000EC6r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y
000EC8r 2  FE 03 02                 inc         PIPE_TAIL,X
000ECBr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE
000ECDr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000ECFr 2  C8                       iny
000ED0r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BYTE
000ED2r 2  C4 rr                    cpy         ZP_IO_TMP
000ED4r 2  D0 E5                    bne         @next
000ED6r 2               
000ED6r 2               @end:
000ED6r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000ED8r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE                          ; The count read (the ring holds 255 at most)
000EDAr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000EDCr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000EDEr 2  C8                       iny
000EDFr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000EE1r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000EE3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000EE6r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT                       ; There's room now
000EE8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000EEBr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_OK
000EEEr 2               
000EEEr 2               PIPE_WRITE:
000EEEr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000EF0r 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_READERS,X
000EF3r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+
000EF5r 2  A9 7A                    lda         #ERR_IO_BROKEN                      ; Nobody will read it
000EF7r 2  38                       sec
000EF8r 2  60                       rts
000EF9r 2               :
000EF9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000EFCr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT
000EFEr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000F00r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; Bytes offered (1-256; 256 = 0)
000F02r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; The data area
000F04r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_BYTE
000F06r 2               
000F06r 2               @next:
000F06r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_BYTE
000F08r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000F0Ar 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000F0Cr 2  BC 02 02                 ldy         PIPE_HEAD,X
000F0Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_OFS),Y                       ; (The slot at head is always free)
000F11r 2  C8                       iny
000F12r 2  98                       tya
000F13r 2  DD 03 02                 cmp         PIPE_TAIL,X
000F16r 2  F0 0F                    beq         @full
000F18r 2  9D 02 02                 sta         PIPE_HEAD,X
000F1Br 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_IO_BYTE
000F1Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE
000F1Fr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IO_TMP
000F21r 2  D0 E3                    bne         @next
000F23r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1                       ; All of it: the count stays
000F25r 2  80 16                    bra         @taken
000F27r 2               
000F27r 2               @full:
000F27r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000F29r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_BYTE
000F2Br 2  D0 07                    bne         @short
000F2Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP                        ; Nothing fits: wait for a reader
000F30r 2  A0 06                    ldy         #PIPE_WR_WAIT
000F32r 2  80 14                    bra         PIPE_WAIT
000F34r 2               
000F34r 2               @short:
000F34r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; The count taken
000F36r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000F38r 2  C8                       iny
000F39r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000F3Br 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000F3Dr 2               
000F3Dr 2               @taken:
000F3Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000F40r 2  A0 04                    ldy         #PIPE_RD_WAIT                       ; There's data now
000F42r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_WAKE
000F45r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PIPE_OK
000F48r 2               
000F48r 2               ; The client waits: add it to a wait mask of the pipe, and tell the IO layer.  IN: .Y = PIPE_RD_WAIT or
000F48r 2               ; PIPE_WR_WAIT
000F48r 2               PIPE_WAIT:
000F48r 2  98                       tya
000F49r 2  18                       clc
000F4Ar 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000F4Cr 2  AA                       tax                                             ; PIPE_TABLE,X = the mask
000F4Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000F4Fr 2  29 07                    and         #7
000F51r 2  A8                       tay
000F52r 2  B9 rr rr                 lda         P2_BIT_MASKS,Y
000F55r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000F57r 2  C0 08                    cpy         #8
000F59r 2  90 01                    bcc         :+
000F5Br 2  E8                       inx                                             ; Tasks 8-15: the mask's high byte
000F5Cr 2               :
000F5Cr 2  1D 00 02                 ora         PIPE_TABLE,X
000F5Fr 2  9D 00 02                 sta         PIPE_TABLE,X
000F62r 2  A9 73                    lda         #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
000F64r 2  38                       sec
000F65r 2  60                       rts
000F66r 2               
000F66r 2               ; Wake the tasks in a wait mask of the pipe, and clear it.  IN: .Y = PIPE_RD_WAIT or PIPE_WR_WAIT
000F66r 2               ; OUT: .X = the pipe's table offset.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000F66r 2               PIPE_WAKE:
000F66r 2  98                       tya
000F67r 2  18                       clc
000F68r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000F6Ar 2  AA                       tax
000F6Br 2  BD 00 02                 lda         PIPE_TABLE,X
000F6Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT
000F70r 2  BD 01 02                 lda         PIPE_TABLE + 1,X
000F73r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000F75r 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X
000F78r 2  9E 01 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE + 1,X
000F7Br 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000F7Dr 2               
000F7Dr 2               @loop:
000F7Dr 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_IO_LEFT + 1
000F7Fr 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_IO_LEFT
000F81r 2  90 04                    bcc         :+
000F83r 2  98                       tya
000F84r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WAKE
000F87r 2               :
000F87r 2  C8                       iny
000F88r 2  C0 10                    cpy         #16
000F8Ar 2  D0 F1                    bne         @loop
000F8Cr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000F8Er 2  60                       rts
000F8Fr 2               
000F8Fr 2               ; Count a new fd on the pipe's end(s), from the request's mode.  OUT: .X = the pipe's table offset
000F8Fr 2               PIPE_ADD_FD:
000F8Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         PIPE_MODE
000F92r 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = read end
000F93r 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
000F95r 2  FE 00 02                 inc         PIPE_READERS,X
000F98r 2               :
000F98r 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; C = write end
000F99r 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
000F9Br 2  FE 01 02                 inc         PIPE_WRITERS,X
000F9Er 2               :
000F9Er 2  60                       rts
000F9Fr 2               
000F9Fr 2               ; The request's mode (the fd's).  OUT: .A = mode, .X = the pipe's table offset.  Modifies: .Y
000F9Fr 2               PIPE_MODE:
000F9Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP_C
000FA2r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #IO_BLK_MODE
000FA4r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
000FA6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
000FA9r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK + 1
000FABr 2  60                       rts
000FACr 2               
000FACr 2               ; IO_SRV_MAP for the client in ZP_IO_CHUNK.  Modifies: .X
000FACr 2               IO_SRV_MAP_C:
000FACr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CHUNK
000FAEr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IO_SRV_MAP
000FB1r 2               
000FB1r 1               .include "io_test.s"
000FB1r 2               .debuginfo
000FB1r 2               
000FB1r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000FB1r 2               ; IO self test (TH_IO_TEST, $F88A; from WOZMON: F88AR).  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2`
000FB1r 2               ; (see all.s).  Runs in the current task and prints "IO test: ok", or "IO test: FAIL x ee" (x = failing
000FB1r 2               ; step, ee = error code or value).  Uses /dev/null, /dev/zero and /dev/cons (it writes "cons "), and a
000FB1r 2               ; 300-byte buffer from the MMU.  The task's other fds (e.g. stdin, stdout, stderr) are left alone.
000FB1r 2               
000FB1r 2               .segment "IO_P2"

001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_NS         call, path, other               ; IO_MOUNT / IO_BIND path, other
001021r 2                           lda         #<other
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF
001021r 2                           lda         #>other
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
001021r 2                           lda         #<path
001021r 2                           ldy         #>path
001021r 2                           jsr         call
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_UNMOUNT    path
001021r 2                           lda         #<path
001021r 2                           ldy         #>path
001021r 2                           jsr         IO_UNMOUNT
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               IT_BUF_SIZE     = 300
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_FAIL_IF_C  step                                ; Fail if the call returned an error
001021r 2                           bcc         :+
001021r 2                           ldx         #step
001021r 2                           jmp         @fail
001021r 2               :
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_FAIL_IF_NC step, error                         ; Fail unless the call failed with this error
001021r 2                           bcs         :+
001021r 2                           lda         #0
001021r 2                           ldx         #step
001021r 2                           jmp         @fail
001021r 2               :
001021r 2                           cmp         #error
001021r 2                           beq         :+
001021r 2                           ldx         #step
001021r 2                           jmp         @fail
001021r 2               :
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_OPEN       name, mode                          ; .A = fd
001021r 2                           lda         #<name
001021r 2                           ldy         #>name
001021r 2                           ldx         #mode
001021r 2                           jsr         IO_OPEN
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_COUNT      fd, count                           ; Set up a transfer of count bytes to/from the buffer
001021r 2                           lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF
001021r 2                           lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
001021r 2                           lda         #<count
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_CNT
001021r 2                           lda         #>count
001021r 2                           sta         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
001021r 2                           lda         fd
001021r 2               .endmacro
001021r 2               
001021r 2               .macro _M_IT_EXPECT_CNT step, count                         ; Fail unless ZP_IO_CNT = count
001021r 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT
001021r 2                           cmp         #<count
001021r 2                           bne         :+
001021r 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT + 1
001021r 2                           cmp         #>count
001021r 2                           beq         :++
001021r 2               :
001021r 2                           lda         ZP_IO_CNT
001021r 2                           ldx         #step
001021r 2                           jmp         @fail
001021r 2               :
001021r 2               .endmacro

0012D4r 2               
0012D4r 2               ; A pipe between two tasks: a child writes IT_PIPE_SIZE bytes (more than the ring holds, so both sides
0012D4r 2               ; wait for each other) and ends, which closes its write end; we read to end of file.  The child gets the
0012D4r 2               ; write end as fd IO_MAX_FDS - 1 (IO_DUP2), and inherits it.

0014F5r 1               .include "post_ram.s"   ; POST paged RAM line tests
0014F5r 2               .debuginfo
0014F5r 2               
0014F5r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0014F5r 2               ; POST paged RAM line tests (see POST in os_main.s).  BIOS ROM page 2, included inside `.scope PAGE2`
0014F5r 2               ; (see all.s); POST calls it through a gate, at boot (task 0, IRQs off, polled serial output).
0014F5r 2               ; Prints the second POST line:
0014F5r 2               ;       RAM U:x bb:x/dd/aaaa bb:x/dd/aaaa ...
0014F5r 2               ;   Each is a hex mask of bad lines (bit set = bad; all 0 when good):
0014F5r 2               ;   U:x      U lines U0-U3 (bit n = Un), tested on shared bank $F0
0014F5r 2               ;   bb:x/dd/aaaa  bank bb at $8000-$9FFF: bank register lines 0-3 (bb ^ 1/2/4/8), data lines D0-D7 and
0014F5r 2               ;            address lines A0-A12.  Tested: the first bank of each shared RAM chip ($F0, $F4, $F8, $FC, with
0014F5r 2               ;            U = 0; a missing chip shows as bad lines) and of each installed task RAM module.
0014F5r 2               ;   Destructive: run before anything is kept in paged RAM.  Leaves RAM_BANK_REG and U at 0.
0014F5r 2               ;   Records the chips that failed, for the MMU to leave unused (in task 0): ZP_M_BAD_SH (shared RAM chips,
0014F5r 2               ;   used by SHARED_RAM_INIT) and ZP_M_BAD_MODS (task RAM modules, used by TASK_RAM_INIT).
0014F5r 2               
0014F5r 2               .segment "POST_P2"
000000r 2               
000000r 2  0D 0A 52 41  POST_S_RAM: .byte ASCII_CR, ASCII_LF, "RAM U:", 0
000004r 2  4D 20 55 3A  
000008r 2  00           
000009r 2               
000009r 2               POST_RAM_TEST:
000009r 2  A2 00                    ldx                 #0
00000Br 2               :
00000Br 2  BD rr rr                 lda                 POST_S_RAM,X
00000Er 2  F0 06                    beq                 :+
000010r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000013r 2  E8                       inx
000014r 2  80 F5                    bra                 :-
000016r 2               :
000016r 2  A9 FF 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           U_REGISTER, ZP_TEMP_VEC2
00001Ar 2  A9 F1 85 rr  
00001Er 2  A9 F0                    lda                 #$F0
000020r 2  85 rr                    sta                 RAM_BANK_REG
000022r 2  A9 00                    lda                 #0                                  ; U lines, on shared bank $F0
000024r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_LINE_TEST
000027r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
00002Ar 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           RAM_BANK_REG, ZP_TEMP_VEC2
00002Er 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000032r 2  A9 F0                    lda                 #$F0                                ; Shared RAM (U = 0): each chip's first bank
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @shared:
000034r 2  A2 03                    ldx                 #$03                                ; Bank lines 2-3 pick the chip: only lines
000036r 2  86 rr                    stx                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ;   0-1 count against this one
000038r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_BANK_TEST                      ; Z = 0: bad
00003Br 2  18                       clc
00003Cr 2  F0 01                    beq                 :+
00003Er 2  38                       sec
00003Fr 2               :
00003Fr 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chip c -> bit 4 + c
000041r 2  18                       clc
000042r 2  69 04                    adc                 #4
000044r 2  90 EE                    bcc                 @shared
000046r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chip c -> bit c
000048r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Ar 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Cr 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_BAD_SH
00004Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 MMU_PROBE_MODULES                   ; Task RAM: each installed module's first bank
000051r 2  A9 00                    lda                 #0
000053r 2               
000053r 2               @module:
000053r 2  46 rr                    lsr                 ZP_M_MODS + 1
000055r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_MODS
000057r 2  90 0B                    bcc                 :+                                  ; Not installed (C = 0: not bad)
000059r 2  A2 0F                    ldx                 #$0F                                ; All 4 bank lines are the module's
00005Br 2  86 rr                    stx                 ZP_M_TEMP
00005Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_BANK_TEST                      ; Z = 0: bad
000060r 2  18                       clc
000061r 2  F0 01                    beq                 :+
000063r 2  38                       sec
000064r 2               :
000064r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_MODS + 1                   ; Module m -> bit m (after all 16)
000066r 2  66 rr                    ror                 ZP_M_BAD_MODS
000068r 2  18                       clc
000069r 2  69 10                    adc                 #$10
00006Br 2  90 E6                    bcc                 @module                             ; (Module 15 is the shared banks: never
00006Dr 2  64 rr                    stz                 RAM_BANK_REG                        ;   installed, so never tested)
00006Fr 2  9C F1 FF                 stz                 U_REGISTER
000072r 2  60                       rts
000073r 2               
000073r 2               ; Test paged RAM bank .A ($8000-$9FFF) and print " bb:x/dd/aaaa": the bad bank lines (x, see
000073r 2               ; POST_LINE_TEST), data lines (dd: D0-D7) and address lines (aaaa: A0-A12); bit set = bad line.
000073r 2               ; ZP_TEMP_VEC2 must point at RAM_BANK_REG.  Leaves the bank selected.
000073r 2               ; IN: ZP_M_TEMP = the bank lines that count against this bank.  OUT: Z = 0 if any line is bad.  Preserves .A
000073r 2               POST_BANK_TEST:
000073r 2  48                       pha
000074r 2  48                       pha
000075r 2  A9 20                    lda                 #' '
000077r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
00007Ar 2  68                       pla
00007Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
00007Er 2  A9 3A                    lda                 #':'
000080r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000083r 2  68                       pla
000084r 2  48                       pha
000085r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_LINE_TEST
000088r 2  48                       pha
000089r 2  25 rr                    and                 ZP_M_TEMP
00008Br 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ; ZP_M_TEMP = all of this bank's bad lines
00008Dr 2  68                       pla
00008Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
000091r 2  A9 2F                    lda                 #'/'
000093r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000096r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP                             ; Data lines: walking one at $8000
000098r 2  A2 01                    ldx                 #1
00009Ar 2               :
00009Ar 2  8E 00 80                 stx                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
00009Dr 2  8A                       txa
00009Er 2  4D 00 80                 eor                 PAGED_RAM_BASE                      ; Bits that read back wrong
0000A1r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP
0000A3r 2  8A                       txa
0000A4r 2  0A                       asl
0000A5r 2  AA                       tax
0000A6r 2  D0 F2                    bne                 :-
0000A8r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP
0000AAr 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
0000ACr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
0000AFr 2  A9 2F                    lda                 #'/'
0000B1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2               ; Address lines: $8000 = 0, $8000 + 2^n = n + 1 (n = 0-12).  Line n is bad if $8000 + 2^n reads back
0000B4r 2               ; wrong, or its write landed on $8000.
0000B4r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Bad lines
0000B6r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000B8r 2  9C 00 80                 stz                 PAGED_RAM_BASE

0000BDr 2               
0000BDr 2               @pass:
0000BDr 2  A9 01                    lda                 #1
0000BFr 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4                        ; 2^n
0000C1r 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000C3r 2  A0 01                    ldy                 #1                                  ; n + 1
0000C5r 2

0000CBr 2  09 80                    ora                 #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
0000CDr 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
0000CFr 2  98                       tya
0000D0r 2  E0 00                    cpx                 #0
0000D2r 2  D0 04                    bne                 :+
0000D4r 2  92 rr                    sta                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC3)
0000D6r 2  80 11                    bra                 @next
0000D8r 2               :
0000D8r 2  CD 00 80                 cmp                 PAGED_RAM_BASE
0000DBr 2  F0 04                    beq                 @bad
0000DDr 2  D2 rr                    cmp                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC3)
0000DFr 2  F0 08                    beq                 @next
0000E1r 2               
0000E1r 2               @bad:
0000E1r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4
0000E3r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP_VEC
0000E5r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000E7r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000E9r 2               
0000E9r 2               @next:
0000E9r 2  C8                       iny
0000EAr 2  06 rr                    asl                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4
0000ECr 2  26 rr                    rol                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000EEr 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC4 + 1
0000F0r 2  C9 20                    cmp                 #$20                                ; Past A12
0000F2r 2  D0 D1                    bne                 @line
0000F4r 2  E8                       inx
0000F5r 2  E0 02                    cpx                 #2
0000F7r 2  D0 C4                    bne                 @pass
0000F9r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000FBr 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
0000FDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
000100r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP_VEC
000102r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_M_TEMP
000104r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
000107r 2  68                       pla
000108r 2  A6 rr                    ldx                 ZP_M_TEMP                           ; Z = 0: bad
00010Ar 2  60                       rts
00010Br 2               
00010Br 2               ; Bank line test: the byte at $8000, with the register at (ZP_TEMP_VEC2) (RAM_BANK_REG or U) set to
00010Br 2               ; .A ^ 8, .A ^ 4, .A ^ 2, .A ^ 1 and .A.  OUT: .A = bad lines (bit n = register bit n, n = 0-3); the
00010Br 2               ; register is left at .A.  Modifies: .X, .Y
00010Br 2               POST_LINE_TEST:
00010Br 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_TEMP_2
00010Dr 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_TEMP
00010Fr 2  A0 00                    ldy                 #0                                  ; Pass 0 writes, pass 1 checks
000111r 2               
000111r 2               @pass:
000111r 2  A2 10                    ldx                 #$10
000113r 2               
000113r 2               @line:
000113r 2  8A                       txa

000115r 2  AA                       tax                                                     ; 8, 4, 2, 1, 0 (the marker)
000116r 2  45 rr                    eor                 ZP_TEMP_2
000118r 2  92 rr                    sta                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC2)
00011Ar 2  8A                       txa
00011Br 2  C0 00                    cpy                 #0
00011Dr 2  D0 05                    bne                 :+

000122r 2  80 07                    bra                 @next
000124r 2               :

000127r 2  F0 02                    beq                 @next
000129r 2  04 rr                    tsb                 ZP_TEMP
00012Br 2

00012Br 2  8A                       txa
00012Cr 2  D0 E5                    bne                 @line
00012Er 2  C8                       iny
00012Fr 2  C0 02                    cpy                 #2
000131r 2  D0 DE                    bne                 @pass
000133r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_TEMP
000135r 2  60                       rts
000136r 2               
000136r 2               ; Print .A as 2 hex digits (POST_PUTBYTE) or its low nibble as 1 (POST_PUTHEX).  Modifies: .Y
000136r 2               POST_PUTBYTE:

000139r 2  4A                       lsr
00013Ar 2  4A                       lsr
00013Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
00013Er 2  68                       pla
00013Fr 2

000147r 2               :

00014Cr 2
