00014Cr 1               
00014Cr 1               ; BIOS ROM page 3 (W = 3): storage.  Its own scope, so page 3 code binds to the page 3 gates in page3.s
00014Cr 1               .scope PAGE3
00014Cr 1               .include "page3.s"      ; must be first in the scope
00014Cr 2               .debuginfo
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00014Cr 2               ; BIOS ROM page 3 (W = 3): storage (SPI, the SD card, its server, and later the FAT32 server).
00014Cr 2               ;
00014Cr 2               ;   This file is included inside `.scope PAGE3` (see all.s), before the rest of page 3, so the gate labels
00014Cr 2               ;   below take precedence over the page 0 routines of the same name for all page 3 code.  Page 3 code
00014Cr 2               ;   runs with W = 3; it's entered through the compact gates in storage.s (page 0).
00014Cr 2               
00014Cr 2               .segment "GATES_P3"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Gates from page 3 to page 0 routines (they run in the current task: the storage task)
000000r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_SRV_MAP,     ::IO_SRV_MAP,           0
000004r 2  rr 00        
000006r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_SRV_UNMAP,   ::IO_SRV_UNMAP,         0
00000Ar 2  rr 00        
00000Cr 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     MM_ALLOC,       ::MM_ALLOC,             0
000010r 2  rr 00        
000012r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     MM_LOCK,        ::MM_LOCK,              0
000016r 2  rr 00        
000018r 2               
000018r 1               .include "spi.s"        ; SPI (bit-banged on the VIA's port B)
000018r 2               .debuginfo
000018r 2               
000018r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000018r 2               ; SPI, bit-banged on the VIA's port B (BIOS ROM page 3, the storage page; included inside `.scope PAGE3`,
000018r 2               ; see all.s).  Only the storage task uses it (the SD card server), so its state is that task's ZP.
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;   Port B: PB0 = SCLK, PB1 = /CS enable (low: the device selected by PB3-PB6 is selected), PB2 = MOSI,
000018r 2               ;   PB3-PB5 = device 0-7 (a 74HC138 on the board: /nSPI_CS0-7, the SPI headers J18-J25), PB6 = 1 for
000018r 2               ;   devices 8-15 (decoded on the card slots), PB7 = MISO (input).
000018r 2               ;   Mode 0: SCLK idles low, both sides sample on the rising edge.  About 90 kHz for SPI_XFER and 130 kHz
000018r 2               ;   for SPI_RECV at 3.58 MHz: under the 400 kHz an SD card allows while it starts up.
000018r 2               
000018r 2               .segment "STORAGE_P3"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up port B for SPI (nothing selected).  Doesn't touch the VIA's timers or interrupts (T1 is the
000000r 2               ; scheduler's tick).  Modifies: .A
000000r 2               SPI_INIT:
000000r 2  A9 06                    lda         #SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI         ; Deselected, SCLK low, MOSI high
000002r 2  8D rr rr                 sta         IOR_SPI_DATA
000005r 2  A9 7F                    lda         #SPI_DDR_BITS
000007r 2  8D rr rr                 sta         IOR_SPI_DDR
00000Ar 2  60                       rts
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               ; Select SPI device .A (0-15).  Modifies: .A
00000Br 2               SPI_SELECT:
00000Br 2  0A                       asl
00000Cr 2  0A                       asl
00000Dr 2  0A                       asl
00000Er 2  29 78                    and         #SPI_DEV_F                          ; (PB3-PB6)
000010r 2  09 04                    ora         #SPI_BIT_MOSI                       ; /CS enable low, SCLK low, MOSI high
000012r 2  85 FF                    sta         SPI_PORT
000014r 2  8D rr rr                 sta         IOR_SPI_DATA
000017r 2  60                       rts
000018r 2               
000018r 2               ; Deselect (the device keeps its number; the enable goes high).  Preserves .X, .Y, .A
000018r 2               SPI_DESELECT:
000018r 2  48                       pha
000019r 2  A5 FF                    lda         SPI_PORT
00001Br 2  09 02                    ora         #SPI_BIT_CSB
00001Dr 2  85 FF                    sta         SPI_PORT
00001Fr 2  8D rr rr                 sta         IOR_SPI_DATA
000022r 2  68                       pla
000023r 2  60                       rts
000024r 2               
000024r 2               ; Send .A and return the byte received at the same time.  Preserves .X, .Y
000024r 2               SPI_XFER:
000024r 2  DA                       phx
000025r 2  5A                       phy
000026r 2  85 FD                    sta         SPI_OUT
000028r 2  A5 FF                    lda         SPI_PORT
00002Ar 2  29 FB                    and         #<~SPI_BIT_MOSI
00002Cr 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = the port with MOSI low
00002Dr 2  09 04                    ora         #SPI_BIT_MOSI
00002Fr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = with MOSI high
000030r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1                                  ; (The 1 comes out after 8 bits)
000032r 2  85 FE                    sta         SPI_IN
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @bit:
000034r 2  06 FD                    asl         SPI_OUT                             ; C = the bit to send
000036r 2  B0 05                    bcs         @one
000038r 2  8E rr rr                 stx         IOR_SPI_DATA
00003Br 2  80 03                    bra         @clock
00003Dr 2               
00003Dr 2               @one:
00003Dr 2  8C rr rr                 sty         IOR_SPI_DATA
000040r 2               
000040r 2               @clock:
000040r 2  EE rr rr                 inc         IOR_SPI_DATA                        ; SCLK high: both sides sample
000043r 2  AD rr rr                 lda         IOR_SPI_DATA                        ; (MISO = bit 7)
000046r 2  CE rr rr                 dec         IOR_SPI_DATA                        ; SCLK low
000049r 2  0A                       asl
00004Ar 2  26 FE                    rol         SPI_IN
00004Cr 2  90 E6                    bcc         @bit
00004Er 2  7A                       ply
00004Fr 2  FA                       plx
000050r 2  A5 FE                    lda         SPI_IN
000052r 2  60                       rts
000053r 2               
000053r 2               ; Receive a byte (sending $FF, MOSI high).  OUT: .A.  Preserves .X, .Y
000053r 2               SPI_RECV:
000053r 2  A5 FF                    lda         SPI_PORT                            ; (MOSI high)
000055r 2  8D rr rr                 sta         IOR_SPI_DATA
000058r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
00005Ar 2  85 FE                    sta         SPI_IN
00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               @bit:
00005Cr 2  EE rr rr                 inc         IOR_SPI_DATA                        ; SCLK high
00005Fr 2  AD rr rr                 lda         IOR_SPI_DATA
000062r 2  CE rr rr                 dec         IOR_SPI_DATA                        ; SCLK low
000065r 2  0A                       asl
000066r 2  26 FE                    rol         SPI_IN
000068r 2  90 F2                    bcc         @bit
00006Ar 2  A5 FE                    lda         SPI_IN
00006Cr 2  60                       rts
00006Dr 2               
00006Dr 2               ; Clock .A * 8 cycles with nothing selected and MOSI high (an SD card needs 74 before it starts).
00006Dr 2               ; Modifies: .A
00006Dr 2               SPI_IDLE_CLOCKS:
00006Dr 2  48                       pha
00006Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_DESELECT
000071r 2  68                       pla
000072r 2               
000072r 2               @byte:
000072r 2  48                       pha
000073r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
000076r 2  68                       pla
000077r 2  3A                       dec
000078r 2  D0 F8                    bne         @byte
00007Ar 2  60                       rts
00007Br 2               
00007Br 1               .include "sd.s"         ; The SD card (blocks)
00007Br 2               .debuginfo
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00007Br 2               ; SD card, SPI mode (BIOS ROM page 3, the storage page; included inside `.scope PAGE3`, see all.s).  Block
00007Br 2               ; level: SD_INIT, SD_READ_BLOCK and SD_WRITE_BLOCK move 512-byte blocks (sectors) by number, for the
00007Br 2               ; /dev/sd server and (later) the FAT32 server.  Runs in the storage task (its ZP: SD_* in zero.s).
00007Br 2               ;   SDHC / SDXC cards take block numbers; SDSC (v1, and v2 standard capacity) cards take byte addresses
00007Br 2               ;   (block * 512), set up by SD_INIT.
00007Br 2               ;   Errors: C = 1 with .A = ERR_IO_DEVICE (no card, or it didn't answer), ERR_IO_NOT_READY (SD_INIT not
00007Br 2               ;   done) or ERR_IO_MEDIA (the card refused the command or the data); SD_R1 = the card's last answer.
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               .segment "STORAGE_P3"
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               SD_CMD0             = 0         ; GO_IDLE_STATE
00007Br 2               SD_CMD8             = 8         ; SEND_IF_COND
00007Br 2               SD_CMD16            = 16        ; SET_BLOCKLEN
00007Br 2               SD_CMD17            = 17        ; READ_SINGLE_BLOCK
00007Br 2               SD_CMD24            = 24        ; WRITE_BLOCK
00007Br 2               SD_CMD55            = 55        ; APP_CMD (the next one is an ACMD)
00007Br 2               SD_CMD58            = 58        ; READ_OCR
00007Br 2               SD_ACMD41           = 41        ; SD_SEND_OP_COND
00007Br 2               SD_R1_IDLE          = $01
00007Br 2               SD_TOKEN_DATA       = $FE       ; Start of a data block (both ways)
00007Br 2               SD_INIT_TRIES       = 1000      ; ACMD41 tries (about a second at 3.58 MHz)
00007Br 2               SD_TOKEN_TRIES      = 4000      ; Bytes to wait for a data token or the end of busy (about 0.3 s / try)
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               ; Start the card: SDHC/SDXC or SDSC.  OUT: C = 0 (SD_STATE = SD_STATE_SDHC or SD_STATE_SDSC); or C = 1,
00007Br 2               ; .A = error.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00007Br 2               SD_INIT:
00007Br 2  64 FC                    stz         SD_STATE
00007Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_INIT
000080r 2  A9 0A                    lda         #10                                 ; 80 clocks, nothing selected
000082r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_IDLE_CLOCKS
000085r 2  A9 00                    lda         #SD_SPI_DEVICE
000087r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_SELECT
00008Ar 2  A0 0A                    ldy         #10                                 ; CMD0: to SPI mode, idle
00008Cr 2               
00008Cr 2               @cmd0:
00008Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO
00008Fr 2  A9 00                    lda         #SD_CMD0
000091r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
000094r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SD_R1_IDLE
000096r 2  F0 06                    beq         @cmd8
000098r 2  88                       dey
000099r 2  D0 F1                    bne         @cmd0
00009Br 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SD_NO_CARD
00009Er 2               
00009Er 2               @cmd8:                                                      ; CMD8: a v2 card echoes the pattern
00009Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO
0000A1r 2  A9 01                    lda         #$01                                ; (2.7-3.6 V)
0000A3r 2  85 F6                    sta         SD_ARG + 2
0000A5r 2  A9 AA                    lda         #$AA                                ; (The pattern)
0000A7r 2  85 F7                    sta         SD_ARG + 3
0000A9r 2  A9 08                    lda         #SD_CMD8
0000ABr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
0000AEr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; HCS = 0 for a v1 card (illegal command)
0000B0r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SD_R1_IDLE
0000B2r 2  D0 15                    bne         @acmd41
0000B4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; The R7 answer: 3 bytes, then the pattern
0000B7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
0000BAr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
0000BDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
0000C0r 2  C9 AA                    cmp         #$AA
0000C2r 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
0000C4r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SD_NO_CARD
0000C7r 2               :
0000C7r 2  A2 40                    ldx         #$40                                ; HCS: we take high-capacity cards
0000C9r 2               
0000C9r 2               @acmd41:
0000C9r 2  86 FA                    stx         SD_TMP                              ; (HCS)
0000CBr 2  A9 E8                    lda         #<SD_INIT_TRIES
0000CDr 2  85 F8                    sta         SD_COUNT
0000CFr 2  A9 03                    lda         #>SD_INIT_TRIES
0000D1r 2  85 F9                    sta         SD_COUNT + 1
0000D3r 2               
0000D3r 2               @acmd41_loop:                                               ; ACMD41 until the card leaves idle
0000D3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO
0000D6r 2  A9 37                    lda         #SD_CMD55
0000D8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
0000DBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO
0000DEr 2  A5 FA                    lda         SD_TMP
0000E0r 2  85 F4                    sta         SD_ARG
0000E2r 2  A9 29                    lda         #SD_ACMD41
0000E4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
0000E7r 2  F0 09                    beq         @ready                              ; (R1 = 0)
0000E9r 2  30 41                    bmi         SD_NO_CARD                          ; (No answer)
0000EBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_COUNT_DOWN
0000EEr 2  D0 E3                    bne         @acmd41_loop
0000F0r 2  80 3A                    bra         SD_NO_CARD
0000F2r 2               
0000F2r 2               @ready:
0000F2r 2  A9 01                    lda         #SD_STATE_SDSC
0000F4r 2  A6 FA                    ldx         SD_TMP
0000F6r 2  F0 1D                    beq         @sdsc                               ; (A v1 card is SDSC)
0000F8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO                         ; CMD58: CCS in the OCR says SDHC/SDXC
0000FBr 2  A9 3A                    lda         #SD_CMD58
0000FDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
000100r 2  D0 31                    bne         SD_REFUSED
000102r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; OCR bits 31-24
000105r 2  AA                       tax
000106r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
000109r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
00010Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
00010Fr 2  A9 02                    lda         #SD_STATE_SDHC
000111r 2  E0 C0                    cpx         #$C0                                ; Powered up (bit 31) and CCS (bit 30)?
000113r 2  B0 10                    bcs         @done
000115r 2               
000115r 2               @sdsc:                                                      ; Standard capacity: 512-byte blocks
000115r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_ARG_ZERO
000118r 2  A9 02                    lda         #>512
00011Ar 2  85 F6                    sta         SD_ARG + 2
00011Cr 2  A9 10                    lda         #SD_CMD16
00011Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
000121r 2  D0 10                    bne         SD_REFUSED
000123r 2  A9 01                    lda         #SD_STATE_SDSC
000125r 2               
000125r 2               @done:
000125r 2  85 FC                    sta         SD_STATE
000127r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_END
00012Ar 2  18                       clc
00012Br 2  60                       rts
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               SD_NO_CARD:
00012Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_END
00012Fr 2  A9 79                    lda         #ERR_IO_DEVICE
000131r 2  38                       sec
000132r 2  60                       rts
000133r 2               
000133r 2               SD_REFUSED:
000133r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_END
000136r 2  A9 7F                    lda         #ERR_IO_MEDIA
000138r 2  38                       sec
000139r 2  60                       rts
00013Ar 2               
00013Ar 2               SD_NOT_READY:
00013Ar 2  A9 7E                    lda         #ERR_IO_NOT_READY
00013Cr 2  38                       sec
00013Dr 2  60                       rts
00013Er 2               
00013Er 2               ; Read block SD_LBA (32 bits) into the 512 bytes at SD_BUF.  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1, .A = error
00013Er 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00013Er 2               SD_READ_BLOCK:
00013Er 2  A5 FC                    lda         SD_STATE
000140r 2  F0 F8                    beq         SD_NOT_READY
000142r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_BLOCK_ARG
000145r 2  A9 00                    lda         #SD_SPI_DEVICE
000147r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_SELECT
00014Ar 2  A9 11                    lda         #SD_CMD17
00014Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
00014Fr 2  D0 E2                    bne         SD_REFUSED
000151r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_TOKEN_WAIT                       ; The data token
000154r 2  C9 FE                    cmp         #SD_TOKEN_DATA
000156r 2  D0 DB                    bne         SD_REFUSED
000158r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                                  ; 512 bytes: 2 pages from SD_BUF
00015Ar 2               
00015Ar 2               @first:
00015Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
00015Dr 2  91 EE                    sta         (SD_BUF),Y
00015Fr 2  C8                       iny
000160r 2  D0 F8                    bne         @first
000162r 2  E6 EF                    inc         SD_BUF + 1
000164r 2               
000164r 2               @second:
000164r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
000167r 2  91 EE                    sta         (SD_BUF),Y
000169r 2  C8                       iny
00016Ar 2  D0 F8                    bne         @second
00016Cr 2  C6 EF                    dec         SD_BUF + 1
00016Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; (The CRC: not checked)
000171r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
000174r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_END
000177r 2  18                       clc
000178r 2  60                       rts
000179r 2               
000179r 2               ; Write the 512 bytes at SD_BUF to block SD_LBA.  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1, .A = error
000179r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000179r 2               SD_WRITE_BLOCK:
000179r 2  A5 FC                    lda         SD_STATE
00017Br 2  F0 BD                    beq         SD_NOT_READY
00017Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_BLOCK_ARG
000180r 2  A9 00                    lda         #SD_SPI_DEVICE
000182r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_SELECT
000185r 2  A9 18                    lda         #SD_CMD24
000187r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CMD
00018Ar 2  D0 A7                    bne         SD_REFUSED
00018Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; (A byte's gap)
00018Fr 2  A9 FE                    lda         #SD_TOKEN_DATA
000191r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
000194r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000196r 2               
000196r 2               @first:
000196r 2  B1 EE                    lda         (SD_BUF),Y
000198r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
00019Br 2  C8                       iny
00019Cr 2  D0 F8                    bne         @first
00019Er 2  E6 EF                    inc         SD_BUF + 1
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 2               @second:
0001A0r 2  B1 EE                    lda         (SD_BUF),Y
0001A2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001A5r 2  C8                       iny
0001A6r 2  D0 F8                    bne         @second
0001A8r 2  C6 EF                    dec         SD_BUF + 1
0001AAr 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF                                ; (The CRC: not checked in SPI mode)
0001ACr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001AFr 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF
0001B1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001B4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; The data response: xxx0 0101 = accepted
0001B7r 2  85 FB                    sta         SD_R1
0001B9r 2  29 1F                    and         #$1F
0001BBr 2  C9 05                    cmp         #$05
0001BDr 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
0001BFr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SD_REFUSED
0001C2r 2               :
0001C2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_BUSY_WAIT                        ; While it writes
0001C5r 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
0001C7r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SD_NO_CARD
0001CAr 2               :
0001CAr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_END
0001CDr 2  18                       clc
0001CEr 2  60                       rts
0001CFr 2               
0001CFr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0001CFr 2               ; Helpers
0001CFr 2               
0001CFr 2               ; Send command .A with argument SD_ARG (MSB first), and get its R1 answer.
0001CFr 2               ; OUT: .A = R1 (N = 1: no answer), Z from it.  Modifies: .X
0001CFr 2               SD_CMD:
0001CFr 2  48                       pha
0001D0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV                            ; (A byte before each command)
0001D3r 2  68                       pla
0001D4r 2  48                       pha
0001D5r 2  09 40                    ora         #$40
0001D7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001DAr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0001DCr 2               
0001DCr 2               @arg:
0001DCr 2  B5 F4                    lda         SD_ARG,X
0001DEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001E1r 2  E8                       inx
0001E2r 2  E0 04                    cpx         #4
0001E4r 2  D0 F6                    bne         @arg
0001E6r 2  68                       pla                                             ; The CRC: only CMD0 and CMD8 need a real one
0001E7r 2  A2 95                    ldx         #$95                                ;   (CRC checking is off in SPI mode)
0001E9r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #SD_CMD0
0001EBr 2  F0 08                    beq         @crc
0001EDr 2  A2 87                    ldx         #$87
0001EFr 2  C9 08                    cmp         #SD_CMD8
0001F1r 2  F0 02                    beq         @crc
0001F3r 2  A2 01                    ldx         #$01                                ; (The end bit)
0001F5r 2               
0001F5r 2               @crc:
0001F5r 2  8A                       txa
0001F6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_XFER
0001F9r 2  A2 0A                    ldx         #10                                 ; The answer: within 8 bytes
0001FBr 2               
0001FBr 2               @answer:
0001FBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
0001FEr 2  10 03                    bpl         @got
000200r 2  CA                       dex
000201r 2  D0 F8                    bne         @answer
000203r 2               
000203r 2               @got:
000203r 2  85 FB                    sta         SD_R1
000205r 2  09 00                    ora         #0
000207r 2  60                       rts
000208r 2               
000208r 2               ; SD_ARG = 0.  Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000208r 2               SD_ARG_ZERO:
000208r 2  64 F4                    stz         SD_ARG
00020Ar 2  64 F5                    stz         SD_ARG + 1
00020Cr 2  64 F6                    stz         SD_ARG + 2
00020Er 2  64 F7                    stz         SD_ARG + 3
000210r 2  60                       rts
000211r 2               
000211r 2               ; SD_ARG = block SD_LBA's address: the block number (SDHC), or * 512 (SDSC).  Modifies: .A
000211r 2               SD_BLOCK_ARG:
000211r 2  A5 FC                    lda         SD_STATE
000213r 2  C9 02                    cmp         #SD_STATE_SDHC
000215r 2  D0 11                    bne         @bytes
000217r 2  A5 F3                    lda         SD_LBA + 3                          ; (MSB first)
000219r 2  85 F4                    sta         SD_ARG
00021Br 2  A5 F2                    lda         SD_LBA + 2
00021Dr 2  85 F5                    sta         SD_ARG + 1
00021Fr 2  A5 F1                    lda         SD_LBA + 1
000221r 2  85 F6                    sta         SD_ARG + 2
000223r 2  A5 F0                    lda         SD_LBA
000225r 2  85 F7                    sta         SD_ARG + 3
000227r 2  60                       rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               @bytes:                                                     ; SD_LBA << 9
000228r 2  64 F7                    stz         SD_ARG + 3
00022Ar 2  A5 F0                    lda         SD_LBA
00022Cr 2  0A                       asl
00022Dr 2  85 F6                    sta         SD_ARG + 2
00022Fr 2  A5 F1                    lda         SD_LBA + 1
000231r 2  2A                       rol
000232r 2  85 F5                    sta         SD_ARG + 1
000234r 2  A5 F2                    lda         SD_LBA + 2
000236r 2  2A                       rol
000237r 2  85 F4                    sta         SD_ARG
000239r 2  60                       rts
00023Ar 2               
00023Ar 2               ; Wait for a byte other than $FF (a data token).  OUT: .A = it ($FF: gave up).  Modifies: .X
00023Ar 2               SD_TOKEN_WAIT:
00023Ar 2  A9 A0                    lda         #<SD_TOKEN_TRIES
00023Cr 2  85 F8                    sta         SD_COUNT
00023Er 2  A9 0F                    lda         #>SD_TOKEN_TRIES
000240r 2  85 F9                    sta         SD_COUNT + 1
000242r 2               
000242r 2               @wait:
000242r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
000245r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #$FF
000247r 2  D0 07                    bne         @got
000249r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_COUNT_DOWN
00024Cr 2  D0 F4                    bne         @wait
00024Er 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF
000250r 2               
000250r 2               @got:
000250r 2  85 FB                    sta         SD_R1
000252r 2  60                       rts
000253r 2               
000253r 2               ; Wait while the card is busy (it sends 0s).  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1 if it's still busy.  Modifies: .A
000253r 2               SD_BUSY_WAIT:
000253r 2  A9 A0                    lda         #<SD_TOKEN_TRIES
000255r 2  85 F8                    sta         SD_COUNT
000257r 2  A9 0F                    lda         #>SD_TOKEN_TRIES
000259r 2  85 F9                    sta         SD_COUNT + 1
00025Br 2               
00025Br 2               @wait:
00025Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
00025Er 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #$FF
000260r 2  F0 07                    beq         @done                               ; $FF: not busy any more
000262r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_COUNT_DOWN
000265r 2  D0 F4                    bne         @wait
000267r 2  38                       sec
000268r 2  60                       rts
000269r 2               
000269r 2               @done:
000269r 2  18                       clc
00026Ar 2  60                       rts
00026Br 2               
00026Br 2               ; SD_COUNT - 1; Z = 1 when it reaches 0.  Modifies: .A
00026Br 2               SD_COUNT_DOWN:
00026Br 2  A5 F8                    lda         SD_COUNT
00026Dr 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
00026Fr 2  C6 F9                    dec         SD_COUNT + 1
000271r 2               :
000271r 2  C6 F8                    dec         SD_COUNT
000273r 2  A5 F8                    lda         SD_COUNT
000275r 2  05 F9                    ora         SD_COUNT + 1
000277r 2  60                       rts
000278r 2               
000278r 2               ; Deselect, and one more byte of clocks (the card lets go of MISO).  Preserves .A
000278r 2               SD_END:
000278r 2  48                       pha
000279r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_DESELECT
00027Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_RECV
00027Fr 2  68                       pla
000280r 2  60                       rts
000281r 2               
000281r 1               .include "sd_srv.s"     ; /dev/sd, and the storage task's init
000281r 2               .debuginfo
000281r 2               
000281r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000281r 2               ; The storage task's file server: /dev/sd, the SD card as one big file of bytes (BIOS ROM page 3, the
000281r 2               ; storage page; included inside `.scope PAGE3`, see all.s).  The storage task (STORAGE_TASK_NUM) owns
000281r 2               ; the SPI bus, so its requests run one at a time.  The FAT32 server will live here too, on the same
000281r 2               ; block cache.
000281r 2               ;   Open: starts the card (SD_INIT) if it isn't yet.
000281r 2               ;   Read / write: at the fd's offset (IO_SEEK), through a one-block cache (SD_CACHE, 512 bytes from the
000281r 2               ;         MMU); writes go through to the card at once.  The offset is 32 bits, so /dev/sd reaches the
000281r 2               ;         first 4 GB of the card (the FAT32 server uses block numbers, for all of it).
000281r 2               ;   Ctl: SD_CTL_INIT starts the card again (e.g. after changing it).
000281r 2               ; Server ZP (the storage task's): SD_* (zero.s); ZP_IO_REQ (IO_SRV_MAP).
000281r 2               
000281r 2               .segment "STORAGE_P3"
000281r 2               
000281r 2               ; The storage task's init (from STORAGE_INIT on page 0, in the task): the block cache, SPI idle.
000281r 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or C = 1, .A = error
000281r 2               STORAGE_INIT3:
000281r 2  64 FC                    stz         SD_STATE                            ; (The card starts at the first open)
000283r 2  64 E7                    stz         SD_CVALID
000285r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SPI_INIT
000288r 2  A9 00                    lda         #<512
00028Ar 2  A0 02                    ldy         #>512
00028Cr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
00028Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_ALLOC                            ; Whole pages: they don't move
000291r 2  B0 08                    bcs         @done
000293r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_LOCK                             ; .A.Y = the address
000296r 2  85 EC                    sta         SD_CACHE
000298r 2  84 ED                    sty         SD_CACHE + 1
00029Ar 2  18                       clc
00029Br 2               
00029Br 2               @done:
00029Br 2  60                       rts
00029Cr 2               
00029Cr 2               ; IN: .A = request, .X = client, .Y = fid
00029Cr 2               SD_SERVE:
00029Cr 2  86 E6                    stx         SD_CLIENT
00029Er 2  C9 02                    cmp         #H9_READ
0002A0r 2  F0 32                    beq         SD_RW
0002A2r 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
0002A4r 2  F0 2E                    beq         SD_RW
0002A6r 2  C9 01                    cmp         #H9_OPEN
0002A8r 2  F0 0C                    beq         @open
0002AAr 2  C9 06                    cmp         #H9_CTL
0002ACr 2  F0 14                    beq         @ctl
0002AEr 2  C9 05                    cmp         #H9_STAT
0002B0r 2  F0 1E                    beq         @bad
0002B2r 2               
0002B2r 2               @ok:                                                        ; H9_CLUNK, H9_DUP
0002B2r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0002B4r 2  18                       clc
0002B5r 2  60                       rts
0002B6r 2               
0002B6r 2               @open:
0002B6r 2  A5 FC                    lda         SD_STATE
0002B8r 2  D0 F8                    bne         @ok                                 ; (Started already)
0002BAr 2               
0002BAr 2               @init:
0002BAr 2  64 E7                    stz         SD_CVALID
0002BCr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_INIT
0002BFr 2  90 F1                    bcc         @ok
0002C1r 2  60                       rts
0002C2r 2               
0002C2r 2               @ctl:
0002C2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
0002C5r 2  A0 0B                    ldy         #IO_BLK_CTL_CODE
0002C7r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0002C9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0002CCr 2  C9 01                    cmp         #SD_CTL_INIT
0002CEr 2  F0 EA                    beq         @init
0002D0r 2               
0002D0r 2               @bad:
0002D0r 2  A9 78                    lda         #ERR_IO_BAD_REQ
0002D2r 2  38                       sec
0002D3r 2  60                       rts
0002D4r 2               
0002D4r 2               ; A read or write: .A = H9_READ / H9_WRITE.  Up to 256 bytes at the offset, a block (or two) at a time.
0002D4r 2               SD_RW:
0002D4r 2  85 E5                    sta         SD_OP
0002D6r 2  A5 FC                    lda         SD_STATE
0002D8r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+
0002DAr 2  A9 7E                    lda         #ERR_IO_NOT_READY
0002DCr 2  38                       sec
0002DDr 2  60                       rts
0002DEr 2               :
0002DEr 2  A6 E6                    ldx         SD_CLIENT
0002E0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_MAP
0002E3r 2  A0 07                    ldy         #IO_BLK_OFS + 3                     ; SD_POS = the offset
0002E5r 2  A2 03                    ldx         #3
0002E7r 2               :
0002E7r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0002E9r 2  95 E1                    sta         SD_POS,X
0002EBr 2  88                       dey
0002ECr 2  CA                       dex
0002EDr 2  10 F8                    bpl         :-
0002EFr 2  A0 08                    ldy         #IO_BLK_COUNT                       ; SD_LEFT = the count (1-256)
0002F1r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0002F3r 2  85 DF                    sta         SD_LEFT
0002F5r 2  C8                       iny
0002F6r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_REQ),Y
0002F8r 2  85 E0                    sta         SD_LEFT + 1
0002FAr 2  64 DE                    stz         SD_DONE
0002FCr 2               
0002FCr 2               @piece:                                                     ; The part of this block the request wants
0002FCr 2  A5 DF                    lda         SD_LEFT
0002FEr 2  05 E0                    ora         SD_LEFT + 1
000300r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000302r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @finished
000305r 2               :
000305r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_POS_TO_LBA                       ; SD_LBA = SD_POS >> 9
000308r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_CACHE_LOAD                       ; The block, in the cache
00030Br 2  90 03                    bcc         :+
00030Dr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @error
000310r 2               :
000310r 2  A5 E2                    lda         SD_POS + 1                          ; SD_N = min(512 - (SD_POS & 511), SD_LEFT)
000312r 2  29 01                    and         #1
000314r 2  49 01                    eor         #1                                  ; (Bytes to the block's end, high byte)
000316r 2  AA                       tax
000317r 2  A5 E1                    lda         SD_POS
000319r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
00031Br 2  18                       clc
00031Cr 2  69 01                    adc         #1                                  ; (Low byte: 0 - SD_POS)
00031Er 2  85 DC                    sta         SD_N
000320r 2  D0 01                    bne         :+
000322r 2  E8                       inx                                             ; (SD_POS & 511 = 0: 512 bytes)
000323r 2               :
000323r 2  86 DD                    stx         SD_N + 1
000325r 2  A5 DD                    lda         SD_N + 1                            ; SD_N > SD_LEFT?  Take SD_LEFT
000327r 2  C5 E0                    cmp         SD_LEFT + 1
000329r 2  90 12                    bcc         @copy
00032Br 2  D0 08                    bne         @left
00032Dr 2  A5 DC                    lda         SD_N
00032Fr 2  C5 DF                    cmp         SD_LEFT
000331r 2  90 0A                    bcc         @copy
000333r 2  F0 08                    beq         @copy
000335r 2               
000335r 2               @left:
000335r 2  A5 DF                    lda         SD_LEFT
000337r 2  85 DC                    sta         SD_N
000339r 2  A5 E0                    lda         SD_LEFT + 1
00033Br 2  85 DD                    sta         SD_N + 1
00033Dr 2               
00033Dr 2               @copy:                                                      ; SD_N bytes (1-256): the cache <-> the data area
00033Dr 2  A5 E1                    lda         SD_POS                              ; SD_SRC = the cache + (SD_POS & 511)
00033Fr 2  18                       clc
000340r 2  65 EC                    adc         SD_CACHE
000342r 2  85 DA                    sta         SD_SRC
000344r 2  A5 E2                    lda         SD_POS + 1
000346r 2  29 01                    and         #1
000348r 2  65 ED                    adc         SD_CACHE + 1
00034Ar 2  85 DB                    sta         SD_SRC + 1
00034Cr 2  A5 DE                    lda         SD_DONE                             ; SD_DST = the data area + SD_DONE
00034Er 2  85 D8                    sta         SD_DST
000350r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000352r 2  1A                       inc                                             ; (IO_BLK_DATA = $100)
000353r 2  85 D9                    sta         SD_DST + 1
000355r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000357r 2  A5 E5                    lda         SD_OP
000359r 2  C9 03                    cmp         #H9_WRITE
00035Br 2  F0 0B                    beq         @into_cache
00035Dr 2               
00035Dr 2               @out:
00035Dr 2  B1 DA                    lda         (SD_SRC),Y                          ; Read: the cache -> the data area
00035Fr 2  91 D8                    sta         (SD_DST),Y
000361r 2  C8                       iny
000362r 2  C4 DC                    cpy         SD_N                                ; (SD_N = 256: 0, so .Y wraps round to it)
000364r 2  D0 F7                    bne         @out
000366r 2  80 1A                    bra         @advance
000368r 2               
000368r 2               @into_cache:
000368r 2  B1 D8                    lda         (SD_DST),Y                          ; Write: the data area -> the cache
00036Ar 2  91 DA                    sta         (SD_SRC),Y
00036Cr 2  C8                       iny
00036Dr 2  C4 DC                    cpy         SD_N
00036Fr 2  D0 F7                    bne         @into_cache
000371r 2  A5 EC                    lda         SD_CACHE                            ; ... -> the card
000373r 2  85 EE                    sta         SD_BUF
000375r 2  A5 ED                    lda         SD_CACHE + 1
000377r 2  85 EF                    sta         SD_BUF + 1
000379r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_WRITE_BLOCK
00037Cr 2  90 04                    bcc         @advance
00037Er 2  64 E7                    stz         SD_CVALID                           ; (The cache and the card may differ now)
000380r 2  80 33                    bra         @error
000382r 2               
000382r 2               @advance:
000382r 2  A5 E1                    lda         SD_POS                              ; SD_POS += SD_N
000384r 2  18                       clc
000385r 2  65 DC                    adc         SD_N
000387r 2  85 E1                    sta         SD_POS
000389r 2  A5 E2                    lda         SD_POS + 1
00038Br 2  65 DD                    adc         SD_N + 1
00038Dr 2  85 E2                    sta         SD_POS + 1
00038Fr 2  90 06                    bcc         :+
000391r 2  E6 E3                    inc         SD_POS + 2
000393r 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
000395r 2  E6 E4                    inc         SD_POS + 3
000397r 2               :
000397r 2  A5 DF                    lda         SD_LEFT                             ; SD_LEFT -= SD_N
000399r 2  38                       sec
00039Ar 2  E5 DC                    sbc         SD_N
00039Cr 2  85 DF                    sta         SD_LEFT
00039Er 2  A5 E0                    lda         SD_LEFT + 1
0003A0r 2  E5 DD                    sbc         SD_N + 1
0003A2r 2  85 E0                    sta         SD_LEFT + 1
0003A4r 2  A5 DE                    lda         SD_DONE                             ; SD_DONE += SD_N (at most 256: then 0)
0003A6r 2  18                       clc
0003A7r 2  65 DC                    adc         SD_N
0003A9r 2  85 DE                    sta         SD_DONE
0003ABr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @piece
0003AEr 2               
0003AEr 2               @finished:                                                  ; The whole count: it stays
0003AEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0003B1r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0003B3r 2  18                       clc
0003B4r 2  60                       rts
0003B5r 2               
0003B5r 2               @error:
0003B5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_SRV_UNMAP
0003B8r 2  38                       sec
0003B9r 2  60                       rts
0003BAr 2               
0003BAr 2               ; SD_LBA = SD_POS >> 9 (32 bits).  Modifies: .A
0003BAr 2               SD_POS_TO_LBA:
0003BAr 2  A5 E4                    lda         SD_POS + 3
0003BCr 2  4A                       lsr
0003BDr 2  85 F2                    sta         SD_LBA + 2
0003BFr 2  A5 E3                    lda         SD_POS + 2
0003C1r 2  6A                       ror
0003C2r 2  85 F1                    sta         SD_LBA + 1
0003C4r 2  A5 E2                    lda         SD_POS + 1
0003C6r 2  6A                       ror
0003C7r 2  85 F0                    sta         SD_LBA
0003C9r 2  64 F3                    stz         SD_LBA + 3
0003CBr 2  60                       rts
0003CCr 2               
0003CCr 2               ; Make sure block SD_LBA is in the cache.  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1, .A = error.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0003CCr 2               SD_CACHE_LOAD:
0003CCr 2  A5 E7                    lda         SD_CVALID
0003CEr 2  F0 0D                    beq         @read
0003D0r 2  A2 03                    ldx         #3
0003D2r 2               
0003D2r 2               @same:
0003D2r 2  B5 F0                    lda         SD_LBA,X
0003D4r 2  D5 E8                    cmp         SD_CBLOCK,X
0003D6r 2  D0 05                    bne         @read
0003D8r 2  CA                       dex
0003D9r 2  10 F7                    bpl         @same
0003DBr 2  18                       clc
0003DCr 2  60                       rts
0003DDr 2               
0003DDr 2               @read:
0003DDr 2  64 E7                    stz         SD_CVALID
0003DFr 2  A5 EC                    lda         SD_CACHE
0003E1r 2  85 EE                    sta         SD_BUF
0003E3r 2  A5 ED                    lda         SD_CACHE + 1
0003E5r 2  85 EF                    sta         SD_BUF + 1
0003E7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SD_READ_BLOCK
0003EAr 2  B0 0C                    bcs         @done
0003ECr 2  A2 03                    ldx         #3
0003EEr 2               
0003EEr 2               @copy:
0003EEr 2  B5 F0                    lda         SD_LBA,X
0003F0r 2  95 E8                    sta         SD_CBLOCK,X
0003F2r 2  CA                       dex
0003F3r 2  10 F9                    bpl         @copy
0003F5r 2  E6 E7                    inc         SD_CVALID
0003F7r 2  18                       clc
0003F8r 2               
0003F8r 2               @done:
0003F8r 2  60                       rts
0003F9r 2

0002F1r 2               ; Page 1 copy of WRITE_HSTRING: the HString has to be read from page 1, where the caller's strings are.
0002F1r 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
0002F1r 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X

0000ABr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0000ABr 2               ; BIOS ROM page 1: included inside `.scope PAGE1` (see all.s), so MM_* / SH_* / TASK_CALL calls go through
0000ABr 2               ; the page 1 gates (page1.s).  Routines passed to TASK_CALL are named with :: (their page 0 addresses),
0000ABr 2               ; because TASK_CALL runs them on page 0.
0000ABr 2               
0000ABr 2               .segment "TESTS_P1"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; MMU self test (TH_MMU_TEST, $F833; from WOZMON: F833R; runs on ROM page 1).  Runs the handle calls in the current task and
000000r 2               ; prints "MMU test: ok", or "MMU test: FAIL x ee" (x = failing step, ee = error code or value read).
000000r 2               ; Allocates and frees task RAM pages (at the top of the free area), chunks, 2 RAM banks and shared memory,
000000r 2               ; and borrows (and resets) task MMU_TEST_TASK.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C  step                                ; Fail if the call returned an error
000000r 2                           bcc         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_FAIL_IF_NC step                                ; Fail if the call should have failed but didn't
000000r 2                           bcs         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro _M_MT_EXPECT     step, value                         ; Fail if .A <> value
000000r 2                           cmp         #value
000000r 2                           beq         :+
000000r 2                           ldx         #step
000000r 2                           jmp         @fail
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2  0A 4D 4D 55  NamedHString    S_MMU_TEST, "MMU test: "
000004r 2  20 74 65 73  
000008r 2  74 3A 20     
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               MMU_TEST_TASK   = 2                                         ; An idle task the test borrows (and resets)

0002CFr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK

0002D3r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0002D5r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0002D7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                           ; Handle 1 in the other task
0002DAr 2  90 05 A2 33              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     '3'
0002DEr 2  4C rr rr     
0002E1r 2  A9 02                    lda         #2
0002E3r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0002E5r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0002E7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL                           ; Handle 2
0002EAr 2  90 05 A2 33              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     '3'
0002EEr 2  4C rr rr     
0002F1r 2  C9 02 F0 05              _M_MT_EXPECT        '3', 2
0002F5r 2  A2 33 4C rr  
0002F9r 2  rr           
0002FAr 2  A9 02                    lda         #MMU_TEST_TASK
0002FCr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_TASK_RESET
0002FFr 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   ::MM_ALLOC, ZP_TC_VEC
000303r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000307r 2  A9 02                    lda         #MMU_TEST_TASK
000309r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
00030Br 2  A9 02                    lda         #2
00030Dr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
00030Fr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
000311r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL
000314r 2  90 05 A2 34              _M_MT_FAIL_IF_C     '4'
000318r 2  4C rr rr     
00031Br 2  C9 01 F0 05              _M_MT_EXPECT        '4', 1

000329r 2

000335r 2

000335r 2  48                       pha

000364r 2               
000364r 1               .include "sched_test.s"
000364r 2               .debuginfo
000364r 2               
000364r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000364r 2               ; Scheduler self test (TH_SCHED_TEST, $F869; from WOZMON: F869R).  BIOS ROM page 1: included inside
000364r 2               ; `.scope PAGE1` (see all.s), so calls go through the page 1 gates.
000364r 2               ;
000364r 2               ; Prints three lines:
000364r 2               ;   1. Two background tasks print "a" and "b" while this task prints "m": the letters interleave
000364r 2               ;      (preemption on the timer tick)
000364r 2               ;   2. A background task prints "[cccccccccc]" holding NO_PREEMPT while this task prints "m": the
000364r 2               ;      bracketed run is never split
000364r 2               ;   3. A background task waits (TASK_WAIT); this task prints "w", then wakes it (IO_WAKE) and it prints
000364r 2               ;      "W": "wW"
000364r 2               ; then "done".  Uses two free tasks.
000364r 2               
000364r 2               .segment "TESTS_P1"
000364r 2               
000364r 2  0B 53 63 68  NamedHString    S_SCHED_TEST, "Sched test:"
000368r 2  65 64 20 74  
00036Cr 2  65 73 74 3A  
000370r 2  04 64 6F 6E  NamedHString    S_SCHED_DONE, "done"
000374r 2  65           
000375r 2  12 46 41 49  NamedHString    S_SCHED_FAIL, "FAIL: no free task"
000379r 2  4C 3A 20 6E  
00037Dr 2  6F 20 66 72  
000388r 2               
000388r 2               ST_COUNT        = 12                                        ; Letters per task
000388r 2               
000388r 2               ; About 10,000 cycles (~2.8 ms, a little over half a scheduler tick).  Modifies: .X, .Y
000388r 2               ST_DELAY:
000388r 2  A0 08                    ldy         #8 * CPU_CLOCK_MULT
00038Ar 2               :
00038Ar 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
00038Cr 2               :
00038Cr 2  CA                       dex
00038Dr 2  D0 FD                    bne         :-
00038Fr 2  88                       dey
000390r 2  D0 F8                    bne         :--
000392r 2  60                       rts
000393r 2               
000393r 2               ; Print a letter ST_COUNT times, with a delay after each.  IN: .A = letter.  Uses ZP_TEMP, ZP_TEMP_2
000393r 2               ST_PRINT_RUN:
000393r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_2
000395r 2  A9 0C                    lda         #ST_COUNT
000397r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP
000399r 2               :
000399r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_2
00039Br 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
00039Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
0003A1r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP
0003A3r 2  D0 F4                    bne         :-
0003A5r 2  60                       rts
0003A6r 2               
0003A6r 2               ; Background tasks (each has its own ZP, so they can all use ZP_TEMP)
0003A6r 2               ST_WORK_A:
0003A6r 2  A9 61                    lda         #'a'
0003A8r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ST_PRINT_RUN
0003ABr 2               
0003ABr 2               ST_WORK_B:
0003ABr 2  A9 62                    lda         #'b'
0003ADr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ST_PRINT_RUN
0003B0r 2               
0003B0r 2               ST_WORK_C:
0003B0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NO_PREEMPT
0003B3r 2  A9 5B 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'['
0003B7r 2  rr           
0003B8r 2  A9 0A                    lda         #10
0003BAr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP
0003BCr 2               :
0003BCr 2  A9 63 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'c'
0003C0r 2  rr           
0003C1r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
0003C4r 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_TEMP
0003C6r 2  D0 F4                    bne         :-
0003C8r 2  A9 5D 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #']'
0003CCr 2  rr           
0003CDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         PREEMPT
0003D0r 2               
0003D0r 2               ST_WORK_D:
0003D0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_WAIT
0003D3r 2  A9 57 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'W'
0003D7r 2  rr           
0003D8r 2  60                       rts
0003D9r 2               
0003D9r 2               ; Start a background task on ROM page 1.  IN: .A.Y = entry point.  OUT: .A = task (C = 1: none free)
0003D9r 2               .macro _M_ST_RUN    entry
0003D9r 2                           lda         #<entry
0003D9r 2                           ldy         #>entry
0003D9r 2                           ldx         #1
0003D9r 2                           jsr         TASK_RUN
0003D9r 2               .endmacro
0003D9r 2               
0003D9r 2               ; Wait (yielding) until a task has finished.  IN: .A = task.  Uses ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003D9r 2               ST_WAIT_DONE:
0003D9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003DBr 2               :
0003DBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
0003DDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_STATUS
0003E0r 2  29 01                    and         #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
0003E2r 2  F0 05                    beq         :+
0003E4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         YIELD
0003E7r 2  80 F2                    bra         :-
0003E9r 2               :
0003E9r 2  60                       rts
0003EAr 2               
0003EAr 2               SCHED_TEST:
0003EAr 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0003EDr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_TEST
0003F1r 2  20 rr rr     
0003F4r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0003F7r 2               
0003F7r 2               ; 1. Preemption: three tasks printing at once
0003F7r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_A
0003FBr 2  A2 01 20 rr  
0003FFr 2  rr           
000400r 2  B0 6D                    bcs         @fail
000402r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000404r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_B
000408r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
00040Cr 2  rr           
00040Dr 2  B0 60                    bcs         @fail
00040Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
000411r 2  A9 6D                    lda         #'m'
000413r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_PRINT_RUN
000416r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000418r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
00041Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3 + 1
00041Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
000420r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000423r 2               
000423r 2               ; 2. NO_PREEMPT keeps "[cccccccccc]" together
000423r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_C
000427r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
00042Br 2  rr           
00042Cr 2  B0 41                    bcs         @fail
00042Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000430r 2  A9 6D                    lda         #'m'
000432r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_PRINT_RUN
000435r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000437r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
00043Ar 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
00043Dr 2               
00043Dr 2               ; 3. TASK_WAIT / IO_WAKE: "wW"
00043Dr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_ST_RUN   ST_WORK_D
000441r 2  A2 01 20 rr  
000445r 2  rr           
000446r 2  B0 27                    bcs         @fail
000448r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00044Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY                            ; Let it get to TASK_WAIT
00044Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000450r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000453r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_DELAY
000456r 2  A9 77 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #'w'
00045Ar 2  rr           
00045Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00045Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IO_WAKE
000460r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         ST_WAIT_DONE
000463r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000466r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_DONE
00046Ar 2  20 rr rr     
00046Dr 2  80 07                    bra         @end
00046Fr 2               
00046Fr 2               @fail:
00046Fr 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    S_SCHED_FAIL
000473r 2  20 rr rr     
000476r 2               
000476r 2               @end:
000476r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000479r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
00047Cr 2  60                       rts
00047Dr 2               
00047Dr 1               .include "disasm.s"
00047Dr 2               .debuginfo

0000F4r 2  5C 37 17 8E  
0000F8r 2  02 37 46 5C  
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; 0..3 = even indexes, 4..7 = odd
000100r 2               ; x000: one byte
000100r 2               ; xxx1: two byte
000100r 2               ; xxx0 (!x000): three byte
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;x1xx = Indexed
000100r 2               ;   x10x = Indexed by X
000100r 2               ;   x11x (!1110) = Indexed by Y
000100r 2               ;1xxx = Indirect (!100x && !1110)
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; INDEXED SUFFIXES
000100r 2               ; 010x == ,X
000100r 2               ; 110x == ,X)
000100r 2               ; 011x == ,Y
000100r 2               ; 1111 == ),Y
000100r 2               
000100r 2               AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000100r 2               AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000100r 2               AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000100r 2               AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000100r 2               AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000100r 2               AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000100r 2               AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000100r 2               AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000100r 2               AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000100r 2               AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000100r 2               AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000100r 2               AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000100r 2               AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000100r 2               AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000100r 2               AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000100r 2               AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; ADDRESSING MODES
000100r 2               ; Even Opcode Modes (%xxxxyyz0), xxxx = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000100r 2               MN_AMODE_EVEN:
000100r 2  88 33 08 EE      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EE	; %0000yyz0
000104r 2  B1 53 08 4E      .byte $B1,$53,$08,$4E	; %0001yyz0
000108r 2  8E 33 08 EE      .byte $8E,$33,$08,$EE	; %0010yyz0
00010Cr 2  B1 55 08 44      .byte $B1,$55,$08,$44	; %0011yyz0
000110r 2  88 38 08 EE      .byte $88,$38,$08,$EE	; %0100yyz0
000114r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %0101yyz0
000118r 2  88 33 08 EA      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EA	; %0110yyz0
00011Cr 2  B1 55 88 4C      .byte $B1,$55,$88,$4C	; %0111yyz0
000120r 2  81 33 88 EE      .byte $81,$33,$88,$EE	; %1000yyz0
000124r 2  B1 75 88 4E      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$4E	; %1001yyz0
000128r 2  99 33 88 EE      .byte $99,$33,$88,$EE	; %1010yyz0
00012Cr 2  B1 75 88 64      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$64	; %1011yyz0
000130r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1100yyz0
000134r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1101yyz0
000138r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1110yyz0
00013Cr 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1111yyz0
000140r 2               
000140r 2               ; ODD Opcode Modes (%xxxayyz1), a = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000140r 2  8D 33 89 2E      .byte $8D,$33,$89,$2E	; %xxx0yyz1
000144r 2  8F 35 86 24      .byte $8F,$35,$86,$24	; %xxx1yyz1
000148r 2               
000148r 2               MNEMONIC_STR:

0001E9r 2               .segment "DISASM_CODE"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ZP_D_XAM, ZP_D_XAM+1: Address to Disassemble
000000r 2               ; .A.Y: Start address of instruction to disassemble
000000r 2               ; C=0 means single instruction, C=1 means number of instructions/bytes to print in .X
000000r 2               ; set ZP_D_STATE=1 to print disassembly, ZP_D_STATE=0 to print bytes
000000r 2               DISASM_AY:
000000r 2  84 rr            sty         ZP_D_XAM + 1
000002r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_XAM
000004r 2  90 0E            bcc         DISASM
000006r 2  18               clc
000007r 2  86 rr            stx         ZP_D_ICOUNT
000009r 2  D0 0D            bne         DISASM_SAVEY

00000Er 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_XAM
000010r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_WM_XAM + 1
000012r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_XAM + 1

000114r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         PEEK_D_XAM                  ; lda (ZP_D_XAM), from ROM page ZP_D_PAGE
000117r 2  4A               lsr                                     ; /2 and shift LOb to C

0001A3r 1               .include "hyforth/hyforth.s"
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  Patrick Struthers - March 2026
0001A3r 2               ;      - THANKS to AGSB for the starting point here....
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  The HyForth project starts here with AGSB's core Forth engine;
0001A3r 2               ;  the engine will be moved to ROM and the heap will be copied to RAM
0001A3r 2               ;  on cold start.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;  The ulitmate purpose of working this port through is to develop a
0001A3r 2               ;  flexible and powerful operating system for the Hydra-16 of reasonable
0001A3r 2               ;  efficiency and minimal memory footprint.  HyForth will grow and
0001A3r 2               ;  shrink in RAM footprint according to context.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .debuginfo
0001A3r 2               .setcpu "65C02"
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ; macros for dictionary, creates code as follows:
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   h_name:
0001A3r 2               ;   .word  link_to_previous_entry
0001A3r 2               ;   .byte  strlen(name) + flags
0001A3r 2               ;   .byte  name
0001A3r 2               ;   name:
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; label for primitives
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro makelabel arg1, arg2
0001A3r 2               .ident (.concat (arg1, arg2)):
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; header for primitives
0001A3r 2               ; the entry point for dictionary is h_~name~
0001A3r 2               ; the entry point for code is ~name~
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro def_word name, label, flag
0001A3r 2               makelabel "h_", label
0001A3r 2               .ident(.sprintf("H%04X", hcount + 1)):
0001A3r 2                 .word .ident (.sprintf ("H%04X", hcount))
0001A3r 2               hcount .set hcount + 1
0001A3r 2                 .byte .strlen(name) + flag + 0 ; nice trick !
0001A3r 2                 .byte name
0001A3r 2               makelabel "", label
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;  macros for PGS stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro WCRLF_np                ; no push of A
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CRLF
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WCRLF
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro  WSEQ_np  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   phy
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   lda strlbl, y
0001A3r 2                   beq :+
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra :-
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   ply
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro  WSEQ_raw  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   phy
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   lda strlbl, y
0001A3r 2                   beq :+
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra :-
0001A3r 2               :
0001A3r 2                   ply
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WSEQ  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   WSEQ_np  strlbl
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   ANSI screen stuff
0001A3r 2               ; <rr>;<cc>f move cursor to rr,cc
0001A3r 2               ; <cc>m for color/attributes
0001A3r 2               ; 2J for clear screen
0001A3r 2               ; H for 'home'
0001A3r 2               ; <cc>[ABCD] screen moves
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro ANSI b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6,b7
0001A3r 2                   pha
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_ANSI_ESC_SEQ b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6, b7
0001A3r 2                   pla
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;  for error messages
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
0001A3r 2                   .byte <errmsg
0001A3r 2                   .byte >errmsg
0001A3r 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               .macro WERR errptr
0001A3r 2               ;.macro WERR
0001A3r 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2                   lda (errptr),y
0001A3r 2                   sta TEMP0
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   lda (errptr),y
0001A3r 2                   sta TEMP0+1
0001A3r 2                   ldy #0
0001A3r 2               werrloop:
0001A3r 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
0001A3r 2                   beq werrend
0001A3r 2                   iny
0001A3r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
0001A3r 2                   bra werrloop
0001A3r 2               werrend:
0001A3r 2                   WCRLF_np                ; (no rts: wrterror goes on to clear the error)
0001A3r 2               .endmacro
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ; variables for macros
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               hcount .set 0
0001A3r 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               H0000 = 0
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; number conversion
0001A3r 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;TXT2STACK := 1     ; use old TXTGET instead of new
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; error codes
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
0001A3r 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
0001A3r 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
0001A3r 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
0001A3r 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
0001A3r 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
0001A3r 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
0001A3r 2               ERR_IO := $08      ; IO error (the IO layer's error code: ioerr)
0001A3r 2               ERR_BRK := $09     ; break from the console (Ctrl-C)
0001A3r 2                 ; warnings
0001A3r 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
0001A3r 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
0001A3r 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
0001A3r 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
0001A3r 2               MAXSTR = 100
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
0001A3r 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
0001A3r 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
0001A3r 2               ; moves forwards
0001A3r 2               INBUF_end = $FD
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; data stacks
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
0001A3r 2               DSEND = $7E
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; return stack
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
0001A3r 2               RTEND = $FE
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; malloc stack
0001A3r 2               ; moves backwards
0001A3r 2               MEMEND = $01FE
0001A3r 2               MEM_SZ = $04
0001A3r 2               MEM_MMU = $40             ; record type flag: the record is its own MMU block (large records)
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; malloc records (see MALLOC): small ones go in an arena the MMU allocates at 'cold'; records of
0001A3r 2               ; FORTH_LARGE_MIN bytes or more get their own MMU block
0001A3r 2               FORTH_ARENA_SIZE = $0800  ; 2K arena for small records
0001A3r 2               FORTH_LARGE_MIN = $0100   ; records this big or bigger get their own MMU block
0001A3r 2               FORTH_DICT_MARGIN = 2     ; pages above 'here' the MMU must keep free (see DICTCHK)
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ; malloc allocates DOWN from the top of the arena (MEMTOPV), set up at 'cold'
0001A3r 2               
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
0001A3r 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
0001A3r 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
0001A3r 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $A000 in ROM and is only about 500 bytes
0001A3r 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and debug code,
0001A3r 2               ;  then the dictionary follow in the binary, and these stay in ROM.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
0001A3r 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words,
0001A3r 2               ;  then contents of 'primitives.s', then 'hywords.s', followed by
0001A3r 2               ;  the end of the dictionary with critical items such as 'fetch', 'store',
0001A3r 2               ;  'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary stuff.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;       When $A003 is run from WozMon, a jump instruction to 'main' is
0001A3r 2               ;  copied to $0600, followed by the dictionary, with 'exit' at the end.
0001A3r 2               ;  HyForth is start by running '600R' or 'A000R'.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;   Task ZP (see TASK_ZP in defines.s): allocated top-down from $FF, so this
0001A3r 2               ;   list runs from the top of ZP down.  The addresses are unchanged.
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP_BEGIN
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP7, 2       ;    AUTOLOAD                           $FE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP6, 1       ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET            $FD
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP5, 1       ;    WORDS, WFIND                       $FC
0001A3r 2               FFLAG = TEMP5          ; 'find flag' for wfind entry point
0001A3r 2               supprint = TEMP5       ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP BACKHEAP, 2    ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP NXTTOK, 2      ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP4, 2       ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP3, 2       ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0001A3r 2               ramstart = TEMP3       ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP2, 2       ; second                                $F2
0001A3r 2               endsoff = TEMP2        ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP1, 2       ; first                                 $F0
0001A3r 2               mainoff = TEMP1        ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   pointer registers
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP WORKREG, 2     ; working register                      $EE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP INSTPTR, 2     ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP RTPTR, 2       ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DSPTR, 2       ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   internal Forth
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP NEXTHEAP, 2    ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP LASTHEAP, 2    ; last link cell                        $E4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP CURBUF, 2      ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP STATUS, 2      ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ALFLAG, 1      ; autoload flag                         $DF
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP RSEED, 4       ; random # seed                         $DB
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DIGBASE, 1     ; base for number conversion            $DA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ERRPTR, 2      ; ptr to mitigation/message             $D8
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP ERRFLAG, 1     ; error type, 0 = none                  $D7
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DFLAG, 1       ; debug flag                            $D6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TIBEND, 2      ; pointer to end of TIB                 $D4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TIB, 2         ; pointer to input buffer               $D2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMLAST, 2     ;  malloc                               $D0
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMPTR, 2      ;   malloc                              $CE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP9, 2       ;   Imm                                 $CC
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP8, 2       ;  Hstring macro                        $CA
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP TEMP0, 2       ;  DUMPREG                              $C8
0001A3r 2               ZPSTART = TEMP0        ; CLEAR zeroes ZPSTART-$FF; the MMU variables below are set up by 'cold'
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   MMU (not cleared by CLEAR)
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMBOT, 2      ; malloc arena bottom                   $C6
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMTOPV, 2     ; malloc arena top (MEMLAST starts here) $C4
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP MEMHND, 1      ; MMU handle of the arena               $C3
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP DICTLIM, 1     ; MMU page floor: 'here' stays below it $C2
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP HLBANK, 1      ; RAM bank saved by hlock               $C1
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP IOERR, 1       ; the last IO error (ioerr)             $C0
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;                   Pipelines (see PIPECHK; set up by 'cold', not cleared by CLEAR)
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP PIPEIN, 1      ; stdin saved while a pipeline runs ($FF: none) $BF
