0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP BATCH, 1       ; <> 0: a pipeline's left side (a copy)  $BE
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP CHILDSP, 1     ;   its stack pointer, to end the task  $BD
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP PIPER, 1       ; the pipe being set up: read fd        $BC
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP PIPEW, 1       ;   and write fd                        $BB
0001A3r 2               TASK_ZP_END
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ; *** $DO-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
0001A3r 2               ;
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
0001A3r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0001A3r 2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000000r 2               INBUF:
000000r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000004r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000008r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000100r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)
000100r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000104r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000108r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00017Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000180r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)
000180r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000184r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000188r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0001FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000200r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0003FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000400r 2               ;
000400r 2               ;
000400r 2               .segment "FORTH_ROM"              ; regular core
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; ************ the real deal...
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               
000000r 2               forth_main:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp cold
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp COPYTORAM
000006r 2               
000006r 2               HYPROMPT:
000006r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
000008r 2  48 46 3E         .byte "HF>"
00000Br 2  00               .byte 0
00000Cr 2               ;
00000Cr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00000Cr 2               WDISP:
00000Cr 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00000Er 2  57 3D            .byte "W="
000010r 2  00               .byte 0
000011r 2               WATDISP:
000011r 2  20 20 5B 57      .byte "  [W]="
000015r 2  5D 3D        
000017r 2  00               .byte 0
000018r 2               .endif
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               ;
000018r 2               cold:
000018r 2  D8               cld
000019r 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLEAR          ; zero out zero page, INBUF, DS, and RT
00001Cr 2  64 BE            stz BATCH          ; not a pipeline's copy
00001Er 2  64 C0            stz IOERR          ; no IO error yet
000020r 2  A9 FF            lda #$FF
000022r 2  85 BF            sta PIPEIN         ; stdin not redirected
000024r 2  A9 rr            lda #<fbreak       ; Ctrl-C: back to the prompt
000026r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>fbreak
000028r 2  A2 01            ldx #1             ; (HyForth's ROM page)
00002Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr TASK_SET_BREAK
00002Dr 2               
00002Dr 2               ; Forth owns this task's MMU memory: free anything left from before (only the MMU area: typed-ahead
00002Dr 2               ; input in the message rings is kept), then get the malloc arena
00002Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_TASK_INIT
000030r 2  64 C6            stz MEMBOT                 ; no arena (every small malloc fails) unless MM_ALLOC works
000032r 2  64 C7            stz MEMBOT+1
000034r 2  64 C4            stz MEMTOPV
000036r 2  64 C5            stz MEMTOPV+1
000038r 2  64 C2            stz DICTLIM
00003Ar 2  A9 00            lda #<FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00003Cr 2  A0 08            ldy #>FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00003Er 2  A2 00            ldx #0
000040r 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_ALLOC               ; whole pages at the top of task RAM
000043r 2  B0 1B            bcs NOARENA
000045r 2  85 C3            sta MEMHND
000047r 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_LOCK                ; .A.Y = address (page blocks don't move), .X = RAM bank
00004Ar 2  85 C6            sta MEMBOT
00004Cr 2  84 C7            sty MEMBOT+1
00004Er 2  A5 C3            lda MEMHND
000050r 2  20 rr rr         jsr MM_UNLOCK
000053r 2  A5 C6            lda MEMBOT
000055r 2  18               clc
000056r 2  69 00            adc #<FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
000058r 2  85 C4            sta MEMTOPV
00005Ar 2  A5 C7            lda MEMBOT+1
00005Cr 2  69 08            adc #>FORTH_ARENA_SIZE
00005Er 2  85 C5            sta MEMTOPV+1
000060r 2               NOARENA:
000060r 2               
000060r 2               warm:
000060r 2               ; link list of headers
000060r 2  A9 rr            lda #>h_exit               ; initialize HEAP pointers
000062r 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
000064r 2  A9 rr            lda #<h_exit
000066r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000068r 2               
000068r 2               ; next heap free cell
000068r 2  A9 rr            lda #>ends + 1
00006Ar 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
00006Cr 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00006Er 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
000070r 2               
000070r 2               
000070r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
000072r 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000074r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000076r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000078r 2  A4 C4            ldy MEMTOPV                 ; malloc records grow down from the top of the arena
00007Ar 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
00007Cr 2  A4 C5            ldy MEMTOPV+1
00007Er 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
000080r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK                 ; keep the MMU out of the pages above 'here'
000083r 2               
000083r 2  80 07            bra reset
000085r 2               ;
000085r 2               ; A break from the console (Ctrl-C; see TASK_SET_BREAK in 'cold'): the task comes here, with the stack
000085r 2               ; pointer it had at 'cold', wherever it was: back to the prompt, as for an error
000085r 2               fbreak:
000085r 2  A9 09            lda #ERR_BRK
000087r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000089r 2  4C rr rr         jmp abort
00008Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00008Cr 2               ; various reinitialization points
00008Cr 2               ;
00008Cr 2               reset:
00008Cr 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
00008Er 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND
000090r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<INBUF
000092r 2  84 D2            sty TIB
000094r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
000096r 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
000098r 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
00009Ar 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
00009Cr 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
00009Er 2               
00009Er 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
0000A0r 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
0000A2r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
0000A4r 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
0000A6r 2               
0000A6r 2  A9 01            lda #1                       ; DEBUG OFF by default
0000A8r 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG
0000AAr 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                   ; autoload flag OFF
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               abort:                            ; clear DS
0000ACr 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
0000AEr 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error
0000B0r 2               quit:                             ; clear RT
0000B0r 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND
0000B2r 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wrterror                 ; print any error messages
0000B7r 2  A0 00            ldy #0          ; reset INBUF
0000B9r 2  A9 00            lda #0
0000BBr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y     ; clear INBUF stuff
0000BDr 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF    ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
0000BFr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS    ; status is 'interpret' == \0
0000C1r 2               
0000C1r 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, nice trick !
0000C2r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000C2r 2               ; the outer loop
0000C2r 2               
0000C2r 2               resolvept:
0000C2r 2  rr rr            .word okey
0000C4r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000C4r 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
0000C4r 2               resolve:           ; get a token
0000C4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
0000C7r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000C7r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
0000C9r 2  D0 08            bne RVPSKIP
0000CBr 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
0000CEr 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
0000D0r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR            ; DEBUG
0000D3r 2               .endif
0000D3r 2               
0000D3r 2               RVPSKIP:
0000D3r 2               
0000D3r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
0000D3r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0000D5r 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0000D7r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0000D9r 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
0000DBr 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0000DDr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
0000DFr 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
0000E1r 2  D0 12            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?
0000E3r 2               
0000E3r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
0000E3r 2  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG            ; keep an error already raised (e.g. out of memory for a q^...^ string)
0000E5r 2  D0 04            bne WNFERR
0000E7r 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
0000E9r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000EBr 2               WNFERR:
0000EBr 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
0000EEr 2               ;
0000EEr 2               DICTFULL:                  ; the dictionary would grow into memory the MMU has allocated
0000EEr 2  A9 04            lda #ERR_MEM
0000F0r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000F2r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
0000F5r 2               
0000F5r 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
0000F5r 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0000F7r 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
0000F9r 2               
0000F9r 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000FBr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000FDr 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
000100r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
000102r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
000104r 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
000106r 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
000106r 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
000108r 2  38               sec
000109r 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
00010Br 2  D0 CE            bne RESLOOP
00010Dr 2  C8               iny
00010Er 2               ; compare chars
00010Er 2               RESEQUAL:
00010Er 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
000110r 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE            ; space ends
000112r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
000114r 2  38               sec                 ; verify
000115r 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
000117r 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
000118r 2  D0 C1            bne RESLOOP
00011Ar 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
00011Br 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL
00011Dr 2               
00011Dr 2               RESDONE:
00011Dr 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
00011Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
000121r 2               
000121r 2               eval:
000121r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
000121r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
000123r 2  F0 20            beq execute
000125r 2               ;
000125r 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
000125r 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
000125r 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
000127r 2  30 1C            bmi immediate
000129r 2               
000129r 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
000129r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000129r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
00012Br 2  D0 08            bne CMPSKIP
00012Dr 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
000130r 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
000132r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000135r 2               CMPSKIP:
000135r 2               .endif
000135r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK         ; room for the dictionary to grow?
000138r 2  B0 03            bcs CMPROOM
00013Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp DICTFULL
00013Dr 2               CMPROOM:
00013Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
000140r 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
000142r 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
000145r 2               ;
000145r 2               immediate:
000145r 2               execute:
000145r 2               
000145r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000145r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
000147r 2  D0 08            bne EXESKIP
000149r 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
00014Cr 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
00014Er 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000151r 2               EXESKIP:
000151r 2               .endif
000151r 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
000153r 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
000155r 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
000157r 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000159r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
00015Cr 2               
00015Cr 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
00015Cr 2               try:
00015Cr 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
00015Er 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
000160r 2  C8               iny
000161r 2  49 20            eor #ASCII_SPACE    ; return 0 in  A if a space
000163r 2  60               rts
000164r 2               
000164r 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
000164r 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
000164r 2  68               pla
000165r 2  68               pla
000166r 2  20 rr rr         jsr PIPEEND          ; a pipeline's line is done: stdin back from the pipe
000169r 2  A5 BE            lda BATCH            ; a pipeline's left side (a copy of the shell): all done, end the task
00016Br 2  F0 04            beq GLAUTO
00016Dr 2  A6 BD            ldx CHILDSP
00016Fr 2  9A               txs
000170r 2  60               rts
000171r 2               GLAUTO:
000171r 2               ;
000171r 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
000171r 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
000171r 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
000171r 2               ;
000171r 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
000173r 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000175r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
000178r 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
00017Br 2               
00017Br 2               GLNORMAL:
00017Br 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
00017Fr 2  rr rr F0 06  
000183r 2  C8 20 rr rr  
00018Ar 2               ;
00018Ar 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
00018Cr 2               GETLOOP:
00018Cr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
00018Er 2  C8               iny
00018Fr 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
000191r 2  F0 24            beq GETLNEND
000193r 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000195r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000197r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
000199r 2               GETREADLOOP:
000199r 2  20 rr rr         jsr GET_CHAR      ; (sleeps until a key comes in)
00019Cr 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
00019Er 2  C9 0D            cmp #ASCII_CR
0001A0r 2  F0 15            beq GETLNEND
0001A2r 2  C9 08            cmp #ASCII_BACKSPACE         ; handle backspace
0001A4r 2  D0 E6            bne GETLOOP
0001A6r 2  C0 02            cpy #2
0001A8r 2  90 06            bcc GETBSNONE     ; nothing typed yet: nothing to erase
0001AAr 2  88               dey
0001ABr 2  88               dey
0001ACr 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
0001AEr 2  80 DC            bra GETLOOP
0001B0r 2               GETBSNONE:            ; (/dev/cons's echo erased the prompt's '>': put it back)
0001B0r 2  A9 3E            lda #'>'
0001B2r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
0001B5r 2  80 E2            bra GETREADLOOP
0001B7r 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
0001B7r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CRLF
0001BAr 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
0001BAr 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
0001BCr 2  5A               phy
0001BDr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001BFr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
0001C1r 2  7A               ply
0001C2r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
0001C4r 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
0001C6r 2  C8               iny
0001C7r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
0001C9r 2  88               dey
0001CAr 2               ; start it
0001CAr 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
0001CCr 2  20 rr rr         jsr PIPECHK          ; a pipeline ( ... | ... )?  start its left side
0001CFr 2               
0001CFr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001CFr 2               ; in place every token,
0001CFr 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
0001CFr 2               ; no rewinds
0001CFr 2               token:
0001CFr 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
0001D1r 2               
0001D1r 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
0001D1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001D4r 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
0001D6r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
0001D7r 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001D9r 2               
0001D9r 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
0001D9r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001DCr 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
0001DEr 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
0001DFr 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0001E1r 2               
0001E1r 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
0001E1r 2  98               tya
0001E2r 2  38               sec
0001E3r 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
0001E5r 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
0001E7r 2  88               dey
0001E8r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
0001EAr 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001ECr 2                   ;
0001ECr 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
0001ECr 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
0001ECr 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
0001ECr 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
0001ECr 2                   ;
0001ECr 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
0001ECr 2                   ;
0001ECr 2               .ifdef numbers
0001ECr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001EEr 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0001F0r 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
0001F1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
0001F4r 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
0001F6r 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
0001F8r 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
0001F8r 2               CHKFERTXT:
0001F8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr TEXTGET         ; returns length +4 in X
0001FBr 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
0001FDr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001FFr 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
000201r 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
000204r 2               TOKCLR0:
000204r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000206r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
000208r 2  AA               tax
000209r 2  E8               inx
00020Ar 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE          ; copy spaces over entire converted string
00020Cr 2               TOKCLR:
00020Cr 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
00020Er 2  C8               iny
00020Fr 2  CA               dex
000210r 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
000212r 2               TOKNEXT:
000212r 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
000215r 2               
000215r 2               
000215r 2               TOKENEND:
000215r 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
000216r 2  60               rts
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
000217r 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
000217r 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
000217r 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
000217r 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
000217r 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
000217r 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
000217r 2               ;
000217r 2               wcomma:
000217r 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
000219r 2               comma:
000219r 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
00021Br 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
00021Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Br 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
00021Br 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00021Br 2               ;
00021Br 2               copyinto:
00021Br 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00021Er 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000220r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
000223r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000226r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000228r 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
00022Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00022Br 2               ;
00022Br 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
00022Br 2               ;
00022Br 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
00022Br 2               spush_2:
00022Br 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
00022Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000230r 2               spush_1:
000230r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
000232r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000235r 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
000235r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
000237r 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
000237r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000237r 2               ; PUSH a cell
000237r 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
000237r 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000237r 2               spush:
000237r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000239r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
00023Br 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS                ; ditto
00023Dr 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s              ; ditto
00023Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
000242r 2               rpush:
000242r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000244r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR               ; ditto
000246r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; ditto
000248r 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
00024Ar 2               
00024Ar 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
00024Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00024Ar 2               ; classic stack backwards
00024Ar 2               push:
00024Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
00024Dr 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000250r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000252r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000255r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000258r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00025Ar 2  60               rts
00025Br 2               ;
00025Br 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
00025Br 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
00025Br 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
00025Dr 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
00025Fr 2               ptrerr_s:
00025Fr 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
000261r 2               ptrerr_cont:
000261r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000263r 2  68               pla
000264r 2  68               pla
000265r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
000268r 2               ;
000268r 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
000268r 2               ;
000268r 2               spull_2:
000268r 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
00026Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
00026Dr 2               
00026Dr 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
00026Dr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
00026Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000272r 2               
000272r 2               spull_0:
000272r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
000274r 2               ;
000274r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000274r 2               ;
000274r 2               ; PULL a cell
000274r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000274r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000274r 2               ;
000274r 2               spull:
000274r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000276r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
000278r 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
00027Ar 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
00027Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
00027Fr 2               rpull:                ;
00027Fr 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000281r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
000283r 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
000285r 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
000287r 2               ;
000287r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000287r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000287r 2               ;
000287r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000287r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000287r 2               pull:
000287r 2               copyfrom:
000287r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000289r 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
00028Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
00028Fr 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000291r 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
000294r 2                ;
000294r 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
000294r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000294r 2               ; increment a word in page zero. offset by X
000294r 2               ;
000294r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx
000294r 2               ;
000294r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000294r 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
000294r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000294r 2               ;   pointer location.
000294r 2               ;
000294r 2               incwx:
000294r 2  A9 01            lda #01
000296r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000296r 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. offset by X
000296r 2               addwx:
000296r 2  18               clc
000297r 2  75 00            adc 0, x
000299r 2  95 00            sta 0, x
00029Br 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
00029Dr 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
00029Fr 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
0002A0r 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
0002A0r 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
0002A0r 2               addwx_end:
0002A0r 2  60               rts
0002A1r 2               
0002A1r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0002A1r 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
0002A1r 2               ; decrement a word in page zero. offset by X
0002A1r 2               ;
0002A1r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
0002A1r 2               ;
0002A1r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
0002A1r 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
0002A1r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
0002A1r 2               ;   pointer location.
0002A1r 2               ;
0002A1r 2               decwx:
0002A1r 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
0002A3r 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
0002A5r 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
0002A7r 2               decwx_end:
0002A7r 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
0002A9r 2  60               rts
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ENGINEEND:
0002AAr 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE
0002AAr 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ;                        DICTIONARY and ADDITIONS
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ;    upper.s -- error messaging, CLEAR on start, debug, utilities
0002AAr 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               upper_ram:
0002AAr 2                  .include "upper.s"
0002AAr 3               ;
0002AAr 3               ;   upper.s  - utility functions for HyForth - eventually ROM resident
0002AAr 3               ;
0002AAr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0002AAr 3               ;  error messaging
0002AAr 3               ;
0002AAr 3               wrterror:
0002AAr 3  A9 rr            lda #>err_jumptable
0002ACr 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002AEr 3  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG
0002B0r 3  F0 27            beq ERREND
0002B2r 3  0A               asl a
0002B3r 3  18               clc
0002B4r 3  69 rr            adc #<err_jumptable
0002B6r 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002B8r 3  90 02            bcc ERRSKIP
0002BAr 3  E6 D9            inc ERRPTR+1
0002BCr 3               ERRSKIP:
0002BCr 3  20 rr rr A0      WERR ERRPTR
0002C0r 3  00 B1 D8 85  
0002C4r 3  C8 C8 B1 D8  
0002D9r 3               ERREND:
0002D9r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0002DBr 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0002DDr 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002DFr 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002E1r 3  60               rts
0002E2r 3               ;
0002E2r 3               ;
0002E2r 3               err_jumptable:
0002E2r 3  xx xx            .res 2
0002E4r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
0002E6r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
0002E8r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
0002EAr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
0002ECr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
0002EEr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
0002F0r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
0002F2r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry IO_ERR                ; IO error (see ioerr) - error $08
0002F4r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry BRK_ERR               ; break from the console - error $09
0002F6r 3               LASTERR = 9
0002F6r 3               ;
0002F6r 3               ;  error messages
0002F6r 3               RPTR_ERR:
0002F6r 3  20 21 52 54      .byte " !RT PTR ERROR!"
0002FAr 3  20 50 54 52  
0002FEr 3  20 45 52 52  
000305r 3  00               .byte 0
000306r 3               SPTR_ERR:
000306r 3  20 21 44 53      .byte " !DS PTR ERROR!"
00030Ar 3  20 50 54 52  
00030Er 3  20 45 52 52  
000315r 3  00               .byte 0
000316r 3               DIV_ERR:
000316r 3  20 21 44 49      .byte " !DIV ZERO!"
00031Ar 3  56 20 5A 45  
00031Er 3  52 4F 21     
000321r 3  00               .byte 0
000322r 3               OOM_ERR:
000322r 3  20 21 4C 4F      .byte " !LOW MEM!"
000326r 3  57 20 4D 45  
00032Ar 3  4D 21        
00032Cr 3  00               .byte 0
00032Dr 3               UKW_ERR:
00032Dr 3  20 21 55 4E      .byte " !UNK WORD!"
000331r 3  4B 20 57 4F  
000335r 3  52 44 21     
000338r 3  00               .byte 0
000339r 3               SEC_ERR:
000339r 3  20 21 53 45      .byte " !SECURITY!"
00033Dr 3  43 55 52 49  
000341r 3  54 59 21     
000344r 3  00               .byte 0
000345r 3               SYS_ERR:
000345r 3  20 21 53 59      .byte " !SYS ERR!"
000349r 3  53 20 45 52  
00034Dr 3  52 21        
00034Fr 3  00               .byte 0
000350r 3               IO_ERR:
000350r 3  20 21 49 4F      .byte " !IO ERR!"
000354r 3  20 45 52 52  
000358r 3  21           
000359r 3  00               .byte 0
00035Ar 3               BRK_ERR:
00035Ar 3  20 21 42 52      .byte " !BREAK!"
00035Er 3  45 41 4B 21  
000362r 3  00               .byte 0
000363r 3               
000363r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
000363r 3               ;
000363r 3               ;           CODE called by engine AND heap
000363r 3               ;
000363r 3               ;------- used by 'exit'
000363r 3               ;------------------------NOTE:  this is ALL of 'exit' but the screaming.
000363r 3               ;----------------- For modularity purposes this is the only sensible place for it.
000363r 3               ;
000363r 3               EXIT:
000363r 3               unnest:                             ; EXIT - done with previous word, on to next whether compiled or primitive
000363r 3               
000363r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
000365r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
000368r 3               
000368r 3               next:                               ;    go on to next word; IP is pointing at next entry in data field of word
000368r 3               ; WORKREG = (INSTPTR) ; INSTPTR += 2
000368r 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
00036Ar 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG
00036Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                    ; W = [IP], IP += 2  ( and either ENTER or EXECUTE )
00036Fr 3               
00036Fr 3               pick:                               ;        'THE SWITCHER' - COMPILED OR PRIMITIVE?
00036Fr 3                                                   ;
00036Fr 3               .ifdef DEBUG                        ;  DEBUG
00036Fr 3  A5 D6            lda DFLAG
000371r 3  D0 2F            bne PRINTWSKIP                  ; print W and [W] etc if DEBUG
000373r 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WDISP
000377r 3  rr rr F0 06  
00037Br 3  C8 20 rr rr  
000382r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG+1
000384r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
000387r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG
000389r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
00038Cr 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WATDISP
000390r 3  rr rr F0 06  
000394r 3  C8 20 rr rr  
00039Br 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00039Dr 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
00039Fr 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
0003A2r 3               PRINTWSKIP:
0003A2r 3               .endif
0003A2r 3               ; .. use old-fashioned way AND new tricks
0003A2r 3               ;
0003A2r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
0003A4r 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
0003A6r 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
0003A8r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte
0003AAr 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
0003ACr 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
0003AEr 3               
0003AEr 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
0003AEr 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
0003B0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
0003B3r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP
0003B5r 3  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
0003B7r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
0003B9r 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1
0003BBr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                        ; next
0003BEr 3               
0003BEr 3               jump:                               ; EXECUTE                      ( PRIMITIVE )
0003BEr 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
0003C1r 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]
0003C1r 3               ;
0003C1r 3               ;------- used by 'autoload'
0003C1r 3               ALOADTIB:
0003C1r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0003C3r 3  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
0003C5r 3  91 D2            sta (TIB),y
0003C7r 3               ALOADLOOP:
0003C7r 3  B1 FE            lda (TEMP7),y
0003C9r 3  F0 05            beq ALOADSKIP
0003CBr 3  C8               iny
0003CCr 3  91 D2            sta (TIB),y
0003CEr 3  80 F7            bra ALOADLOOP
0003D0r 3               ALOADSKIP:
0003D0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADCHKDONE
0003D3r 3  C8               iny
0003D4r 3  60               rts                             ; y points at trailing space
0003D5r 3               
0003D5r 3               ALOADCHKDONE:
0003D5r 3  C8               iny
0003D6r 3  B1 FE            lda (TEMP7),y
0003D8r 3  D0 04            bne ALNOTSKIP                   ; calc next address if not done
0003DAr 3  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                      ; turn off autoload
0003DCr 3  80 0A            bra ALNOTDONE
0003DEr 3               ALNOTSKIP:
0003DEr 3  98               tya
0003DFr 3  18               clc
0003E0r 3  65 FE            adc TEMP7
0003E2r 3  85 FE            sta TEMP7
0003E4r 3  90 02            bcc ALNOTDONE
0003E6r 3  E6 FF            inc TEMP7+1
0003E8r 3               ALNOTDONE:
0003E8r 3  88               dey
0003E9r 3  60               rts
0003EAr 3               ;
0003EAr 3               ;-------- malloc and mlen
0003EAr 3               
0003EAr 3               MALFAIL:
0003EAr 3  18               clc                   ; out of memory
0003EBr 3  60               rts
0003ECr 3               MALLOC:
0003ECr 3                   ;  TEMP1 and TEMP2 should have bytes / record type if 'jsr MALLOC'
0003ECr 3                   ;  OUT: C = 1 and TEMP1 = memory stack slot (holds the record address); C = 0 if out of memory
0003ECr 3                   ;  Records smaller than FORTH_LARGE_MIN go in the arena (MEMLAST grows down, not below MEMBOT);
0003ECr 3                   ;  bigger ones get their own MMU block, marked with MEM_MMU in the type byte.
0003ECr 3                   ;  uses TEMP3, TEMP4, y, x, a
0003ECr 3  A5 CE            lda MEMPTR           ; memory stack full?
0003EEr 3  C9 rr            cmp #<MEMSTK
0003F0r 3  A5 CF            lda MEMPTR+1
0003F2r 3  E9 rr            sbc #>MEMSTK
0003F4r 3  90 F4            bcc MALFAIL
0003F6r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0003F8r 3  D0 30            bne MALLARGE
0003FAr 3                                       ; arena: new record at MEMLAST - 3 - bytes, into TEMP4
0003FAr 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
0003FCr 3  38               sec
0003FDr 3  E9 03            sbc #3
0003FFr 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
000401r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
000403r 3  E9 00            sbc #0
000405r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
000407r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000409r 3  38               sec
00040Ar 3  E5 F0            sbc TEMP1
00040Cr 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
00040Er 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000410r 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
000412r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
000414r 3  90 D4            bcc MALFAIL         ; wrapped
000416r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000418r 3  C5 C6            cmp MEMBOT
00041Ar 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
00041Cr 3  E5 C7            sbc MEMBOT+1
00041Er 3  90 CA            bcc MALFAIL         ; below the arena
000420r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000422r 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
000424r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000426r 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1        ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record
000428r 3  80 27            bra MALHDR
00042Ar 3               MALLARGE:               ; its own MMU block: bytes + 3 for the header
00042Ar 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00042Cr 3  18               clc
00042Dr 3  69 03            adc #3
00042Fr 3  48               pha
000430r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000432r 3  69 00            adc #0
000434r 3  A8               tay
000435r 3  68               pla
000436r 3  B0 B2            bcs MALFAIL          ; more than $FFFF
000438r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
00043Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_ALLOC         ; .A = handle
00043Dr 3  B0 AB            bcs MALFAIL
00043Fr 3  48               pha
000440r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_LOCK          ; .A.Y = address (page blocks don't move), .X = RAM bank
000443r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
000445r 3  84 F7            sty TEMP4+1
000447r 3  68               pla
000448r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_UNLOCK
00044Br 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00044Dr 3  09 40            ora #MEM_MMU
00044Fr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000451r 3               MALHDR:
000451r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000453r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000455r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000457r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
000459r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00045Br 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
00045Dr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
00045Fr 3  C8               iny
000460r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1            ; LSB length
000462r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000464r 3  C8               iny
000465r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1          ; MSB length
000467r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000469r 3  A2 F4            ldx #TEMP3
00046Br 3  A9 03            lda #3
00046Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx            ; increment TEMP3 by 3
000470r 3               MALLOOP:                 ; clear TEMP1 bytes at TEMP3
000470r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000472r 3  05 F1            ora TEMP1+1
000474r 3  F0 14            beq MALCONT
000476r 3  A9 00            lda #0
000478r 3  92 F4            sta (TEMP3)
00047Ar 3  E6 F4            inc TEMP3
00047Cr 3  D0 02            bne MALSK01
00047Er 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
000480r 3               MALSK01:
000480r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000482r 3  D0 02            bne MALSK02
000484r 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
000486r 3               MALSK02:
000486r 3  C6 F0            dec TEMP1
000488r 3  80 E6            bra MALLOOP
00048Ar 3               MALCONT:                   ; now store the record address at MEMPTR
00048Ar 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00048Cr 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
00048Er 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
000490r 3  C8               iny
000491r 3  A5 F7            lda TEMP4+1
000493r 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
000495r 3  A5 CF            lda MEMPTR+1
000497r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000499r 3  A5 CE            lda MEMPTR
00049Br 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1             ; copy to TEMP1 before incrementing
00049Dr 3  38               sec                   ; MEMPTR + 2
00049Er 3  E9 02            sbc #2
0004A0r 3  85 CE            sta MEMPTR
0004A2r 3  B0 02            bcs MALLOCEND
0004A4r 3  C6 CF            dec MEMPTR+1
0004A6r 3               MALLOCEND:
0004A6r 3  38               sec                   ; OK
0004A7r 3  60               rts
0004A8r 3               ;
0004A8r 3               ;  Keep the MMU from handing out the pages the dictionary grows into: raise the MMU page floor
0004A8r 3               ;  (DICTLIM) to FORTH_DICT_MARGIN pages above 'here' when needed.
0004A8r 3               ;  OUT: C = 1 OK; C = 0 the dictionary is full (the MMU has allocated those pages)
0004A8r 3               ;  uses a, x, y
0004A8r 3               DICTCHK:
0004A8r 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP+1
0004AAr 3  18               clc
0004ABr 3  69 03            adc #FORTH_DICT_MARGIN + 1   ; floor wanted: page of 'here' + margin + 1
0004ADr 3  C5 C2            cmp DICTLIM
0004AFr 3  90 0B            bcc DICTOK                   ; already at or above it
0004B1r 3  F0 09            beq DICTOK
0004B3r 3  48               pha
0004B4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_SET_FLOOR             ; MMU: C = 0 OK
0004B7r 3  68               pla
0004B8r 3  B0 04            bcs DICTNO
0004BAr 3  85 C2            sta DICTLIM
0004BCr 3               DICTOK:
0004BCr 3  38               sec
0004BDr 3  60               rts
0004BEr 3               DICTNO:
0004BEr 3  18               clc
0004BFr 3  60               rts
0004C0r 3               ;
0004C0r 3               ;  Free a large (MEM_MMU) record's MMU block.  IN: TEMP3 = record address.  uses a, x, y
0004C0r 3               MMUFREE:
0004C0r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0004C2r 3  A4 F5            ldy TEMP3+1
0004C4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_FIND                  ; .A = handle
0004C7r 3  B0 03            bcs MMUFREEND
0004C9r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MM_FREE
0004CCr 3               MMUFREEND:
0004CCr 3  60               rts
0004CDr 3               ;
0004CDr 3               MEMLEN:              ; address in TEMP2
0004CDr 3  A0 00             ldy #0
0004CFr 3  B1 F2             lda (TEMP2),y
0004D1r 3  85 F4             sta TEMP3
0004D3r 3  C8                iny
0004D4r 3  B1 F2             lda (TEMP2),y   ; and deref once
0004D6r 3  85 F5             sta TEMP3+1
0004D8r 3  A0 01             ldy #1
0004DAr 3  B1 F4             lda (TEMP3),y   ; skip over type, get length
0004DCr 3  85 F0             sta TEMP1
0004DEr 3  C8                iny
0004DFr 3  B1 F4             lda (TEMP3),y
0004E1r 3  85 F1             sta TEMP1+1
0004E3r 3  60                rts
0004E4r 3               ;
0004E4r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
0004E4r 3               ;                MATH routines
0004E4r 3               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
0004E4r 3               ;         we just do the math here.
0004E4r 3               ;
0004E4r 3               ;
0004E4r 3               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; TEMP1 and TEMP2 are #'s, TEMP1 will be result
0004E4r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3                       ; with TEMP3 as high bytes
0004E6r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
0004E8r 3  A2 11            ldx #17
0004EAr 3  18               clc
0004EBr 3               MULTLOOP:
0004EBr 3  66 F5            ror TEMP3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
0004EDr 3  66 F4            ror TEMP3
0004EFr 3  66 F1            ror TEMP1+1
0004F1r 3  66 F0            ror TEMP1
0004F3r 3  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
0004F5r 3  18               clc
0004F6r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0004F8r 3  65 F4            adc TEMP3
0004FAr 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0004FCr 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
0004FEr 3  65 F5            adc TEMP3+1
000500r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
000502r 3               MULTDECCNT:
000502r 3  CA               dex
000503r 3  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
000505r 3  60               rts
000506r 3               
000506r 3               ;
000506r 3               ;
000506r 3                      ; 16 x 16 divide; TEMP1 and TEMP2 #'s - TEMP3 is 'overflow'
000506r 3                      ;  TEMP2 divisor, TEMP1 dividend, TEMP1 + 3 = result + remainder
000506r 3               DIV16:
000506r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3
000508r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
00050Ar 3  A2 10            ldx #16
00050Cr 3               UDIVLP:
00050Cr 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
00050Er 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000510r 3  26 F4            rol TEMP3
000512r 3  26 F5            rol TEMP3+1
000514r 3               UDIVCHK:
000514r 3  38               sec
000515r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
000517r 3  E5 F2            sbc TEMP2
000519r 3  A8               tay
00051Ar 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
00051Cr 3  E5 F3            sbc TEMP2+1
00051Er 3  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
000520r 3  84 F4            sty TEMP3
000522r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
000524r 3               UDIVCNT:
000524r 3  CA               dex
000525r 3  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
000527r 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
000529r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00052Br 3  60               rts
00052Cr 3               ;
00052Cr 3               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
00052Cr 3               ;
00052Cr 3               ;  -- boilerplate --
00052Cr 3               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
00052Cr 3               ; Brad Smith, 2019
00052Cr 3               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
00052Cr 3               ;
00052Cr 3               ;
00052Cr 3               galois32o:
00052Cr 3                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
00052Cr 3  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
00052Er 3  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
000530r 3  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
000532r 3                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
000532r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000534r 3  4A               lsr
000535r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
000537r 3  4A               lsr
000538r 3  4A               lsr
000539r 3  4A               lsr
00053Ar 3  4A               lsr
00053Br 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00053Dr 3  4A               lsr
00053Er 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
000540r 3  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
000542r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
000544r 3                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
000544r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000546r 3  0A               asl
000547r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000549r 3  0A               asl
00054Ar 3  0A               asl
00054Br 3  0A               asl
00054Cr 3  0A               asl
00054Dr 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00054Fr 3  0A               asl
000550r 3  0A               asl
000551r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000553r 3  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000555r 3  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
000557r 3  60               rts
000558r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
000558r 3               ;              get delimited text from INBUF, store in string
000558r 3               ;
000558r 3               
000558r 3               .ifndef TXT2STACK
000558r 3               ;
000558r 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3, TEMP5, TEMP6, X, Y
000558r 3               ;
000558r 3               TEXTGET:
000558r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
00055Ar 3  A9 04            lda #$04
00055Cr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
00055Er 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000560r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
000562r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
000564r 3               TX2SKSPC:
000564r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000566r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE                ; skip leading spaces
000568r 3  D0 03            bne TX2SK00
00056Ar 3  C8               iny
00056Br 3  80 F7            bra TX2SKSPC
00056Dr 3               TX2SK00:
00056Dr 3  C9 71            cmp #ASCII_q
00056Fr 3  D0 6C            bne TX2NOGOOD
000571r 3  C8               iny
000572r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000574r 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
000576r 3  D0 65            bne TX2NOGOOD
000578r 3  C8               iny
000579r 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; temp6 stores pos of first char
00057Br 3               TX2SCAN:
00057Br 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
00057Dr 3  C8               iny
00057Er 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
000580r 3  F0 0A            beq TX2FOUND
000582r 3  98               tya
000583r 3  18               clc
000584r 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
000586r 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
000588r 3  B0 53            bcs TX2NOGOOD
00058Ar 3  80 EF            bra TX2SCAN
00058Cr 3               TX2FOUND:
00058Cr 3  88               dey
00058Dr 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; temp5 pos+1 last letter
00058Fr 3  98               tya
000590r 3  38               sec
000591r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
000593r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
000595r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1                       ; and add one for zero at end
000597r 3               
000597r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack
00059Ar 3  B0 07            bcs TX2ROOM
00059Cr 3  A9 04            lda #ERR_MEM                    ; out of memory: reported when the token isn't found
00059Er 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0005A0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX2NOGOOD
0005A3r 3               TX2ROOM:
0005A3r 3               
0005A3r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0005A5r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
0005A7r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0005A9r 3  C8               iny
0005AAr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
0005ACr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; address in memory area
0005AEr 3               
0005AEr 3                   ; now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
0005AEr 3                   ; as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
0005AEr 3                   ; the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
0005AEr 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0005B0r 3  38               sec
0005B1r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005B3r 3  B0 02            bcs TX2SK01
0005B5r 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
0005B7r 3               TX2SK01:
0005B7r 3  18               clc
0005B8r 3  69 03            adc #3
0005BAr 3  90 02            bcc TX2SK02
0005BCr 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
0005BEr 3               TX2SK02:
0005BEr 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
0005C0r 3               TX2CPYLOOP:
0005C0r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005C2r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
0005C4r 3  C8               iny
0005C5r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0005C7r 3  D0 F7            bne TX2CPYLOOP
0005C9r 3               TX2SK99:
0005C9r 3  A9 00            lda #0
0005CBr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                   ; put zero on end
0005CDr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push address from mem stack on DS
0005D0r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5
0005D2r 3  38               sec
0005D3r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0005D5r 3  18               clc
0005D6r 3  69 04            adc #4
0005D8r 3  AA               tax
0005D9r 3  18               clc
0005DAr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX2END
0005DDr 3               TX2NOGOOD:
0005DDr 3  38               sec
0005DEr 3               TX2END:
0005DEr 3  60               rts
0005DFr 3               ;
0005DFr 3               ;  end of TXG2  (new text capture)
0005DFr 3               ;
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3               .else  ;  TXT2STACK
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3               TEXTGET:
0005DFr 3                   stz TEMP6
0005DFr 3                   stz TEMP1
0005DFr 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0005DFr 3                   ldy #1                          ; skip len
0005DFr 3               TXTSKIPSPC:
0005DFr 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005DFr 3                   cmp #ASCII_SPACE                ; skip leading spaces
0005DFr 3                   bne TXTSPCS
0005DFr 3                   iny
0005DFr 3                   bra TXTSKIPSPC
0005DFr 3               TXTSPCS:
0005DFr 3                   cmp #ASCII_q
0005DFr 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0005DFr 3                   iny
0005DFr 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005DFr 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0005DFr 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0005DFr 3                   sta TEMP3                       ; remember....
0005DFr 3                   iny
0005DFr 3               TXTSCAN:
0005DFr 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
0005DFr 3                   iny
0005DFr 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0005DFr 3                   beq TXTFOUND
0005DFr 3                   tya
0005DFr 3                   clc
0005DFr 3                   adc NXTTOK
0005DFr 3                   cmp #MAXSTR
0005DFr 3                   bcs TEXTNOGOOD
0005DFr 3                   bra TXTSCAN
0005DFr 3               TXTFOUND:
0005DFr 3                   ldx #0
0005DFr 3                   dey
0005DFr 3                   dey                             ; now points at last letter
0005DFr 3                   tya
0005DFr 3                   sec
0005DFr 3                   sbc TEMP3                       ; and this should be the length
0005DFr 3                   stx TEMP3
0005DFr 3                   and #1
0005DFr 3                   beq TEXTGLOOP
0005DFr 3                   inc TEMP3
0005DFr 3                   inx
0005DFr 3               TEXTGLOOP:
0005DFr 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0005DFr 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET                        ; when we hit the other end again...
0005DFr 3                   beq TEXTOK
0005DFr 3                   sta TEMP6
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0005DFr 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0005DFr 3               .endif
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3                   txa
0005DFr 3                   and #1
0005DFr 3                   bne TEXTODD
0005DFr 3                   lda TEMP6
0005DFr 3                   sta TEMP1
0005DFr 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0005DFr 3                   bra TEXTSKIP2
0005DFr 3               TEXTODD:
0005DFr 3                   lda TEMP6
0005DFr 3                   sta TEMP1+1
0005DFr 3                   phx
0005DFr 3                   phy
0005DFr 3                   jsr spush_0
0005DFr 3                   ply
0005DFr 3                   plx
0005DFr 3               TEXTSKIP2:
0005DFr 3                   inx
0005DFr 3                   dey
0005DFr 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0005DFr 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0005DFr 3               .endif
0005DFr 3                   bra TEXTGLOOP
0005DFr 3               TEXTOK:
0005DFr 3                   phy
0005DFr 3                   phx
0005DFr 3                   txa
0005DFr 3                   and #1
0005DFr 3                   beq TEXTCONT
0005DFr 3                   jsr spush_0
0005DFr 3               TEXTCONT:
0005DFr 3                   plx
0005DFr 3                   txa
0005DFr 3                   sec
0005DFr 3                   sbc TEMP3
0005DFr 3                   sta TEMP3
0005DFr 3                   stz TEMP3+1
0005DFr 3                   jsr spush_2                     ; push length on top
0005DFr 3                   ply
0005DFr 3                   clc
0005DFr 3                   bra TEXTGEND
0005DFr 3               TEXTNOGOOD:
0005DFr 3                   sec
0005DFr 3               TEXTGEND:
0005DFr 3                   rts
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3               .endif  ; ---TXT2STACK
0005DFr 3               
0005DFr 3               ;-----------------------   NUMBER CONVERSIONS
0005DFr 3               ;
0005DFr 3               DEC2ASCII:       ;  X is # - return as two digits in TEMP3, TEMP3+1
0005DFr 3  A9 30            lda #ASCII_0
0005E1r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0005E3r 3  8A               txa
0005E4r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005E6r 3               D2ASCLOOP:
0005E6r 3  38               sec
0005E7r 3  E9 0A            sbc #ASCII_LF
0005E9r 3  90 04            bcc D2ASCNEXT
0005EBr 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
0005EDr 3  80 F7            bra D2ASCLOOP
0005EFr 3               D2ASCNEXT:
0005EFr 3  18               clc
0005F0r 3  69 3A            adc #$3A
0005F2r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005F4r 3  60               rts
0005F5r 3               
0005F5r 3               .ifdef numbers
0005F5r 3               ;------------------------
0005F5r 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
0005F5r 3               
0005F5r 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
0005F5r 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0005F7r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0005F9r 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
0005FBr 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
0005FDr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0005FFr 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
000601r 3  A9 0A            lda #10
000603r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000605r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000607r 3  C9 2D            cmp #ASCII_MINUS
000609r 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
00060Br 3  C9 24            cmp #ASCII_DOLLAR
00060Dr 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
00060Fr 3  C9 25            cmp #ASCII_PERCENT
000611r 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
000613r 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
000615r 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
000615r 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
000617r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
00061Ar 3               DIGBIN1:
00061Ar 3  A9 02            lda #2
00061Cr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
00061Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
000621r 3               DIGHEX1:
000621r 3  A9 10            lda #16
000623r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000625r 3               DIG_XY:
000625r 3  C8               iny
000626r 3  CA               dex
000627r 3               DIG_SNG:
000627r 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
000627r 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
000629r 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
00062Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
00062Er 3                 .endif
00062Er 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
00062Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG
000631r 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0
000633r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
000636r 3               DIGCONT0:
000636r 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
000637r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
000639r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00063Br 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
00063Dr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00063Fr 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
000641r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000643r 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
000645r 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
000647r 3               DIGDEC:
000647r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
000649r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00064Br 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
00064Dr 3  18               clc
00064Er 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
000650r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000652r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
000654r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
000656r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000658r 3               DIGDEC2:
000658r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
00065Ar 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00065Cr 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
00065Dr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
00065Fr 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
000661r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
000664r 3               DIGHEX:
000664r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000666r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000668r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00066Ar 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00066Cr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00066Er 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
000670r 3  68               pla
000671r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
000674r 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
000674r 3  68               pla
000675r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000677r 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
000679r 3               DIGCONT:
000679r 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
00067Ar 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
00067Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00067Er 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
000680r 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
000682r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
000684r 3               DIGCONT2:
000684r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000686r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000688r 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
00068Ar 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00068Cr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
00068Er 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
000690r 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
000692r 3               DIGCONT3:
000692r 3  C8               iny
000693r 3  CA               dex
000694r 3  D0 98            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
000696r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000698r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00069Ar 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
00069Cr 3               DECFINISH:
00069Cr 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
00069Er 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
0006A0r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0006A2r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006A4r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0006A6r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0006A8r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
0006AAr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0006ACr 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0006AEr 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
0006B0r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0006B2r 3               DIGCONT4:
0006B2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
0006B5r 3  18               clc
0006B6r 3  60               rts
0006B7r 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
0006B7r 3  38               sec
0006B8r 3  60               rts
0006B9r 3               
0006B9r 3               ;--------------------------
0006B9r 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
0006B9r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0006BBr 3  38               sec
0006BCr 3  E9 30            sbc #ASCII_0
0006BEr 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006C0r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10

0006C6r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0006C8r 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0006CAr 3  C9 10            cmp #16

0006CEr 3  18               clc
0006CFr 3  60               rts
0006D0r 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
0006D0r 3  38               sec
0006D1r 3  60               rts

0006D2r 3               ;
0006D2r 3  27 10        H2NUM: .byte $27,$10

0006D8r 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A

0006E5r 3  E8               inx
0006E6r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0006E9r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
0006EBr 3  E8               inx
0006ECr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0006EEr 3               H2DLOOP:
0006EEr 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
0006F0r 3  C5 F7            cmp TEMP4+1

0006F8r 3  C5 F6            cmp TEMP4
0006FAr 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1

0006FCr 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3

000701r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000703r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
000705r 3  E5 F7            sbc TEMP4+1
000707r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1

00072Fr 3               ;
00072Fr 3               .endif    ; numbers

000901r 3               ;
000901r 3               ;    load mainoff (TEMP1 $F0), ramstart (TEMP3 $F4), and endsoff (TEMP2 $F2)
000901r 3               ;     with current ROM offsets (main = start, ends = finish)
000901r 3               ;     and ramstart with destination corresponding to main.
000901r 3               ;     Set 'supprint' <> 0 (TEMP5 $FC) to suppress printing of progress bar.
000901r 3               ;
000901r 3               MEMCPY:
000901r 3               COPYLOOP:
000901r 3  B2 F0 92 F4      MOV (mainoff), (ramstart)
000905r 3  A5 F0 C5 F2      BNE16 mainoff, endsoff, SKIP1
000909r 3  D0 07 A5 F1  
00090Dr 3  C5 F3 D0 01  
000911r 3  60               rts
000912r 3               
000912r 3               SKIP1:
000912r 3  A5 FC            lda supprint
000914r 3  D0 05            bne SKIP01
000916r 3  A9 2E 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR #ASCII_PERIOD
00091Ar 3  rr           
00091Br 3               
00091Br 3               SKIP01:
00091Br 3  E6 F0 D0 02      INC16_BARE mainoff
00091Fr 3  E6 F1        
000921r 3  E6 F4 D0 02      INC16_BARE ramstart
000925r 3  E6 F5        
000927r 3  80 D8            bra COPYLOOP

00033Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000340r 3  7A               ply
000341r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000343r 3  88               dey

000346r 3  D0 ED            bne HSFLOOP

000349r 3               ;
000349r 3               ;   replace leading zeros with spcs when creating decimal ascii string
000349r 3               ;
000349r 3               DECSFINISH:

00034Br 3               DECSCLRLOOP:             ; replace $00 or leading $30 with spaces

0003ABr 3               SLZERO:

00046Cr 3               ; ( w1 w2 -- NOT(w1 AND w2) )
00046Cr 3  rr rr 04 6E  def_word "nand", "nand", 0
000470r 3  61 6E 64

000487r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
00048Ar 3

000491r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0        ; then TEMP1

00049Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
0004A2r 3

0004ACr 3  38               sec

0004B7r 3  18               clc            ; not sure why, but just in case?
0004B8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
0004BBr 3               
0004BBr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0004BBr 3               ; ( 0 -- $0000) | ( n -- $FFFF)  normalize boolean - change TOS to $0000 if false, $FFFF if TRUE
0004BBr 3  rr rr 04 62  def_word "bool", "normbool", 0

0004C9r 3  F0 19            beq PUSHFALSE
0004CBr 3  4C rr rr         jmp PUSHTRUE
0004CEr 3               ;

0004D2r 3  52 55 45

0004DCr 3               ;

0004E0r 3  41 4C 53 45

0004E6r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0004E8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
