000512r 2               ;
000512r 2               ;
000512r 2               ;------------ CRITICAL CORE PRIMITIVES (AGSB and PGS) ----------------
000512r 2               ;
000512r 2               ;   COMPILE (:), FINISH (;), FETCH (@), STORE (!)
000512r 2               ;      including:  keeps, this, next, finish
000512r 2               ;       ...and other stuff.
000512r 2               ;
000512r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000512r 2               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to a reference at top of data stack
000512r 2  rr rr 04 65  def_word "exec", "exec", 0
000516r 2  78 65 63     
000519r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
00051Cr 2  6C F0 00         jmp (TEMP1)        ; assumes an address on top of DS
00051Fr 2               
00051Fr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00051Fr 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to a reference at IP
00051Fr 2  rr rr 02 3A  def_word ":$", "docode", 0
000523r 2  24           
000524r 2  6C EC 00         jmp (INSTPTR)                 ;  DOCODE (thus the ':')
000527r 2               
000527r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000527r 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to next
000527r 2  rr rr 02 3B  def_word ";$", "donext", 0        ;  'next' (thus the ';')
00052Br 2  24           
00052Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00052Fr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00052Fr 2               ;
00052Fr 2               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
00052Fr 2  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
000533r 2               storew:
000533r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
000536r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
000539r 2  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
00053Br 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
00053Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
000540r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
000543r 2               ;
000543r 2               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
000543r 2               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
000543r 2  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
000547r 2               fetchw:
000547r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
00054Ar 2  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
00054Cr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
00054Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
000551r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000551r 2               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives
000551r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000551r 2               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
000551r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000554r 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000556r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000559r 2               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
000559r 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00055Br 2               this:                       ; same as above
00055Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
00055Er 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000561r 2               ;
000561r 2               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
000561r 2  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
000565r 2               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
000565r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
000567r 2  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
000569r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
00056Br 2  18               clc
00056Cr 2  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
00056Er 2  85 CC            sta TEMP9
000570r 2  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
000572r 2  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
000574r 2               IMMSKIP:
000574r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000576r 2  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
000578r 2  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
00057Ar 2  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
00057Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00057Fr 2               ;
00057Fr 2  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
000583r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS
000585r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000588r 2               ;
000588r 2  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
00058Cr 2  A9 01            lda #1
00058Er 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
000590r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000593r 2               ;
000593r 2               ;
000593r 2                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
000593r 2                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
000593r 2                                                        ;
000593r 2  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here', adjust
000597r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ; 'here' to point at next cell
00059Ar 2  A0 00            ldy #0
00059Cr 2  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00059Er 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
0005A0r 2  C8               iny
0005A1r 2  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0005A3r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
0005A5r 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
0005A7r 2  A9 02            lda #2
0005A9r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0005ACr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005AFr 2               
0005AFr 2               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
0005AFr 2  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
0005B3r 2  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
0005B5r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
0005B7r 2  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
0005B9r 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
0005BBr 2               
0005BBr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
0005BDr 2               
0005BDr 2               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
0005BDr 2  A9 rr            lda #<exit
0005BFr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG
0005C1r 2  A9 rr            lda #>exit
0005C3r 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
0005C5r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
0005C8r 2                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
0005C8r 2                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
0005C8r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005CBr 2               ;
0005CBr 2               ;
0005CBr 2               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
0005CBr 2  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
0005CFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK                 ; room for the dictionary to grow?
0005D2r 2  B0 03            bcs COLONROOM
0005D4r 2  4C rr rr         jmp DICTFULL
0005D7r 2               COLONROOM:
0005D7r 2  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
0005D9r 2  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
0005DBr 2  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
0005DDr 2  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
0005DFr 2               
0005DFr 2  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
0005E1r 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
0005E3r 2               
0005E3r 2               COMPHEADER:
0005E3r 2               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
0005E3r 2  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
0005E5r 2  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
0005E8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
0005EBr 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
0005EDr 2                                                ; code field comes with later proc
0005EDr 2               COMPLOOP:
0005EDr 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0005EFr 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
0005F1r 2  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
0005F3r 2  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
0005F5r 2  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
0005F7r 2  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
0005F9r 2               COMCOPY:
0005F9r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
0005FBr 2  C8               iny
0005FCr 2  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
0005FEr 2               COMPEND:
0005FEr 2  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
0005FFr 2  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
000601r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
000604r 2               
000604r 2               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
000604r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
000607r 2               ;
000607r 2               ;---------------------ADD IN EXTRA HARDCODED LOGIC / NUMERALS / ETC--
000607r 2               .ifdef HYWORDS
000607r 2                  .include "hywords.s"

000703r 3               ;

000709r 3  20 rr rr         jsr DICTCHK                 ; room for the dictionary to grow?
00070Cr 3  B0 03            bcs VARROOM
00070Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp DICTFULL
000711r 3               VARROOM:
000711r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
000713r 3  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
000715r 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
000717r 3  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
000719r 3               
000719r 3               VCHEAD:
000719r 3               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
000719r 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
00071Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
00071Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
000721r 3  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
000723r 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc
000723r 3               VCLOOP:
000723r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
000725r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE                    ; copy in length and name
000727r 3  F0 05            beq VCMID
000729r 3               VCCOPY:
000729r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
00072Br 3  C8               iny
00072Cr 3  D0 F5            bne VCLOOP
00072Er 3               VCMID:
00072Er 3  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
00072Fr 3  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
000731r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
000734r 3               
000734r 3               
000734r 3  A2 14            ldx #(VCEND0-VCCODE)        ; calc offset
000736r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000738r 3               VCLOOP2:
000738r 3  B9 rr rr         lda VCCODE, y
00073Br 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
00073Dr 3  C8               iny
00073Er 3  CA               dex
00073Fr 3  D0 F7            bne VCLOOP2
000741r 3  5A               phy                        ; save for later

000744r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000746r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
000748r 3  18               clc
000749r 3  69 0F            adc #15                     ; calc addresses for indirect data
00074Br 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                   ; read.
00074Dr 3  90 02            bcc VCSKIP00
00074Fr 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
000751r 3               VCSKIP00:                      ; update addresses for lda's
000751r 3  A0 03            ldy #3                     ; <VCDATA
000753r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000755r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000757r 3  A0 07            ldy #7                     ; >VCDATA
000759r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00075Br 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
00075Dr 3  A9 00            lda #0
00075Fr 3  A0 0F            ldy #15

000763r 3  C8               iny
000764r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000766r 3               
000766r 3  68               pla                        ; to update
000767r 3  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP
000769r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
00076Cr 3               VCFINISH:
00076Cr 3  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP

000770r 3  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1

00077Ar 3               ;    VCCODE - 'dovar' - copied into a var word ( 'DOES>' )

00077Ar 3               VCCODE:         ; NEXTHEAP ('here') should start here

00077Er 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

00080Cr 3  68               pla                        ; update NEXTHEAP

000C88r 3               ; ( -- addr )  the built-in training scripts, for autoload:  ftrain autoload

001137r 3               ;-------- IO: files (see os_rom IO_PLAN.md).  A failed call gives !IO ERR!, and 'ioerr' the IO layer's
001137r 3               ;         error code ($70 not found, $71 bad fd, $72 wrong mode, $73 would block, $75 no fds, ...).
001137r 3               ;         fds 0, 1, 2 are the console (key, emit); buffers must be in task RAM ($0000-$7FFF).
001137r 3               ;
001137r 3               ; ( sz mode -- fd )  open a file: sz = a q^...^ string, e.g. q^/dev/cons^; mode 1 = read, 2 = write,
001137r 3               ;                   3 = both, + $80 = don't wait (reads and writes give ioerr $73 instead)

0012A2r 3               ;

0012DEr 3               ;
0012DEr 3               ; ( -- )  copy stdin to stdout, to the end of the file (e.g. the right side of a pipeline:  words | cat)
0012DEr 3  rr rr 03 63  def_word "cat", "cat", 0
0012E2r 3  61 74        
0012E4r 3               CATLOOP:
0012E4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GET_CHAR

0012E9r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
0012ECr 3  80 F6            bra CATLOOP
0012EEr 3               CATEND:
0012EEr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0012F1r 3               ;
0012F1r 3               ; ( -- lines words chars )  count stdin's lines (ended by CR, LF or CR LF), words (runs of characters
0012F1r 3               ;                           other than space, tab, CR and LF) and characters, to the end of the file
0012F1r 3               ;                           (e.g.  words | wc .S; or typed, ended with Ctrl-D)
0012F1r 3  rr rr 02 77  def_word "wc", "wc", 0
0012F5r 3  63           
0012F6r 3  A2 05            ldx #5
0012F8r 3               WCCLR:
0012F8r 3  74 F0            stz TEMP1,x       ; TEMP1 = characters, TEMP2 = words, TEMP3 = lines
0012FAr 3  CA               dex
0012FBr 3  10 FB            bpl WCCLR
0012FDr 3  64 F6            stz TEMP4         ; TEMP4 <> 0: in a word
0012FFr 3  64 F7            stz TEMP4+1       ; TEMP4+1 <> 0: the last character was a CR
001301r 3               WCLOOP:
001301r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GET_CHAR
001304r 3  90 42            bcc WCEND
001306r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1         ; a character
001308r 3  D0 02            bne WCSK0
00130Ar 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
00130Cr 3               WCSK0:
00130Cr 3  A6 F7            ldx TEMP4+1       ; (the last one a CR?)
00130Er 3  64 F7            stz TEMP4+1
001310r 3  C9 0D            cmp #ASCII_CR
001312r 3  D0 04            bne WCNOTCR
001314r 3  E6 F7            inc TEMP4+1
001316r 3  80 08            bra WCLINE
001318r 3               WCNOTCR:
001318r 3  C9 0A            cmp #ASCII_LF
00131Ar 3  D0 0A            bne WCSK1
00131Cr 3  E0 00            cpx #0
00131Er 3  D0 06            bne WCSK1         ; CR LF: one line
001320r 3               WCLINE:
001320r 3  E6 F4            inc TEMP3         ; a line
001322r 3  D0 02            bne WCSK1
001324r 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
001326r 3               WCSK1:
001326r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
001328r 3  F0 1A            beq WCGAP
00132Ar 3  C9 09            cmp #ASCII_TAB
00132Cr 3  F0 16            beq WCGAP
00132Er 3  C9 0D            cmp #ASCII_CR
001330r 3  F0 12            beq WCGAP
001332r 3  C9 0A            cmp #ASCII_LF
001334r 3  F0 0E            beq WCGAP
001336r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
001338r 3  D0 C7            bne WCLOOP        ; (still in the word)
00133Ar 3  E6 F6            inc TEMP4         ; a new word
00133Cr 3  E6 F2            inc TEMP2
00133Er 3  D0 C1            bne WCLOOP
001340r 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
001342r 3  80 BD            bra WCLOOP
001344r 3               WCGAP:
001344r 3  64 F6            stz TEMP4
001346r 3  80 B9            bra WCLOOP
001348r 3               WCEND:
001348r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1         ; ( -- lines words chars )
00134Ar 3  48               pha
00134Br 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00134Dr 3  48               pha
00134Er 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
001350r 3  48               pha
001351r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
001353r 3  48               pha
001354r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
001356r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001358r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
00135Ar 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00135Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0       ; lines
00135Fr 3  68               pla
001360r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
001362r 3  68               pla
001363r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
001365r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0       ; words
001368r 3  68               pla
001369r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00136Br 3  68               pla
00136Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00136Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp this          ; characters
001371r 3               ;
001371r 3               ;-------- Pipelines: a line  left | right  runs 'left' in a copy of this task (TASK_CLONE), with its
001371r 3               ;         stdout into a pipe, and 'right' here, with stdin from the pipe.  Called by getline.
001371r 3               ;
001371r 3               ; If the line (TIB) has a '|' with spaces around it (outside q^...^ strings) and we're interpreting,
001371r 3               ; start the left side, and go on after the '|'; and again for each '|' after it (a | b | c: 'b' runs in a
001371r 3               ; copy too, between two pipes).  (No '|': returns with the line untouched.)
001371r 3               PIPECHK:
001371r 3  A5 E0            lda STATUS
001373r 3  D0 2B            bne PCNONE        ; compiling
001375r 3  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF        ; (What's left of the line)
001377r 3  C8               iny
001378r 3  A2 00            ldx #0            ; '^'s so far: odd = inside a string
00137Ar 3               PCSCAN:
00137Ar 3  B1 D2            lda (TIB),y
00137Cr 3  F0 22            beq PCNONE
00137Er 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
001380r 3  D0 03            bne PCBAR
001382r 3  E8               inx
001383r 3  80 18            bra PCNEXT
001385r 3               PCBAR:
001385r 3  C9 7C            cmp #ASCII_PIPE
001387r 3  D0 14            bne PCNEXT
001389r 3  8A               txa
00138Ar 3  4A               lsr
00138Br 3  B0 10            bcs PCNEXT        ; inside a string
00138Dr 3  88               dey
00138Er 3  B1 D2            lda (TIB),y
001390r 3  C8               iny
001391r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
001393r 3  D0 08            bne PCNEXT
001395r 3  C8               iny
001396r 3  B1 D2            lda (TIB),y
001398r 3  88               dey
001399r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
00139Br 3  F0 04            beq PCFOUND
00139Dr 3               PCNEXT:
00139Dr 3  C8               iny
00139Er 3  D0 DA            bne PCSCAN
0013A0r 3               PCNONE:
0013A0r 3  60               rts
0013A1r 3               PCFOUND:                   ; .Y = the '|'
0013A1r 3  5A               phy
0013A2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_PIPE            ; .A = read fd, .X = write fd
0013A5r 3  B0 4E            bcs PCFAIL
0013A7r 3  85 BC            sta PIPER
0013A9r 3  86 BB            stx PIPEW
0013ABr 3  7A               ply
0013ACr 3  5A               phy
0013ADr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0013AFr 3  91 D2            sta (TIB),y            ; the copy's line ends at the '|'...
0013B1r 3  A9 rr            lda #<child_start
0013B3r 3  A0 rr            ldy #>child_start
0013B5r 3  A2 01            ldx #1                 ; (HyForth's ROM page)
0013B7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr TASK_CLONE
0013BAr 3  7A               ply
0013BBr 3  48               pha
0013BCr 3  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
0013BEr 3  91 D2            sta (TIB),y            ; ...and this one goes on after it
0013C0r 3  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0013C2r 3  68               pla
0013C3r 3  B0 22            bcs PCNOTASK
0013C5r 3  A5 BB            lda PIPEW              ; the copy has the write end now
0013C7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
0013CAr 3  A5 BF            lda PIPEIN
0013CCr 3  10 09            bpl PCSAVED            ; (a later '|': stdin is the previous pipe; the new one replaces it)
0013CEr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0013D0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_DUP             ; save stdin
0013D3r 3  B0 21            bcs PCFAIL2
0013D5r 3  85 BF            sta PIPEIN
0013D7r 3               PCSAVED:
0013D7r 3  A5 BC            lda PIPER
0013D9r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0013DBr 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_DUP2            ; stdin from the pipe
0013DEr 3  B0 16            bcs PCFAIL2
0013E0r 3  A5 BC            lda PIPER
0013E2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
0013E5r 3  80 8A            bra PIPECHK            ; another '|'?
0013E7r 3               PCNOTASK:                  ; no task for the copy: no pipeline
0013E7r 3  48               pha
0013E8r 3  A5 BB            lda PIPEW
0013EAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
0013EDr 3  A5 BC            lda PIPER
0013EFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
0013F2r 3  68               pla
0013F3r 3  80 01            bra PCFAIL2
0013F5r 3               PCFAIL:
0013F5r 3  7A               ply
0013F6r 3               PCFAIL2:                   ; drop the returns to getline and 'resolve', then the error
0013F6r 3  7A               ply
0013F7r 3  7A               ply
0013F8r 3  7A               ply
0013F9r 3  7A               ply
0013FAr 3  4C rr rr         jmp IOFAIL
0013FDr 3               ;
0013FDr 3               ; The end of a pipeline's line (getline): stdin back
0013FDr 3               PIPEEND:
0013FDr 3  A5 BF            lda PIPEIN
0013FFr 3  30 0E            bmi PEDONE             ; ($FF: not redirected)
001401r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
001403r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_DUP2
001406r 3  A5 BF            lda PIPEIN
001408r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
00140Br 3  A9 FF            lda #$FF
00140Dr 3  85 BF            sta PIPEIN
00140Fr 3               PEDONE:
00140Fr 3  60               rts
001410r 3               ;
001410r 3               ; A pipeline's left side starts here, in a copy of the shell's task (TASK_CLONE, ROM page 1): stdout into
001410r 3               ; the pipe, then run the line, which ends at the '|'.  At its end, getline ends the task (BATCH).
001410r 3               child_start:
001410r 3  BA               tsx
001411r 3  86 BD            stx CHILDSP
001413r 3  A9 01            lda #1
001415r 3  85 BE            sta BATCH
001417r 3  A5 BB            lda PIPEW
001419r 3  A2 01            ldx #1
00141Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_DUP2
00141Er 3  A5 BB            lda PIPEW
001420r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
001423r 3  A5 BC            lda PIPER
001425r 3  20 rr rr         jsr IO_CLOSE
001428r 3  4C rr rr         jmp resolve
00142Br 3               ;
00142Br 3               ; ( fd addr n -- ) -> ZP_IO_BUF = addr, ZP_IO_CNT = n, .A = fd
00142Br 3               IOARGS:
00142Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_2       ; n
00142Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1       ; addr
001431r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0       ; fd
001434r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
001436r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_IO_BUF
001438r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
00143Ar 3  85 rr            sta ZP_IO_BUF+1
00143Cr 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
00143Er 3  85 rr            sta ZP_IO_CNT
001440r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
001442r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_IO_CNT+1
001444r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
001446r 3  60               rts
001447r 3               ;
001447r 3               ;
001447r 3               .ifdef YSOUND
001447r 3               ;

00144Br 3  6E 64 69 6E  
00144Fr 3  69 74

001454r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next

00145Br 3  6E 64 74 65

001467r 3

001467r 3  rr rr 06 79  def_word "ywrite", "ywrite", 0
00146Br 3  77 72 69 74  
00146Fr 3  65           
001470r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
001473r 3  A6 F1            ldx TEMP1+1
001475r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
001477r 3  20 rr rr         jsr YM_WRITE
00147Ar 3  90 03            bcc YMGOOD
00147Cr 3  4C rr rr         jmp PUSHFALSE
00147Fr 3               YMGOOD:
00147Fr 3  4C rr rr         jmp PUSHTRUE
001482r 3               .endif

001792r 3               ;
001792r 3               ;  BLOAD test code
001792r 3               ;
001792r 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
001792r 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines), fully relocatable.  That means NO jumps
001792r 3               ;   or branches to labels!  Calculate all jumps and branches accordingly
001792r 3               ;   and use numercal offsets.  jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels
001792r 3               ;   (next, keeps, this, etc) or ROM locations will work fine.
001792r 3               ;
001792r 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
001792r 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) must be
001792r 3               ;   the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
001792r 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
001792r 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
001792r 3               ;
001792r 3               test00:

000029r 2               
000029r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000029r 2               ; Serial driver: the file server for /dev/cons and /dev/ser (see IO_PLAN.md).  Runs in its own Resident
000029r 2               ; task (SERIAL_TASK_NUM, started by DRV_START at boot), so its state (ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS, ZP_SER_CAPTURE,
000029r 2               ; the SER_* task ZP in zero.s) and its RX and TX rings (SER_RX_BUF, SER_TX_BUF) live in that task.
000029r 2               ;   RX: the IRQ handler puts each received byte into the RX ring, and wakes the tasks waiting to read.
000029r 2               ;   TX: bytes go into the TX ring (or straight to the ACIA when it's idle); SER_TX_NEXT sends the next
000029r 2               ;       one each time the ACIA's transmit register empties: the Rockwell 65C51's TDRE interrupt, or for
000029r 2               ;       the WDC 65C51 (whose TDRE doesn't work) VIA timer 2, a character's time after each byte (SER_ACIA).
000029r 2               ;   /dev/cons reads only for the foreground task (ZP_SER_CAPTURE: the shell to start with); others
000029r 2               ;   wait until they're brought to the foreground.  /dev/ser is the raw port.  The requests themselves
000029r 2               ;   are handled on ROM page 2 (ser_srv.s).
000029r 2               ;   READ_CHAR / WRITE_CHAR use the task's fd 0 / fd 1, or the rings directly if the task has none.
000029r 2               
000029r 2               SERIAL_DRIVER:
000029r 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_INIT             ; DriverInfo::init
00002Br 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_STOP             ; DriverInfo::stop
00002Dr 2  rr rr                        .word           SERIAL_NAME             ; DriverInfo::name
00002Fr 2  06 53 45 52  NamedHString SERIAL_NAME, "SERIAL"
000033r 2  49 41 4C     
000036r 2  63 6F 6E 73  CONS_NAME:      .byte           "cons", 0
00003Ar 2  00           
00003Br 2  73 65 72 00  SER_NAME:       .byte           "ser", 0
00003Fr 2               
00003Fr 2               ; Gate into the serial task: set the foreground task (the one /dev/cons reads for)
00003Fr 2               ; IN: .A = task
00003Fr 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE       SER_CALL_SET_CAPTURE, SERIAL_SET_CAPTURE, SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000043r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000047r 2  rr A9 0F 85  
000050r 2               
000050r 2               ; Serve routines (page 2, ser_srv.s)
000050r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE CONS_SERVE,     PAGE2::CONS_SERVE,      2
000054r 2  rr 02        
000056r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE SER_SERVE,      PAGE2::SER_SERVE,       2
00005Ar 2  rr 02        
00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               ; Driver init (runs in the serial task).  OUT: C = 0 on success, or C = 1 and .A = error
00005Cr 2               SERIAL_INIT:
00005Cr 2  08                           php                                     ; Save caller's I flag
00005Dr 2  78                           sei
00005Er 2  A9 1F                        lda             #$10 | SR_SELECT    ; 8-N-1
000060r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CTRL
000063r 2               .if SER_ACIA = SER_ACIA_ROCKWELL
000063r 2  A9 05                        lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLIE  ; No parity, no echo, tx & rx interrupts.
000065r 2               .else
000065r 2                               lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID  ; No parity, no echo, rx interrupts
000065r 2               .endif                                                                  ;   (WDC: TX paced by VIA timer 2)
000065r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CMD
000068r 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
00006Ar 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
00006Cr 2  A9 01                        lda             #SHELL_TASK_NUM         ; The shell is in the foreground to start with
00006Er 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
000070r 2  A2 07                        ldx             #SER_RX_HEAD - SER_WR_WAIT
000072r 2               :
000072r 2  74 F8                        stz             SER_WR_WAIT,X           ; Empty rings, nobody waiting
000074r 2  CA                           dex
000075r 2  10 FB                        bpl             :-
000077r 2  A2 06                        ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL
000079r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00007Br 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00007Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IRQ_REGISTER            ; Handler runs in this (the serial) task
000080r 2  B0 22                        bcs             @done
000082r 2               .if SER_ACIA = SER_ACIA_WDC
000082r 2                               lda             VIA_R_AUX_CTRL          ; VIA timer 2: one-shot (ACR bit 5 = 0)
000082r 2                               and             #<~VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000082r 2                               sta             VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
000082r 2                               lda             #VIA_INT_ENABLE | VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000082r 2                               sta             VIA_R_INT_ENABLE        ; Its IRQ on
000082r 2                               ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_VIA
000082r 2                               lda             #<SERIAL_T2_HANDLER
000082r 2                               ldy             #>SERIAL_T2_HANDLER
000082r 2                               jsr             IRQ_REGISTER            ; (After the scheduler's VIA handler: T1)
000082r 2                               bcs             @done
000082r 2               .endif
000082r 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR       CONS_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC   ; The files
000086r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00008Ar 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<CONS_NAME
00008Cr 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>CONS_NAME
00008Er 2  A2 0F                        ldx             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000090r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DEV_REGISTER
000093r 2  B0 0F                        bcs             @done
000095r 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR       SER_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
000099r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
00009Dr 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SER_NAME
00009Fr 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SER_NAME
0000A1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DEV_REGISTER
0000A4r 2               
0000A4r 2               @done:
0000A4r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             MM_RETURN               ; Restore caller's I flag, keep C
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               .assert         SER_RX_HEAD - SER_WR_WAIT = 7, error, "SERIAL_INIT clears the serial task ZP as one block"
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               SERIAL_STOP:
0000A7r 2  18                           clc
0000A8r 2  60                           rts
0000A9r 2               
0000A9r 2               ; Set the foreground task: /dev/cons reads for it (runs in the serial task; use SER_CALL_SET_CAPTURE,
0000A9r 2               ; or IO_CTL SER_CTL_FOREGROUND on a /dev/cons fd).  Wakes the tasks waiting to read, so the new
0000A9r 2               ; foreground task gets its input and the others go back to waiting.
0000A9r 2               ; IN: .A = task.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0000A9r 2               SERIAL_SET_CAPTURE:
0000A9r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
0000ABr 2  A2 FA                        ldx             #SER_RD_WAIT
0000ADr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_WAKE
0000B0r 2  18                           clc
0000B1r 2  60                           rts
0000B2r 2               
0000B2r 2               ; Input a character, if there is one: from fd 0 (stdin), without waiting.  (A task without an fd 0
0000B2r 2               ; gets nothing.)  /dev/cons echoes it.
0000B2r 2               ; On return, carry flag indicates whether a key was pressed
0000B2r 2               ; If a key was pressed, the key value will be in the A register
0000B2r 2               ;
0000B2r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0000B2r 2               READ_CHAR:
0000B2r 2               SERIAL_READ:
0000B2r 2  DA                           phx
0000B3r 2  AE A0 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER            ; fd 0 open?
0000B6r 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
0000B8r 2  F0 17                        beq             @none
0000BAr 2  AD A2 7D                     lda             IO_FD_MODE              ; Just this once, don't wait
0000BDr 2  48                           pha
0000BEr 2  09 80                        ora             #IO_MODE_NONBLOCK
0000C0r 2  8D A2 7D                     sta             IO_FD_MODE
0000C3r 2  A2 00                        ldx             #0
0000C5r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_GETC                 ; C = 0: .A = byte
0000C8r 2  FA                           plx
0000C9r 2  8E A2 7D                     stx             IO_FD_MODE
0000CCr 2  B0 03                        bcs             @none
0000CEr 2  FA                           plx
0000CFr 2  38                           sec
0000D0r 2  60                           rts
0000D1r 2               
0000D1r 2               @none:
0000D1r 2  FA                           plx
0000D2r 2  18                           clc
0000D3r 2  60                           rts
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               ; Input a character, waiting for it: from fd 0, so the task sleeps until one comes in (or until it's
0000D4r 2               ; brought to the foreground, for /dev/cons, which echoes it).  A task without an fd 0 (or with a
0000D4r 2               ; non-blocking one) polls READ_CHAR, yielding in between.
0000D4r 2               ; OUT: .A = the character, C = 1; or .A = error (e.g. ERR_IO_EOF: the end of a pipe), C = 0
0000D4r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0000D4r 2               GET_CHAR:
0000D4r 2  DA                           phx
0000D5r 2  AE A0 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER            ; fd 0 open?
0000D8r 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
0000DAr 2  F0 0B                        beq             @poll
0000DCr 2  A2 00                        ldx             #0
0000DEr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_GETC                 ; Waits for it
0000E1r 2  90 0E                        bcc             @got
0000E3r 2  C9 73                        cmp             #ERR_IO_WOULD_BLOCK
0000E5r 2  D0 0D                        bne             @error                  ; (A non-blocking fd 0: poll)
0000E7r 2               
0000E7r 2               @poll:
0000E7r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR
0000EAr 2  B0 06                        bcs             @done
0000ECr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             YIELD
0000EFr 2  80 F6                        bra             @poll
0000F1r 2               
0000F1r 2               @got:
0000F1r 2  38                           sec
0000F2r 2               
0000F2r 2               @done:
0000F2r 2  FA                           plx
0000F3r 2  60                           rts
0000F4r 2               
0000F4r 2               @error:
0000F4r 2  FA                           plx
0000F5r 2  18                           clc
0000F6r 2  60                           rts
0000F7r 2               
0000F7r 2               WRITE_BYTE:
0000F7r 2  48                           pha                                         ; Save A for LSD.
0000F8r 2  4A                           lsr
0000F9r 2  4A                           lsr
0000FAr 2  4A                           lsr
0000FBr 2  4A                           lsr                                         ; MSD to LSD position.
0000FCr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX                   ; Output hex digit.
0000FFr 2  68                           pla                                         ; Restore A.
000100r 2               
000100r 2               WRITE_HEX_MASK:
000100r 2  29 0F                        and             #$0F                        ; Mask LSD for hex print.
000102r 2               
000102r 2               WRITE_HEX:
000102r 2  DA                           phx
000103r 2  AA                           tax
000104r 2  BD rr rr                     lda             HEX_MAP,x
000107r 2  22                           SKIPNEXT
000108r 2               
000108r 2               ; Output a character (from the A register): to fd 1 (stdout), or straight to the serial port if the
000108r 2               ; task has no fd 1 (system and driver tasks).  If fd 1 fails (e.g. a pipe nobody reads any more), the
000108r 2               ; character is dropped.  Not with IRQs off: it may have to wait for the TX IRQ.
000108r 2               ;
000108r 2               ; Modifies: flags
000108r 2               WRITE_CHAR:
000108r 2               SERIAL_WRITE:
000108r 2  DA                           phx                                         ; Must stay 1 byte (WRITE_HEX SKIPNEXTs over it)
000109r 2  5A                           phy
00010Ar 2  AE A8 7D                     ldx             IO_FD_SERVER + IO_FD_SIZE   ; fd 1 open?
00010Dr 2  E0 FF                        cpx             #IO_FD_CLOSED
00010Fr 2  F0 09                        beq             @direct
000111r 2  48                           pha
000112r 2  A2 01                        ldx             #1
000114r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_PUTC
000117r 2  68                           pla
000118r 2  80 08                        bra             @done
00011Ar 2               
00011Ar 2               @direct:
00011Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_TX_TRY
00011Dr 2  90 03                        bcc             @done
00011Fr 2  CB                           wai                                         ; The TX ring is full: wait for the TX IRQ
000120r 2  80 F8                        bra             @direct
000122r 2               
000122r 2               @done:
000122r 2  7A                           ply
000123r 2  FA                           plx
000124r 2  60                           rts
000125r 2               
000125r 2               ; Queue a byte for the serial port, from any task: into the TX ring, or straight to the ACIA when it's
000125r 2               ; idle (the ring is empty then).  IN: .A = byte.  OUT: C = 0, or C = 1 if the ring is full
000125r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X; modifies .Y
000125r 2               SER_TX_TRY:
000125r 2  08                           php                                         ; Save caller's I flag
000126r 2  78                           sei
000127r 2  DA                           phx
000128r 2  AE F0 FF                     ldx             T_REGISTER
00012Br 2  A0 0F                        ldy             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
00012Dr 2  8C F0 FF                     sty             T_REGISTER                  ; Quick switch to the serial task (no stack use!)
000130r 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000132r 2  D0 07                        bne             @queue                      ; Busy: the TX IRQ sends it
000134r 2  8D rr rr                     _M_SER_TX_BYTE
000137r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS          ; SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
000139r 2  80 0C                        bra             @ok
00013Br 2               
00013Br 2               @queue:
00013Br 2  A4 FD                        ldy             SER_TX_HEAD
00013Dr 2  99 00 03                     sta             SER_TX_BUF,Y
000140r 2  C8                           iny
000141r 2  C4 FC                        cpy             SER_TX_TAIL
000143r 2  F0 09                        beq             @full                       ; (The byte stored isn't counted: head stays)
000145r 2  84 FD                        sty             SER_TX_HEAD
000147r 2               
000147r 2               @ok:
000147r 2  8E F0 FF                     stx             T_REGISTER                  ; Back to the calling task
00014Ar 2  FA                           plx
00014Br 2  28                           plp
00014Cr 2  18                           clc
00014Dr 2  60                           rts
00014Er 2               
00014Er 2               @full:
00014Er 2  8E F0 FF                     stx             T_REGISTER                  ; Back to the calling task
000151r 2  FA                           plx
000152r 2  28                           plp
000153r 2  38                           sec
000154r 2  60                           rts
000155r 2               
000155r 2               ; The transmitter is free (the Rockwell 65C51's TDRE interrupt, or the WDC 65C51's timer 2 ran out): send
000155r 2               ; the next byte from the TX ring, or go idle.  Runs in the serial task, in an IRQ handler.
000155r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000155r 2               SER_TX_NEXT:
000155r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000157r 2  F0 17                        beq             @done                   ; Idle: nothing to send
000159r 2  A4 FC                        ldy             SER_TX_TAIL             ; The next byte from the TX ring
00015Br 2  C4 FD                        cpy             SER_TX_HEAD
00015Dr 2  D0 03                        bne             :+
00015Fr 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS      ; SER_SEND_STATUS_READY: the ring is empty
000161r 2  60                           rts
000162r 2               :
000162r 2  B9 00 03                     lda             SER_TX_BUF,Y
000165r 2  C8                           iny
000166r 2  84 FC                        sty             SER_TX_TAIL
000168r 2  8D rr rr                     _M_SER_TX_BYTE
00016Br 2  A2 F8                        ldx             #SER_WR_WAIT            ; There's room: wake the waiting writers
00016Dr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SER_WAKE
000170r 2               
000170r 2               @done:
000170r 2  60                           rts
000171r 2               
000171r 2               .if SER_ACIA = SER_ACIA_WDC
000171r 2               ; The WDC 65C51's TX pacing: VIA timer 2 ran out (a character's time since the last byte went).  Registered
000171r 2               ; by SERIAL_INIT on the VIA's IRQ (after the scheduler's handler); runs in the serial task.
000171r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T2 was interrupting
000171r 2               SERIAL_T2_HANDLER:
000171r 2                               lda             #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
000171r 2                               and             VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
000171r 2                               beq             @not_mine
000171r 2                               lda             VIA_R_T2C_L             ; Clears its IRQ
000171r 2                               jsr             SER_TX_NEXT             ; (Starts it again for the next byte)
000171r 2                               sec
000171r 2                               rts
000171r 2               
000171r 2               @not_mine:
000171r 2                               clc
000171r 2                               rts
000171r 2               .endif
000171r 2               
000171r 2               ; A break or kill key (the IRQ handler, in the serial task, IRQs off): the foreground task gets .A
000171r 2               ; (TASK_BREAK_FLAG or TASK_KILL_FLAG), and the tasks it started (and theirs, 4 levels) are killed; they
000171r 2               ; stop waiting, so they run and see it (SCHED_RESUME).  The keys typed before it are dropped.  A killed
000171r 2               ; foreground task other than the shell hands the console back to the shell.
000171r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y, ZP_TEMP
000171r 2               SER_BREAK:
000171r 2  48                           pha                                         ; (The foreground task's flag)
000172r 2  A5 FF                        lda             SER_RX_HEAD                 ; Drop the typed-ahead keys
000174r 2  85 FE                        sta             SER_RX_TAIL
000176r 2  A2 0F                        ldx             #MAX_TASK_NUMBER            ; Tasks 15-1
000178r 2               
000178r 2               @task:
000178r 2  E4 rr                        cpx             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
00017Ar 2  F0 22                        beq             @foreground
00017Cr 2  A9 04                        lda             #4                          ; Started by the foreground task, or by one
00017Er 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_TEMP                     ;   of those, ...?
000180r 2  8A                           txa
000181r 2  A8                           tay                                         ; .Y = the task, then its owner, ...
000182r 2               
000182r 2               @owner:
000182r 2  8C F0 FF                     sty             T_REGISTER                  ; Quick look (no stack use!)
000185r 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_TASK_OWNER
000187r 2  A9 0F                        lda             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
000189r 2  8D F0 FF                     sta             T_REGISTER
00018Cr 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
00018Er 2  F0 0A                        beq             @child
000190r 2  C0 10                        cpy             #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
000192r 2  B0 22                        bcs             @next                       ; ($FF: nobody)
000194r 2  C6 rr                        dec             ZP_TEMP
000196r 2  D0 EA                        bne             @owner
000198r 2  80 1C                        bra             @next
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               @child:
00019Ar 2  A0 20                        ldy             #TASK_KILL_FLAG
00019Cr 2  80 03                        bra             @flag
00019Er 2               
00019Er 2               @foreground:
00019Er 2  68                           pla
00019Fr 2  48                           pha
0001A0r 2  A8                           tay
0001A1r 2               
0001A1r 2               @flag:                                                      ; .Y = the flag for task .X
0001A1r 2  8E F0 FF                     stx             T_REGISTER                  ; Quick switch (no stack use!)
0001A4r 2  A5 rr                        lda             TASK_STATUS_REG
0001A6r 2  29 09                        and             #TASK_BUSY_FLAG | TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG
0001A8r 2  C9 01                        cmp             #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
0001AAr 2  D0 05                        bne             :+                          ; (Free, or a driver)
0001ACr 2  98                           tya
0001ADr 2  04 rr                        tsb             TASK_STATUS_REG
0001AFr 2  27 rr                        rmb2            TASK_STATUS_REG             ; Not waiting any more (TASK_WAITING_FLAG)
0001B1r 2               :
0001B1r 2  A9 0F                        lda             #SERIAL_TASK_NUM
0001B3r 2  8D F0 FF                     sta             T_REGISTER
0001B6r 2               
0001B6r 2               @next:
0001B6r 2  CA                           dex
0001B7r 2  D0 BF                        bne             @task
0001B9r 2  68                           pla
0001BAr 2  C9 20                        cmp             #TASK_KILL_FLAG
0001BCr 2  D0 0A                        bne             @done
0001BEr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SER_CAPTURE              ; A killed foreground task: the shell gets the
0001C0r 2  C9 01                        cmp             #SHELL_TASK_NUM             ;   console back
0001C2r 2  F0 04                        beq             @done
0001C4r 2  A9 01                        lda             #SHELL_TASK_NUM
0001C6r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_CAPTURE
0001C8r 2               
0001C8r 2               @done:
0001C8r 2  60                           rts
0001C9r 2               
0001C9r 2               ; Wake every task in a wait mask of the serial task (SER_RD_WAIT or SER_WR_WAIT), and clear the mask.
0001C9r 2               ; Runs in the serial task.  IN: .X = the mask's ZP address.  Modifies: .A, .Y
0001C9r 2               SER_WAKE:
0001C9r 2  08                           php
0001CAr 2  78                           sei
0001CBr 2  B5 00                        lda             0,X
0001CDr 2  15 01                        ora             1,X
0001CFr 2  F0 11                        beq             @done
0001D1r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
0001D3r 2               
0001D3r 2               @loop:
0001D3r 2  56 01                        lsr             1,X
0001D5r 2  76 00                        ror             0,X
0001D7r 2  90 04                        bcc             :+
0001D9r 2  98                           tya
0001DAr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IO_WAKE
0001DDr 2               :
0001DDr 2  C8                           iny
0001DEr 2  C0 10                        cpy             #16
0001E0r 2  D0 F1                        bne             @loop
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               @done:
0001E2r 2  28                           plp
0001E3r 2  60                           rts
0001E4r 2               
0001E4r 2               ; Convenience method to write CR/LF to output stream
0001E4r 2               WRITE_CRLF:
0001E4r 2  A9 0D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_CR, #ASCII_LF
0001E8r 2  rr A9 0A 4C  
0001ECr 2  rr rr        
0001EEr 2               
0001EEr 2               WRITE_PROMPT:
0001EEr 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0001F1r 2  A9 54 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_T
0001F5r 2  rr           
0001F6r 2  AD F0 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF0
0001FAr 2  rr rr        
0001FCr 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
000200r 2  rr           
000201r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      RAM_BANK_REG
000205r 2  rr           
000206r 2  C9 F0                        cmp             #$F0
000208r 2  90 10                        bcc             @not_shared
00020Ar 2  A9 28 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_LPAREN
00020Er 2  rr           
00020Fr 2  AD F1 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF1                       ; Shared RAM sub-bank
000213r 2  rr rr        
000215r 2  A9 29 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_RPAREN
000219r 2  rr           
00021Ar 2               
00021Ar 2               @not_shared:
00021Ar 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_COLON
00021Er 2  rr           
00021Fr 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ROM_BANK_REG
000223r 2  rr           
000224r 2  A9 3E 4C rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_GT
000228r 2  rr           
000229r 2               
000229r 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
000229r 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X
000229r 2               WRITE_HSTRING:
000229r 2  DA                           phx
00022Ar 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_HS_TEMP
00022Cr 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_HS_TEMP + 1
00022Er 2  B2 rr                        lda             (ZP_HS_TEMP)                ; Length of HString
000230r 2  AA                           tax
000231r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
000233r 2               @write_loop:
000233r 2  C8                           iny
000234r 2  B1 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      {(ZP_HS_TEMP),Y}
000238r 2  rr           
000239r 2  CA                           dex
00023Ar 2  D0 F7                        bne             @write_loop
00023Cr 2  FA                           plx
00023Dr 2  60                           rts
00023Er 2               
00023Er 2               ; Escape sequences
00023Er 2               CLEAR_SCR:
00023Er 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_2, #ASCII_J
000242r 2  rr A9 5B 20  
000246r 2  rr rr A9 32  
000252r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ_JMP #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_SEMI, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_f
000256r 2  rr A9 5B 20  
00025Ar 2  rr rr A9 30  
000270r 2               
000270r 2               ; Serial IRQ handler (registered with IRQ_REGISTER; runs in the serial task)
000270r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if the ACIA was interrupting
000270r 2               SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER:
000270r 2  AD rr rr                     lda             ACIA_R_STATUS           ; Read once: it clears the IRQ flag, so a second
000273r 2  10 3A                        bpl             @not_mine 	            ;   read could lose a TDRE that came in between
000275r 2  48                           pha
000276r 2               .if SER_ACIA = SER_ACIA_ROCKWELL                        ; (The WDC 65C51's TDRE doesn't work: timer 2 instead)
000276r 2  29 10                        and             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
000278r 2  F0 03                        beq             @check_recv             ; Transmit register still full
00027Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_TX_NEXT
00027Dr 2               .endif
00027Dr 2               
00027Dr 2               @check_recv:
00027Dr 2  68                           pla
00027Er 2  29 08                        and             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_RDRF   ; is read register full?
000280r 2  F0 1C                        beq             @int_done
000282r 2  AD rr rr                     IO_PORT_READ    ACIA_R_DATA
000285r 2  C9 03                        cmp             #SER_KEY_BREAK          ; Break or kill: act on it now (the task
000287r 2  F0 17                        beq             @break                  ;   may not be reading)
000289r 2  C9 1C                        cmp             #SER_KEY_KILL
00028Br 2  F0 17                        beq             @kill
00028Dr 2  A4 FF                        ldy             SER_RX_HEAD             ; Into the RX ring
00028Fr 2  99 00 02                     sta             SER_RX_BUF,Y
000292r 2  C8                           iny
000293r 2  C4 FE                        cpy             SER_RX_TAIL
000295r 2  F0 07                        beq             @int_done               ; The ring is full: the byte is dropped
000297r 2  84 FF                        sty             SER_RX_HEAD
000299r 2  A2 FA                        ldx             #SER_RD_WAIT            ; Wake the waiting readers
00029Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_WAKE
00029Er 2               
00029Er 2               @int_done:
00029Er 2  38                           sec
00029Fr 2  60                           rts
0002A0r 2               
0002A0r 2               @break:
0002A0r 2  A9 10                        lda             #TASK_BREAK_FLAG
0002A2r 2  80 02                        bra             :+
0002A4r 2               @kill:
0002A4r 2  A9 20                        lda             #TASK_KILL_FLAG
0002A6r 2               :
0002A6r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SER_BREAK
0002A9r 2  A9 A5                        lda             #SCHED_RESCHED_A        ; Switch tasks now, so it happens (SCHED_RESUME)
0002ABr 2  A0 5A                        ldy             #SCHED_RESCHED_Y
0002ADr 2  38                           sec
0002AEr 2  60                           rts
0002AFr 2               
0002AFr 2               @not_mine:
0002AFr 2  18                           clc
0002B0r 2  60                           rts
0002B1r 2               
0002B1r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0002B1r 2               ; VIA (the scheduler's tick is its timer 1; see SCHED_START; SPI uses port B, spi.s)
0002B1r 2               
0002B1r 2               ; Must be called from the system task (the VIA handler runs there)
0002B1r 2               VIA_INIT:
0002B1r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0002B4r 2  A2 07                    ldx     #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_VIA
0002B6r 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<VIA_IRQ_HANDLER
0002B8r 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>VIA_IRQ_HANDLER
0002BAr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_REGISTER
0002BDr 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0002C0r 2  60                       rts
0002C1r 2               
0002C1r 2               ; VIA IRQ handler (registered by VIA_INIT; runs in the system task).
0002C1r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T1 was interrupting
0002C1r 2               VIA_IRQ_HANDLER:
0002C1r 2               ; check which sub-device is triggering the IRQ
0002C1r 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
0002C3r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002C6r 2  F0 09                    beq     :+
0002C8r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002CBr 2  A9 A5                    lda     #SCHED_RESCHED_A        ; T1 is the scheduler's tick: ask the dispatcher for a task switch
0002CDr 2  A0 5A                    ldy     #SCHED_RESCHED_Y
0002CFr 2  38                       sec
0002D0r 2  60                       rts
0002D1r 2               
0002D1r 2               :
0002D1r 2  18                       clc
0002D2r 2  60                       rts
0002D3r 2

000074r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000078r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000080r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_8:      .tag IO_Port
000084r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000088r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000090r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_9:      .tag IO_Port
000094r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000098r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000A0r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_A:      .tag IO_Port
0000A4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000A8r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000B0r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_B:      .tag IO_Port
0000B4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000B8r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000C0r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_C:      .tag IO_Port
0000C4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000C8r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000D0r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_D:      .tag IO_Port
0000D4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000D8r 2  xx xx xx xx

0000E4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000E8r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000F0r 2  xx xx xx xx  IO_PORT_F:      .tag IO_Port_10_Bytes
0000F4r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0000F8r 2  xx xx        
0000FAr 2               
0000FAr 2               .macro VECTORS
0000FAr 2                               .word   NMI_ENTRY       ; NMI vector (COMMON block, same address on every page)
0000FAr 2                               .word   RESET_ENTRY     ; RESET vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   IRQ_STUB_F      ; IRQ vector (COMMON block S/W IRQ stub; unused, see below)
0000FAr 2                               ;       will actually be pulled from the Vector RAM, depending on lowest priority IRQ currently triggered,
0000FAr 2                               ;       or vector for IRQ# set in V[0..3] if none are triggered.  Can trigger S/W IRQs by setting V and then
0000FAr 2                               ;       calling BRK.  S/W IRQ# is $F, so setting V[0..3] to $F and v[4..7] to a different number, you can
0000FAr 2                               ;       have up to 16 unique S/W IRQs.  You can invoke a hardware device IRQ handler in the same way
0000FAr 2               .endmacro
0000FAr 2               
0000FAr 2               .segment "RESETVEC_P0"
000000r 2  rr rr 00 E0      VECTORS
000004r 2  rr rr

0000ABr 2               
0000ABr 1               .include "tasks.s"
0000ABr 2               .debuginfo
0000ABr 2               .segment "TASKS"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_0_VECTOR           = $E000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_BUSY_FLAG          = 1
000000r 2               TASK_PAUSED_FLAG        = 2
000000r 2               TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG      = 8
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; TASK STATUS REGISTER BITS
000000r 2               ;   0: 0 = Available, 1 = In Use
000000r 2               ;   1: 0 = Active, 1 = Paused
000000r 2               ;   2: 0 = Not Waiting, 1 = Awaiting I/O
000000r 2               ;   3: 0 = , 1 = Resident (driver task: runs only from IRQs / TASK_CALLs, never scheduled)
000000r 2               ;   4: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   5: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   6: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   7: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_TASK      task
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$F0
000000r 2                           ora     #(task & $0F)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_SHARED_BANK bank
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$0F
000000r 2                           ora     #(bank << 4)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Initialize the tasks, their stacks, etc.
000000r 2               TASKS_INIT:
000000r 2  78                       sei                                     ; Turn off interrupts
000001r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
000004r 2  D0 2E                    bne     @cleanup                        ; Only support tasks init when on task 0
000006r 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
000008r 2               
000008r 2               @loop:
000008r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to task X
00000Br 2  64 rr                    stz     RAM_BANK_REG
00000Dr 2  64 rr                    stz     ROM_BANK_REG
00000Fr 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG
000011r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_D_PAGE                       ; Disassembler reads the BIOS page by default
000013r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_NO_PREEMPT                   ; Scheduler
000015r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
000017r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_TC_GUEST
000019r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
00001Br 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1                ; No break handler
00001Dr 2  A9 FF                    lda     #$FF
00001Fr 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_PARENT                     ; No parent
000021r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TASK_OWNER
000023r 2  85 rr                    sta     STACK_SAVE_REG
000025r 2  A0 60                    ldy     #IO_MAX_FDS * IO_FD_SIZE        ; All fds closed
000027r 2               
000027r 2               @fds:
000027r 2  88                       dey
000028r 2  99 A0 7D                 sta     IO_FD_TABLE,Y
00002Br 2  D0 FA                    bne     @fds
00002Dr 2  CA                       dex
00002Er 2  10 D8                    bpl     @loop                           ; Loop back as long as X >= 0
000030r 2  A9 01                    lda     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
000032r 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Mark task zero as "busy"
000034r 2               
000034r 2                           ; setup interrupt handler and interrupt timer
000034r 2               
000034r 2                           ; Will fall through when X = $FF, leaving us in Task 0, as required
000034r 2               
000034r 2               @cleanup:
000034r 2  58                       cli                                     ; Turn interrupts back on
000035r 2  60                       rts
000036r 2               
000036r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000036r 2               ; Scheduler (see IO_PLAN.md, Phase 1)
000036r 2               ;
000036r 2               ;   Every task that isn't running keeps the same frame on its own stack: the one IRQ_DISPATCH builds
000036r 2               ;   (interrupt frame, A, X, W, Y, the TASK_CALL scratch bytes), plus U.  Top of stack first:
000036r 2               ;       U, ZP_TC_TASK, ZP_TC_VEC + 1, ZP_TC_VEC, Y, W, X, A, P, PCL, PCH
000036r 2               ;   and STACK_SAVE_REG is the SP below it.  So a task switch is always the same: save SP, pick a task,
000036r 2               ;   load its SP, unwind its frame (SCHED_RESUME), whether the task stopped for the timer tick (IRQ),
000036r 2               ;   YIELD, or to wait for IO.  W and U are in the frame because they're global pseudo-registers.
000036r 2               ;
000036r 2               ;   Runnable: busy, and not paused, waiting or resident.  Round-robin over tasks 1-15; task 0 is the
000036r 2               ;   idle task and only runs when nothing else can.
000036r 2               
000036r 2               TASK_WAITING_FLAG       = 4                         ; Bit 2: awaiting IO (TASK_WAIT / IO_WAKE)
000036r 2               TASK_BREAK_FLAG         = $10                       ; Bit 4: a break is due (console: SER_BREAK)
000036r 2               TASK_KILL_FLAG          = $20                       ; Bit 5: a kill is due (console: SER_BREAK)
000036r 2               TASK_RUN_MASK           = TASK_BUSY_FLAG | TASK_PAUSED_FLAG | TASK_WAITING_FLAG | TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG
000036r 2               TASK_FRAME_SP           = $F4                       ; STACK_SAVE_REG of a new task (11-byte frame at $01F5)
000036r 2               SCHED_RESCHED_A         = $A5                       ; An IRQ handler returns C = 1, .A and .Y = these
000036r 2               SCHED_RESCHED_Y         = $5A                       ;   to ask for a task switch (the timer tick)
000036r 2               
000036r 2               ; Switch tasks.  IRQs off, and the current task's full frame (including U) on its stack.
000036r 2               SCHED_SWITCH:
000036r 2  BA                       tsx
000037r 2  86 rr                    stx     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; The current task's SP
000039r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     SCHED_PICK                      ; .A = next task (maybe the same one)
00003Cr 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Its ZP and stack
00003Fr 2  A6 rr                    ldx     STACK_SAVE_REG
000041r 2  9A                       txs
000042r 2               
000042r 2               ; Unwind a task's frame and continue it; or, if a break or kill is due, continue it at BREAK_ENTRY
000042r 2               ; instead (on ROM page 0, IRQs on, U = 0)
000042r 2               SCHED_RESUME:
000042r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
000044r 2  29 30                    and     #TASK_BREAK_FLAG | TASK_KILL_FLAG
000046r 2  F0 14                    beq     @resume
000048r 2  BA                       tsx                                     ; The frame: $0101,X = U ... $010B,X = PCH
000049r 2  9E 01 01                 stz     $0101,X                         ; U
00004Cr 2  9E 06 01                 stz     $0106,X                         ; W
00004Fr 2  9E 09 01                 stz     $0109,X                         ; P
000052r 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<BREAK_ENTRY
000054r 2  9D 0A 01                 sta     $010A,X
000057r 2  A9 rr                    lda     #>BREAK_ENTRY
000059r 2  9D 0B 01                 sta     $010B,X
00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               @resume:
00005Cr 2  68                       pla
00005Dr 2  8D F1 FF                 sta     U_REGISTER
000060r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     IRQ_RESTORE                     ; The rest is the IRQ frame (irq.s)
000063r 2               
000063r 2               ; Pick the next task to run: the next runnable task after the current one (1-15, round-robin); else
000063r 2               ; the current one if it's runnable; else task 0 (idle).  No stack use while looking at other tasks.
000063r 2               ; OUT: .A = task
000063r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000063r 2               SCHED_PICK:
000063r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
000066r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
000068r 2  A8                       tay                                     ; .Y = current task
000069r 2  AA                       tax                                     ; .X = candidate
00006Ar 2  A9 0F                    lda     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00006Cr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_SCHED_CNT
00006Er 2               
00006Er 2               @next:
00006Er 2  E8                       inx
00006Fr 2  8A                       txa
000070r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
000072r 2  AA                       tax
000073r 2  F0 0E                    beq     @skip                           ; Task 0 isn't in the rotation
000075r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick look at the candidate (no stack use!)
000078r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
00007Ar 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00007Dr 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
00007Fr 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
000081r 2  F0 11                    beq     @found
000083r 2               
000083r 2               @skip:
000083r 2  C6 rr                    dec     ZP_SCHED_CNT
000085r 2  D0 E7                    bne     @next
000087r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Nobody else: keep going if we can
000089r 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
00008Br 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
00008Dr 2  F0 03                    beq     @stay
00008Fr 2  A9 00                    lda     #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM                ; Idle
000091r 2  60                       rts
000092r 2               
000092r 2               @stay:
000092r 2  98                       tya
000093r 2  60                       rts
000094r 2               
000094r 2               @found:
000094r 2  8A                       txa
000095r 2  60                       rts
000096r 2               
000096r 2               ; Can the interrupted (current) task be preempted?  Not if it's running a TASK_CALL routine for another
000096r 2               ; task (a guest), isn't runnable (e.g. a resident driver task), or holds NO_PREEMPT (then the switch is
000096r 2               ; noted, for PREEMPT).
000096r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 switch, C = 0 don't
000096r 2               SCHED_CAN_PREEMPT:
000096r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_GUEST
000098r 2  D0 10                    bne     @no
00009Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
00009Cr 2  29 0F                    and     #TASK_RUN_MASK
00009Er 2  C9 01                    cmp     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
0000A0r 2  D0 08                    bne     @no
0000A2r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000A4r 2  F0 06                    beq     @yes
0000A6r 2  A9 01                    lda     #1
0000A8r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE                  ; Switch at PREEMPT
0000AAr 2               
0000AAr 2               @no:
0000AAr 2  18                       clc
0000ABr 2  60                       rts
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               @yes:
0000ACr 2  38                       sec
0000ADr 2  60                       rts
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               ; Give up the CPU: switch to the next runnable task.  Returns when this task is picked again (at once
0000AEr 2               ; if nothing else can run).
0000AEr 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y and the flags
0000AEr 2               YIELD:
0000AEr 2  08                       php
0000AFr 2  78                       sei
0000B0r 2  48                       pha                                     ; Build the frame an IRQ would: P is there already,
0000B1r 2  DA                       phx                                     ;   under it the return address (made RTI-style below)
0000B2r 2  BA                       tsx
0000B3r 2  FE 04 01                 inc     $0104,X                         ; Return address (S+4 = PCL, S+5 = PCH) + 1
0000B6r 2  D0 03                    bne     :+
0000B8r 2  FE 05 01                 inc     $0105,X
0000BBr 2               :
0000BBr 2  AD F3 FF                 lda     W_REGISTER
0000BEr 2  48                       pha
0000BFr 2  5A                       phy
0000C0r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_VEC
0000C2r 2  48                       pha
0000C3r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000C5r 2  48                       pha
0000C6r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_TASK
0000C8r 2  48                       pha
0000C9r 2  AD F1 FF                 lda     U_REGISTER
0000CCr 2  48                       pha
0000CDr 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
0000CFr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     SCHED_SWITCH
0000D2r 2               
0000D2r 2               ; Hold the CPU: no task switches until the matching PREEMPT (nestable).  IRQs and their handlers keep
0000D2r 2               ; running.  For short sections, sei / cli also works (but holds off all IRQs).
0000D2r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000D2r 2               NO_PREEMPT:
0000D2r 2  E6 rr                    inc     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000D4r 2  60                       rts
0000D5r 2               
0000D5r 2               ; Undo NO_PREEMPT; if a task switch came due meanwhile (and this was the outermost), switch now.
0000D5r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000D5r 2               PREEMPT:
0000D5r 2  08                       php
0000D6r 2  78                       sei
0000D7r 2  48                       pha
0000D8r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000DAr 2  F0 0D                    beq     @done                           ; Not holding it
0000DCr 2  C6 rr                    dec     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
0000DEr 2  D0 09                    bne     @done                           ; Still nested
0000E0r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
0000E2r 2  F0 05                    beq     @done
0000E4r 2  68                       pla
0000E5r 2  28                       plp
0000E6r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     YIELD                           ; (YIELD clears ZP_PREEMPT_DUE)
0000E9r 2               
0000E9r 2               @done:
0000E9r 2  68                       pla
0000EAr 2  28                       plp
0000EBr 2  60                       rts
0000ECr 2               
0000ECr 2               ; Wait for IO: the task isn't run again until IO_WAKE.
0000ECr 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000ECr 2               TASK_WAIT:
0000ECr 2  08                       php
0000EDr 2  78                       sei
0000EEr 2  A7 rr                    smb2    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; TASK_WAITING_FLAG
0000F0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     YIELD
0000F3r 2  28                       plp
0000F4r 2  60                       rts
0000F5r 2               
0000F5r 2               ; Let a task waiting for IO (TASK_WAIT) run again.  Can be called from IRQ handlers.
0000F5r 2               ; IN: .A = task
0000F5r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0000F5r 2               IO_WAKE:
0000F5r 2  08                       php
0000F6r 2  78                       sei
0000F7r 2  5A                       phy
0000F8r 2  48                       pha
0000F9r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
0000FCr 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
0000FEr 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the task (no stack use!)
000101r 2  27 rr                    rmb2    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; TASK_WAITING_FLAG
000103r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000106r 2  68                       pla
000107r 2  7A                       ply
000108r 2  28                       plp
000109r 2  60                       rts
00010Ar 2               
00010Ar 2               ; A task's status (TASK_STATUS_REG).
00010Ar 2               ; IN: .A = task.  OUT: .A = status
00010Ar 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00010Ar 2               TASK_STATUS:
00010Ar 2  08                       php
00010Br 2  78                       sei
00010Cr 2  5A                       phy
00010Dr 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
000110r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
000112r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the task (no stack use!)
000115r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
000117r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00011Ar 2  7A                       ply
00011Br 2  28                       plp
00011Cr 2  60                       rts
00011Dr 2               
00011Dr 2               ; Start the scheduler's tick: VIA T1 free-running, one IRQ every TIMER_TASK_INT_H/L cycles (~5 ms).
00011Dr 2               ; VIA_IRQ_HANDLER turns each tick into a task switch.
00011Dr 2               SCHED_START:
00011Dr 2  08                       php
00011Er 2  78                       sei
00011Fr 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
000122r 2  29 3F                    and     #$3F
000124r 2  09 40                    ora     #$40                            ; T1 continuous, no PB7 output
000126r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
000129r 2  A9 A9                    lda     #TIMER_TASK_INT_L
00012Br 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_T1C_L                     ; Latch low
00012Er 2  A9 45                    lda     #TIMER_TASK_INT_H
000130r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_T1C_H                     ; Latch high, load and start
000133r 2  A9 C0                    lda     #VIA_INT_ENABLE | VIA_T1_INT_BIT
000135r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000138r 2  28                       plp
000139r 2  60                       rts
00013Ar 2               
00013Ar 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00013Ar 2               ; Starting tasks
00013Ar 2
