00013Ar 2               ; Build a new task's starting frame: it starts in TASK_TRAMPOLINE, which calls its entry point (on ROM
00013Ar 2               ; page ZP_TEMP) and ends the task when that returns.  It gets copies of the current task's open fds.
00013Ar 2               ; IRQs must be off; .X must not be the current task.
00013Ar 2               ; IN: ZP_TEMP_VEC = entry point, ZP_TEMP = its ROM page, .X = task
00013Ar 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
00013Ar 2               TASK_BUILD_FRAME:
00013Ar 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER                      ; .Y = current task, .X = new task
00013Dr 2               
00013Dr 2               ; !! NO STACK MANIPULATIONS WHILE IN THE NEW TASK !!
00013Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TEMP_VEC
00013Fr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER
000142r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TASK_ENTRY
000144r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000147r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000149r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER
00014Cr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TASK_ENTRY + 1
00014Er 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000151r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TEMP
000153r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER
000156r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TASK_PAGE
000158r 2  A9 rr                    lda     #>TASK_TRAMPOLINE               ; RTI frame: PCH, PCL, P (IRQs on)
00015Ar 2  8D FF 01                 sta     $01FF
00015Dr 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<TASK_TRAMPOLINE
00015Fr 2  8D FE 01                 sta     $01FE
000162r 2  A9 00                    lda     #0
000164r 2  8D FD 01                 sta     $01FD                           ; P
000167r 2  8D FC 01                 sta     $01FC                           ; A
00016Ar 2  8D FB 01                 sta     $01FB                           ; X
00016Dr 2  8D FA 01                 sta     $01FA                           ; W
000170r 2  8D F9 01                 sta     $01F9                           ; Y
000173r 2  8D F8 01                 sta     $01F8                           ; ZP_TC_VEC
000176r 2  8D F7 01                 sta     $01F7                           ; ZP_TC_VEC + 1
000179r 2  8D F6 01                 sta     $01F6                           ; ZP_TC_TASK
00017Cr 2  8D F5 01                 sta     $01F5                           ; U
00017Fr 2  A9 F4                    lda     #TASK_FRAME_SP
000181r 2  85 rr                    sta     STACK_SAVE_REG
000183r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_NO_PREEMPT
000185r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
000187r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_TC_GUEST
000189r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
00018Br 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1                ; No break handler
00018Dr 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TASK_OWNER                   ; Started by the current task
00018Fr 2  A9 FF                    lda     #$FF
000191r 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_PARENT                     ; No parent to wake (TASK_START sets one)
000193r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Back to the current task
000196r 2  8A                       txa
000197r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     IO_INHERIT                      ; The fds (preserves .X)
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; Every task started with TASK_BUILD_FRAME begins here (ROM page 0, IRQs on)
00019Ar 2               TASK_TRAMPOLINE:
00019Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TASK_ENTRY
00019Cr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_VEC
00019Er 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TASK_ENTRY + 1
0001A0r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
0001A2r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TASK_PAGE
0001A4r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_PAGE
0001A6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     FAR_CALL_A                      ; Run the task
0001A9r 2               
0001A9r 2               ; The task's entry point returned: free everything it had, wake its parent, and never run again
0001A9r 2               TASK_EXIT:
0001A9r 2  78                       sei
0001AAr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
0001ADr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     MM_TASK_RESET
0001B0r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_PARENT
0001B2r 2  C9 10                    cmp     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
0001B4r 2  B0 0B                    bcs     @no_parent
0001B6r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
0001B9r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the parent (no stack use!)
0001BCr 2  17 rr                    rmb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; TASK_PAUSED_FLAG: it's waiting for us (TASK_START)
0001BEr 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
0001C1r 2               
0001C1r 2               @no_parent:
0001C1r 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Free: never picked again
0001C3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     YIELD
0001C6r 2               
0001C6r 2               @halt:
0001C6r 2  80 FE                    bra     @halt                           ; (not reached)
0001C8r 2               
0001C8r 2               ; A break or kill from the console (SER_BREAK flags the task; SCHED_RESUME continues it here, on ROM page
0001C8r 2               ; 0 with IRQs on, instead of where it was).  A break goes to the task's break handler (TASK_SET_BREAK),
0001C8r 2               ; with the stack pointer it had then; a kill, or a break without a handler, ends the task, except the
0001C8r 2               ; shell, which starts again from scratch.
0001C8r 2               BREAK_ENTRY:
0001C8r 2  78                       sei
0001C9r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
0001CBr 2  AA                       tax
0001CCr 2  29 CB                    and     #<~(TASK_BREAK_FLAG | TASK_KILL_FLAG | TASK_WAITING_FLAG)
0001CEr 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; (It may have been waiting for IO)
0001D0r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_NO_PREEMPT                   ; (Or holding the CPU, e.g. in IO_SERVE)
0001D2r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_PREEMPT_DUE
0001D4r 2  64 rr                    stz     RAM_BANK_REG                    ; (Or in the middle of a bank switch)
0001D6r 2  58                       cli
0001D7r 2  8A                       txa
0001D8r 2  29 20                    and     #TASK_KILL_FLAG
0001DAr 2  D0 16                    bne     @kill
0001DCr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1
0001DEr 2  F0 12                    beq     @kill                           ; No handler
0001E0r 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_BREAK_SP
0001E2r 2  9A                       txs
0001E3r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_BREAK_VEC
0001E5r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_VEC
0001E7r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1
0001E9r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_VEC + 1
0001EBr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_BREAK_PAGE
0001EDr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_FAR_PAGE
0001EFr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     FAR_JUMP
0001F2r 2               
0001F2r 2               @kill:
0001F2r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
0001F5r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
0001F7r 2  C9 01                    cmp     #SHELL_TASK_NUM
0001F9r 2  F0 03                    beq     @shell
0001FBr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     TASK_EXIT
0001FEr 2               
0001FEr 2               @shell:                                     ; The shell: free everything, and start it again
0001FEr 2  A2 FF                    ldx     #$FF
000200r 2  9A                       txs
000201r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     MM_TASK_RESET                   ; (Its fds and namespace too)
000204r 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1
000206r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     TASK_TRAMPOLINE                 ; (Its entry point is still SHELL_MAIN)
000209r 2               
000209r 2               ; Set the current task's break handler: where a break from the console (SER_KEY_BREAK) sends it, with
000209r 2               ; the stack pointer it has now (the handler never returns).  .A.Y = 0: no handler (a break ends the task).
000209r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = handler, .X = its ROM page.  Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000209r 2               TASK_SET_BREAK:
000209r 2  08                       php
00020Ar 2  78                       sei
00020Br 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_BREAK_VEC
00020Dr 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_BREAK_VEC + 1
00020Fr 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_BREAK_PAGE
000211r 2  DA                       phx
000212r 2  BA                       tsx
000213r 2  E8                       inx                                     ; (The phx, the php and the return address)
000214r 2  E8                       inx
000215r 2  E8                       inx
000216r 2  E8                       inx
000217r 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_BREAK_SP
000219r 2  FA                       plx
00021Ar 2  28                       plp
00021Br 2  60                       rts
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 2               ; Start a task in the background: it runs alongside the caller, and ends when its entry point returns.
00021Cr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = entry point, .X = its ROM page (0 for RAM or page 0 code)
00021Cr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = task, C = 0
00021Cr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE, C = 1
00021Cr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00021Cr 2               TASK_RUN:
00021Cr 2  08                       php
00021Dr 2  78                       sei
00021Er 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000220r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TEMP_VEC
000222r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000224r 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_TEMP
000226r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     RESERVE_TASK                    ; C = 1: .A = task (busy + paused)
000229r 2  90 13                    bcc     @none
00022Br 2  AA                       tax
00022Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     TASK_BUILD_FRAME
00022Fr 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
000232r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the new task (no stack use!)
000235r 2  17 rr                    rmb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Runnable
000237r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00023Ar 2  8A                       txa
00023Br 2  18                       clc
00023Cr 2  80 03                    bra     @done
00023Er 2               
00023Er 2               @none:
00023Er 2  A9 F1                    lda     #ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE
000240r 2  38                       sec
000241r 2               
000241r 2               @done:
000241r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000243r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
000246r 2               
000246r 2               ; Let a task that RESERVE_TASK left paused run (TASK_CLONE sets it up first).  IN: .A = task
000246r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000246r 2               TASK_GO:
000246r 2  08                       php
000247r 2  78                       sei
000248r 2  5A                       phy
000249r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
00024Cr 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the task (no stack use!)
00024Fr 2  17 rr                    rmb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Runnable
000251r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000254r 2  7A                       ply
000255r 2  28                       plp
000256r 2  60                       rts
000257r 2               
000257r 2               ; .A.Y: Address of task entrypoint
000257r 2               SPAWN_TASK:
000257r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TEMP_VEC
000259r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00025Br 2               
00025Br 2               ; Start a task at the address in ZP_TEMP_VEC (RAM or ROM page 0), and wait for it to finish.
00025Br 2               ; Return task # in A and C == 1
00025Br 2               ;   OR error in A and C == 0 (if no task available)
00025Br 2               TASK_START:
00025Br 2  08                       php
00025Cr 2  78                       sei
00025Dr 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_TEMP                         ; ROM page 0
00025Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     RESERVE_TASK                    ; C = 1: .A = task (busy + paused)
000262r 2  B0 05                    bcs     @start_task
000264r 2  28                       plp
000265r 2  A9 F1                    lda     #ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE
000267r 2  18                       clc
000268r 2  60                       rts
000269r 2               
000269r 2               @start_task:
000269r 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_SAVE_REG                   ; Child task #, returned when it's done
00026Br 2  AA                       tax
00026Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     TASK_BUILD_FRAME
00026Fr 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
000272r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the child (no stack use!)
000275r 2  84 rr                    sty     TASK_PARENT                     ; It wakes us when it's done (TASK_EXIT)
000277r 2  17 rr                    rmb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Runnable
000279r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00027Cr 2  97 rr                    smb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; We're paused until then
00027Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     YIELD
000281r 2  28                       plp
000282r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_SAVE_REG
000284r 2  38                       sec
000285r 2  60                       rts
000286r 2               
000286r 2               
000286r 2               ; Find an available task
000286r 2               ; Modifies: A, CNZ Flags
000286r 2               ; Returns C = 1 AND A = TaskNumber (when found)
000286r 2               ; Returns C = 0 AND A = $FF        (when not found)
000286r 2               RESERVE_TASK:
000286r 2  08                       php                                     ; Save caller's I flag
000287r 2  78                       sei                                     ; Disable interrupts
000288r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00028Ar 2               
00028Ar 2               ; !! NO STACK MANIPULATIONS UNTIL SWITCHING BACK TO ORIGINAL TASK !!
00028Ar 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
00028Dr 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #$F                             ; Start search with Task $F
00028Fr 2               
00028Fr 2               @task_busy:
00028Fr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick task switch to task in .X
000292r 2  0F rr 07                 bbr0    TASK_STATUS_REG, @task_found    ; Is Bit 0 (TASK_BUSY_FLAG) reset/clear?
000295r 2  CA                       dex                                     ; Not found, so check next
000296r 2  D0 F7                    bne     @task_busy                      ; Until we reach the system task (0), loop
000298r 2  18                       clc                                     ; Not found
000299r 2  CA                       dex                                     ; .X == $FF
00029Ar 2  80 05                    bra     @cleanup
00029Cr 2               
00029Cr 2               @task_found:
00029Cr 2  87 rr                    smb0    TASK_STATUS_REG
00029Er 2  97 rr                    smb1    TASK_STATUS_REG
0002A0r 2  38                       sec                                     ; Found
0002A1r 2               
0002A1r 2               @cleanup:
0002A1r 2  8A                       txa                                     ; Return the task number in A (OR $FF if not found)
0002A2r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Switch back to the original task
0002A5r 2               
0002A5r 2               ; Back on the original task, so restore the registers
0002A5r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0002A7r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN                       ; Restore caller's I flag, keep C
0002AAr 2               
0002AAr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0002AAr 2               ; Run a routine in another task's context: its ZP, stack (below its saved SP), RAM bank and MMU area.
0002AAr 2               ; Used by the IRQ dispatcher and DRV_START, and by gates into driver tasks.
0002AAr 2               ;
0002AAr 2               ; IN:  ZP_TC_VEC = routine, ZP_TC_TASK = task to run it in, .A/.X/.Y/C = routine's inputs
0002AAr 2               ; OUT: .A/.X/.Y/flags as returned by the routine
0002AAr 2               ; The routine runs with the caller's I flag.  The target task must not be running, or it's the
0002AAr 2               ; current task (then this is a plain call).
0002AAr 2               ; The calling task # is kept on the target task's stack, so IRQs during the routine are safe.
0002AAr 2               .macro _M_TC_COPY_TO    zp                  ; ZP byte: calling task (.X) -> target task (.Y); ends in calling task
0002AAr 2                           lda     zp
0002AAr 2                           sty     T_REGISTER
0002AAr 2                           sta     zp
0002AAr 2                           stx     T_REGISTER
0002AAr 2               .endmacro
0002AAr 2               
0002AAr 2               .macro _M_TC_COPY_BACK  zp                  ; ZP byte: target task (.Y) -> calling task (.X); ends in target task
0002AAr 2                           lda     zp
0002AAr 2                           stx     T_REGISTER
0002AAr 2                           sta     zp
0002AAr 2                           sty     T_REGISTER
0002AAr 2               .endmacro
0002AAr 2               
0002AAr 2               TASK_CALL:
0002AAr 2  08                       php
0002ABr 2  78                       sei
0002ACr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_A
0002AEr 2  68                       pla
0002AFr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_P                         ; Caller's flags (C in, I state)
0002B1r 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_TC_X
0002B3r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TC_Y
0002B5r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_TASK
0002B7r 2  CD F0 FF                 cmp     T_REGISTER
0002BAr 2  D0 09                    bne     @switch
0002BCr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_P                         ; Same task: restore flags and tail-call
0002BEr 2  48                       pha
0002BFr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_A
0002C1r 2  28                       plp
0002C2r 2  6C rr rr                 jmp     (ZP_TC_VEC)
0002C5r 2               
0002C5r 2               @switch:
0002C5r 2  A5 rr                    lda     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; Preserve the calling task's saved SP
0002C7r 2  48                       pha
0002C8r 2  BA                       tsx
0002C9r 2  86 rr                    stx     STACK_SAVE_REG
0002CBr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
0002CEr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_FROM
0002D0r 2  AA                       tax                                     ; .X = calling task
0002D1r 2  A4 rr                    ldy     ZP_TC_TASK                      ; .Y = target task
0002D3r 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_VEC
0002D7r 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
0002DBr 2  F0 FF        
0002DDr 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0002E1r 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
0002E5r 2  F0 FF        
0002E7r 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_A
0002EBr 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
0002EFr 2  F0 FF        
0002F1r 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_X
0002F5r 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
0002F9r 2  F0 FF        
0002FBr 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_Y
0002FFr 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
000303r 2  F0 FF        
000305r 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_P
000309r 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
00030Dr 2  F0 FF        
00030Fr 2  A5 rr 8C F0              _M_TC_COPY_TO   ZP_TC_FROM
000313r 2  FF 85 rr 8E  
000317r 2  F0 FF        
000319r 2               
000319r 2               ; !! NO STACK MANIPULATIONS UNTIL THE TARGET TASK'S STACK IS SELECTED !!
000319r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Switch to the target task
00031Cr 2  A6 rr                    ldx     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; ...and its stack
00031Er 2  9A                       txs
00031Fr 2  E6 rr                    inc     ZP_TC_GUEST                     ; Running for another task: the scheduler mustn't switch
000321r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_FROM
000323r 2  48                       pha                                     ; Keep the calling task # on the target's stack
000324r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_P
000326r 2  48                       pha
000327r 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_TC_X
000329r 2  A4 rr                    ldy     ZP_TC_Y
00032Br 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_A
00032Dr 2  28                       plp                                     ; Caller's flags
00032Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     @call
000331r 2  08                       php
000332r 2  78                       sei
000333r 2  C6 rr                    dec     ZP_TC_GUEST
000335r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_A
000337r 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_TC_X
000339r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TC_Y
00033Br 2  68                       pla
00033Cr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_P                         ; Routine's result flags
00033Er 2  68                       pla
00033Fr 2  AA                       tax                                     ; .X = calling task
000340r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER                      ; .Y = target task
000343r 2  A5 rr 8E F0              _M_TC_COPY_BACK ZP_TC_A
000347r 2  FF 85 rr 8C  
00034Br 2  F0 FF        
00034Dr 2  A5 rr 8E F0              _M_TC_COPY_BACK ZP_TC_X
000351r 2  FF 85 rr 8C  
000355r 2  F0 FF        
000357r 2  A5 rr 8E F0              _M_TC_COPY_BACK ZP_TC_Y
00035Br 2  FF 85 rr 8C  
00035Fr 2  F0 FF        
000361r 2  A5 rr 8E F0              _M_TC_COPY_BACK ZP_TC_P
000365r 2  FF 85 rr 8C  
000369r 2  F0 FF        
00036Br 2               
00036Br 2               ; !! NO STACK MANIPULATIONS UNTIL THE CALLING TASK'S STACK IS SELECTED !!
00036Br 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Back to the calling task
00036Er 2  A6 rr                    ldx     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; ...and its stack
000370r 2  9A                       txs
000371r 2  68                       pla
000372r 2  85 rr                    sta     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; Restore the calling task's saved SP
000374r 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_TC_X
000376r 2  A4 rr                    ldy     ZP_TC_Y
000378r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_P
00037Ar 2  48                       pha
00037Br 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_A
00037Dr 2  28                       plp                                     ; Routine's result flags
00037Er 2  60                       rts
00037Fr 2               
00037Fr 2               @call:
00037Fr 2  6C rr rr                 jmp     (ZP_TC_VEC)
000382r 2               
000382r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000382r 2               ; Make a free task (a specific one) ready to run from an entry point on ROM page 0: marks it busy and
000382r 2               ; runnable, so the scheduler starts it.  (The shell task at boot.)
000382r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = entry point, .X = task#
000382r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000382r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_BAD_TASK or ERR_TASK_BUSY, C = 1
000382r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
000382r 2               TASK_PREPARE:
000382r 2  08                       php                                     ; Save caller's I flag
000383r 2  78                       sei
000384r 2  E0 10                    cpx     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
000386r 2  B0 32                    bcs     @bad_task
000388r 2  EC F0 FF                 cpx     T_REGISTER
00038Br 2  F0 2D                    beq     @bad_task
00038Dr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TEMP_VEC
00038Fr 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000391r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER                      ; .Y = calling task, .X = new task
000394r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick look at the new task (no stack use!)
000397r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
000399r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
00039Cr 2  D0 16                    bne     @busy
00039Er 2  64 rr                    stz     ZP_TEMP                         ; ROM page 0
0003A0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     TASK_BUILD_FRAME
0003A3r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
0003A6r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the new task (no stack use!)
0003A9r 2  A9 01                    lda     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG                 ; Busy and runnable
0003ABr 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG
0003ADr 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Back to the calling task
0003B0r 2  18                       clc
0003B1r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
0003B4r 2               
0003B4r 2               @busy:
0003B4r 2  A9 F2                    lda     #ERR_TASK_BUSY
0003B6r 2  38                       sec
0003B7r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
0003BAr 2               
0003BAr 2               @bad_task:
0003BAr 2  A9 F3                    lda     #ERR_BAD_TASK
0003BCr 2  38                       sec
0003BDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
0003C0r 2               
0003C0r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0003C0r 2               ; Drivers
0003C0r 2               
0003C0r 2               .struct     DriverInfo
0003C0r 2                           init        .word                       ; Runs in the driver's task.  OUT: C = 0 OK, or C = 1 and .A = error
0003C0r 2                           stop        .word                       ; (future) Runs in the driver's task before the task is reset
0003C0r 2                           name        .word                       ; HString
0003C0r 2               .endstruct
0003C0r 2               
0003C0r 2               ; Start a driver in a (free) task, as a Resident task: its init runs in that task, so the driver's
0003C0r 2               ; state lives in that task's ZP/RAM, and IRQ handlers it registers run in that task.
0003C0r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = DriverInfo, .X = task#
0003C0r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = task#, C = 0
0003C0r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1 (the task is left free)
0003C0r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0003C0r 2               DRV_START:
0003C0r 2  08                       php                                     ; Save caller's I flag
0003C1r 2  78                       sei
0003C2r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_DRV_PTR
0003C4r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_DRV_PTR + 1
0003C6r 2  E0 10                    cpx     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER + 1
0003C8r 2  B0 4A                    bcs     @bad_task
0003CAr 2  EC F0 FF                 cpx     T_REGISTER
0003CDr 2  F0 45                    beq     @bad_task
0003CFr 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_TC_TASK
0003D1r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
0003D4r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the driver task (no stack use!)
0003D7r 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_STATUS_REG
0003D9r 2  D0 30                    bne     @busy
0003DBr 2  A9 09                    lda     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG | TASK_RESIDENT_FLAG
0003DDr 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG
0003DFr 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Back to the calling task
0003E2r 2  A0 00                    ldy     #DriverInfo::init
0003E4r 2  B1 rr                    lda     (ZP_DRV_PTR),Y
0003E6r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_VEC
0003E8r 2  C8                       iny
0003E9r 2  B1 rr                    lda     (ZP_DRV_PTR),Y
0003EBr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0003EDr 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_TASK                      ; init gets its task# in .A
0003EFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     TASK_CALL
0003F2r 2  B0 06                    bcs     @init_failed
0003F4r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_TC_TASK
0003F6r 2  18                       clc
0003F7r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
0003FAr 2               
0003FAr 2               @init_failed:
0003FAr 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_TC_TASK
0003FCr 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
0003FFr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to the driver task (no stack use!)
000402r 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Leave it free
000404r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000407r 2  38                       sec                                     ; .A = error from init
000408r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
00040Br 2               
00040Br 2               @busy:
00040Br 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Back to the calling task
00040Er 2  A9 F2                    lda     #ERR_TASK_BUSY
000410r 2  38                       sec
000411r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
000414r 2               
000414r 2               @bad_task:
000414r 2  A9 F3                    lda     #ERR_BAD_TASK
000416r 2  38                       sec
000417r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     MM_RETURN
00041Ar 2               
00041Ar 2               ; Non-maskable interrupt handler, called from NMI_ENTRY (COMMON block) on ROM page 0
00041Ar 2               NMI_HANDLER:
00041Ar 2  60                       rts
00041Br 2               
00041Br 1               .include "wozmon.s"
00041Br 2               ;
00041Br 2               ;   Taken mostly from Steve Wozniak's Apple 1 Monitor for the 6502, or WOZMON
00041Br 2               ;
00041Br 2               .debuginfo
00041Br 2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000400r 2               IN:
000400r 2  xx xx xx xx                  .res            $100
000404r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000408r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000500r 2               
000500r 2               .segment "WOZMON"
000000r 2               ; WOZMON Entrypoint
000000r 2               MON_START:
000000r 2  D8                           cld                             ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
000001r 2  58                           cli                             ; Enable interrupts
000002r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_D_STATE      ; Byte mode, not DISAM mode
000004r 2  80 0B                        bra             @is_start
000006r 2               
000006r 2               @not_cr:
000006r 2  C9 08                        cmp             #ASCII_BACKSPACE
000008r 2  F0 0F                        beq             @is_backspace
00000Ar 2  C9 1B                        cmp             #ASCII_ESC
00000Cr 2  F0 03                        beq             @is_start
00000Er 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00000Fr 2  10 0B                        bpl             @get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
000011r 2               
000011r 2               @is_start:
000011r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
000014r 2               
000014r 2               @get_line:
000014r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_PROMPT
000017r 2  A0 01                        ldy             #1              ; Initialize text index.
000019r 2               
000019r 2               @is_backspace:
000019r 2  88                           dey                             ; Back up text index.
00001Ar 2  30 F8                        bmi             @get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
00001Cr 2               
00001Cr 2               @get_next_char:
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             GET_CHAR        ; (Sleeps until a key comes in)
00001Fr 2  90 FB                        bcc             @get_next_char
000021r 2  99 rr rr                     sta             IN,y            ; Add to text buffer.
000024r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #ASCII_CR
000026r 2  D0 DE                        bne             @not_cr
000028r 2  A0 FF                        ldy             #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
00002Ar 2  A9 00                        lda             #$00            ; For ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00002Cr 2  AA                           tax                             ; .X=0.
00002Dr 2               
00002Dr 2               @set_block:
00002Dr 2  0A                           asl
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2               @set_store:
00002Er 2  0A                           asl                             ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
00002Fr 2               
00002Fr 2               @set_mode:
00002Fr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_WM_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_WM_XAM.
000031r 2               
000031r 2               @skip_delim:
000031r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000032r 2               
000032r 2               @next_item:
000032r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character.
000035r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #ASCII_CR       ; CR?
000037r 2  F0 DB                        beq             @get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000039r 2  C9 2E                        cmp             #ASCII_PERIOD
00003Br 2  90 F4                        bcc             @skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
00003Dr 2  F0 EE                        beq             @set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00003Fr 2  C9 3A                        cmp             #ASCII_COLON
000041r 2  F0 EB                        beq             @set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
000043r 2  C9 4D                        cmp             #ASCII_M
000045r 2  10 0A                        bpl             @check_rtow
000047r 2  C9 4B                        cmp             #ASCII_K
000049r 2  30 2B                        bmi             @not_tuvw
00004Br 2  49 4B                        eor             #ASCII_K        ; 'K' resets DASTATE to 0
00004Dr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_D_STATE
00004Fr 2  80 E0                        bra             @skip_delim
000051r 2               
000051r 2               @check_rtow:
000051r 2  C9 52                        cmp             #ASCII_R
000053r 2  F0 5B                        beq             @run_prog
000055r 2  90 1F                        bcc             @not_tuvw
000057r 2  C9 58                        cmp             #ASCII_X        ; R, S, T, U, V, or W
000059r 2  B0 1B                        bcs             @not_tuvw
00005Br 2  C9 53                        cmp             #ASCII_S
00005Dr 2  D0 09                        bne             @not_spawn
00005Fr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
000061r 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
000063r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SPAWN_TASK
000066r 2  80 98                        bra             MON_START
000068r 2               
000068r 2               @not_spawn:
000068r 2  18                           clc
000069r 2  69 9C                        adc             #($F0 - ASCII_T); T=FFF0, U=FFF1, V=FFF2, W=FFF3
00006Br 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP       ;
00006Dr 2  A9 FF                        lda             #$FF            ;
00006Fr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ;
000071r 2  C8                           iny                             ; skip the mnemonic
000072r 2  80 2C                        bra             @not_hex_or_escape
000074r 2               
000074r 2               @bra_is_start:
000074r 2  80 9B                        bra             @is_start       ; unrecognized
000076r 2               
000076r 2               @not_tuvw:
000076r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000078r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
00007Ar 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
00007Cr 2               
00007Cr 2               @next_hex:
00007Cr 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character for hex test.
00007Fr 2  49 30                        eor             #ASCII_0        ; Map digits to $0-9.
000081r 2  C9 0A                        cmp             #$0A            ; Digit?
000083r 2  90 06                        bcc             @is_digit       ; Yes.
000085r 2  69 88                        adc             #$88            ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000087r 2  C9 FA                        cmp             #$FA            ; Hex letter?
000089r 2  90 11                        bcc             @not_hex        ; No, character not hex.
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2               @is_digit:
00008Br 2  0A                           asl                             ; LSD to MSD of A.
00008Cr 2  0A                           asl
00008Dr 2  0A                           asl
00008Er 2  0A                           asl
00008Fr 2  A2 04                        ldx             #4              ; Shift count.
000091r 2               
000091r 2               @hex_shift:
000091r 2  0A                           asl                             ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
000092r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
000094r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
000096r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Done 4 shifts?
000097r 2  D0 F8                        bne             @hex_shift      ; No, loop.
000099r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00009Ar 2  D0 E0                        bne             @next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
00009Cr 2               
00009Cr 2               @not_hex:
00009Cr 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
00009Er 2  F0 D4                        beq             @bra_is_start   ; Yes, back to start.
0000A0r 2               
0000A0r 2               @not_hex_or_escape:
0000A0r 2  24 rr                        bit             ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
0000A2r 2  50 15                        bvc             @not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is XAM and BLOCK XAM.
0000A4r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
0000A6r 2  92 rr                        sta             (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
0000A8r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
0000AAr 2  D0 86                        bne             @next_item      ; Get next item (no carry).
0000ACr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               @to_next_item:
0000AEr 2  80 82                        bra             @next_item      ; Get next command item.
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               @run_prog:
0000B0r 2  A9 rr 48 A9                  _M_JSRR         ZP_WM_XAM, MON_START
0000B4r 2  rr 48 6C rr  
0000B8r 2  rr           
0000B9r 2               
0000B9r 2               @not_store:
0000B9r 2  30 1A                        bmi             @examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for XAM, 1 for BLOCK XAM.
0000BBr 2  A2 02                        ldx             #2              ; Byte count.
0000BDr 2               
0000BDr 2               @set_addr:
0000BDr 2  B5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
0000BFr 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0000C1r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM - 1,x ; And to 'ZP_WM_XAM index'.
0000C3r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Next of 2 bytes.
0000C4r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0000C6r 2               
0000C6r 2               @print_next_addr:
0000C6r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DISASM_WM       ; DISASM
0000C9r 2  80 0A                        bra             @examine_next
0000CBr 2               
0000CBr 2               @print_data:
0000CBr 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
0000CFr 2  rr           
0000D0r 2  B2 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      {(ZP_WM_XAM)}
0000D4r 2  rr           
0000D5r 2               
0000D5r 2               @examine_next:
0000D5r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (XAM mode).
0000D7r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000D9r 2  C5 rr                        cmp             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0000DBr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000DDr 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0000DFr 2  B0 CD                        bcs             @to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0000E1r 2  38                           sec
0000E2r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000E4r 2  65 rr                        adc             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E6r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E8r 2  90 02                        bcc             :+
0000EAr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000ECr 2               
0000ECr 2               :
0000ECr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_STATE      ; if disassembling, always print the address
0000EEr 2  D0 D6                        bne             @print_next_addr
0000F0r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM       ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0000F2r 2  29 07                        and             #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0000F4r 2  F0 D0                        beq             @print_next_addr
0000F6r 2  80 D3                        bra             @print_data
0000F8r 2               
0000F8r 1               .include "mmu.s"
0000F8r 2               .debuginfo
0000F8r 2               
0000F8r 2               .segment "MMU"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Handle table entry status byte =>
000000r 2               ; Bit 0: Paged      (1=Paged, 0=Task)               Points to Paged RAM (8K banks at $8000)
000000r 2               ; Bit 1: Shared     (1=Shared, 0=Task)              Points to Shared RAM when Shared == 1 && Paged == 1
000000r 2               ; Bit 2: Block      (1=Pages, 0=other)              Points to a run of 256-byte task RAM pages
000000r 2               ; Bit 3: Small      (1=3 or fewer bytes)            Bytes are stored in the handle entry itself, not in a separate
000000r 2               ;                                                   allocation; bits 0-1 then hold the length (1-3)
000000r 2               ; Bit 4: Allocated  (1=Allocated, 0=Free)           Is the memory free or allocated
000000r 2               ; Bit 5: Readonly   (1=not writable, 0=writable)    Allocation is read-only
000000r 2               ; Bit 6: Locked     (1=Locked)                      MM_LOCK handed out a raw pointer; can't be freed
000000r 2               ; Bit 7: IsValid    (1=Yes, 0=NO)                   Is this structure in use?
000000r 2               
000000r 2               AI_PAGED     = $01
000000r 2               AI_SHARED    = $02
000000r 2               AI_BLOCK     = $04
000000r 2               AI_SMALL     = $08      ; No actual allocation: bytes are stored in the entry, length in bits 0..1 of status
000000r 2               AI_ALLOCATED = $10
000000r 2               AI_READONLY  = $20
000000r 2               AI_LOCKED    = $40
000000r 2               AI_VALID     = $80
000000r 2               
000000r 2               AI_SMALL_LEN = $03      ; Length mask for AI_SMALL entries
000000r 2               AI_IN_USE    = AI_VALID | AI_ALLOCATED
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Handle table entry (4 bytes).  Small allocations keep their data in addr_l, addr_h, bank.
000000r 2               .struct     Handle
000000r 2                           addr_l      .byte                               ; Start address of the allocation
000000r 2                           addr_h      .byte
000000r 2                           bank        .byte                               ; First RAM bank (AI_PAGED)
000000r 2                           status      .byte                               ; AI_* bits; 0 = free entry
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; Per-task MMU area (see MMU_PLAN.md)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Lives at the top of Task RAM, directly below the paged RAM window.  Every
000000r 2               ;   task has its own copy, so a task switch (T) swaps the tables in for free.
000000r 2               ;   The page just below it is the task system page (IRQ tables, etc.).
000000r 2               ;   Task pages are allocated top-down from MMU_PAGE_TOP to MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MMU_TASK_PAGES   = 2                                        ; Size of the MMU area in 256-byte pages
000000r 2               MMU_AREA         = $8000 - (MMU_TASK_PAGES * $100)          ; $7E00 for 2 pages
000000r 2               MMU_SYS_PAGE     = (>MMU_AREA) - 1                          ; Task system page ($7D for 2 pages)
000000r 2               MMU_PAGE_TOP     = MMU_SYS_PAGE - 1                         ; Highest allocatable task page
000000r 2               MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM  = $08                                      ; $0000-$07FF: ZP, stack and BUFFERS
000000r 2               MMU_BANK_TOP     = $EF                                      ; Task RAM banks are $00-$EF
000000r 2               MMU_VERSION      = $01                                      ; Non-zero status == initialized
000000r 2               PAGED_RAM_BASE   = $8000                                    ; RAM bank window ($8000-$9FFF, bank in RAM_BANK_REG)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .assert     MMU_SYS_PAGE >= $78, error, "MMU_TASK_PAGES too large for top page-map byte pre-mark"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     MmuHeader
000000r 2                           status      .byte                               ; MMU_VERSION when initialized
000000r 2                           page_map    .res 16                             ; 1 bit per page $00-$7F (1 = in use)
000000r 2                           bank_map    .res 30                             ; 1 bit per bank $00-$EF (1 = in use)
000000r 2                           chunk_heads .res 6                              ; First page of each chunk size class (0 = none)
000000r 2                           page_ends   .res 16                             ; 1 bit per page, marks the last page of each run
000000r 2                           bank_ends   .res 30                             ; 1 bit per bank, marks the last bank of each run
000000r 2                           low_water   .byte                               ; Lowest allocated page (MMU_SYS_PAGE when empty)
000000r 2                           page_floor  .byte                               ; Lowest page MM_PAGE_ALLOC may hand out (MM_SET_FLOOR)
000000r 2                           handles     .byte                               ; Start of the handle table (rest of the MMU area)
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MMU_HDR          = MMU_AREA                                 ; Absolute access: MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::field
000000r 2               MMU_PAGE_MAP     = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_map
000000r 2               MMU_PAGE_ENDS    = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_ends
000000r 2               MMU_BANK_MAP     = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::bank_map
000000r 2               MMU_BANK_ENDS    = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::bank_ends
000000r 2               MMU_LOW_WATER    = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
000000r 2               MMU_PAGE_FLOOR   = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_floor
000000r 2               MMU_HANDLE_TBL   = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::handles             ; Entry for handle h: MMU_HANDLE_TBL + (h - 1) * 4
000000r 2               MMU_MAX_HANDLES  = ($8000 - MMU_HANDLE_TBL) / .sizeof(Handle)   ; 103 for 2 pages
000000r 2               MMU_CHUNK_HEADS  = MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::chunk_heads         ; First chunk page of each size class (0 = none)
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Chunk pages: a 256-byte task RAM page split into chunks of one size (4, 8, 16, 32 or 64 bytes).  The
000000r 2               ; first chunk holds the page header; free chunks are linked through their first byte (the in-page offset
000000r 2               ; of the next free chunk, 0 = none).
000000r 2               MMU_CHUNK_MIN    = 4                                        ; Smallest chunk (class 0)
000000r 2               MMU_CHUNK_MAX    = 64                                       ; Largest chunk; bigger allocations take whole pages
000000r 2               MMU_CHUNK_CLASSES = 5                                       ; 4, 8, 16, 32, 64
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     ChunkPage
000000r 2                           size        .byte                               ; Chunk size
000000r 2                           free_count  .byte                               ; Free chunks in this page
000000r 2                           free_head   .byte                               ; Offset of the first free chunk (0 = none)
000000r 2                           next_page   .byte                               ; Next chunk page of the same size (0 = none)
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .assert     .sizeof(ChunkPage) <= MMU_CHUNK_MIN, error, "Chunk page header must fit in the first chunk"
000000r 2               .assert     MMU_CHUNK_CLASSES <= 6, error, "MmuHeader::chunk_heads has 6 entries"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MMU_BIT_MASKS:
000000r 2  01 02 04 08              .byte       $01, $02, $04, $08, $10, $20, $40, $80
000004r 2  10 20 40 80  
000008r 2               
000008r 2               ; Initialize the MMU for all tasks, and the shared RAM tables.  Must be called from the system task.
000008r 2               ; OUT: C = 0 on success; C = 1 and .A = ERR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK if not called from the system task
000008r 2               MMU_INIT:
000008r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_RAM_INIT
00000Br 2  B0 03                    bcs         :+
00000Dr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SHARED_RAM_INIT
000010r 2               :
000010r 2  60                       rts
000011r 2               
000011r 2               ; Initialize the MMU area of every task.  Must be called from the system task.
000011r 2               TASK_RAM_INIT:
000011r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000014r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM
000016r 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
000018r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ERROR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK
00001Br 2               
00001Br 2               :
00001Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00001Cr 2  78                       sei                                             ; No IRQs while switching tasks
00001Dr 2  DA                       phx
00001Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MMU_PROBE_MODULES                   ; Installed RAM modules -> ZP_M_MODS (task 0)
000021r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_MODS                       ; Leave out the ones that failed the POST
000023r 2  14 rr                    trb         ZP_M_MODS
000025r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_MODS + 1
000027r 2  14 rr                    trb         ZP_M_MODS + 1
000029r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00002Br 2               
00002Br 2               @task_loop:
00002Br 2  9C F0 FF                 stz         T_REGISTER                          ; Copy task 0's ZP_M_MODS to task X
00002Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_MODS
000030r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_MODS + 1
000032r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Quick switch to task X
000035r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_MODS
000037r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_MODS + 1
000039r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_TASK_INIT                        ; Preserves .X
00003Cr 2  CA                       dex
00003Dr 2  10 EC                    bpl         @task_loop
00003Fr 2  A9 00                    lda         #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM
000041r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta         T_REGISTER                          ; Back to the system task
000044r 2  FA                       plx
000045r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000046r 2  18                       clc
000047r 2  60                       rts
000048r 2               
000048r 2               ; Find the installed RAM modules (16 banks each: module m = banks m*16 - m*16+15), by write/read-back
000048r 2               ; of the first byte of each module's first bank (the byte is restored).  Run in task 0 with IRQs off.
000048r 2               ; OUT: ZP_M_MODS = bit m set if module m is installed
000048r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000048r 2               MMU_PROBE_MODULES:
000048r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_MODS
00004Ar 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_MODS + 1
00004Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         RAM_BANK_REG
00004Er 2  48                       pha
00004Fr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Module#
000051r 2               
000051r 2               @loop:
000051r 2  8A                       txa
000052r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; First bank of the module
000053r 2  0A                       asl
000054r 2  0A                       asl
000055r 2  0A                       asl
000056r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000058r 2  AC 00 80                 ldy         PAGED_RAM_BASE                      ; Save the byte
00005Br 2  A9 55                    lda         #$55
00005Dr 2  8D 00 80                 sta         PAGED_RAM_BASE
000060r 2  AD 00 80                 lda         PAGED_RAM_BASE
000063r 2  C9 55                    cmp         #$55
000065r 2  D0 23                    bne         @next                               ; Not installed
000067r 2  A9 AA                    lda         #$AA
000069r 2  8D 00 80                 sta         PAGED_RAM_BASE
00006Cr 2  AD 00 80                 lda         PAGED_RAM_BASE
00006Fr 2  C9 AA                    cmp         #$AA
000071r 2  D0 17                    bne         @next                               ; Not installed
000073r 2  8C 00 80                 sty         PAGED_RAM_BASE                      ; Restore the byte
000076r 2  DA                       phx
000077r 2  8A                       txa
000078r 2  4A                       lsr
000079r 2  4A                       lsr
00007Ar 2  4A                       lsr
00007Br 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = ZP_M_MODS byte
00007Cr 2  8A                       txa
00007Dr 2  29 07                    and         #7
00007Fr 2  AA                       tax
000080r 2  BD rr rr                 lda         MMU_BIT_MASKS,X
000083r 2  19 rr rr                 ora         ZP_M_MODS,Y
000086r 2  99 rr rr                 sta         ZP_M_MODS,Y
000089r 2  FA                       plx
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               @next:
00008Ar 2  E8                       inx
00008Br 2  E0 0F                    cpx         #NUM_RAM_MODULES
00008Dr 2  D0 C2                    bne         @loop
00008Fr 2  68                       pla
000090r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000092r 2  60                       rts
000093r 2               
000093r 2               ; Reset the current task's MMU area: all task pages and banks become free, except banks of RAM
000093r 2               ; modules that aren't installed (ZP_M_MODS, see MMU_PROBE_MODULES).
000093r 2               ; Also used on task completion (MM_TASK_RESET).
000093r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
000093r 2               MM_TASK_INIT:
000093r 2  DA                       phx
000094r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000096r 2  A8                       tay
000097r 2               
000097r 2               @clear:
000097r 2  99 00 7E 99  .repeat     MMU_TASK_PAGES, I
00009Br 2  00 7F        
00009Dr 2                           sta         MMU_AREA + (I * $100),Y
00009Dr 2               .endrepeat
00009Dr 2  C8                       iny
00009Er 2  D0 F7                    bne         @clear
0000A0r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF                                ; Pages $00-$07: ZP, stack and BUFFERS
0000A2r 2  8D 01 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_map
0000A5r 2  A9 E0                    lda         #($FF << (MMU_SYS_PAGE & 7)) & $FF  ; System page and MMU area pages
0000A7r 2  8D 10 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_map + 15
0000AAr 2  A9 7D                    lda         #MMU_SYS_PAGE
0000ACr 2  8D 63 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
0000AFr 2  A9 08                    lda         #MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM
0000B1r 2  8D 64 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_floor
0000B4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_MODS                           ; Mark the banks of missing modules in use
0000B6r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
0000B8r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_MODS + 1
0000BAr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP2
0000BCr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Bank map byte: module * 2
0000BEr 2               
0000BEr 2               @modules:
0000BEr 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; C = next module's installed bit
0000C0r 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_M_TEMP
0000C2r 2  B0 08                    bcs         @installed
0000C4r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF
0000C6r 2  9D 11 7E                 sta         MMU_BANK_MAP,X
0000C9r 2  9D 12 7E                 sta         MMU_BANK_MAP + 1,X
0000CCr 2               
0000CCr 2               @installed:
0000CCr 2  E8                       inx
0000CDr 2  E8                       inx
0000CEr 2  E0 1E                    cpx         #NUM_RAM_MODULES * 2
0000D0r 2  D0 EC                    bne         @modules
0000D2r 2  A9 01                    lda         #MMU_VERSION
0000D4r 2  8D 00 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::status
0000D7r 2  FA                       plx
0000D8r 2  60                       rts
0000D9r 2               
0000D9r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0000D9r 2               ; Task page and bank allocators
0000D9r 2               
0000D9r 2               ; Allocate a run of contiguous 256-byte task pages, top-down.
0000D9r 2               ; IN: .A = number of pages (1+)
0000D9r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = first (lowest) page of the run, C = 0
0000D9r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
0000D9r 2               ; Modifies: .A
0000D9r 2               MM_PAGE_ALLOC:
0000D9r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0000DAr 2  78                       sei
0000DBr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0000DDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_PAGE_MAPS_SETUP
0000E0r 2  AC 64 7E                 ldy         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_floor     ; Never below the page floor
0000E3r 2  C0 7D                    cpy         #MMU_PAGE_TOP + 1
0000E5r 2  B0 15                    bcs         @no_mem                             ; (BM_ALLOC_RUN needs lowest <= highest)
0000E7r 2  A2 7C                    ldx         #MMU_PAGE_TOP
0000E9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_ALLOC_RUN
0000ECr 2  B0 09                    bcs         @done
0000EEr 2  CD 63 7E                 cmp         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water      ; New lowest page?
0000F1r 2  B0 03                    bcs         @ok
0000F3r 2  8D 63 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
0000F6r 2               
0000F6r 2               @ok:
0000F6r 2  18                       clc
0000F7r 2               
0000F7r 2               @done:
0000F7r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0000F9r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0000FCr 2               
0000FCr 2               @no_mem:
0000FCr 2  A9 02                    lda         #ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY
0000FEr 2  38                       sec
0000FFr 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000101r 2               
000101r 2               ; Set the page floor: MM_PAGE_ALLOC (and so chunk and page allocations) will only hand out pages at or
000101r 2               ; above it.  Lets a task keep the space above its own data free to grow into (HyForth's dictionary).
000101r 2               ; The floor can't go above the lowest page already allocated.
000101r 2               ; IN: .A = page (raised to MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM if below it)
000101r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000101r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1 (pages below .A are already allocated; floor unchanged)
000101r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000101r 2               MM_SET_FLOOR:
000101r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000102r 2  78                       sei
000103r 2  C9 08                    cmp         #MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM
000105r 2  B0 02                    bcs         :+
000107r 2  A9 08                    lda         #MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM
000109r 2               :
000109r 2  CD 63 7E                 cmp         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
00010Cr 2  F0 02                    beq         @set
00010Er 2  B0 07                    bcs         @no_mem
000110r 2               
000110r 2               @set:
000110r 2  8D 64 7E                 sta         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::page_floor
000113r 2  18                       clc
000114r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000117r 2               
000117r 2               @no_mem:
000117r 2  A9 02                    lda         #ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY
000119r 2  38                       sec
00011Ar 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00011Dr 2               
00011Dr 2               ; Free a run of task pages allocated with MM_PAGE_ALLOC.
00011Dr 2               ; IN: .A = first page of the run
00011Dr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00011Dr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
00011Dr 2               ; Modifies: .A
00011Dr 2               MM_PAGE_FREE:
00011Dr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00011Er 2  78                       sei
00011Fr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000121r 2  C9 08                    cmp         #MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM
000123r 2  90 1E                    bcc         @bad_arg
000125r 2  C9 7D                    cmp         #MMU_PAGE_TOP + 1
000127r 2  B0 1A                    bcs         @bad_arg
000129r 2  48                       pha
00012Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_PAGE_MAPS_SETUP
00012Dr 2  68                       pla
00012Er 2  A2 7C                    ldx         #MMU_PAGE_TOP
000130r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_FREE_RUN
000133r 2  B0 09                    bcs         @done
000135r 2  CD 63 7E                 cmp         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water      ; Freed the lowest run?
000138r 2  D0 03                    bne         @done_ok
00013Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_UPDATE_LOW_WATER
00013Dr 2               
00013Dr 2               @done_ok:
00013Dr 2  18                       clc
00013Er 2               
00013Er 2               @done:
00013Er 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000140r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000143r 2               
000143r 2               @bad_arg:
000143r 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
000145r 2  38                       sec
000146r 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000148r 2               
000148r 2               ; Allocate a run of contiguous 8K task RAM banks, top-down.
000148r 2               ; IN: .A = number of banks (1+)
000148r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = first (lowest) bank of the run, C = 0
000148r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
000148r 2               ; Modifies: .A
000148r 2               MM_BANK_ALLOC:
000148r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000149r 2  78                       sei
00014Ar 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00014Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_BANK_MAPS_SETUP
00014Fr 2  A2 EF                    ldx         #MMU_BANK_TOP
000151r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000153r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_ALLOC_RUN
000156r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000158r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00015Br 2               
00015Br 2               ; Free a run of task RAM banks allocated with MM_BANK_ALLOC.
00015Br 2               ; IN: .A = first bank of the run
00015Br 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00015Br 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
00015Br 2               ; Modifies: .A
00015Br 2               MM_BANK_FREE:
00015Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00015Cr 2  78                       sei
00015Dr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00015Fr 2  C9 F0                    cmp         #MMU_BANK_TOP + 1
000161r 2  B0 0F                    bcs         @bad_arg
000163r 2  48                       pha
000164r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         MM_BANK_MAPS_SETUP
000167r 2  68                       pla
000168r 2  A2 EF                    ldx         #MMU_BANK_TOP
00016Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_FREE_RUN
00016Dr 2               
00016Dr 2               @done:
00016Dr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
00016Fr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000172r 2               
000172r 2               @bad_arg:
000172r 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
000174r 2  38                       sec
000175r 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000177r 2               
000177r 2               ; Common exit for routines that start with php/sei: restore the caller's I flag, keep C
000177r 2               MM_RETURN:
000177r 2  B0 03                    bcs         :+
000179r 2  28                       plp
00017Ar 2  18                       clc
00017Br 2  60                       rts
00017Cr 2               :
00017Cr 2  28                       plp
00017Dr 2  38                       sec
00017Er 2  60                       rts
00017Fr 2               
00017Fr 2               ; Recompute the low-water mark: the lowest allocated page between MMU_PAGE_BOTTOM and MMU_PAGE_TOP,
00017Fr 2               ; or MMU_SYS_PAGE if none.  Expects ZP_M_BM to point at the page map.
00017Fr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00017Fr 2               MM_UPDATE_LOW_WATER:
00017Fr 2  AE 63 7E                 ldx         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
000182r 2               
000182r 2               @loop:
000182r 2  E0 7D                    cpx         #MMU_SYS_PAGE
000184r 2  B0 0B                    bcs         @store
000186r 2  8A                       txa
000187r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
00018Ar 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BM),Y
00018Cr 2  D0 03                    bne         @store                              ; Found an allocated page
00018Er 2  E8                       inx
00018Fr 2  80 F1                    bra         @loop
000191r 2               
000191r 2               @store:
000191r 2  8E 63 7E                 stx         MMU_HDR + MmuHeader::low_water
000194r 2  60                       rts
000195r 2               
000195r 2               ; Point ZP_M_BM / ZP_M_BE at the current task's page map / page run-end map.  Preserves .A
000195r 2               MM_PAGE_MAPS_SETUP:
000195r 2  48                       pha
000196r 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_PAGE_MAP, ZP_M_BM
00019Ar 2  A9 01 85 rr  
00019Er 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_PAGE_ENDS, ZP_M_BE
0001A2r 2  A9 35 85 rr  
0001A6r 2  68                       pla
0001A7r 2  60                       rts
0001A8r 2               
0001A8r 2               ; Point ZP_M_BM / ZP_M_BE at the current task's bank map / bank run-end map.  Preserves .A
0001A8r 2               MM_BANK_MAPS_SETUP:
0001A8r 2  48                       pha
0001A9r 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_BANK_MAP, ZP_M_BM
0001ADr 2  A9 11 85 rr  
0001B1r 2  A9 7E 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   MMU_BANK_ENDS, ZP_M_BE
0001B5r 2  A9 45 85 rr  
0001B9r 2  68                       pla
0001BAr 2  60                       rts
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0001BBr 2               ; Generic bitmap run allocator.  Used for task pages and banks, and later for shared banks.
0001BBr 2               ;   ZP_M_BM -> allocation bitmap (1 = in use), ZP_M_BE -> run-end bitmap (1 = last bit of a run)
0001BBr 2               ;   Bit n lives in byte (n >> 3), mask MMU_BIT_MASKS[n & 7]
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               ; IN: .A = bit number
0001BBr 2               ; OUT: .Y = byte offset into the bitmap, .A = bit mask
0001BBr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .Y
0001BBr 2               BM_BIT:
0001BBr 2  48                       pha
0001BCr 2  4A                       lsr
0001BDr 2  4A                       lsr
0001BEr 2  4A                       lsr
0001BFr 2  A8                       tay
0001C0r 2  68                       pla
0001C1r 2  DA                       phx
0001C2r 2  29 07                    and         #7
0001C4r 2  AA                       tax
0001C5r 2  BD rr rr                 lda         MMU_BIT_MASKS,X
0001C8r 2  FA                       plx
0001C9r 2  60                       rts
0001CAr 2               
0001CAr 2               ; Find, mark and return the highest run of .A free bits in [.Y, .X]
0001CAr 2               ; IN: .A = run length (1+), .X = highest bit to consider, .Y = lowest bit to consider
0001CAr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = first (lowest) bit of the run, C = 0
0001CAr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
0001CAr 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0001CAr 2               BM_ALLOC_RUN:
0001CAr 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
0001CCr 2  F0 41                    beq         @bad_arg
0001CEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_CNT
0001D0r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_LO
0001D2r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_RUN
0001D4r 2               
0001D4r 2               @loop:
0001D4r 2  8A                       txa
0001D5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
0001D8r 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BM),Y
0001DAr 2  D0 0A                    bne         @used
0001DCr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_RUN                            ; One more free bit in this run
0001DEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_RUN
0001E0r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_CNT
0001E2r 2  F0 0B                    beq         @found
0001E4r 2  80 02                    bra         @next
0001E6r 2               
0001E6r 2               @used:
0001E6r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_RUN                            ; Run broken, start over below this bit
0001E8r 2               
0001E8r 2               @next:
0001E8r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_M_LO
0001EAr 2  F0 1F                    beq         @no_mem
0001ECr 2  CA                       dex
0001EDr 2  80 E5                    bra         @loop
0001EFr 2               
0001EFr 2               @found:
0001EFr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_LO                             ; .X = first bit of the run, save it for return
0001F1r 2               
0001F1r 2               @mark:
0001F1r 2  8A                       txa
0001F2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
0001F5r 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_M_BM),Y
0001F7r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_BM),Y
0001F9r 2  E8                       inx
0001FAr 2  C6 rr                    dec         ZP_M_CNT
0001FCr 2  D0 F3                    bne         @mark
0001FEr 2  CA                       dex                                             ; Last bit of the run
0001FFr 2  8A                       txa
000200r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
000203r 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_M_BE),Y
000205r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_BE),Y
000207r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_LO
000209r 2  18                       clc
00020Ar 2  60                       rts
00020Br 2               
00020Br 2               @no_mem:
00020Br 2  A9 02                    lda         #ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY
00020Dr 2  38                       sec
00020Er 2  60                       rts
00020Fr 2               
00020Fr 2               @bad_arg:
00020Fr 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
000211r 2  38                       sec
000212r 2  60                       rts
000213r 2               
000213r 2               ; Free the run of bits starting at .A, up to and including the next run-end bit
000213r 2               ; IN: .A = first bit of the run, .X = highest valid bit
000213r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = first bit of the run, C = 0
000213r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_ALLOC, C = 1 (nothing is changed if the first bit is free)
000213r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
000213r 2               BM_FREE_RUN:
000213r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_LO                             ; Highest valid bit
000215r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_RUN                            ; Save first bit for return
000217r 2  AA                       tax
000218r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
00021Br 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BM),Y
00021Dr 2  F0 27                    beq         @not_alloc                          ; First bit isn't allocated
00021Fr 2               
00021Fr 2               @loop:
00021Fr 2  8A                       txa
000220r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
000223r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
000225r 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BM),Y                         ; Clear the allocated bit
000227r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_BM),Y
000229r 2  8A                       txa
00022Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
00022Dr 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BE),Y
00022Fr 2  D0 07                    bne         @end_found
000231r 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_M_LO
000233r 2  F0 03                    beq         @end_found                          ; Never run past the end of the map
000235r 2  E8                       inx
000236r 2  80 E7                    bra         @loop
000238r 2               
000238r 2               @end_found:
000238r 2  8A                       txa
000239r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_BIT
00023Cr 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
00023Er 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_BE),Y                         ; Clear the run-end bit
000240r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_BE),Y
000242r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_RUN
000244r 2  18                       clc
000245r 2  60                       rts
000246r 2               
000246r 2               @not_alloc:
000246r 2  A9 40                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_ALLOC
000248r 2  38                       sec
000249r 2  60                       rts
00024Ar 2               
00024Ar 2               .macro _M_ERROR_RETURN  errno
00024Ar 2                           lda         #errno
00024Ar 2                           sec
00024Ar 2                           rts
00024Ar 2               .endmacro
00024Ar 2               
00024Ar 2               ERROR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK:
00024Ar 2  A9 01 38 60              _M_ERROR_RETURN     ERR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK
00024Er 2               
00024Er 2               ERROR_OUT_OF_MEMORY:
00024Er 2  A9 02 38 60              _M_ERROR_RETURN     ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY
000252r 2               
000252r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000252r 2               ; Handles (per task).  A handle is a 1-byte index (1 - MMU_MAX_HANDLES) into the current task's handle
000252r 2               ; table; 0 is never a valid handle.  All calls: C = 0 on success, C = 1 with the error in .A.
000252r 2               ;
000252r 2               ;   Allocation tiers:
000252r 2               ;       1-3 bytes       AI_SMALL: the bytes are kept in the handle entry itself
000252r 2               ;       4-64 bytes      Chunk (no tier bit): a 4, 8, 16, 32 or 64-byte chunk of a shared chunk page
000252r 2               ;       65+ bytes       AI_BLOCK: a run of 256-byte task RAM pages ($0800-$7CFF, top-down)
000252r 2               ;       .X = AI_PAGED   AI_PAGED: a run of 8K RAM banks, seen at $8000-$9FFF (MM_LOCK selects the bank)
000252r 2               
000252r 2               ; Allocate memory for the current task.
000252r 2               ; IN: .A.Y = size in bytes (1 - $FFFF; .A = low byte), .X = 0 or AI_PAGED (8K RAM banks)
000252r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = handle, C = 0
000252r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES or ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1
000252r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000252r 2               MM_ALLOC:
000252r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000253r 2  78                       sei
000254r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000256r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Size low
000258r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Size high
00025Ar 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_SV                             ; Flags
00025Cr 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP2
00025Er 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
000260r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @bad_arg                            ; Zero bytes
000263r 2               :
