0006C3r 2  rr           
0006C4r 2  A9 3A 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COLON
0006C8r 2  rr           
0006C9r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0006CCr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0006CEr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0006D0r 2               @next_cell:
0006D0r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_TEMP
0006D2r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
0006D4r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV                             ; save the old value
0006D6r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0006D8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006DAr 2               @loop_start:
0006DAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006DCr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0006DEr 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0006E0r 2  D0 08                    bne         :+
0006E2r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF                                ; test the inverse
0006E4r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0006E6r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0006E8r 2  F0 06                    beq         @shift
0006EAr 2               :
0006EAr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0006ECr 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; add bit to set of bad bits found
0006EEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
0006F0r 2               @shift:
0006F0r 2  06 rr                    asl         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Walk the bit forward
0006F2r 2  D0 E6                    bne         @loop_start
0006F4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; get the set of bad bits we found
0006F6r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0006F9r 2  E8                       inx
0006FAr 2  E0 10                    cpx         #$10
0006FCr 2  F0 07                    beq         :+
0006FEr 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_SPACE
000702r 2  rr           
000703r 2  80 05                    bra         :++
000705r 2               :
000705r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000708r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
00070Ar 2               :
00070Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV
00070Cr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; restore the old value
00070Er 2  C8                       iny
00070Fr 2  D0 BF                    bne         @next_cell
000711r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
000713r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_SP2_H
000715r 2  F0 05                    beq         @done
000717r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_SP1_H
000719r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @next_page
00071Cr 2               @done:
00071Cr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
00071Er 2  60                       rts
00071Fr 2               
00071Fr 1               .include "irq.s"
00071Fr 2               .debuginfo
00071Fr 2               
00071Fr 2               .segment "IRQ"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; IRQ dispatch (see MMU_PLAN.md, IO subsystem)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Every IRQ (and BRK) enters through a stub in the COMMON block, which saves W and switches to ROM
000000r 2               ;   page 0, then jumps to IRQ_DISPATCH.  The dispatcher looks the IRQ up in the registration table and
000000r 2               ;   runs each registered handler IN THE TASK THAT REGISTERED IT (via TASK_CALL), then switches back.
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   The registration tables live in the task system page and are replicated into every task, so the
000000r 2               ;   dispatcher can read them from whichever task was interrupted.  Registration writes all 16 copies.
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   Handler convention:
000000r 2               ;       IN:  .A = logical IRQ# (0-14), or S/W interrupt# (0-15) for S/W interrupts;  I flag set
000000r 2               ;       OUT: C = 1 if the interrupt was claimed (stops the chain), C = 0 if not
000000r 2               ;       May clobber .A, .X, .Y.  Must not re-enable interrupts, and must not use FAR_GATEs.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IRQ_MAX_CHAIN       = 2                                     ; Handlers per hardware IRQ
000000r 2               IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE      = 3                                     ; {task, handler.w}
000000r 2               IRQ_LOGICAL_SW      = 15                                    ; Logical IRQ# of the S/W interrupt (IRQ_NUMBER_SW)
000000r 2               IRQ_NO_TASK         = $FF                                   ; Empty table entry
000000r 2               
000000r 2               IRQ_SYS_BASE        = MMU_SYS_PAGE * $100                   ; Task system page ($7D00)
000000r 2               IRQ_TABLE           = IRQ_SYS_BASE                          ; 16 IRQs x IRQ_MAX_CHAIN x {task, handler.w}
000000r 2               IRQ_SWI_OFFSET      = 16 * IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE   ; 16 S/W interrupts x {task, handler.w} follow
000000r 2               IRQ_TABLE_SIZE      = IRQ_SWI_OFFSET + (16 * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE)
000000r 2               IRQ_SPURIOUS        = IRQ_TABLE + IRQ_TABLE_SIZE            ; 16 counters, by logical IRQ# ($7D90-$7D9F in the interrupted
000000r 2                                                                           ; task): IRQs not claimed by their own handlers
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .assert     IRQ_TABLE_SIZE <= 256, error, "IRQ table must be indexable by .X"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up the IRQ tables in every task's system page, and point all 16 vectors at the IRQ stubs.
000000r 2               ; Must be called before interrupts are enabled.
000000r 2               IRQ_INIT:
000000r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000001r 2  78                       sei
000002r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000005r 2  AE F0 FF                 ldx         T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 holds the home task # during the loop
000008r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz         T_REGISTER
00000Br 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00000Dr 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00000Fr 2               
00000Fr 2               @task_loop:
00000Fr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Quick switch to task X (no stack use!)
000012r 2  A0 90                    ldy         #IRQ_TABLE_SIZE
000014r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
000016r 2               
000016r 2               @clear_table:
000016r 2  88                       dey
000017r 2  99 00 7D                 sta         IRQ_TABLE,Y
00001Ar 2  D0 FA                    bne         @clear_table
00001Cr 2  A0 10                    ldy         #16
00001Er 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000020r 2               
000020r 2               @clear_spurious:
000020r 2  88                       dey
000021r 2  99 90 7D                 sta         IRQ_SPURIOUS,Y
000024r 2  D0 FA                    bne         @clear_spurious
000026r 2  CA                       dex
000027r 2  10 E6                    bpl         @task_loop                          ; Ends in task 0
000029r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00002Br 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Back to the home task
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   IRQ_STUB_0, ZP_TEMP_VEC
000032r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000036r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Logical IRQ#
000038r 2               
000038r 2               @vector_loop:
000038r 2  DA                       phx
000039r 2  8A                       txa
00003Ar 2  49 07                    eor         #7                                  ; IRQ_NUMBER(): logical -> vector index
00003Cr 2  AA                       tax
00003Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC
00003Fr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000041r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SET_VECTOR
000044r 2  FA                       plx
000045r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Next stub (6 bytes each)
000047r 2  18                       clc
000048r 2  69 06                    adc         #6
00004Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC
00004Cr 2  90 02                    bcc         :+
00004Er 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000050r 2               :
000050r 2  E8                       inx
000051r 2  E0 10                    cpx         #16
000053r 2  D0 E3                    bne         @vector_loop
000055r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000058r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000059r 2  18                       clc
00005Ar 2  60                       rts
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               ; X: IRQ# (vector index, i.e. IRQ_NUMBER(n)), .A.Y: Vector Addr
00005Br 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y, V and the caller's I flag
00005Br 2               IRQ_SET_VECTOR:
00005Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00005Cr 2  78                       sei
00005Dr 2  DA                       phx
00005Er 2  48                       pha
00005Fr 2  AD F2 FF                 lda         V_REGISTER                          ; Save V (shared pseudo-register)
000062r 2  8E F2 FF                 stx         V_REGISTER                          ; Select the vector for IRQ# .X
000065r 2  AA                       tax                                             ; .X = prior V
000066r 2  68                       pla
000067r 2  8D FE FF                 sta         $FFFE
00006Ar 2  8C FF FF                 sty         $FFFF
00006Dr 2  8E F2 FF                 stx         V_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior V
000070r 2  FA                       plx
000071r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000072r 2  60                       rts
000073r 2               
000073r 2               ; Entered from IRQ_ENTRY (COMMON block) on ROM page 0.
000073r 2               ; .A = logical IRQ#, .X = interrupted ROM page.  Stack: .X, .A, then the interrupt frame.
000073r 2               IRQ_DISPATCH:
000073r 2  DA                       phx                                             ; Interrupted ROM page, for IRQ_EXIT
000074r 2  5A                       phy
000075r 2  D8                       cld
000076r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_VEC                           ; The interrupted code may be setting up a TASK_CALL
000078r 2  DA                       phx
000079r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
00007Br 2  DA                       phx
00007Cr 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_TC_TASK
00007Er 2  DA                       phx
00007Fr 2  C9 0F                    cmp         #IRQ_LOGICAL_SW
000081r 2  F0 29                    beq         @swi
000083r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_NUM
000085r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x6 = IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000086r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_NUM
000088r 2  0A                       asl
000089r 2  AA                       tax
00008Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
00008Dr 2  B0 2F                    bcs         @done
00008Fr 2  E8                       inx
000090r 2  E8                       inx
000091r 2  E8                       inx
000092r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
000095r 2  B0 27                    bcs         @done
000097r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_NUM                          ; Nobody on its own chain claimed it
000099r 2  FE 90 7D                 inc         IRQ_SPURIOUS,X
00009Cr 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Offer it to every registered hardware handler: each
00009Er 2                                                                           ; checks its own device, so an IRQ that arrives on an
00009Er 2               @poll_all:                                                  ; unexpected vector still gets cleared (no IRQ storm)
00009Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
0000A1r 2  B0 1B                    bcs         @done
0000A3r 2  E8                       inx
0000A4r 2  E8                       inx
0000A5r 2  E8                       inx
0000A6r 2  E0 60                    cpx         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
0000A8r 2  D0 F4                    bne         @poll_all
0000AAr 2  80 12                    bra         @done
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               @swi:
0000ACr 2  AD F2 FF                 lda         V_REGISTER                          ; S/W interrupt# is in V[4..7]
0000AFr 2  4A                       lsr
0000B0r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B1r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B2r 2  4A                       lsr
0000B3r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000B5r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x3 = IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
0000B6r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000B8r 2  69 60                    adc         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
0000BAr 2  AA                       tax
0000BBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_CALL_ENTRY
0000BEr 2               
0000BEr 2               @done:
0000BEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED                      ; A handler asked for a task switch (timer tick)?
0000C0r 2  F0 0E                    beq         IRQ_RESTORE
0000C2r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
0000C4r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SCHED_CAN_PREEMPT
0000C7r 2  90 07                    bcc         IRQ_RESTORE
0000C9r 2  AD F1 FF                 lda         U_REGISTER                          ; Complete the task's frame (tasks.s) and switch
0000CCr 2  48                       pha
0000CDr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SCHED_SWITCH
0000D0r 2               
0000D0r 2               ; Unwind the dispatcher's part of a task's frame and return to the interrupted code (also the end of
0000D0r 2               ; SCHED_RESUME)
0000D0r 2               IRQ_RESTORE:
0000D0r 2  68                       pla
0000D1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
0000D3r 2  68                       pla
0000D4r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000D6r 2  68                       pla
0000D7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC
0000D9r 2  7A                       ply
0000DAr 2  68                       pla                                             ; Interrupted ROM page
0000DBr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         IRQ_EXIT
0000DEr 2               
0000DEr 2               ; Run the handler in table entry .X (if any), in its task.
0000DEr 2               ; IN: .X = table offset.  OUT: C = 1 if claimed.  Preserves .X
0000DEr 2               IRQ_CALL_ENTRY:
0000DEr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0000E0r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0000E3r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0000E5r 2  F0 23                    beq         @none
0000E7r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_TASK
0000E9r 2  BD 01 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 1,X
0000ECr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC
0000EEr 2  BD 02 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 2,X
0000F1r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
0000F3r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_NUM
0000F5r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_CALL
0000F8r 2  90 0D                    bcc         @done                               ; Not claimed
0000FAr 2  C9 A5                    cmp         #SCHED_RESCHED_A                    ; Claimed; a task switch asked for?
0000FCr 2  D0 08                    bne         @claimed
0000FEr 2  C0 5A                    cpy         #SCHED_RESCHED_Y
000100r 2  D0 04                    bne         @claimed
000102r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
000104r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_RESCHED
000106r 2               
000106r 2               @claimed:
000106r 2  38                       sec
000107r 2               
000107r 2               @done:
000107r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000109r 2  60                       rts
00010Ar 2               
00010Ar 2               @none:
00010Ar 2  18                       clc
00010Br 2  60                       rts
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00010Cr 2               ; Registration.  A handler always runs in the task that registered it (the current task), so drivers
00010Cr 2               ; register from their init routine, which DRV_START runs in the driver's task.
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               ; Register a handler for a hardware IRQ.  Registering the same handler twice from the same task is OK.
00010Cr 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ# (IRQ_NUMBER(n), as used for V), .A.Y = handler
00010Cr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00010Cr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL, C = 1
00010Cr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00010Cr 2               IRQ_REGISTER:
00010Cr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00010Dr 2  78                       sei
00010Er 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000110r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_HW_SETUP
000113r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REG_AT
000116r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000118r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00011Br 2               
00011Br 2               ; Unregister a handler registered from the current task.
00011Br 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ# (IRQ_NUMBER(n)), .A.Y = handler
00011Br 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00011Br 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND, C = 1
00011Br 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00011Br 2               IRQ_UNREGISTER:
00011Br 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00011Cr 2  78                       sei
00011Dr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00011Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_HW_SETUP
000122r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_UNREG_AT
000125r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000127r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00012Ar 2               
00012Ar 2               ; Register a handler for a S/W interrupt (SW_INT).  One handler per S/W interrupt#.
00012Ar 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt# ($0-$F), .A.Y = handler
00012Ar 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
00012Ar 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL, C = 1
00012Ar 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
00012Ar 2               SWI_REGISTER:
00012Ar 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00012Br 2  78                       sei
00012Cr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00012Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SWI_SETUP
000131r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REG_AT
000134r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000136r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000139r 2               
000139r 2               ; Unregister a S/W interrupt handler registered from the current task.
000139r 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt# ($0-$F), .A.Y = handler
000139r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000139r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND, C = 1
000139r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000139r 2               SWI_UNREGISTER:
000139r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
00013Ar 2  78                       sei
00013Br 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00013Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_SWI_SETUP
000140r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_UNREG_AT
000143r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000145r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000148r 2               
000148r 2               ; IN: .X = IRQ_NUMBER(n), .A.Y = handler.  OUT: .X = first table offset, ZP_IRQ_CNT, ZP_IRQ_H set
000148r 2               IRQ_HW_SETUP:
000148r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_H
00014Ar 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
00014Cr 2  8A                       txa
00014Dr 2  49 07                    eor         #7                                  ; Vector index -> logical IRQ#
00014Fr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000151r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000153r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x6 = IRQ_MAX_CHAIN * IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000154r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000156r 2  0A                       asl
000157r 2  AA                       tax
000158r 2  A9 02                    lda         #IRQ_MAX_CHAIN
00015Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_CNT
00015Cr 2  60                       rts
00015Dr 2               
00015Dr 2               ; IN: .X = S/W interrupt#, .A.Y = handler.  OUT: .X = table offset, ZP_IRQ_CNT, ZP_IRQ_H set
00015Dr 2               IRQ_SWI_SETUP:
00015Dr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_H
00015Fr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
000161r 2  8A                       txa
000162r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000164r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000166r 2  0A                       asl                                             ; x3 = IRQ_ENTRY_SIZE
000167r 2  65 rr                    adc         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000169r 2  69 60                    adc         #IRQ_SWI_OFFSET
00016Br 2  AA                       tax
00016Cr 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
00016Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000170r 2  60                       rts
000171r 2               
000171r 2               ; Add (current task, ZP_IRQ_H) to the ZP_IRQ_CNT entries starting at offset .X, unless already there.
000171r 2               IRQ_REG_AT:
000171r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000173r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000175r 2               
000175r 2               @find_existing:
000175r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_MATCH
000178r 2  F0 31                    beq         @ok                                 ; Already registered
00017Ar 2  E8                       inx
00017Br 2  E8                       inx
00017Cr 2  E8                       inx
00017Dr 2  88                       dey
00017Er 2  D0 F5                    bne         @find_existing
000180r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_TMP
000182r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
000184r 2               
000184r 2               @find_free:
000184r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
000187r 2  C9 FF                    cmp         #IRQ_NO_TASK
000189r 2  F0 0A                    beq         @store
00018Br 2  E8                       inx
00018Cr 2  E8                       inx
00018Dr 2  E8                       inx
00018Er 2  88                       dey
00018Fr 2  D0 F3                    bne         @find_free
000191r 2  A9 50                    lda         #ERR_IRQ_CHAIN_FULL
000193r 2  38                       sec
000194r 2  60                       rts
000195r 2               
000195r 2               @store:
000195r 2  8A                       txa
000196r 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
000197r 2  C8                       iny
000198r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_H
00019Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
00019Dr 2  C8                       iny
00019Er 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
0001A0r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001A3r 2  88                       dey
0001A4r 2  88                       dey
0001A5r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER                          ; Task byte last: it makes the entry live
0001A8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001ABr 2               
0001ABr 2               @ok:
0001ABr 2  18                       clc
0001ACr 2  60                       rts
0001ADr 2               
0001ADr 2               ; Remove (current task, ZP_IRQ_H) from the ZP_IRQ_CNT entries starting at offset .X.
0001ADr 2               IRQ_UNREG_AT:
0001ADr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_IRQ_CNT
0001AFr 2               
0001AFr 2               @find:
0001AFr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_MATCH
0001B2r 2  F0 0A                    beq         @remove
0001B4r 2  E8                       inx
0001B5r 2  E8                       inx
0001B6r 2  E8                       inx
0001B7r 2  88                       dey
0001B8r 2  D0 F5                    bne         @find
0001BAr 2  A9 51                    lda         #ERR_IRQ_NOT_FOUND
0001BCr 2  38                       sec
0001BDr 2  60                       rts
0001BEr 2               
0001BEr 2               @remove:
0001BEr 2  8A                       txa
0001BFr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
0001C0r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0001C2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001C5r 2  18                       clc
0001C6r 2  60                       rts
0001C7r 2               
0001C7r 2               ; Unregister every IRQ and S/W interrupt handler of a task (for MM_TASK_RESET).
0001C7r 2               ; IN: .A = task
0001C7r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
0001C7r 2               IRQ_UNREGISTER_TASK:
0001C7r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0001C8r 2  78                       sei
0001C9r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0001CCr 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
0001CEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0001D0r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0001D2r 2               
0001D2r 2               @loop:
0001D2r 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0001D5r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_TMP
0001D7r 2  D0 09                    bne         @next
0001D9r 2  DA                       phx
0001DAr 2  8A                       txa
0001DBr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = entry offset
0001DCr 2  A9 FF                    lda         #IRQ_NO_TASK
0001DEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         IRQ_REPLICATE
0001E1r 2  FA                       plx
0001E2r 2               
0001E2r 2               @next:
0001E2r 2  E8                       inx
0001E3r 2  E8                       inx
0001E4r 2  E8                       inx
0001E5r 2  E0 90                    cpx         #IRQ_TABLE_SIZE
0001E7r 2  D0 E9                    bne         @loop
0001E9r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0001ECr 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
0001EDr 2  60                       rts
0001EEr 2               
0001EEr 2               ; Z = 1 if table entry .X is (current task, ZP_IRQ_H).  Preserves .X, .Y
0001EEr 2               IRQ_MATCH:
0001EEr 2  BD 00 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE,X
0001F1r 2  CD F0 FF                 cmp         T_REGISTER
0001F4r 2  D0 0C                    bne         @done
0001F6r 2  BD 01 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 1,X
0001F9r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_H
0001FBr 2  D0 05                    bne         @done
0001FDr 2  BD 02 7D                 lda         IRQ_TABLE + 2,X
000200r 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_IRQ_H + 1
000202r 2               
000202r 2               @done:
000202r 2  60                       rts
000203r 2               
000203r 2               ; Write .A to IRQ_TABLE + .Y in every task's system page.  I flag must be set.
000203r 2               ; Preserves .A, .Y
000203r 2               IRQ_REPLICATE:
000203r 2  AE F0 FF                 ldx         T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 holds the home task # during the loop
000206r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz         T_REGISTER
000209r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
00020Br 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
00020Dr 2               
00020Dr 2               @loop:
00020Dr 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Quick switch to task X (no stack use!)
000210r 2  99 00 7D                 sta         IRQ_TABLE,Y
000213r 2  CA                       dex
000214r 2  10 F7                    bpl         @loop                               ; Ends in task 0
000216r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IRQ_HOME
000218r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER                          ; Back to the home task
00021Br 2  60                       rts
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 1               .include "shared.s"
00021Cr 2               .debuginfo
00021Cr 2               
00021Cr 2               .segment "SHARED"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; Shared memory (see MMU_PLAN.md)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   256 shared 8K banks: shared bank ID = U << 4 | (bank & $0F), seen at $8000-$9FFF with RAM_BANK_REG =
000000r 2               ;   $F0-$FF.  The system data lives in shared bank ID $00 (U = 0, bank $F0: SYS_BANK, _M_SYS_ENTER):
000000r 2               ;       $8200-$821F  SH_MAP     shared bank bitmap (1 = in use)
000000r 2               ;       $8220-$823F  SH_ENDS    run-end bitmap (last bank of each allocation)
000000r 2               ;       $8400-$87FF  SH_HANDLES shared handle table: 255 entries of ShHandle
000000r 2               ;       $8800-$88FF  IO_DEV_TABLE the IO device table (io.s)
000000r 2               ;   Bank IDs $00 (system) and $09 (IO transfer areas) are reserved, as are the banks of any U macro-page
000000r 2               ;   whose RAM isn't installed, and the banks of any RAM chip that failed the POST (ZP_M_BAD_SH: chip c
000000r 2               ;   holds bank IDs 4c - 4c+3 of every U).
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;   A shared handle is a 1-byte index (1-255) that any task can use, so it can be passed to another task.
000000r 2               ;   Each task that uses it holds a reference (SH_ALLOC / SH_ATTACH); the banks are freed when the last
000000r 2               ;   reference goes (SH_DETACH, or MM_TASK_RESET of the task).  Only tasks holding a reference can read or
000000r 2               ;   write through the handle.  All calls: C = 0 on success, C = 1 with the error in .A.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SH_MAP              = $8200
000000r 2               SH_ENDS             = $8220
000000r 2               SH_HANDLES          = $8400
000000r 2               SH_MAX_HANDLES      = 255
000000r 2               SH_FIRST_FREE_ID    = $01                                   ; (ID $00: system data; $09, the IO transfer
000000r 2                                                                           ;   areas, is reserved in the map)
000000r 2               SH_WINDOW           = PAGED_RAM_BASE                        ; Where SH_LOCK maps a shared allocation
000000r 2               SH_PROBE_ADDR       = $9FFF                                 ; Probe byte (bank $F0 of each U)
000000r 2               SH_PROBE_MARK       = $50                                   ; Probe marker: SH_PROBE_MARK + U
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     ShHandle
000000r 2                           bank        .byte                               ; First shared bank ID
000000r 2                           count       .byte                               ; Banks (0 = free entry)
000000r 2                           mask_lo     .byte                               ; Tasks $0-$7 holding a reference (bit = task)
000000r 2                           mask_hi     .byte                               ; Tasks $8-$F
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Set up the shared memory tables: find which U macro-pages have RAM, clear the bitmaps and handle
000000r 2               ; table, and reserve the system and IO transfer banks.  Called by MMU_INIT at boot.
000000r 2               SHARED_RAM_INIT:
000000r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000001r 2  78                       sei
000002r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000005r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Save RAM bank / U; select shared bank ID $00
000009r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00000Dr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000015r 2               
000015r 2               ; Probe: write a marker into every U's bank $F0, highest U first, so if U decoding aliases, the
000015r 2               ; lower (real) macro-page's marker wins and the alias reads back the wrong marker.
000015r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #15
000017r 2               
000017r 2               @mark:
000017r 2  8E F1 FF                 stx         U_REGISTER
00001Ar 2  8A                       txa
00001Br 2  09 50                    ora         #SH_PROBE_MARK
00001Dr 2  8D FF 9F                 sta         SH_PROBE_ADDR
000020r 2  CA                       dex
000021r 2  10 F4                    bpl         @mark
000023r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC                         ; Present macro-pages, bit = U
000025r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000027r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #15
000029r 2               
000029r 2               @check:
000029r 2  8E F1 FF                 stx         U_REGISTER
00002Cr 2  8A                       txa
00002Dr 2  09 50                    ora         #SH_PROBE_MARK
00002Fr 2  CD FF 9F                 cmp         SH_PROBE_ADDR
000032r 2  D0 14                    bne         @next                               ; Missing (or an alias)
000034r 2  DA                       phx
000035r 2  8A                       txa
000036r 2  4A                       lsr
000037r 2  4A                       lsr
000038r 2  4A                       lsr
000039r 2  A8                       tay                                             ; .Y = mask byte
00003Ar 2  8A                       txa
00003Br 2  29 07                    and         #7
00003Dr 2  AA                       tax
00003Er 2  BD rr rr                 lda         MMU_BIT_MASKS,X
000041r 2  19 rr rr                 ora         ZP_TEMP_VEC,Y
000044r 2  99 rr rr                 sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC,Y
000047r 2  FA                       plx
000048r 2               
000048r 2               @next:
000048r 2  CA                       dex
000049r 2  10 DE                    bpl         @check
00004Br 2  A0 00                    ldy         #SYS_SHARED_U
00004Dr 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER                          ; Back to shared bank ID $00
000050r 2               
000050r 2  A9 00                    lda         #0                                  ; Clear the bitmaps and handle table
000052r 2  A8                       tay
000053r 2               
000053r 2               @clear:
000053r 2  99 00 82                 sta         SH_MAP,Y
000056r 2  99 00 84                 sta         SH_HANDLES,Y
000059r 2  99 00 85                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $100,Y
00005Cr 2  99 00 86                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $200,Y
00005Fr 2  99 00 87                 sta         SH_HANDLES + $300,Y
000062r 2  99 00 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE,Y                      ; The IO device table ($8800, io.s)
000065r 2  C8                       iny
000066r 2  D0 EB                    bne         @clear
000068r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_SH                         ; Chips that failed the POST (post_ram.s): chip c
00006Ar 2  29 03                    and         #$03                                ;   = bank IDs 4c - 4c+3 of every U, so a nibble
00006Cr 2  AA                       tax                                             ;   of each U's two SH_MAP bytes
00006Dr 2  BD rr rr                 lda         SH_CHIP_MASKS,X
000070r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC2                        ; Bank IDs $x0-$x7: chips 0, 1
000072r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_BAD_SH
000074r 2  4A                       lsr
000075r 2  4A                       lsr
000076r 2  29 03                    and         #$03
000078r 2  AA                       tax
000079r 2  BD rr rr                 lda         SH_CHIP_MASKS,X
00007Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1                    ; Bank IDs $x8-$xF: chips 2, 3
00007Er 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Reserve the banks of missing macro-pages and bad chips
000080r 2               
000080r 2               @macro_pages:
000080r 2  A9 FF                    lda         #$FF
000082r 2  A8                       tay
000083r 2  46 rr                    lsr         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1                     ; C = this U's present bit
000085r 2  66 rr                    ror         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000087r 2  90 04                    bcc         @reserve                            ; Missing: all of its banks
000089r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TEMP_VEC2                        ; Present: the banks on bad chips
00008Br 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1
00008Dr 2               
00008Dr 2               @reserve:
00008Dr 2  9D 00 82                 sta         SH_MAP,X
000090r 2  98                       tya
000091r 2  9D 01 82                 sta         SH_MAP + 1,X
000094r 2  E8                       inx
000095r 2  E8                       inx
000096r 2  E0 20                    cpx         #32
000098r 2  D0 E6                    bne         @macro_pages
00009Ar 2  A9 01                    lda         #$01                                ; Reserve bank IDs $00 (system data) and
00009Cr 2  0D 00 82                 ora         SH_MAP                              ;   $09 (IO transfer areas)
00009Fr 2  8D 00 82                 sta         SH_MAP
0000A2r 2  A9 02                    lda         #$02
0000A4r 2  0D 01 82                 ora         SH_MAP + 1
0000A7r 2  8D 01 82                 sta         SH_MAP + 1
0000AAr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
0000AEr 2  7A 84 rr     
0000B1r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0000B4r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
0000B5r 2  60                       rts
0000B6r 2               
0000B6r 2  00 0F F0 FF  SH_CHIP_MASKS:  .byte   $00, $0F, $F0, $FF                  ; 2 chips' bad bits -> SH_MAP byte (4 bank IDs each)
0000BAr 2               
0000BAr 2               ; Allocate shared memory (whole 8K banks); the calling task holds the first reference.
0000BAr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = size in bytes (1 - $FFFF; .A = low byte)
0000BAr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = shared handle, C = 0
0000BAr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG, ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES or ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1
0000BAr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0000BAr 2               SH_ALLOC:
0000BAr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0000BBr 2  78                       sei
0000BCr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0000BEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Size low
0000C0r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Size high
0000C2r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP2
0000C4r 2  F0 5B                    beq         @bad_arg                            ; Zero bytes
0000C6r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
0000CAr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0000CEr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
0000D6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_NEW_HANDLE                       ; Free entry -> ZP_M_HP, ZP_M_HANDLE
0000D9r 2  B0 3A                    bcs         @leave
0000DBr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SZ1                            ; Banks = ((size - 1) >> 13) + 1
0000DDr 2  C9 01                    cmp         #1
0000DFr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0000E1r 2  E9 00                    sbc         #0
0000E3r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E4r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E5r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E6r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E7r 2  4A                       lsr
0000E8r 2  1A                       inc
0000E9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV
0000EBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_MAPS_SETUP
0000EEr 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #$FF
0000F0r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #SH_FIRST_FREE_ID
0000F2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_ALLOC_RUN                        ; .A = first bank ID
0000F5r 2  B0 1E                    bcs         @leave
0000F7r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0000F9r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0000FBr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
0000FDr 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ShHandle::mask_lo
0000FFr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000101r 2  C8                       iny
000102r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000104r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000107r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
000109r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_SET_TASK_BIT                     ; The caller's reference
00010Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV
00010Er 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ShHandle::count                    ; Count last: it makes the entry live
000110r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000112r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
000114r 2  18                       clc
000115r 2               
000115r 2               @leave:
000115r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
000119r 2  7A 84 rr     
00011Cr 2               
00011Cr 2               @done:
00011Cr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
00011Er 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000121r 2               
000121r 2               @bad_arg:
000121r 2  A9 43                    lda         #ERR_MEM_BAD_ARG
000123r 2  38                       sec
000124r 2  80 F6                    bra         @done
000126r 2               
000126r 2               ; Take a reference to a shared allocation for the calling task (e.g. a handle received in a message).
000126r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle
000126r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000126r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1
000126r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000126r 2               SH_ATTACH:
000126r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000127r 2  78                       sei
000128r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00012Ar 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
00012Er 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
000132r 2  00 8C F1 FF  
00013Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_HANDLE_PTR
00013Dr 2  B0 09                    bcs         @leave
00013Fr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000142r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
000144r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_SET_TASK_BIT
000147r 2  18                       clc
000148r 2               
000148r 2               @leave:
000148r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
00014Cr 2  7A 84 rr     
00014Fr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000151r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000154r 2               
000154r 2               ; Drop the calling task's reference; the banks are freed when no task holds a reference.
000154r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle
000154r 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
000154r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 (no such handle, or the task has no reference)
000154r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000154r 2               SH_DETACH:
000154r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000155r 2  78                       sei
000156r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000158r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
00015Cr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
000160r 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000168r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_HANDLE_PTR
00016Br 2  B0 08                    bcs         @leave
00016Dr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000170r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
000172r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_DROP_TASK_REF
000175r 2               
000175r 2               @leave:
000175r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
000179r 2  7A 84 rr     
00017Cr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
00017Er 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000181r 2               
000181r 2               ; Read a byte of a shared allocation (the calling task must hold a reference).
000181r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle, .Y = offset (0-255, in the first bank)
000181r 2               ; OUT (success): .A = byte, C = 0
000181r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1
000181r 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
000181r 2               SH_READ:
000181r 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000182r 2  78                       sei
000183r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000185r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_COFS
000187r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
00018Br 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00018Fr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000197r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_ACCESS_SETUP                     ; Selects the allocation's first bank
00019Ar 2  B0 06                    bcs         @leave
00019Cr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_COFS
00019Er 2  B9 00 80                 lda         SH_WINDOW,Y
0001A1r 2  18                       clc
0001A2r 2

0001A6r 2  7A 84 rr     
0001A9r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0001ABr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0001AEr 2               
0001AEr 2               ; Write a byte of a shared allocation (the calling task must hold a reference).
0001AEr 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle, .Y = offset (0-255, in the first bank), .X = byte
0001AEr 2               ; OUT (success): C = 0
0001AEr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1

0001AFr 2  78                       sei

0001B6r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
0001BAr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0001BEr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
0001C6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_ACCESS_SETUP                     ; Selects the allocation's first bank
0001C9r 2  B0 08                    bcs         @leave
0001CBr 2  A4 rr                    ldy         ZP_M_COFS
0001CDr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SZ1
0001CFr 2  99 00 80                 sta         SH_WINDOW,Y
0001D2r 2  18                       clc
0001D3r 2               
0001D3r 2               @leave:
0001D3r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE                                    ; Restores the RAM bank and U
0001D7r 2  7A 84 rr     
0001DAr 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0001DCr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
0001DFr 2               
0001DFr 2               ; Map a shared allocation's first bank into the $8000-$9FFF window (SH_WINDOW), for speed.  The calling
0001DFr 2               ; task must hold a reference.  Undo with SH_UNLOCK.
0001DFr 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle
0001DFr 2               ; OUT (success): .X = previous RAM bank, .Y = previous U (pass both to SH_UNLOCK), C = 0
0001DFr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 (.X, .Y preserved)
0001DFr 2               SH_LOCK:
0001DFr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0001E0r 2  78                       sei
0001E1r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0001E3r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
0001E7r 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
0001EBr 2  00 8C F1 FF  
0001F3r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_HANDLE_PTR
0001F6r 2  B0 14                    bcs         @fail
0001F8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_CHECK_REF
0001FBr 2  B0 0F                    bcs         @fail
0001FDr 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0001FFr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000201r 2  7A                       ply                                             ; Previous U        } Don't restore them:
000202r 2  FA                       plx                                             ; Previous RAM bank } they're returned
000203r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_SELECT_BANK                      ; Map the allocation
000206r 2  68                       pla                                             ; Drop the caller's .Y
000207r 2  68                       pla                                             ; Drop the caller's .X
000208r 2  18                       clc
000209r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
00020Cr 2               
00020Cr 2               @fail:
00020Cr 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
000210r 2  7A 84 rr     
000213r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000215r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000218r 2               
000218r 2               ; Undo SH_LOCK.
000218r 2               ; IN: .X = RAM bank, .Y = U (from SH_LOCK)
000218r 2               ; OUT: C = 0
000218r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000218r 2               SH_UNLOCK:
000218r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG
00021Ar 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER
00021Dr 2  18                       clc
00021Er 2  60                       rts
00021Fr 2               
00021Fr 2               ; Drop all of a task's shared references, freeing banks nobody else references.  For MM_TASK_RESET.
00021Fr 2               ; IN: .A = task
00021Fr 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
00021Fr 2               SH_RESET_TASK:
00021Fr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
000220r 2  78                       sei
000221r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000224r 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000226r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
000228r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00
00022Cr 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
000230r 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000238r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
00023Ar 2               
00023Ar 2               @loop:
00023Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
00023Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_HANDLE_PTR
00023Fr 2  B0 03                    bcs         @next                               ; Free entry
000241r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_DROP_TASK_REF                    ; C = 1 if the task had no reference: fine
000244r 2               
000244r 2               @next:
000244r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
000246r 2  1A                       inc
000247r 2  D0 F1                    bne         @loop                               ; Handles 1-255
000249r 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
00024Dr 2  7A 84 rr     
000250r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000253r 2  28                       plp                                             ; Restore caller's I flag
000254r 2  60                       rts
000255r 2               
000255r 2               ; ---- helpers (shared bank ID $00 selected, IRQs off)
000255r 2               
000255r 2               ; Point ZP_M_BM / ZP_M_BE at the shared bank maps.  Preserves .A
000255r 2               SH_MAPS_SETUP:
000255r 2  48                       pha
000256r 2  A9 82 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   SH_MAP, ZP_M_BM
00025Ar 2  A9 00 85 rr  
00025Er 2  A9 82 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   SH_ENDS, ZP_M_BE
000262r 2  A9 20 85 rr  
000266r 2  68                       pla
000267r 2  60                       rts
000268r 2               
000268r 2               ; Find a free shared handle.  OUT: ZP_M_HANDLE, ZP_M_HP = its entry, C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES, C = 1
000268r 2               SH_NEW_HANDLE:
000268r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
00026Ar 2               
00026Ar 2               @loop:
00026Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
00026Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_ENTRY_ADDR
00026Fr 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ShHandle::count
000271r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000273r 2  F0 09                    beq         @found
000275r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
000277r 2  1A                       inc
000278r 2  D0 F0                    bne         @loop
00027Ar 2  A9 44                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NO_HANDLES
00027Cr 2  38                       sec
00027Dr 2  60                       rts
00027Er 2               
00027Er 2               @found:
00027Er 2  18                       clc
00027Fr 2  60                       rts
000280r 2               
000280r 2               ; ZP_M_HP = entry of shared handle ZP_M_HANDLE: SH_HANDLES + (handle - 1) * 4.  Modifies: .A
000280r 2               SH_ENTRY_ADDR:
000280r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HANDLE
000282r 2  3A                       dec
000283r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_HP + 1
000285r 2  0A                       asl
000286r 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_M_HP + 1
000288r 2  0A                       asl
000289r 2  26 rr                    rol         ZP_M_HP + 1
00028Br 2  18                       clc
00028Cr 2  69 00                    adc         #<SH_HANDLES
00028Er 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP
000290r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_HP + 1
000292r 2  69 84                    adc         #>SH_HANDLES
000294r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HP + 1
000296r 2  60                       rts
000297r 2               
000297r 2               ; Point ZP_M_HP at a shared handle's entry and check it's allocated.
000297r 2               ; IN: .A = shared handle.  OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1.  Modifies: .A, .Y
000297r 2               SH_HANDLE_PTR:
000297r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_HANDLE
000299r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0
00029Br 2  F0 0B                    beq         @bad
00029Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_ENTRY_ADDR
0002A0r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ShHandle::count
0002A2r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002A4r 2  F0 02                    beq         @bad
0002A6r 2  18                       clc
0002A7r 2  60                       rts
0002A8r 2               
0002A8r 2               @bad:
0002A8r 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
0002AAr 2  38                       sec
0002ABr 2  60                       rts
0002ACr 2               
0002ACr 2               ; Task ZP_M_TEMP's bit in an entry's mask.  OUT: .Y = ShHandle::mask_lo or mask_hi, .A = bit.  Modifies: .X
0002ACr 2               SH_TASK_BIT:
0002ACr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP
0002AEr 2  29 07                    and         #7
0002B0r 2  AA                       tax
0002B1r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ShHandle::mask_lo
0002B3r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP
0002B5r 2  29 08                    and         #$08
0002B7r 2  F0 01                    beq         :+
0002B9r 2  C8                       iny                                             ; mask_hi
0002BAr 2               :
0002BAr 2  BD rr rr                 lda         MMU_BIT_MASKS,X
0002BDr 2  60                       rts
0002BEr 2               
0002BEr 2               ; Give task ZP_M_TEMP a reference to entry ZP_M_HP.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0002BEr 2               SH_SET_TASK_BIT:
0002BEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_TASK_BIT
0002C1r 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002C3r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002C5r 2  60                       rts
0002C6r 2               
0002C6r 2               ; C = 0 if the calling task holds a reference to entry ZP_M_HP; else .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1.
0002C6r 2               ; Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0002C6r 2               SH_CHECK_REF:
0002C6r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
0002C9r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
0002CBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_TASK_BIT
0002CEr 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002D0r 2  F0 02                    beq         @no_ref
0002D2r 2  18                       clc
0002D3r 2  60                       rts
0002D4r 2               
0002D4r 2               @no_ref:
0002D4r 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
0002D6r 2  38                       sec
0002D7r 2  60                       rts
0002D8r 2               
0002D8r 2               ; Drop task ZP_M_TEMP's reference to entry ZP_M_HP; free the banks if it was the last reference.
0002D8r 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1 if the task had no reference.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
0002D8r 2               SH_DROP_TASK_REF:
0002D8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_TASK_BIT
0002DBr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV
0002DDr 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002DFr 2  F0 25                    beq         @no_ref
0002E1r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV                             ; Clear the task's bit
0002E3r 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
0002E5r 2  31 rr                    and         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002E7r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002E9r 2  A0 02                    ldy         #ShHandle::mask_lo
0002EBr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002EDr 2  C8                       iny
0002EEr 2  11 rr                    ora         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002F0r 2  D0 12                    bne         @done                               ; Still referenced
0002F2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_MAPS_SETUP                       ; Last reference: free the banks
0002F5r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
0002F7r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
0002F9r 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #$FF
0002FBr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         BM_FREE_RUN
0002FEr 2  A9 00                    lda         #0
000300r 2  A0 01                    ldy         #ShHandle::count                    ; Free the entry
000302r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000304r 2               
000304r 2               @done:
000304r 2  18                       clc
000305r 2  60                       rts
000306r 2               
000306r 2               @no_ref:
000306r 2  A9 41                    lda         #ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID
000308r 2  38                       sec
000309r 2  60                       rts
00030Ar 2               
00030Ar 2               ; Check the calling task's reference to shared handle .A and map its first bank.
00030Ar 2               ; OUT: C = 0; or .A = ERR_MEM_NOT_VALID, C = 1.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00030Ar 2               SH_ACCESS_SETUP:
00030Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_HANDLE_PTR
00030Dr 2  B0 0D                    bcs         @done
00030Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_CHECK_REF
000312r 2  B0 08                    bcs         @done
000314r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #ShHandle::bank
000316r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_HP),Y
000318r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SH_SELECT_BANK
00031Br 2  18                       clc
00031Cr 2               
00031Cr 2               @done:
00031Cr 2  60                       rts
00031Dr 2               
00031Dr 2               ; Map shared bank ID .A at $8000-$9FFF: U = ID >> 4, RAM bank = $F0 | (ID & $0F).  Preserves .X, .Y
00031Dr 2               SH_SELECT_BANK:
00031Dr 2  48                       pha
00031Er 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
000320r 2  09 F0                    ora         #$F0
000322r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000324r 2  68                       pla
000325r 2  4A                       lsr
000326r 2  4A                       lsr
000327r 2  4A                       lsr
000328r 2  4A                       lsr
000329r 2  8D F1 FF                 sta         U_REGISTER
00032Cr 2  60                       rts
00032Dr 2               
00032Dr 1               .include "io_p0.s"
00032Dr 2               .debuginfo
00032Dr 2               
00032Dr 2               .segment "IO_P0"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000000r 2               ; IO subsystem, the BIOS ROM page 0 part (see IO_PLAN.md; the IO layer itself is io.s, on page 2).
000000r 2               ; Drivers (on page 0) use these: DEV_REGISTER, and IO_SRV_MAP / IO_SRV_UNMAP in their serve routines.
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; The IO layer's calls, for page 0 callers and the $F8xx thunks (compact gates into page 2)
000000r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_OPEN,        PAGE2::IO_OPEN,         2
000004r 2  rr 02        
000006r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_CLOSE,       PAGE2::IO_CLOSE,        2

000046r 2  rr 02

00004Cr 2  rr 02        
00004Er 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     TASK_CLONE,     PAGE2::TASK_CLONE,      2
000052r 2  rr 02

000058r 2  rr 02        
00005Ar 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_MOUNT,       PAGE2::IO_MOUNT,        2
00005Er 2  rr 02        
000060r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_BIND,        PAGE2::IO_BIND,         2
000064r 2  rr 02        
000066r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_UNMOUNT,     PAGE2::IO_UNMOUNT,      2
00006Ar 2  rr 02        
00006Cr 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     NS_CLEAR_ALL,   PAGE2::NS_CLEAR_ALL,    2
000070r 2  rr 02        
000072r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_NS_LIST,     PAGE2::IO_NS_LIST,      2
000076r 2  rr 02

00007Cr 2  rr 02        
00007Er 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_CLOSE_ALL,   PAGE2::IO_CLOSE_ALL,    2
000082r 2  rr 02        
000084r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_INHERIT,     PAGE2::IO_INHERIT,      2
000088r 2  rr 02        
00008Ar 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     IO_ADOPT_FDS,   PAGE2::IO_ADOPT_FDS,    2  ; (Run in a new task by IO_INHERIT)
00008Er 2  rr 02        
000090r 2               
000090r 2               ; Serve routines must be page 0 addresses (TASK_CALL runs them on page 0): gates to the page 2 servers
000090r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     NULL_SERVE,     PAGE2::NULL_SERVE,      2
000094r 2  rr 02        
000096r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     ZERO_SERVE,     PAGE2::ZERO_SERVE,      2
00009Ar 2  rr 02        
00009Cr 2               
00009Cr 2  6E 75 6C 6C  NULL_NAME:  .byte   "null", 0
0000A0r 2  00           
0000A1r 2  7A 65 72 6F  ZERO_NAME:  .byte   "zero", 0
0000A5r 2  00           
0000A6r 2               
0000A6r 2               ; The pipe server (pipe_srv.s): a Resident task (PIPE_TASK_NUM), started at boot like the drivers
0000A6r 2               PIPE_DRIVER:
0000A6r 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_INIT                           ; DriverInfo::init
0000A8r 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_STOP                           ; DriverInfo::stop
0000AAr 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_DNAME                          ; DriverInfo::name
0000ACr 2  04 50 49 50  NamedHString PIPE_DNAME, "PIPE"
0000B0r 2  45           
0000B1r 2  70 69 70 65  PIPE_NAME:  .byte   "pipe", 0
0000B5r 2  00           
0000B6r 2               
0000B6r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     PIPE_SERVE,     PAGE2::PIPE_SERVE,      2
0000BAr 2  rr 02        
0000BCr 2               
0000BCr 2               ; Runs in the pipe task: no pipes yet, and register /dev/pipe
0000BCr 2               PIPE_INIT:
0000BCr 2  A2 3F                    ldx         #PIPE_MAX * PIPE_ENTRY_SIZE - 1
0000BEr 2               :
0000BEr 2  9E 00 02                 stz         PIPE_TABLE,X
0000C1r 2  CA                       dex
0000C2r 2  10 FA                    bpl         :-
0000C4r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   PIPE_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
0000C8r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0000CCr 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<PIPE_NAME
0000CEr 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>PIPE_NAME
0000D0r 2  A2 0D                    ldx         #PIPE_TASK_NUM
0000D2r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         DEV_REGISTER
0000D5r 2               
0000D5r 2               PIPE_STOP:
0000D5r 2  18                       clc
0000D6r 2  60                       rts
0000D7r 2               
0000D7r 2               ; Clear the tasks' namespaces, and register the IO layer's own devices.  Called at boot, after
0000D7r 2               ; SHARED_RAM_INIT (which clears the device table).
0000D7r 2               IO_INIT:
0000D7r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         NS_CLEAR_ALL
0000DAr 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   NULL_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
0000DEr 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0000E2r 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<NULL_NAME
0000E4r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>NULL_NAME
0000E6r 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK
0000E8r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         DEV_REGISTER
0000EBr 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   ZERO_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
0000EFr 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
0000F3r 2  A9 rr                    lda         #<ZERO_NAME
0000F5r 2  A0 rr                    ldy         #>ZERO_NAME
0000F7r 2  A2 FF                    ldx         #IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK
0000F9r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         DEV_REGISTER
0000FCr 2               
0000FCr 2               ; Register a device (a file server): /dev/<name> is served by the serve routine, running in a task.
0000FCr 2               ; Drivers call it from their init (which runs in the driver's task).
0000FCr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = name (zero-terminated, 1-8 characters; in RAM or on ROM page 0), .X = task the serve routine
0000FCr 2               ;     runs in (IO_DEV_CALLER_TASK = the task making each request), ZP_TC_VEC = serve routine (page 0)
0000FCr 2               ; OUT (success): .A = device index, C = 0
0000FCr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERR_IO_NAME (empty or too long) or ERR_IO_NO_DEVS, C = 1
0000FCr 2               ; Preserves .X, .Y
0000FCr 2               DEV_REGISTER:
0000FCr 2  08                       php                                             ; Save caller's I flag
0000FDr 2  78                       sei
0000FEr 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
000100r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_BUF                           ; The name
000102r 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_IO_BUF + 1
000104r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_TMP                           ; The task
000106r 2  A4 rr 5A AC              _M_SYS_ENTER                                    ; Select shared bank ID $00 (device table)
00010Ar 2  F1 FF 5A A0  
00010Er 2  00 8C F1 FF  
000116r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF)
000118r 2  F0 4E                    beq         @empty_name
00011Ar 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0                                  ; Device table offset
00011Cr 2               
00011Cr 2               @find:
00011Cr 2  BD 00 88                 lda         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
00011Fr 2  F0 0B                    beq         @free                               ; Free entry
000121r 2  8A                       txa
000122r 2  18                       clc
000123r 2  69 10                    adc         #IO_DEV_SIZE
000125r 2  AA                       tax
000126r 2  90 F4                    bcc         @find                               ; (16 entries x 16 bytes: ends at 256)
000128r 2  A9 76                    lda         #ERR_IO_NO_DEVS
00012Ar 2  80 3E                    bra         @fail
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               @free:
00012Cr 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_IO_CNT                           ; The entry's offset
00012Er 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0                                  ; Copy the name, zero-padded
000130r 2               
000130r 2               @name:
000130r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_IO_BUF),Y
000132r 2  F0 0B                    beq         @pad
000134r 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
000136r 2  B0 2B                    bcs         @bad_name                           ; Too long
000138r 2  9D 00 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
00013Br 2  E8                       inx
00013Cr 2  C8                       iny
00013Dr 2  80 F1                    bra         @name
00013Fr 2               
00013Fr 2               @pad:
00013Fr 2  C0 08                    cpy         #IO_DEV_NAME_LEN
000141r 2  B0 07                    bcs         @padded
000143r 2  9E 00 88                 stz         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X
000146r 2  E8                       inx
000147r 2  C8                       iny
000148r 2  80 F5                    bra         @pad
00014Ar 2               
00014Ar 2               @padded:
00014Ar 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CNT                           ; Back to the entry's start
00014Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_TMP
00014Er 2  9D 08 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_TASK,X
000151r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_VEC
000153r 2  9D 09 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE,X
000156r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_TC_VEC + 1
000158r 2  9D 0A 88                 sta         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_SERVE + 1,X
00015Br 2  8A                       txa                                             ; Device index = offset / 16
00015Cr 2  4A                       lsr
00015Dr 2  4A                       lsr
00015Er 2  4A                       lsr
00015Fr 2  4A                       lsr
000160r 2  18                       clc
000161r 2  80 08                    bra         @done
000163r 2               
000163r 2               @bad_name:
000163r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         ZP_IO_CNT
000165r 2  9E 00 88                 stz         IO_DEV_TABLE + IO_DEV_NAME,X        ; Leave the entry free
000168r 2               
000168r 2               @empty_name:
000168r 2  A9 77                    lda         #ERR_IO_NAME
00016Ar 2               
00016Ar 2               @fail:
00016Ar 2  38                       sec
00016Br 2               
00016Br 2               @done:
00016Br 2  7A 8C F1 FF              _M_SYS_LEAVE
00016Fr 2  7A 84 rr     
000172r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000174r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         MM_RETURN
000177r 2               
000177r 2               ; Server side: map the client's request block (in its IO transfer area) into $8000-$9FFF, in the
000177r 2               ; server's task.  Undo with IO_SRV_UNMAP before returning from the serve routine.
000177r 2               ; IN: .X = client task.  OUT: ZP_IO_REQ = the request block (data at ZP_IO_REQ + IO_BLK_DATA)
000177r 2               ; Preserves .A, .X, .Y
000177r 2               IO_SRV_MAP:
000177r 2  48                       pha
000178r 2  A5 rr                    lda         RAM_BANK_REG
00017Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_SAVEB
00017Cr 2  AD F1 FF                 lda         U_REGISTER
00017Fr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_SAVEU
000181r 2  9C F1 FF                 stz         U_REGISTER
000184r 2  A9 F9                    lda         #IO_XFER_BANK
000186r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000188r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_IO_REQ
00018Ar 2  8A                       txa
00018Br 2  29 0F                    and         #$0F
00018Dr 2  0A                       asl                                             ; $8000 + task * $200
00018Er 2  09 80                    ora         #>PAGED_RAM_BASE
000190r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_IO_REQ + 1
000192r 2  68                       pla
000193r 2  60                       rts
000194r 2               
000194r 2               ; Undo IO_SRV_MAP.  Preserves .A, .X, .Y and C
000194r 2               IO_SRV_UNMAP:
000194r 2  48                       pha
000195r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_SAVEU
000197r 2  8D F1 FF                 sta         U_REGISTER
00019Ar 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_IO_SAVEB
00019Cr 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
00019Er 2  68                       pla
00019Fr 2  60                       rts
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 1               .include "storage.s"    ; The storage task (page 0 part)
0001A0r 2               .debuginfo
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 2               .segment "IO_P0"
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
0001A0r 2               ; The storage task (STORAGE_TASK_NUM): a Resident task, started at boot like the drivers, that owns the
0001A0r 2               ; SPI bus and serves /dev/sd (and later the FAT32 filesystem).  Its code is on BIOS ROM page 3 (spi.s,
0001A0r 2               ; sd.s, sd_srv.s); this is the page 0 part: its DriverInfo, init, and the gates into page 3.
0001A0r 2               
0001A0r 2               STORAGE_DRIVER:
0001A0r 2  rr rr                    .word       STORAGE_INIT                        ; DriverInfo::init
0001A2r 2  rr rr                    .word       PIPE_STOP                           ; DriverInfo::stop (nothing to do)
0001A4r 2  rr rr                    .word       STORAGE_DNAME                       ; DriverInfo::name
0001A6r 2  07 53 54 4F  NamedHString STORAGE_DNAME, "STORAGE"
0001AAr 2  52 41 47 45  
0001AEr 2  73 64 00     SD_NAME:    .byte   "sd", 0
0001B1r 2               
0001B1r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     STORAGE_INIT3,  PAGE3::STORAGE_INIT3,   3
0001B5r 2  rr 03        
0001B7r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     SD_SERVE,       PAGE3::SD_SERVE,        3
0001BBr 2  rr 03        
0001BDr 2               
0001BDr 2               ; Runs in the storage task: the block cache and SPI (page 3), then register /dev/sd.  (The card isn't
0001BDr 2               ; touched until /dev/sd is opened.)  OUT: C = 0; or C = 1, .A = error
0001BDr 2               STORAGE_INIT:
0001BDr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         STORAGE_INIT3
0001C0r 2  B0 11                    bcs         @done
0001C2r 2  A9 rr 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR   SD_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
