00000Cr 1               .include "sound.s"
00000Cr 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00000Cr 2               ; Sound driver.  Runs in its own Resident task (SOUND_TASK_NUM, started by DRV_START at boot), so its
00000Cr 2               ; ZP is task ZP.  Other tasks call it through the SND_CALL_* gates, which run the routine in the
00000Cr 2               ; sound task with TASK_CALL, or through its file, /dev/snd (snd_srv.s, on ROM page 2).
00000Cr 2               
00000Cr 2               SOUND_DRIVER:
00000Cr 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_DRV_INIT              ; DriverInfo::init
00000Er 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_STOP                  ; DriverInfo::stop
000010r 2  rr rr                        .word       SOUND_NAME                  ; DriverInfo::name
000012r 2  05 53 4F 55  NamedHString SOUND_NAME, "SOUND"
000016r 2  4E 44        
000018r 2  73 6E 64 00  SND_NAME:       .byte       "snd", 0
00001Cr 2               
00001Cr 2               ; Gates into the sound task: .A/.X/.Y/C pass through to the routine and back
00001Cr 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_INIT, SOUND_INIT, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000020r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000024r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
00002Dr 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_TEST, SOUND_TEST, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000031r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000035r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
00003Er 2  48 A9 rr 85  TASK_GATE           SND_CALL_YM_WRITE, YM_WRITE, SOUND_TASK_NUM
000042r 2  rr A9 rr 85  
000046r 2  rr A9 0E 85  
00004Fr 2               
00004Fr 2               ; The serve routine (page 2, snd_srv.s), and the test tune (page 2, snd_test.s; its ZP is in zero.s)
00004Fr 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     SND_SERVE,      PAGE2::SND_SERVE,       2
000053r 2  rr 02        
000055r 2  20 rr rr rr  FAR_GATE_INLINE     SOUND_TEST,     PAGE2::SOUND_TEST,      2
000059r 2  rr 02        
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               ; Driver init (runs in the sound task): the chip, then the file.  OUT: C = 0, or C = 1 and .A = error
00005Br 2               SOUND_DRV_INIT:
00005Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         SOUND_INIT
00005Er 2  A9 rr 85 rr                  LOAD_ADDR   SND_SERVE, ZP_TC_VEC
000062r 2  A9 rr 85 rr  
000066r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SND_NAME
000068r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SND_NAME
00006Ar 2  A2 0E                        ldx         #SOUND_TASK_NUM
00006Cr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         DEV_REGISTER
00006Fr 2               
00006Fr 2               ; zero out all YM-2151 registers $28-$FF
00006Fr 2               SOUND_INIT:
00006Fr 2  48                           pha
000070r 2  DA                           phx
000071r 2  A9 00                        lda         #0
000073r 2  A2 14                        ldx         #$14        ; turn off the clocks
000075r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000078r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28
00007Ar 2               
00007Ar 2               @write_z:
00007Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00007Dr 2  B0 0C                        bcs         @error
00007Fr 2  E8                           inx
000080r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @write_z
000082r 2  A2 03                        ldx         #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SOUND
000084r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000086r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000088r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         IRQ_REGISTER            ; Handler runs in this (the sound) task
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2               @error:
00008Br 2  FA                           plx
00008Cr 2  68                           pla
00008Dr 2  60                           rts
00008Er 2               
00008Er 2               SOUND_STOP:
00008Er 2  18                           clc
00008Fr 2  60                           rts
000090r 2               
000090r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if the interrupt was claimed
000090r 2               SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER:
000090r 2                               ; check which
000090r 2  18                           clc
000091r 2  60                           rts
000092r 2               
000092r 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64 * CPU_CLOCK_MULT    ; Busy-wait loop count (<= 128: the loop ends when .Y goes negative)
000092r 2               
000092r 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
000092r 2               YM_WRITE:
000092r 2  78                           sei
000093r 2  5A                           phy
000094r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000096r 2               
000096r 2               @ym_wait1:
000096r 2  88                           dey
000097r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
000099r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
00009Cr 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
00009Er 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
0000A1r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
0000A3r 2               
0000A3r 2               @ym_wait2:
0000A3r 2  88                           dey
0000A4r 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
0000A6r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
0000A9r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
0000ABr 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
0000AEr 2  18                           clc
0000AFr 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
0000B1r 2               
0000B1r 2               @timeout:
0000B1r 2  38                           sec
0000B2r 2               
0000B2r 2               @cleanup:
0000B2r 2  7A                           ply
0000B3r 2  58                           cli
0000B4r 2  60                           rts
0000B5r 2

0000B5r 1               .include "os_main.s"
0000B5r 2               .debuginfo
0000B5r 2               .macro ZERO_W
0000B5r 2                           lda     #0
0000B5r 2                           sta     W_REGISTER
0000B5r 2               .endmacro
0000B5r 2               
0000B5r 2               .segment "BIOS_P0"
000000r 2               RESET_VECTOR_START:
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              ZERO_W
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2  D8                       cld                                                     ; We don't like decimal mode
000006r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta                 T_REGISTER                          ; Make sure task 0 is selected
000009r 2  85 00                    sta                 $00                                 ; Init RAM Bank selector
00000Br 2  85 01                    sta                 $01                                 ; Init ROM Bank selector
00000Dr 2  A2 FF                    ldx                 #$FF                                ; Init stack pointer
00000Fr 2  9A                       txs
000010r 2               
000010r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST                                ; Power-on self test (polled serial, no IRQs)
000013r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 IRQ_INIT                            ; Must be first: IRQ tables and vectors
000016r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 TASKS_INIT                          ; Must be called before the drivers and MMU_INIT
000019r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 MMU_INIT
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 IO_INIT                             ; The IO layer's devices (/dev/null, /dev/zero)
00001Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 VIA_INIT
000022r 2  A9 rr                    lda                 #<SERIAL_DRIVER                     ; Serial driver in its own (Resident) task;
000024r 2  A0 rr                    ldy                 #>SERIAL_DRIVER                     ;   first: it must be started before
000026r 2  A2 0F                    ldx                 #SERIAL_TASK_NUM                    ;   anything prints (DRV_BOOT too)
000028r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DRV_START
00002Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DO_WELCOME                          ; (It clears the screen: before DRV_BOOT's reports)
00002Er 2  A9 rr                    lda                 #<SOUND_DRIVER                      ; Sound driver in its own (Resident) task
000030r 2  A0 rr                    ldy                 #>SOUND_DRIVER
000032r 2  A2 0E                    ldx                 #SOUND_TASK_NUM
000034r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DRV_BOOT
000037r 2  A9 rr                    lda                 #<PIPE_DRIVER                       ; Pipe server in its own (Resident) task
000039r 2  A0 rr                    ldy                 #>PIPE_DRIVER
00003Br 2  A2 0D                    ldx                 #PIPE_TASK_NUM
00003Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DRV_BOOT
000040r 2  A9 rr                    lda                 #<STORAGE_DRIVER                    ; Storage (/dev/sd) in its own (Resident) task
000042r 2  A0 rr                    ldy                 #>STORAGE_DRIVER
000044r 2  A2 0C                    ldx                 #STORAGE_TASK_NUM
000046r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DRV_BOOT
000049r 2                           ;jsr                 SND_CALL_TEST
000049r 2               
000049r 2               ; Start the shell in its own task (the default serial-capture task), start the scheduler's tick, and
000049r 2               ; hand the CPU over
000049r 2  A9 rr                    lda                 #<SHELL_MAIN
00004Br 2  A0 rr                    ldy                 #>SHELL_MAIN
00004Dr 2  A2 01                    ldx                 #SHELL_TASK_NUM
00004Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 TASK_PREPARE
000052r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 SCHED_START
000055r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 YIELD
000058r 2               
000058r 2               ; The system task is the idle task: the scheduler only runs it when no other task can run
000058r 2               @idle:
000058r 2  CB                       wai
000059r 2  80 FD                    bra                 @idle
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               ; ****************************************************************************
00005Br 2               ; Power-on self test.  Checks the memory mapping the task system depends on, using only polled serial
00005Br 2               ; output (no IRQs, no drivers) and task switches that don't touch the stack.  Prints e.g.:
00005Br 2               ;       POST ZP:T ST:T LO:T 7D:T SH:S P1:4C
00005Br 2               ;   ZP/ST/LO/7D: $0080 / $0180 / $0280 / $7D80 are per-Task (T) or common to all tasks (C)
00005Br 2               ;   SH: shared RAM bank $F0 (U = 0) is Shared between tasks (S) or not (X)
00005Br 2               ;   P1: byte at $EA00 on ROM page 1 (4C expected: the jmp at forth_main)
00005Br 2               ; and a second line of paged RAM line tests (hex masks of bad lines, all 0 when good):
00005Br 2               ;       RAM U:0 F0:0/00/0000 F4:0/00/0000 F8:0/00/0000 FC:0/00/0000 00:0/00/0000 10:0/00/0000 ...
00005Br 2               ;   U:x      U lines U0-U3 (bit n = Un), tested on shared bank $F0
00005Br 2               ;   bb:x/dd/aaaa  bank bb at $8000-$9FFF: bank register lines 0-3 (bb ^ 1/2/4/8), data lines D0-D7 and
00005Br 2               ;            address lines A0-A12.  Tested: the first bank of each shared RAM chip ($F0, $F4, $F8, $FC, with
00005Br 2               ;            U = 0; a missing chip shows as bad lines) and of each installed task RAM module.
00005Br 2               ;   Destructive: run before anything is kept in paged RAM.
00005Br 2               POST_ACIA_CMD   = ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID | ACIA_CMD_BIT_RID    ; No IRQs
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               .macro _M_POST_TASK_TEST    addr, label
00005Br 2                           ldx                 #label - POST_STRINGS
00005Br 2                           jsr                 POST_PUTS
00005Br 2                           lda                 #$A5
00005Br 2                           stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0 (no stack use until back in task 0)
00005Br 2                           sta                 addr
00005Br 2                           ldx                 #1
00005Br 2                           stx                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 1
00005Br 2                           lda                 #$5A
00005Br 2                           sta                 addr
00005Br 2                           stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0
00005Br 2                           lda                 addr
00005Br 2                           ldx                 #'T'
00005Br 2                           cmp                 #$A5
00005Br 2                           beq                 :+
00005Br 2                           ldx                 #'C'
00005Br 2               :
00005Br 2                           txa
00005Br 2                           jsr                 POST_PUTC
00005Br 2               .endmacro
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               POST:
00005Br 2  08                       php
00005Cr 2  78                       sei
00005Dr 2  A9 1F                    lda                 #$10 | SR_SELECT                    ; 8-N-1
00005Fr 2  8D rr rr                 sta                 ACIA_R_CTRL
000062r 2  A9 0B                    lda                 #POST_ACIA_CMD
000064r 2  8D rr rr                 sta                 ACIA_R_CMD
000067r 2  A2 00 20 rr              _M_POST_TASK_TEST   $0080, POST_S_ZP
00006Br 2  rr A9 A5 9C  
00006Fr 2  F0 FF 85 80  
00008Dr 2  A2 0B 20 rr              _M_POST_TASK_TEST   $0180, POST_S_ST
000091r 2  rr A9 A5 9C  
000095r 2  F0 FF 8D 80  
0000B6r 2  A2 10 20 rr              _M_POST_TASK_TEST   $0280, POST_S_LO
0000BAr 2  rr A9 A5 9C  
0000BEr 2  F0 FF 8D 80  
0000DFr 2  A2 15 20 rr              _M_POST_TASK_TEST   $7D80, POST_S_7D
0000E3r 2  rr A9 A5 9C  
0000E7r 2  F0 FF 8D 80  
000108r 2               
000108r 2  A2 1A                    ldx                 #POST_S_SH - POST_STRINGS
00010Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTS
00010Dr 2  9C F1 FF                 stz                 U_REGISTER
000110r 2  A2 F0                    ldx                 #$F0
000112r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0: shared bank $F0
000115r 2  86 rr                    stx                 RAM_BANK_REG
000117r 2  A9 C3                    lda                 #$C3
000119r 2  8D 00 80                 sta                 $8000
00011Cr 2  A9 01                    lda                 #1
00011Er 2  8D F0 FF                 sta                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 1: shared bank $F0
000121r 2  86 rr                    stx                 RAM_BANK_REG
000123r 2  AD 00 80                 lda                 $8000
000126r 2  64 rr                    stz                 RAM_BANK_REG
000128r 2  9C F0 FF                 stz                 T_REGISTER                          ; Task 0
00012Br 2  64 rr                    stz                 RAM_BANK_REG
00012Dr 2  A2 53                    ldx                 #'S'
00012Fr 2  C9 C3                    cmp                 #$C3
000131r 2  F0 02                    beq                 :+
000133r 2  A2 58                    ldx                 #'X'
000135r 2               :
000135r 2  8A                       txa
000136r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000139r 2               
000139r 2  A2 1F                    ldx                 #POST_S_P1 - POST_STRINGS
00013Br 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTS
00013Er 2  A9 EA 85 rr              LOAD_ADDR           $EA00, ZP_D_XAM
000142r 2  A9 00 85 rr  
000146r 2  A9 01                    lda                 #1
000148r 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_D_PAGE
00014Ar 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 PEEK_D_XAM                          ; Byte at $EA00 on ROM page 1
00014Dr 2  64 rr                    stz                 ZP_D_PAGE
00014Fr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTBYTE
000152r 2               
000152r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_RAM_TEST                       ; Paged RAM lines (page 2, post_ram.s)
000155r 2  A2 24                    ldx                 #POST_S_CRLF - POST_STRINGS
000157r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTS
00015Ar 2  28                       plp
00015Br 2  60                       rts
00015Cr 2               
00015Cr 2               ; Print the POST string at offset .X in POST_STRINGS.  Modifies: .A, .X, .Y
00015Cr 2               POST_PUTS:
00015Cr 2  BD rr rr                 lda                 POST_STRINGS,X
00015Fr 2  F0 06                    beq                 :+
000161r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTC
000164r 2  E8                       inx
000165r 2  80 F5                    bra                 POST_PUTS
000167r 2               :
000167r 2  60                       rts
000168r 2               
000168r 2               ; Modifies: .X, .Y
000168r 2               POST_PUTBYTE:
000168r 2  48                       pha
000169r 2  4A                       lsr
00016Ar 2  4A                       lsr
00016Br 2  4A                       lsr
00016Cr 2  4A                       lsr
00016Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 POST_PUTHEX
000170r 2  68                       pla
000171r 2               
000171r 2               POST_PUTHEX:
000171r 2  29 0F                    and                 #$0F
000173r 2  AA                       tax
000174r 2  BD rr rr                 lda                 HEX_MAP,X
000177r 2               
000177r 2               ; Polled serial output (Rockwell 65C51: wait for TDRE, with a timeout; WDC 65C51: its TDRE always says
000177r 2               ; empty, so wait a character's time after each byte).  Modifies: .Y
000177r 2               POST_PUTC:
000177r 2  A0 00                    ldy                 #0
000179r 2               :
000179r 2  48                       pha
00017Ar 2  AD rr rr                 lda                 ACIA_R_STATUS
00017Dr 2  29 10                    and                 #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
00017Fr 2  D0 05                    bne                 :+
000181r 2  68                       pla
000182r 2  88                       dey
000183r 2  D0 F4                    bne                 :-
000185r 2  48                       pha
000186r 2               :
000186r 2  68                       pla
000187r 2  8D rr rr                 sta                 ACIA_R_DATA
00018Ar 2               .if SER_ACIA = SER_ACIA_WDC                                         ; Its TDRE always says empty: wait a
00018Ar 2                           phx                                                     ;   whole character's time
00018Ar 2                           ldx                 #(SER_CHAR_CYCLES + 1279) / 1280    ; (1280 cycles per .X)
00018Ar 2                           ldy                 #0
00018Ar 2               :
00018Ar 2                           dey
00018Ar 2                           bne                 :-
00018Ar 2                           dex
00018Ar 2                           bne                 :-
00018Ar 2                           plx
00018Ar 2               .else
00018Ar 2  A0 00                    ldy                 #0                                  ; Short delay after each byte
00018Cr 2               :
00018Cr 2  88                       dey
00018Dr 2  D0 FD                    bne                 :-
00018Fr 2               .endif
00018Fr 2  60                       rts
000190r 2               
000190r 2               POST_STRINGS:
000190r 2  0D 0A 50 4F  POST_S_ZP:  .byte ASCII_CR, ASCII_LF, "POST ZP:", 0
000194r 2  53 54 20 5A  
000198r 2  50 3A 00     
00019Br 2  20 53 54 3A  POST_S_ST:  .byte " ST:", 0
00019Fr 2  00           
0001A0r 2  20 4C 4F 3A  POST_S_LO:  .byte " LO:", 0
0001A4r 2  00           
0001A5r 2  20 37 44 3A  POST_S_7D:  .byte " 7D:", 0
0001A9r 2  00           
0001AAr 2  20 53 48 3A  POST_S_SH:  .byte " SH:", 0
0001AEr 2  00           
0001AFr 2  20 50 31 3A  POST_S_P1:  .byte " P1:", 0
0001B3r 2  00           
0001B4r 2  0D 0A 00     POST_S_CRLF: .byte ASCII_CR, ASCII_LF, 0
0001B7r 2               
0001B7r 2               ; Start a driver at boot (DRV_START), and say so if its init fails: "<NAME> FAIL ee" (ee = the error).
0001B7r 2               ; The serial driver must be running already.  IN: .A.Y = DriverInfo, .X = task
0001B7r 2               DRV_BOOT:
0001B7r 2  48                       pha
0001B8r 2  5A                       phy
0001B9r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 DRV_START
0001BCr 2  90 3D                    bcc                 @done
0001BEr 2  7A                       ply
0001BFr 2  84 rr                    sty                 ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1                     ; The DriverInfo
0001C1r 2  7A                       ply
0001C2r 2  84 rr                    sty                 ZP_TEMP_VEC
0001C4r 2  48                       pha
0001C5r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0001C8r 2  A0 05                    ldy                 #DriverInfo::name + 1
0001CAr 2  B1 rr                    lda                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
0001CCr 2  AA                       tax
0001CDr 2  88                       dey
0001CEr 2  B1 rr                    lda                 (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
0001D0r 2  DA                       phx
0001D1r 2  7A                       ply
0001D2r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 WRITE_HSTRING                       ; Its name
0001D5r 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR          #' ', #'F', #'A', #'I', #'L', #' '
0001D9r 2  rr A9 46 20  
0001DDr 2  rr rr A9 41  
0001F3r 2  68                       pla
0001F4r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0001F7r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0001FAr 2  60                       rts
0001FBr 2               
0001FBr 2               @done:
0001FBr 2  7A                       ply
0001FCr 2  68                       pla
0001FDr 2  60                       rts
0001FEr 2               
0001FEr 2               DO_WELCOME:
0001FEr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr                 CLEAR_SCR
000201r 2  A9 rr A0 rr              _M_WRITE_HSTRING    HYDRA_WELCOME
000205r 2  20 rr rr     
000208r 2  4C rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF_JMP
00020Br 2               
00020Br 2               ; A: S/W interrupt number
00020Br 2               ; Preserves .X and V
00020Br 2               SW_INT:
00020Br 2  08                       php                                                     ; No task switch while V is ours (BRK runs
00020Cr 2  78                       sei                                                     ;   even with IRQs off)
00020Dr 2  DA                       phx
00020Er 2  AE F2 FF                 ldx                 V_REGISTER                          ; Save V (shared pseudo-register)
000211r 2  0A                       asl                                                     ; move int# to V[4..7]
000212r 2  0A                       asl
000213r 2  0A                       asl
000214r 2  0A                       asl
000215r 2  09 08                    ora                 #IRQ_NUMBER_SW                      ; S/W IRQ vector in V[0..3]
000217r 2  8D F2 FF                 sta                 V_REGISTER
00021Ar 2  00                       brk                                                     ; force an interrupt
00021Br 2  00                       .byte               $00                                 ; BRK signature byte (RTI returns past it)
00021Cr 2  8E F2 FF                 stx                 V_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior V
00021Fr 2  FA                       plx
000220r 2  28                       plp
000221r 2  60                       rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               ; Start of every other BIOS page: the RESET entry at $E000 zeroes W, and execution continues on page 0
000222r 2               ; right after RESET_VECTOR_START's ZERO_W.  The rest of each page is filled with NOPs by the linker
000222r 2               ; (fillval), except for the COMMON block and the vectors.
000222r 2               .macro OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000222r 2                           ZERO_W
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .segment "BIOS_P1"                                                  ; HyForth and the disassembler follow (page1.s)
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P2"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P3"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P4"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P5"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P6"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER
000004r 2  FF           
000005r 2               .segment "BIOS_P7"
000000r 2  A9 00 8D F3              OTHER_PAGE_FILLER

000005r 2               ; Gates from BIOS ROM page 0 to page 1 routines (HyForth and the disassembler, see page1.s), and to the
000005r 2               ; POST RAM tests on page 2 (post_ram.s)

000005r 2               ; (FAR_GATE_INLINE) gates, to save page 0 space.
